## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:05:29 PM

Saya akan susun sebagai peta kemampuan praktis untuk microservice Jersey/JAX-RS: bukan sekadar “library list”, tapi alur dari upload/download, parsing, mapping, validasi, processing, storage, sampai observability dan failure handling.

## Introduction

Topik ini bukan hanya soal “cara baca CSV/Excel/PDF”. Dalam Jersey/JAX-RS microservice, file handling biasanya menjadi **pipeline teknis**:

**HTTP upload/download → content detection → parsing/deserialization → mapping ke DTO/domain → validation → processing/enrichment → persistence/eventing → file generation/export → observability/audit/security.**

Untuk stack Anda, fokus praktisnya adalah bagaimana Java 17+ service memproses file secara aman, hemat memori, dapat diaudit, dan mudah diintegrasikan dengan PostgreSQL, MyBatis, Camunda, object storage, Kafka/RabbitMQ, maupun batch/background worker.

Beberapa fondasi teknologinya: Jakarta RESTful Web Services mendefinisikan API untuk REST service; Jersey menyediakan implementasi dan modul multipart untuk upload file; Jackson/JSON-B/JAXB menangani object mapping; Apache POI menangani dokumen Microsoft Office; PDFBox menangani PDF; Apache Tika berguna untuk deteksi tipe file dan ekstraksi konten lintas format; Apache Parquet Java mendukung read/write Parquet untuk workload data/analytics. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skills / domain | Fungsi utama di microservice | Library / teknologi umum | Hal yang perlu dikuasai |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| API file boundary | Upload, download, stream file | Menyediakan endpoint untuk menerima dan mengirim file | Jersey/JAX-RS, `InputStream`, `StreamingOutput`, multipart | `@Consumes`, `@Produces`, binary response, HTTP headers, filename encoding, status code |
| Multipart handling | File + metadata dalam satu request | Upload CSV/Excel/PDF dengan metadata JSON | `jersey-media-multipart`, `FormDataParam`, `FormDataMultiPart` | Register `MultiPartFeature`, stream file, batasi ukuran, validasi part metadata |
| Content negotiation | Representasi berbeda untuk resource yang sama | JSON/XML/PDF/CSV export dari endpoint yang sama | JAX-RS `MediaType`, `MessageBodyReader`, `MessageBodyWriter` | `Accept`, `Content-Type`, custom provider, fallback error |
| File intake validation | Validasi awal sebelum parsing | Mencegah file salah format, terlalu besar, atau berbahaya | Tika, custom validator, antivirus integration | MIME sniffing, extension mismatch, magic bytes, zip bomb check, reject/quarantine |
| Temporary file strategy | Menyimpan file sementara saat diproses | Menghindari heap memory penuh | Java NIO, temp directory, object storage staging | Disk quota, cleanup, lifecycle, encryption-at-rest |
| CSV parsing | Import/export data tabular sederhana | Import master data, reconciliation, bulk operation | Apache Commons CSV, OpenCSV, Jackson CSV | Delimiter, quote, escape, header mapping, encoding, malformed row |
| Excel processing | Import/export workbook bisnis | Report, template, data upload dari user bisnis | Apache POI XSSF/SXSSF/HSSF | Sheet/row/cell model, formula, date cell, streaming writer, memory issue |
| Word document processing | Generate/read DOCX | Template surat, dokumen legal/operasional | Apache POI XWPF, docx4j | Placeholder replacement, table generation, paragraph/run handling, style preservation |
| PDF extraction | Extract text/metadata dari PDF | Search indexing, OCR-adjacent processing, validation dokumen | Apache PDFBox, Tika | Text extraction, page iteration, encrypted PDF, scanned vs text PDF |
| PDF generation | Generate dokumen final | Invoice, agreement, official report | PDFBox, OpenPDF, iText commercial option | Layout, font embedding, pagination, signature, PDF/A requirement |
| JSON serialization | API payload, config, event payload | Object ↔ JSON | Jackson, JSON-B | DTO mapping, Java time module, enum strategy, unknown fields, null handling |
| XML serialization | Legacy integration, standards-based payload | Object ↔ XML | Jakarta XML Binding/JAXB, Jackson XML, StAX | XSD, namespace, marshalling/unmarshalling, secure XML parsing |
| Streaming XML | XML besar tanpa load full DOM | Import file besar, extract node tertentu | StAX, SAX, Woodstox | Event-driven parsing, namespace-aware parsing, XXE prevention |
| Parquet processing | Columnar file untuk data platform | Export/import analytics dataset | Apache Parquet Java, Avro, Hadoop FS APIs | Schema, compression, row group, column projection, predicate pushdown |
| Generic content extraction | Ekstraksi teks/metadata lintas format | Indexing, preview, classification | Apache Tika | Detect content type, extract metadata, parse many document formats via one interface |
| Canonical mapping | Menyamakan berbagai file menjadi domain model | CSV/Excel/XML/JSON masuk ke command/domain yang sama | MapStruct, manual mapper, Jackson, validation layer | Raw record model, normalized DTO, domain command, field lineage |
| Schema/versioning | Mengelola perubahan format file | Backward compatibility import/export | JSON Schema, XSD, Avro schema, custom CSV schema | Version column, optional fields, deprecation, migration adapter |
| Validation pipeline | Menolak atau menandai data bermasalah | Data quality sebelum persist | Jakarta Bean Validation, custom rules | Required field, type coercion, cross-field validation, row-level error |
| Error reporting | Memberi feedback file upload | Menghasilkan error report CSV/Excel/PDF | Commons CSV, POI | Row number, column name, original value, error code, fix suggestion |
| Batch processing | File besar diproses async | Import ribuan/jutaan row | Executor, queue, Kafka/RabbitMQ, Camunda | Job status, progress, retry, checkpoint, cancellation |
| Idempotency | Aman saat retry upload/import | Mencegah duplicate insert/process | PostgreSQL unique key, checksum, idempotency key | File hash, row hash, business key, replay detection |
| Persistence integration | Simpan hasil parse ke DB | Insert/update data hasil import | MyBatis, PostgreSQL, COPY, batch insert | Transaction boundary, batch size, upsert, partial failure |
| Large file strategy | Hemat memory dan stabil di Kubernetes | Menghindari OOM dan timeout | Streaming parser, temp file, object storage | Backpressure, chunking, memory limit, pod eviction risk |
| Export generation | Membuat file hasil query | Report download, reconciliation output | CSV/Excel/PDF/JSON/XML/Parquet libs | Cursor query, streaming response, paging, compression |
| Security | Mencegah exploit via file | Hardening file ingestion | Tika, antivirus, XML secure parser, zip constraints | XXE, zip bomb, macro risk, path traversal, content spoofing |
| Observability | Melacak performa dan kegagalan | Operasional import/export | Micrometer/OpenTelemetry/logging | Parse duration, row count, failed row count, file size, memory usage |
| Audit & lineage | Regulatory defensibility | Siapa upload apa, kapan, hasilnya apa | PostgreSQL audit table, object storage metadata | Original file retention, checksum, version, status history |
| Testing strategy | Validasi parser/generator | Mencegah regression format | JUnit, golden files, Testcontainers | Fixture files, malformed files, property-based test, compatibility test |
| Deployment concern | Runtime behavior di Docker/K8s | File processing stabil di container | Docker, Kubernetes, JVM flags | `/tmp` size, ephemeral storage, CPU/memory request-limit, timeout ingress |

---

## Format-Specific Practical Notes

### 1. CSV / TSV

CSV tampak sederhana, tetapi banyak bug produksi muncul dari encoding, delimiter, quote, newline, empty value, dan header mismatch. Apache Commons CSV menyediakan model `CSVFormat` untuk parsing dan printing CSV; dokumentasinya menekankan pemilihan format sebagai langkah awal parsing. ([Apache Commons](https://commons.apache.org/proper/commons-csv/apidocs/index.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

**Skill penting:**

| Skill | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| Header-based mapping | Jangan mapping by index kalau format bisa berubah |
| Encoding control | UTF-8, BOM, Windows-1252 bisa menghasilkan karakter rusak |
| Row-level validation | Import tidak harus gagal total karena 1 row buruk |
| Error report generation | User bisnis perlu tahu row mana yang salah |
| Streaming parse | File besar jangan di-load seluruhnya |

---

### 2. Excel XLS/XLSX

Apache POI adalah API Java untuk manipulasi format Microsoft Office berbasis OOXML dan OLE2, termasuk Excel, Word, dan PowerPoint. ([Apache POI](https://poi.apache.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

**Skill penting:**

| Skill | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| XLS vs XLSX | Format lama dan baru punya model berbeda |
| Cell type handling | Numeric/date/string/formula sering keliru terbaca |
| Formula handling | Perlu tahu kapan formula dievaluasi atau hanya dibaca |
| SXSSF streaming writer | Penting untuk export Excel besar |
| Template-based report | Biasanya lebih realistis daripada generate layout dari nol |

---

### 3. JSON

Untuk JSON, ada dua jalur umum: **Jackson** dan **Jakarta JSON-B**. Jackson adalah suite data-processing untuk JVM dengan parser/generator streaming, databinding, dan modul format seperti CSV/XML/Avro/Protobuf/YAML; JSON-B adalah standar Jakarta untuk binding Java object ↔ JSON. ([GitHub](https://github.com/fasterxml/jackson?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

**Skill penting:**

| Skill | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| DTO boundary | Jangan expose entity database langsung |
| Unknown field policy | Tentukan strict vs lenient |
| Null handling | Bedakan absent, explicit null, default value |
| Java time handling | `Instant`, `OffsetDateTime`, timezone harus konsisten |
| Streaming JSON / NDJSON | Penting untuk event/log/export besar |

---

### 4. XML

Jakarta XML Binding menyediakan API dan tools untuk mapping otomatis antara XML document dan Java object. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/xml-binding/3.0/jakarta-xml-binding-spec-3.0?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk XML besar, streaming parser seperti StAX/Woodstox lebih cocok daripada DOM karena tidak perlu memuat seluruh dokumen ke memory.

**Skill penting:**

| Skill | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| Namespace governance | Banyak integrasi XML gagal karena namespace |
| XSD validation | Penting untuk kontrak legacy/enterprise |
| JAXB mapping | Cocok untuk XML contract-first |
| StAX/SAX parsing | Cocok untuk XML besar |
| XXE prevention | Wajib untuk security parser XML |

---

### 5. PDF

PDF punya dua mode besar: **extraction** dan **generation**. Apache PDFBox mendukung ekstraksi Unicode text, split/merge, form filling, PDF creation, save as image, dan digital signing. ([Apache PDFBox](https://pdfbox.apache.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

**Skill penting:**

| Skill | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| Text PDF vs scanned PDF | PDFBox tidak otomatis menyelesaikan OCR scanned image |
| Page-level extraction | Berguna untuk audit dan debugging |
| PDF form extraction | Banyak dokumen enterprise berbasis form |
| Font embedding | Penting agar output konsisten |
| PDF/A / signature | Sering relevan untuk regulatory document |

---

### 6. Word DOC/DOCX

Untuk Word, pilihan umum adalah Apache POI XWPF atau docx4j. Gunakan Word generation terutama untuk **template-driven document**: surat, kontrak, memo, dokumen approval, atau report naratif.

**Skill penting:**

| Skill | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| Placeholder replacement | Hati-hati karena placeholder bisa terpecah antar run |
| Table generation | Banyak dokumen bisnis berisi tabel dinamis |
| Style preservation | Jangan rusak format template |
| Image insertion | Perlu untuk logo, QR, tanda tangan |
| Convert-to-PDF strategy | Biasanya butuh pipeline terpisah |

---

### 7. Parquet

Apache Parquet adalah format column-oriented untuk storage/retrieval efisien; `parquet-java` menyediakan library read/write Parquet di aplikasi Java, termasuk utilitas schema management dan data conversion. ([Parquet](https://parquet.apache.org/docs/overview/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

**Skill penting:**

| Skill | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| Columnar mental model | Cocok untuk analytics, bukan transaksi row-by-row |
| Schema evolution | Field addition/removal perlu governance |
| Compression | Snappy/ZSTD/Gzip memengaruhi ukuran dan CPU |
| Row group | Berpengaruh ke read performance |
| Predicate pushdown | Mengurangi data yang dibaca |

---

## Recommended Microservice Architecture Pattern

```text
POST /files/import
        │
        ▼
[API Layer: Jersey Multipart]
        │
        ▼
[Intake Validator]
- size
- MIME
- extension
- checksum
- tenant/user authorization
        │
        ▼
[Staging Storage]
- temp disk
- object storage
- DB metadata
        │
        ▼
[Async Job Created]
- job_id
- status = PENDING
        │
        ▼
[Worker Pipeline]
- detect format
- parse stream
- map raw record
- validate
- enrich
- persist batch
- collect row errors
        │
        ▼
[Result]
- SUCCESS / PARTIAL_SUCCESS / FAILED
- imported rows
- rejected rows
- error report file
        │
        ▼
GET /files/import/{jobId}
GET /files/import/{jobId}/errors
GET /files/import/{jobId}/original
```

Untuk file kecil dan synchronous use case, endpoint langsung parse masih bisa diterima. Untuk file besar, regulatory-sensitive, atau import yang menyentuh banyak entity, gunakan **job-based async processing** supaya timeout HTTP, retry, audit, dan failure recovery lebih terkendali.

---

## Mental Model: Format vs Use Case

| Use case | Format yang umum | Processing style yang disarankan |
|---|---|---|
| Bulk import data operasional | CSV, Excel | Streaming parse + row validation + batch insert |
| Business report | Excel, PDF | Query cursor + streaming export |
| Legal/formal document | PDF, Word | Template generation + audit metadata |
| API payload | JSON, XML | DTO mapping + schema/versioning |
| Legacy enterprise integration | XML, fixed-width, CSV | Contract-first + strict validation |
| Search indexing | PDF, Word, Excel | Tika/PDFBox extraction + metadata enrichment |
| Data lake / analytics | Parquet | Batch export + schema registry/governance |
| Error feedback ke user | CSV, Excel | Row-level error report |

---

## Key Design Decisions

| Decision | Pilihan A | Pilihan B | Rekomendasi praktis |
|---|---|---|---|
| Parse mode | In-memory | Streaming | Default ke streaming, terutama upload/export besar |
| Import execution | Synchronous | Async job | Async untuk file besar, multi-step, atau high-risk |
| Error handling | Fail-fast | Partial success | Partial success untuk bulk import bisnis; fail-fast untuk contract/system payload |
| Mapping | Direct file → entity | File → DTO → domain command | Gunakan DTO/canonical model agar schema file tidak bocor ke domain |
| Storage | DB only | Object storage + DB metadata | Simpan original file di object storage, metadata/status di DB |
| Validation | Setelah persist | Sebelum persist | Validasi sebelum persist; gunakan staging table jika perlu |
| Export | Build full file in memory | Stream response/temp file | Stream atau temp file untuk output besar |
| Schema change | Ad-hoc | Versioned schema | Gunakan versioning sejak awal |

---

## Failure Modes yang Wajib Diantisipasi

| Failure mode | Contoh | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| OOM karena file besar | Excel export ratusan ribu row | Streaming writer, pagination, temp file |
| Wrong MIME | `.csv` berisi executable atau HTML | MIME sniffing + extension validation |
| Encoding rusak | Nama orang/alamat jadi mojibake | Explicit charset, BOM handling |
| Partial data corruption | Sebagian row valid, sebagian gagal | Row-level error model |
| Duplicate import | User retry upload file sama | File hash + idempotency key |
| Timeout HTTP | Import > gateway timeout | Async job |
| Broken schema | Header berubah tanpa notice | Schema versioning + strict header validation |
| XML attack | XXE/entity expansion | Secure parser config |
| Zip bomb | DOCX/XLSX/PDF compressed payload ekstrem | Compression ratio limit, max entry size |
| Lost audit trail | File original tidak tersimpan | Store original + checksum + actor + timestamp |

---

## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:13:45 PM

Kita mulai **Seri 1 dari 20**. Saya akan fokus ke boundary HTTP di Jersey/JAX-RS: bagaimana file masuk/keluar service dengan aman, streaming-friendly, dan siap dipakai oleh pipeline import/export berikutnya.

# Seri 1/20 - Jersey/JAX-RS File Boundary: Upload, Download, Multipart, Streaming Response

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **boundary HTTP** untuk file dalam Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice:

```text
Client
  │
  ├─ upload file: multipart/form-data
  ├─ download file: binary/streaming response
  ├─ metadata: JSON/form field/header
  └─ status/error contract
        │
        ▼
Jersey/JAX-RS Resource
        │
        ▼
File Intake / Parser / Storage / Async Job
```

JAX-RS/Jakarta REST dirancang sebagai API HTTP-centric dan format-independent: framework menyediakan mapping HTTP/resource/provider, sementara format seperti CSV, Excel, PDF, JSON, XML, dan lainnya diproses melalui provider atau library aplikasi. Ini penting karena file boundary sebaiknya **tidak langsung mencampur parsing bisnis**, tetapi hanya menerima file, validasi awal, lalu menyerahkan ke pipeline processing. ([jakarta.ee](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text
HTTP File Boundary
│
├─ Upload Boundary
│   ├─ multipart/form-data
│   ├─ file part
│   ├─ metadata part
│   ├─ content-type
│   └─ filename
│
├─ Download Boundary
│   ├─ Response entity
│   ├─ StreamingOutput
│   ├─ Content-Type
│   ├─ Content-Disposition
│   └─ cache / range / retry concern
│
├─ Contract Boundary
│   ├─ request validation
│   ├─ error response
│   ├─ job response
│   └─ audit correlation id
│
└─ Runtime Boundary
    ├─ memory safety
    ├─ timeout
    ├─ temp file
    ├─ async handoff
    └─ observability
```

Boundary yang baik tidak bertugas “menyelesaikan semua”. Ia bertugas **menerima file secara aman, membuat kontrak yang jelas, lalu menyerahkan file ke pipeline berikutnya**.

---

## 3. Dua Pendekatan Multipart di Jersey/JAX-RS

| Pendekatan | Cocok untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| **Jersey-specific multipart** | Aplikasi yang eksplisit menggunakan Jersey | Menggunakan `jersey-media-multipart`, `@FormDataParam`, `FormDataMultiPart`, `FormDataContentDisposition` |
| **Jakarta REST multipart API** | Aplikasi yang ingin lebih portable antar implementation Jakarta REST modern | Jakarta REST 3.1+ menambahkan dukungan multipart API; Jersey 3.1.x juga mendokumentasikan penggunaan `EntityPart` |

Jersey menyediakan module `jersey-media-multipart` untuk membaca/menulis multipart entity. Dokumentasi Jersey menyebut module ini memanfaatkan `MessageBodyReader`/`MessageBodyWriter`, sehingga setiap body part dapat diproses berdasarkan media type masing-masing. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/media.html))

Untuk Jersey 3.1.x, dokumentasi menyebut `jersey-media-multipart` dependency dan menyatakan bahwa mulai Jersey 3.1.0 `MultiPartFeature` tidak perlu diregister manual karena sudah otomatis, sedangkan versi sebelum 3.1.0 membutuhkan registrasi feature. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/media.html))

---

## 4. Maven Dependency

Untuk Jersey multipart:

```xml
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.media</groupId>
    <artifactId>jersey-media-multipart</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Gunakan version dari **Jersey BOM / dependency management** yang sama dengan runtime Jersey Anda. Jangan mencampur Jersey 2.x `javax.*` dengan Jersey 3.x/4.x `jakarta.*`.

Contoh dokumentasi Jersey 3.1.11 menggunakan artifact `org.glassfish.jersey.media:jersey-media-multipart`. Sonatype Central juga mencatat artifact ini sebagai “Jersey Multipart entity providers support module”. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/media.html))

---

## 5. Upload File Endpoint - Pattern Dasar

### 5.1 Endpoint sederhana

```java
package com.company.files.api;

import jakarta.ws.rs.Consumes;
import jakarta.ws.rs.POST;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Produces;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

import org.glassfish.jersey.media.multipart.FormDataContentDisposition;
import org.glassfish.jersey.media.multipart.FormDataParam;

import java.io.InputStream;

@Path("/files")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class FileResource {

    @POST
    @Path("/imports")
    @Consumes(MediaType.MULTIPART_FORM_DATA)
    public Response uploadImportFile(
            @FormDataParam("file") InputStream fileStream,
            @FormDataParam("file") FormDataContentDisposition fileMeta,
            @FormDataParam("metadata") String metadataJson
    ) {
        if (fileStream == null || fileMeta == null) {
            return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
                    .entity(new ErrorResponse("FILE_REQUIRED", "File part 'file' is required"))
                    .build();
        }

        String originalFilename = fileMeta.getFileName();

        // Jangan parse file besar langsung di resource method.
        // Idealnya: validate boundary -> stage file -> create async job.
        UploadAcceptedResponse result = new UploadAcceptedResponse(
                "job-123",
                originalFilename,
                "PENDING"
        );

        return Response.accepted(result).build();
    }

    public record UploadAcceptedResponse(
            String jobId,
            String filename,
            String status
    ) {}

    public record ErrorResponse(
            String code,
            String message
    ) {}
}
```

Jersey mendukung injection body part tertentu melalui `@FormDataParam`; dokumentasinya menyebut annotation ini digunakan bersama `multipart/form-data` untuk form yang berisi file, non-ASCII data, dan binary data. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/media.html))

---

## 6. Upload Contract yang Disarankan

Untuk file import di enterprise microservice, hindari endpoint seperti ini:

```text
POST /upload
```

Lebih baik eksplisit:

```text
POST /v1/customer-imports
POST /v1/order-imports
POST /v1/documents
POST /v1/reconciliation-files
```

Karena file biasanya bukan sekadar “file”, tetapi bagian dari domain workflow.

### Recommended request

```http
POST /v1/customer-imports
Content-Type: multipart/form-data

parts:
- file: customers.xlsx
- metadata:
  {
    "sourceSystem": "CRM",
    "schemaVersion": "2026-07",
    "dryRun": false
  }
```

### Recommended response

```json
{
  "jobId": "imp_20260713_000123",
  "status": "PENDING",
  "filename": "customers.xlsx",
  "submittedAt": "2026-07-13T14:30:00Z",
  "links": {
    "status": "/v1/customer-imports/imp_20260713_000123",
    "errors": "/v1/customer-imports/imp_20260713_000123/errors"
  }
}
```

Gunakan `202 Accepted` kalau prosesnya async. Gunakan `200 OK` hanya kalau file kecil dan benar-benar selesai diproses dalam request yang sama.

---

## 7. Download File Endpoint - StreamingOutput

`StreamingOutput` adalah tipe JAX-RS yang dapat dipakai sebagai return value atau entity dalam `Response` ketika aplikasi ingin menulis output secara streaming; method `write(OutputStream output)` dipanggil untuk menulis response body. ([jakarta.ee](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/core/streamingoutput))

### 7.1 Streaming CSV download

```java
package com.company.files.api;

import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Produces;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.HttpHeaders;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.StreamingOutput;

import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

@Path("/exports")
public class ExportResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/customers.csv")
    @Produces("text/csv")
    public Response downloadCustomersCsv() {
        StreamingOutput stream = output -> {
            try (BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(
                    new OutputStreamWriter(output, StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {

                writer.write("id,name,status");
                writer.newLine();

                // Simulasi. Real case: stream dari DB cursor/page.
                writer.write("1,Alice,ACTIVE");
                writer.newLine();

                writer.write("2,Bob,INACTIVE");
                writer.newLine();
            }
        };

        return Response.ok(stream, "text/csv")
                .header(HttpHeaders.CONTENT_DISPOSITION,
                        "attachment; filename=\"customers.csv\"")
                .build();
    }
}
```

Untuk export besar, jangan build seluruh file sebagai `String`, `byte[]`, atau `List<Row>` dulu. Stream dari database/page/cursor langsung ke `OutputStream`.

---

## 8. Header Penting untuk File Response

| Header | Fungsi | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| `Content-Type` | Tipe file yang dikirim | `text/csv`, `application/pdf`, `application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet` |
| `Content-Disposition` | Inline atau attachment + filename | `attachment; filename="report.xlsx"` |
| `Content-Length` | Ukuran file jika diketahui | Berguna untuk progress bar |
| `Cache-Control` | Kontrol caching | `no-store` untuk file sensitif |
| `ETag` | Deteksi versi/cache | Berguna untuk immutable generated file |
| `Last-Modified` | Metadata freshness | Berguna untuk file statis |
| `X-Correlation-Id` | Traceability | Hubungkan request, job, log, audit |

Untuk file sensitif seperti laporan regulatory, reconciliation, customer document, atau dokumen berisi PII, default aman biasanya:

```http
Cache-Control: no-store
Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="report.pdf"
```

---

## 9. Boundary Validation Checklist

| Validasi | Kenapa penting | Lokasi ideal |
|---|---|---|
| File part wajib ada | Request multipart bisa kosong/salah field | Resource/API layer |
| Filename tidak dipercaya | Mencegah path traversal dan spoofing | Resource/API layer |
| Ukuran file | Cegah OOM/disk penuh | Gateway + app |
| Content-Type | Indikasi awal format | Resource/API layer |
| MIME sniffing | Cegah extension spoofing | Intake service |
| Extension allowlist | Batasi format yang diterima | Intake service |
| Checksum | Idempotency dan audit | Intake service |
| Authz domain | User boleh upload tipe file ini? | Resource/service layer |
| Metadata schema | `schemaVersion`, `sourceSystem`, `dryRun` valid | Resource/service layer |
| Correlation ID | Trace upload sampai processing | Filter/API layer |

Prinsip penting: **filename, extension, dan client-provided Content-Type tidak boleh dipercaya sepenuhnya**. Anggap itu hanya hint; validasi ulang di intake pipeline.

---

## 10. Pattern: Resource Method Jangan Terlalu Gemuk

### Hindari

```text
Resource method
  ├─ receive file
  ├─ parse Excel
  ├─ validate rows
  ├─ call DB
  ├─ call Camunda
  ├─ generate error report
  └─ return result
```

Masalahnya:

| Risiko | Dampak |
|---|---|
| HTTP timeout | Client gagal padahal proses mungkin masih jalan |
| Sulit retry | Tidak jelas apakah data sudah masuk sebagian |
| Sulit audit | Boundary, parsing, persistence bercampur |
| OOM | File besar diproses dalam request |
| Transaction panjang | Lock DB lebih lama |
| Tidak scalable | Worker tidak bisa dipisah dari API pod |

### Lebih baik

```text
Resource method
  ├─ receive multipart
  ├─ validate boundary
  ├─ stage original file
  ├─ create import job
  └─ return 202 Accepted

Worker
  ├─ parse
  ├─ validate
  ├─ persist
  ├─ generate error report
  └─ update job status
```

---

## 11. Suggested Package Structure

```text
com.company.files
│
├─ api
│   ├─ FileImportResource.java
│   ├─ FileExportResource.java
│   └─ dto
│       ├─ UploadAcceptedResponse.java
│       ├─ ImportStatusResponse.java
│       └─ ErrorResponse.java
│
├─ application
│   ├─ FileIntakeService.java
│   ├─ ImportJobService.java
│   └─ ExportService.java
│
├─ domain
│   ├─ ImportJob.java
│   ├─ FileObject.java
│   ├─ FileChecksum.java
│   └─ ImportStatus.java
│
├─ infrastructure
│   ├─ storage
│   │   ├─ FileStorage.java
│   │   ├─ LocalFileStorage.java
│   │   └─ ObjectStorageFileStorage.java
│   ├─ db
│   │   └─ ImportJobMapper.java
│   └─ http
│       └─ CorrelationIdFilter.java
│
└─ worker
    └─ ImportWorker.java
```

Resource hanya mengurus HTTP boundary. Parsing CSV/Excel/PDF jangan diletakkan langsung di resource.

---

## 12. API Contract Pattern

### 12.1 Upload import

```http
POST /v1/import-jobs
Content-Type: multipart/form-data
```

Response:

```http
202 Accepted
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json
{
  "jobId": "job_001",
  "status": "PENDING",
  "filename": "customers.xlsx"
}
```

### 12.2 Check status

```http
GET /v1/import-jobs/{jobId}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "jobId": "job_001",
  "status": "PARTIAL_SUCCESS",
  "totalRows": 1000,
  "successRows": 970,
  "failedRows": 30,
  "links": {
    "errorReport": "/v1/import-jobs/job_001/errors.xlsx"
  }
}
```

### 12.3 Download error report

```http
GET /v1/import-jobs/{jobId}/errors.xlsx
```

Response:

```http
200 OK
Content-Type: application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet
Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="job_001_errors.xlsx"
```

---

## 13. Status Code Cheatsheet

| Scenario | Status |
|---|---:|
| Upload diterima untuk async processing | `202 Accepted` |
| Upload kecil selesai diproses langsung | `200 OK` |
| File part tidak ada | `400 Bad Request` |
| Metadata tidak valid | `400 Bad Request` |
| Format file tidak didukung | `415 Unsupported Media Type` |
| File terlalu besar | `413 Payload Too Large` |
| User tidak boleh upload tipe file/domain tersebut | `403 Forbidden` |
| Import job tidak ditemukan | `404 Not Found` |
| Conflict karena duplicate idempotency key | `409 Conflict` |
| Parser gagal karena file corrupt | `422 Unprocessable Content` |
| Internal storage/DB error | `500 Internal Server Error` |
| Worker dependency unavailable | `503 Service Unavailable` |

---

## 14. Error Response Contract

Gunakan error response yang konsisten:

```json
{
  "error": {
    "code": "UNSUPPORTED_FILE_TYPE",
    "message": "Only CSV and XLSX files are supported for customer import.",
    "correlationId": "corr-abc-123",
    "details": [
      {
        "field": "file",
        "reason": "Detected content type application/pdf"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Untuk file import, bedakan dua jenis error:

| Jenis error | Contoh | Response |
|---|---|---|
| Boundary error | File kosong, file terlalu besar, content type salah | Langsung `4xx` |
| Row-level error | Row 15 email invalid, row 30 duplicate | Job tetap berjalan, hasil `PARTIAL_SUCCESS` + error report |

---

## 15. Security Notes untuk Boundary

| Risiko | Contoh | Mitigasi awal |
|---|---|---|
| Path traversal | Filename `../../etc/passwd` | Jangan pakai filename client sebagai path |
| Extension spoofing | `report.pdf.exe` | Allowlist extension + MIME sniffing |
| Content-Type palsu | Client kirim `text/csv`, isi binary | Deteksi server-side |
| Oversized upload | File 5 GB masuk API pod | Limit di ingress + app |
| Zip bomb | XLSX/DOCX sangat terkompresi | Scan sebelum extract |
| PII leak via download | Browser/cache menyimpan file sensitif | `Cache-Control: no-store` |
| Broken access control | User download file job orang lain | Check ownership/tenant per job |

---

## 16. Senior-Level Invariants

Pegang invariant ini saat review desain file endpoint:

| Invariant | Maksud |
|---|---|
| Resource method tidak boleh memproses file besar secara blocking lama | Hindari timeout dan coupling |
| Original file harus punya checksum | Untuk idempotency, audit, dan dedup |
| Filename dari client tidak boleh menjadi filesystem path | Cegah path traversal |
| Download file sensitif harus punya authorization check | Jangan hanya bergantung pada URL sulit ditebak |
| Upload response harus punya correlation/job id | Debugging dan audit butuh trace |
| Error row harus menunjuk row/column/value | User bisnis butuh feedback yang bisa diperbaiki |
| Parser failure tidak boleh menghilangkan original file | Original file penting untuk forensic/audit |
| Content-Type dari client hanya hint | Validasi ulang server-side |

---

## 17. Mini Review Checklist

Sebelum endpoint file dianggap production-ready, cek ini:

```text
[ ] Endpoint punya nama domain-specific, bukan generic /upload
[ ] Multipart field names terdokumentasi
[ ] File size limit jelas
[ ] Supported media types jelas
[ ] Filename disanitasi
[ ] Content-Type divalidasi server-side
[ ] Original file distage sebelum processing
[ ] Ada checksum/idempotency strategy
[ ] Ada response 202 untuk async processing
[ ] Ada endpoint status job
[ ] Ada error report untuk partial failure
[ ] Ada structured error response
[ ] Ada audit actor, timestamp, filename, checksum
[ ] Ada correlation id di log dan response
[ ] Ada authz per tenant/domain/file
[ ] Download memakai Content-Disposition yang benar
[ ] Sensitive download memakai Cache-Control: no-store
```

---

## 18. Kesimpulan Seri 1

Boundary file di Jersey/JAX-RS harus diperlakukan sebagai **kontrak sistem**, bukan sekadar method upload/download.

Desain yang baik:

```text
Upload:
multipart request
  -> validate boundary
  -> stage original file
  -> create job
  -> return 202

Download:
authorize
  -> set correct headers
  -> stream output
  -> avoid full memory buffering
```

Untuk Jersey, `jersey-media-multipart` adalah fondasi umum untuk multipart; untuk download besar, `StreamingOutput` adalah primitive penting agar response bisa ditulis langsung ke `OutputStream` tanpa membangun seluruh payload di memory. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/media.html))

---

## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:15:41 PM

Lanjut **Seri 2 dari 20**. Fokusnya sekarang berpindah dari endpoint HTTP ke **file intake pipeline**: tahap “gerbang keamanan dan staging” sebelum file boleh masuk parser/domain processing.

# Seri 2/20 - File Intake Pipeline: Size Limit, MIME Detection, Checksum, Temporary Storage, Original File Retention & Staging Strategy

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas tahap setelah endpoint Jersey/JAX-RS menerima file, tetapi **sebelum file diproses oleh parser CSV/Excel/PDF/JSON/XML/etc**.

```text id="avd7o2"
HTTP Multipart Upload
        │
        ▼
File Intake Pipeline
        │
        ├─ validate request boundary
        ├─ enforce size limit
        ├─ stage original file
        ├─ calculate checksum
        ├─ detect MIME/content type
        ├─ validate extension + signature
        ├─ persist metadata
        └─ create processing job
        │
        ▼
Parser / Worker / Domain Processing
```

Intake pipeline adalah **gatekeeper**. Ia tidak bertugas membaca isi bisnis file secara penuh, tetapi memastikan file aman, teridentifikasi, dapat diaudit, dan siap diproses.

OWASP merekomendasikan beberapa kontrol untuk file upload seperti allowlist extension, validasi file signature, validasi content type, filename safety, size limit, storage yang aman, authorization, dan proteksi terhadap malicious file. Ini cocok dijadikan baseline untuk desain intake pipeline enterprise. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="yus9aj"
File Intake Pipeline
│
├─ 1. Receive
│   ├─ InputStream dari Jersey multipart
│   ├─ original filename
│   ├─ declared Content-Type
│   └─ upload metadata
│
├─ 2. Guard
│   ├─ max file size
│   ├─ allowed extension
│   ├─ allowed MIME type
│   ├─ tenant/user authorization
│   └─ suspicious filename rejection
│
├─ 3. Stage
│   ├─ write to temp/staging storage
│   ├─ calculate checksum
│   ├─ collect byte size
│   └─ persist file metadata
│
├─ 4. Detect
│   ├─ server-side MIME sniffing
│   ├─ magic byte inspection
│   ├─ container format check
│   └─ extension vs MIME comparison
│
├─ 5. Decide
│   ├─ ACCEPTED_FOR_PROCESSING
│   ├─ REJECTED_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE
│   ├─ REJECTED_TOO_LARGE
│   ├─ REJECTED_SECURITY_RISK
│   └─ QUARANTINED
│
└─ 6. Handoff
    ├─ create import job
    ├─ publish event / enqueue worker
    ├─ return job id
    └─ retain original file for audit
```

---

## 3. Kenapa Intake Pipeline Dipisah dari Parser?

| Kalau langsung parse di endpoint | Risiko |
|---|---|
| `InputStream` langsung dikirim ke parser Excel/PDF | File besar bisa membuat API pod OOM |
| Tidak ada original retention | Sulit audit dan forensic |
| Tidak ada checksum | Sulit mendeteksi duplicate upload |
| Tidak ada staging | Retry dan recovery sulit |
| Validasi MIME hanya pakai `Content-Type` dari client | Bisa spoofed |
| Error parsing bercampur dengan error upload | Error contract tidak jelas |
| Semua dilakukan dalam HTTP request | Timeout dan long transaction |

Pola yang lebih aman:

```text id="u06i65"
Upload request
  -> intake validation
  -> original file staged
  -> metadata persisted
  -> job created
  -> worker parses later
```

---

## 4. State Machine untuk File Intake

Gunakan state eksplisit, jangan hanya boolean `processed=true/false`.

```text id="ue1vg4"
RECEIVED
   │
   ▼
STAGING
   │
   ├── failed size limit ───────────────► REJECTED_TOO_LARGE
   │
   ├── failed MIME/extension validation ─► REJECTED_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE
   │
   ├── suspicious file ─────────────────► QUARANTINED
   │
   └── success
   │
   ▼
STAGED
   │
   ▼
ACCEPTED_FOR_PROCESSING
   │
   ▼
PROCESSING
   │
   ├── parser failure ─────► FAILED
   ├── partial row errors ─► PARTIAL_SUCCESS
   └── all ok ─────────────► SUCCESS
```

### Recommended status table

| Status | Arti |
|---|---|
| `RECEIVED` | Request upload masuk |
| `STAGING` | File sedang disalin ke staging storage |
| `STAGED` | Original file berhasil disimpan |
| `REJECTED_TOO_LARGE` | Melebihi batas ukuran |
| `REJECTED_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE` | Format tidak didukung |
| `REJECTED_SECURITY_RISK` | File/metadata mencurigakan |
| `QUARANTINED` | File disimpan tetapi tidak diproses |
| `ACCEPTED_FOR_PROCESSING` | Siap masuk worker/parser |
| `PROCESSING` | Worker sedang berjalan |
| `FAILED` | Processing gagal total |
| `PARTIAL_SUCCESS` | Sebagian row sukses, sebagian gagal |
| `SUCCESS` | Semua selesai |

---

## 5. Data Model Metadata File

Minimal metadata yang sebaiknya disimpan:

```sql id="ii5ecd"
CREATE TABLE file_object (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id           TEXT NOT NULL,
    owner_user_id       TEXT NOT NULL,

    original_filename   TEXT NOT NULL,
    sanitized_filename  TEXT NOT NULL,

    declared_content_type TEXT,
    detected_content_type TEXT,
    file_extension        TEXT,

    size_bytes          BIGINT NOT NULL,
    sha256_hex          TEXT NOT NULL,

    storage_provider    TEXT NOT NULL,
    storage_key         TEXT NOT NULL,

    status              TEXT NOT NULL,
    rejection_reason    TEXT,

    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_file_object_tenant_sha256
ON file_object (tenant_id, sha256_hex);
```

### Kenapa metadata ini penting?

| Field | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `tenant_id` | Isolasi multi-tenant |
| `owner_user_id` | Authorization dan audit |
| `original_filename` | Audit dan user-facing display |
| `sanitized_filename` | Aman untuk display/storage key |
| `declared_content_type` | Nilai dari request client |
| `detected_content_type` | Hasil deteksi server-side |
| `size_bytes` | Limit, observability, capacity planning |
| `sha256_hex` | Deduplication, idempotency, audit integrity |
| `storage_key` | Lokasi file original |
| `status` | State machine |
| `rejection_reason` | Debugging dan user feedback |

---

## 6. Size Limit Strategy

Batas ukuran file sebaiknya ada di beberapa layer:

```text id="zkeuxd"
Client contract
  -> API Gateway / Ingress
  -> Jersey/container config
  -> Application stream guard
  -> Storage quota
  -> Kubernetes ephemeral-storage limit
```

Kubernetes mendukung tracking, reserving, dan limiting local ephemeral storage yang dikonsumsi Pod; dokumentasi Kubernetes juga menekankan bahwa resource quota untuk `ephemeral-storage` membutuhkan limit di Pod spec agar quota dapat ditegakkan. Ini relevan kalau staging file memakai `/tmp` atau local disk container. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/storage/ephemeral-storage/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Suggested limits

| File type | Synchronous? | Suggested initial limit |
|---|---:|---:|
| JSON API payload | Ya | 1-10 MB |
| CSV import | Tergantung | 10-100 MB |
| Excel import | Sebaiknya async | 10-50 MB |
| PDF document | Bisa sync untuk metadata, async untuk extraction | 10-100 MB |
| Word document | Bisa sync kecil, async besar | 10-50 MB |
| Parquet | Async | 100 MB+ tergantung use case |

Angka di atas bukan standar universal. Tetapkan berdasarkan SLA, memory pod, ingress timeout, worker capacity, dan karakteristik domain.

---

## 7. MIME Detection Strategy

Jangan percaya hanya pada:

```text id="v613mr"
Content-Type: text/csv
filename: customers.csv
```

Client bisa salah, browser bisa mengirim tipe generik, dan attacker bisa spoofing.

Gunakan kombinasi:

```text id="f4y87j"
declared Content-Type
        +
file extension
        +
server-side MIME detection
        +
magic bytes / container inspection
        +
format-specific parser validation
```

Apache Tika menyediakan mekanisme content detection; dokumentasi Tika menjelaskan bahwa deteksi dapat memakai beberapa sinyal seperti magic bytes, filename, metadata, dan detector yang tersedia. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/2.0.0/detection.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Mapping contoh

| Extension | Expected detected MIME |
|---|---|
| `.csv` | `text/csv`, kadang `text/plain` tergantung detector |
| `.json` | `application/json` |
| `.xml` | `application/xml`, `text/xml` |
| `.pdf` | `application/pdf` |
| `.xlsx` | `application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet` |
| `.docx` | `application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document` |
| `.parquet` | `application/x-parquet` atau custom detection tergantung detector |

Untuk CSV, MIME detection sering tidak cukup presisi karena CSV secara byte-level mirip text biasa. Karena itu validasi CSV perlu lanjut ke tahap parser/header validation.

---

## 8. Checksum Strategy

Gunakan checksum cryptographic seperti SHA-256 untuk:

| Fungsi | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Idempotency | File sama tidak diproses dua kali |
| Deduplication | Hindari storage duplicate |
| Audit integrity | Membuktikan original file tidak berubah |
| Debugging | Cocokkan file user vs file sistem |
| Event payload | Worker hanya perlu `fileId` dan `sha256` |

Apache Commons Codec `DigestUtils` menyediakan method SHA-256 digest, termasuk varian yang menghitung digest dari `InputStream` dan mengembalikan hex string. ([commons.apache.org](https://commons.apache.org/proper/commons-codec/apidocs/org/apache/commons/codec/digest/DigestUtils.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Namun dalam intake pipeline, lebih efisien menghitung checksum **sambil menyalin stream ke staging file**, supaya file tidak dibaca dua kali.

---

## 9. Temporary Storage Strategy

Java NIO menyediakan `Files.createTempFile(...)` untuk membuat file kosong di default temp directory atau directory tertentu dengan prefix/suffix tertentu. Untuk aplikasi container, lebih baik gunakan directory staging eksplisit, bukan bergantung diam-diam pada default `/tmp`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/javase/8/docs/api/java/nio/file/Files.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Pilihan staging

| Strategy | Cocok untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| JVM temp dir | Local dev, file kecil | Sulit dikontrol di container |
| Mounted temp directory | Kubernetes pod dengan ephemeral volume | Pod eviction jika storage penuh |
| Persistent volume | Butuh file survive pod restart | Cleanup lebih kompleks |
| Object storage staging | AWS S3/Azure Blob/on-prem object store | Perlu network, retry, consistency handling |
| Database BLOB | File kecil dan governance DB-heavy | Membebani DB, backup membesar |
| Hybrid local → object storage | Umum untuk enterprise | Pipeline lebih kompleks tapi robust |

Rekomendasi praktis: **stage original file ke object storage atau durable storage**, lalu simpan metadata di PostgreSQL. Local temp hanya dipakai sebagai transit sementara.

---

## 10. Java Implementation Pattern

### 10.1 Maven dependencies

```xml id="h9h23v"
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.apache.tika</groupId>
    <artifactId>tika-core</artifactId>
</dependency>

<dependency>
    <groupId>commons-codec</groupId>
    <artifactId>commons-codec</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Tambahkan dependency management/BOM sesuai standar project Anda. Jangan hardcode versi sembarangan kalau project sudah punya parent BOM.

---

### 10.2 Intake config

```java id="v6xyx2"
package com.company.files.application;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Set;

public record FileIntakePolicy(
        long maxSizeBytes,
        Path stagingDirectory,
        Set<String> allowedExtensions,
        Set<String> allowedDetectedContentTypes
) {
    public static FileIntakePolicy customerImport(Path stagingDirectory) {
        return new FileIntakePolicy(
                50L * 1024 * 1024, // 50 MB
                stagingDirectory,
                Set.of("csv", "xlsx"),
                Set.of(
                        "text/csv",
                        "text/plain",
                        "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet"
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Catatan: `text/plain` untuk CSV bisa diterima hanya jika setelah itu ada validasi parser/header CSV. Jangan treat semua `text/plain` sebagai valid CSV.

---

### 10.3 Intake result

```java id="88crer"
package com.company.files.application;

import java.nio.file.Path;

public record StagedFile(
        String originalFilename,
        String sanitizedFilename,
        String extension,
        String declaredContentType,
        String detectedContentType,
        long sizeBytes,
        String sha256Hex,
        Path localPath
) {}
```

---

### 10.4 File intake service

```java id="92c8yp"
package com.company.files.application;

import org.apache.tika.Tika;
import org.apache.commons.codec.binary.Hex;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.security.DigestOutputStream;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.UUID;

import static java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING;

public class FileIntakeService {

    private final Tika tika = new Tika();

    public StagedFile stage(
            InputStream uploadedStream,
            String originalFilename,
            String declaredContentType,
            FileIntakePolicy policy
    ) {
        validateRequired(uploadedStream, originalFilename);

        String sanitizedFilename = sanitizeFilename(originalFilename);
        String extension = extractExtension(sanitizedFilename);

        if (!policy.allowedExtensions().contains(extension)) {
            throw new FileRejectedException(
                    "UNSUPPORTED_EXTENSION",
                    "File extension is not supported: " + extension
            );
        }

        try {
            Files.createDirectories(policy.stagingDirectory());

            Path tempFile = Files.createTempFile(
                    policy.stagingDirectory(),
                    "upload-",
                    "." + extension
            );

            CopyResult copyResult = copyWithLimitAndSha256(
                    uploadedStream,
                    tempFile,
                    policy.maxSizeBytes()
            );

            String detectedContentType = tika.detect(tempFile);

            if (!policy.allowedDetectedContentTypes().contains(detectedContentType)) {
                Files.deleteIfExists(tempFile);

                throw new FileRejectedException(
                        "UNSUPPORTED_CONTENT_TYPE",
                        "Detected content type is not supported: " + detectedContentType
                );
            }

            return new StagedFile(
                    originalFilename,
                    sanitizedFilename,
                    extension,
                    declaredContentType,
                    detectedContentType,
                    copyResult.sizeBytes(),
                    copyResult.sha256Hex(),
                    tempFile
            );

        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new FileIntakeException("Failed to stage uploaded file", e);
        }
    }

    private void validateRequired(InputStream stream, String filename) {
        if (stream == null) {
            throw new FileRejectedException("FILE_REQUIRED", "File stream is required");
        }
        if (filename == null || filename.isBlank()) {
            throw new FileRejectedException("FILENAME_REQUIRED", "Original filename is required");
        }
    }

    private CopyResult copyWithLimitAndSha256(
            InputStream input,
            Path target,
            long maxSizeBytes
    ) throws IOException {
        MessageDigest digest = sha256Digest();

        long total = 0L;
        byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];

        try (var output = new DigestOutputStream(Files.newOutputStream(target), digest)) {
            int read;
            while ((read = input.read(buffer)) != -1) {
                total += read;

                if (total > maxSizeBytes) {
                    throw new FileRejectedException(
                            "FILE_TOO_LARGE",
                            "File exceeds maximum allowed size"
                    );
                }

                output.write(buffer, 0, read);
            }
        } catch (FileRejectedException e) {
            Files.deleteIfExists(target);
            throw e;
        }

        String sha256Hex = Hex.encodeHexString(digest.digest());
        return new CopyResult(total, sha256Hex);
    }

    private MessageDigest sha256Digest() {
        try {
            return MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("SHA-256 is not available", e);
        }
    }

    private String sanitizeFilename(String filename) {
        String base = Path.of(filename).getFileName().toString();

        String sanitized = base
                .replaceAll("[\\r\\n\\t]", "_")
                .replaceAll("[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]", "_");

        if (sanitized.isBlank()) {
            return "upload-" + UUID.randomUUID();
        }

        return sanitized;
    }

    private String extractExtension(String filename) {
        int index = filename.lastIndexOf('.');
        if (index < 0 || index == filename.length() - 1) {
            throw new FileRejectedException(
                    "FILE_EXTENSION_REQUIRED",
                    "File extension is required"
            );
        }

        return filename.substring(index + 1).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }

    private record CopyResult(long sizeBytes, String sha256Hex) {}
}
```

---

### 10.5 Exception model

```java id="q6m3gl"
package com.company.files.application;

public class FileRejectedException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String code;

    public FileRejectedException(String code, String message) {
        super(message);
        this.code = code;
    }

    public String code() {
        return code;
    }
}
```

```java id="c4t28e"
package com.company.files.application;

public class FileIntakeException extends RuntimeException {

    public FileIntakeException(String message, Throwable cause) {
        super(message, cause);
    }
}
```

## 11. Integrasi dengan Jersey Resource

Resource method tetap tipis:

```java id="uhedw9"
@POST
@Path("/customer-imports")
@Consumes(MediaType.MULTIPART_FORM_DATA)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public Response uploadCustomerImport(
        @FormDataParam("file") InputStream fileStream,
        @FormDataParam("file") FormDataContentDisposition fileMeta,
        @FormDataParam("metadata") String metadataJson,
        @HeaderParam("Content-Type") String requestContentType
) {
    String originalFilename = fileMeta != null ? fileMeta.getFileName() : null;

    FileIntakePolicy policy = FileIntakePolicy.customerImport(
            Path.of("/var/app/staging/uploads")
    );

    StagedFile staged = fileIntakeService.stage(
            fileStream,
            originalFilename,
            null,
            policy
    );

    ImportJob job = importJobService.createJob(staged, metadataJson);

    return Response.accepted(new UploadAcceptedResponse(
            job.id(),
            staged.originalFilename(),
            staged.detectedContentType(),
            staged.sizeBytes(),
            "ACCEPTED_FOR_PROCESSING"
    )).build();
}
```

Poin penting: setelah intake selesai, resource membuat job. Parser CSV/Excel tidak masuk ke resource.

## 12. Persist Metadata dengan MyBatis

### 12.1 Mapper interface

```java id="xy2ng0"
package com.company.files.infrastructure.db;

import com.company.files.domain.FileObject;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Mapper;

@Mapper
public interface FileObjectMapper {
    void insert(FileObject fileObject);
    FileObject findById(String id);
    FileObject findByTenantAndChecksum(String tenantId, String sha256Hex);
    void updateStatus(String id, String status, String rejectionReason);
}
```

### 12.2 SQL mapper

```xml id="oeezam"
<mapper namespace="com.company.files.infrastructure.db.FileObjectMapper">

    <insert id="insert">
        INSERT INTO file_object (
            id,
            tenant_id,
            owner_user_id,
            original_filename,
            sanitized_filename,
            declared_content_type,
            detected_content_type,
            file_extension,
            size_bytes,
            sha256_hex,
            storage_provider,
            storage_key,
            status,
            created_at,
            updated_at
        )
        VALUES (
            #{id},
            #{tenantId},
            #{ownerUserId},
            #{originalFilename},
            #{sanitizedFilename},
            #{declaredContentType},
            #{detectedContentType},
            #{fileExtension},
            #{sizeBytes},
            #{sha256Hex},
            #{storageProvider},
            #{storageKey},
            #{status},
            now(),
            now()
        )
    </insert>

    <select id="findByTenantAndChecksum" resultType="com.company.files.domain.FileObject">
        SELECT *
        FROM file_object
        WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND sha256_hex = #{sha256Hex}
        LIMIT 1
    </select>

    <update id="updateStatus">
        UPDATE file_object
        SET status = #{status},
            rejection_reason = #{rejectionReason},
            updated_at = now()
        WHERE id = #{id}
    </update>

</mapper>
```

## 13. Storage Key Design

Jangan jadikan filename user sebagai path utama.

### Buruk

```text id="g3rxhd"
/uploads/customer.xlsx
/uploads/../../secret.txt
/uploads/final-final-v2.xlsx
```

### Lebih baik

```text id="1r47w7"
tenant/{tenantId}/files/{yyyy}/{mm}/{dd}/{fileId}/original
tenant/{tenantId}/files/{yyyy}/{mm}/{dd}/{fileId}/metadata.json
tenant/{tenantId}/files/{yyyy}/{mm}/{dd}/{fileId}/error-report.xlsx
```

Contoh:

```text id="50k2xh"
tenant/acme/files/2026/07/13/0f5b3d8a/original
```

Filename asli tetap disimpan di metadata, bukan dipakai sebagai storage path.

---

## 14. Intake Decision Matrix

| Kondisi | Action | Status |
|---|---|---|
| File kosong | Reject | `REJECTED_EMPTY_FILE` |
| Extension tidak allowlisted | Reject | `REJECTED_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE` |
| Size melebihi limit | Reject + delete temp | `REJECTED_TOO_LARGE` |
| MIME detected tidak cocok | Reject atau quarantine | `REJECTED_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE` / `QUARANTINED` |
| Filename mencurigakan | Sanitize atau reject | `REJECTED_SECURITY_RISK` |
| File sama sudah pernah diproses | Return existing job atau create duplicate reference | `DUPLICATE_DETECTED` |
| File valid | Stage + create job | `ACCEPTED_FOR_PROCESSING` |
| Antivirus unavailable | Reject sementara atau quarantine | `QUARANTINED` / `503` |
| Storage unavailable | Fail request | `FAILED_STAGING` |

---

## 15. Idempotency Pattern

Gunakan kombinasi:

```text id="9b5v92"
tenant_id
+ sha256_hex
+ import_type
+ schema_version
```

Contoh logic:

```text id="6yrjhh"
if same tenant + same checksum + same import type exists:
    if previous status SUCCESS:
        return existing job / duplicate response
    if previous status PROCESSING:
        return current job status
    if previous status FAILED:
        allow reprocess with new job or explicit retry
else:
    create new file object + import job
```

### Contoh response duplicate

```json id="3nx4iz"
{
  "status": "DUPLICATE_DETECTED",
  "message": "The same file has already been submitted.",
  "existingJobId": "job_20260713_001"
}
```

---

## 16. Original File Retention

Untuk sistem enterprise/regulatory, original file sebaiknya tidak langsung dihapus setelah parsing.

| Retention object | Kenapa disimpan |
|---|---|
| Original file | Audit, forensic, reprocessing |
| Metadata snapshot | Bukti upload context |
| Checksum | Integrity |
| Error report | User feedback dan audit |
| Processing log summary | Explainability |
| Parser version | Reproducibility |

### Retention policy contoh

| File type | Retention |
|---|---:|
| Failed upload karena boundary invalid | Tidak disimpan atau quarantine pendek |
| Rejected security risk | Quarantine sesuai security policy |
| Successful import original | 30/90/365 hari tergantung compliance |
| Error report | Sama dengan job retention |
| Generated export | Pendek, misalnya 7-30 hari |
| Regulatory document | Sesuai legal retention policy |

---

## 17. Kubernetes Runtime Concern

Kalau staging memakai local disk container, desain harus memperhitungkan:

| Concern | Risiko | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| `/tmp` tidak cukup | Upload gagal acak | Set staging dir eksplisit |
| Ephemeral storage habis | Pod evicted | Set request/limit `ephemeral-storage` |
| Pod restart | Temp file hilang | Gunakan durable object storage untuk original |
| Banyak upload bersamaan | Disk pressure | Rate limit + concurrency limit |
| Cleanup gagal | Disk leak | Scheduled cleanup + TTL |
| Worker dan API satu pod | Resource saling ganggu | Pisah API pod dan worker pod |

Contoh Kubernetes resource:

```yaml id="t2k3em"
resources:
  requests:
    cpu: "500m"
    memory: "512Mi"
    ephemeral-storage: "1Gi"
  limits:
    cpu: "2"
    memory: "2Gi"
    ephemeral-storage: "5Gi"
```

Kubernetes local ephemeral storage penting untuk container writable layer, logs, dan volume sementara seperti `emptyDir`, sehingga file staging lokal harus dianggap sebagai resource terbatas, bukan ruang tak terbatas. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/storage/ephemeral-storage/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 18. Observability untuk Intake Pipeline

Metrics minimal:

| Metric | Type | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `file_upload_received_total` | Counter | Jumlah upload diterima |
| `file_upload_rejected_total{reason}` | Counter | Alasan reject |
| `file_upload_size_bytes` | Histogram | Distribusi ukuran |
| `file_intake_duration_seconds` | Histogram | Lama staging/detection |
| `file_detected_content_type_total{type}` | Counter | Distribusi MIME |
| `file_duplicate_detected_total` | Counter | Idempotency hit |
| `file_staging_failed_total` | Counter | Storage issue |

Structured log minimal:

```json id="6jauhz"
{
  "event": "file_intake_completed",
  "correlationId": "corr-123",
  "fileId": "file-456",
  "tenantId": "tenant-789",
  "originalFilename": "customers.xlsx",
  "detectedContentType": "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
  "sizeBytes": 1048576,
  "sha256": "abc123...",
  "status": "ACCEPTED_FOR_PROCESSING"
}
```

Jangan log isi file atau data row yang mungkin mengandung PII.

---

## 19. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Menggunakan filename user sebagai path | Path traversal / overwrite |
| Mengandalkan extension saja | File spoofing |
| Mengandalkan `Content-Type` client saja | Bypass validasi |
| Membaca semua file ke `byte[]` | OOM |
| Tidak menyimpan checksum | Duplicate/retry sulit |
| Tidak menyimpan original file | Audit lemah |
| Tidak ada cleanup temp file | Disk leak |
| Tidak ada status intake | Sulit debugging |
| Tidak ada quarantine path | Security handling tidak jelas |
| Upload langsung parse synchronous | Timeout dan poor scalability |

---

## 20. Production Checklist

```text id="m5qa3e"
[ ] Max size ditetapkan per import type
[ ] Limit juga diterapkan di ingress/gateway
[ ] Staging directory eksplisit, bukan default diam-diam
[ ] Local staging punya cleanup policy
[ ] Kubernetes ephemeral-storage request/limit dikonfigurasi
[ ] Filename disanitasi
[ ] Extension allowlist diterapkan
[ ] MIME detection server-side diterapkan
[ ] Extension vs MIME mismatch ditangani
[ ] Checksum SHA-256 dihitung saat copy stream
[ ] Metadata file dipersist
[ ] Original file disimpan di durable storage jika audit diperlukan
[ ] Idempotency policy jelas
[ ] Duplicate upload behavior jelas
[ ] Rejection reason jelas
[ ] Security quarantine flow tersedia
[ ] Structured logs tersedia
[ ] Metrics tersedia
[ ] File content/PII tidak muncul di log
[ ] Worker hanya menerima fileId/storageKey, bukan raw upload stream
```

---

## 21. Kesimpulan Seri 2

File intake pipeline adalah lapisan yang membuat file processing menjadi **aman, scalable, dan defensible**.

Prinsip utamanya:

```text id="dnuepf"
Never trust uploaded metadata.
Never trust filename.
Never parse before staging.
Never process large files in API request.
Always calculate checksum.
Always persist metadata.
Always design for retry, audit, and rejection.
```

Dengan pipeline ini, seri berikutnya bisa membahas file security secara lebih tajam: malicious file, XXE, zip bomb, path traversal, antivirus integration, dan safe extraction.

## Seri 2 selesai

Rekomendasi lanjut: **Seri 3 - File Security: Malicious File, XXE, Zip Bomb, Path Traversal, Antivirus Integration, dan Safe Extraction Policy**.
