## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:17:44 PM

Lanjut **Seri 3 dari 20**. Ini bagian penting karena file upload adalah attack surface besar: fokusnya bukan parsing bisnis, tetapi bagaimana mencegah file menjadi jalur exploit, data leak, atau resource exhaustion.

# Seri 3/20 - File Security: Malicious File, XXE, Zip Bomb, Path Traversal, Antivirus Integration & Safe Extraction Policy

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **security layer** untuk file processing di Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice.

Fokusnya:

```text id="ejoqkd"
Uploaded File
   │
   ├─ Apakah user berhak upload file ini?
   ├─ Apakah nama file aman?
   ├─ Apakah extension valid?
   ├─ Apakah MIME/type cocok?
   ├─ Apakah file mengandung malware?
   ├─ Apakah file bisa menyebabkan parser exploit?
   ├─ Apakah file bisa menyebabkan OOM / CPU spike?
   ├─ Apakah extraction bisa keluar dari directory aman?
   └─ Apakah file boleh masuk processing pipeline?
```

OWASP File Upload Cheat Sheet merekomendasikan kontrol seperti allowlist extension, validasi file type/signature, tidak mempercayai `Content-Type`, mengganti filename dengan nama yang digenerate aplikasi, membatasi ukuran file, menyimpan file secara aman, dan memastikan hanya user authorized yang bisa upload file. Itu baseline yang cocok untuk microservice enterprise. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Threat Model

```text id="loqoos"
File Upload Attack Surface
│
├─ 1. Metadata attack
│   ├─ fake filename
│   ├─ fake Content-Type
│   ├─ double extension
│   └─ path traversal
│
├─ 2. Parser attack
│   ├─ malformed PDF
│   ├─ malicious Office document
│   ├─ XML external entity
│   ├─ entity expansion
│   └─ insecure deserialization
│
├─ 3. Resource exhaustion
│   ├─ huge file
│   ├─ zip bomb
│   ├─ deeply nested archive
│   ├─ huge XML
│   └─ high CPU parsing
│
├─ 4. Malware/content risk
│   ├─ macro malware
│   ├─ embedded executable
│   ├─ polyglot file
│   └─ suspicious embedded object
│
├─ 5. Storage attack
│   ├─ overwrite file
│   ├─ path traversal
│   ├─ public file exposure
│   └─ tenant isolation failure
│
└─ 6. Download/data leak
    ├─ unauthorized download
    ├─ cached sensitive file
    ├─ predictable URL
    └─ missing audit
```

---

## 3. Security Pipeline Position

Security tidak boleh hanya di satu titik. Gunakan layered defense:

```text id="nzcolu"
Client
  │
  ▼
Ingress / API Gateway
  ├─ request size limit
  ├─ authn/authz
  └─ rate limit
  │
  ▼
Jersey Resource
  ├─ multipart field validation
  ├─ basic metadata validation
  └─ correlation id
  │
  ▼
File Intake Security
  ├─ filename sanitation
  ├─ extension allowlist
  ├─ MIME detection
  ├─ checksum
  ├─ antivirus scan
  ├─ quarantine decision
  └─ safe staging
  │
  ▼
Parser Security
  ├─ secure XML parser
  ├─ zip bomb limits
  ├─ parser-specific limits
  ├─ timeout / worker isolation
  └─ no unsafe deserialization
  │
  ▼
Domain Processing
  ├─ row validation
  ├─ authorization by domain
  ├─ audit log
  └─ controlled persistence
```

---

## 4. Security Decision Matrix

| Check | Reject | Quarantine | Continue |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Missing file | Yes | No | No |
| Unsupported extension | Yes | Optional | No |
| MIME mismatch | Usually | Optional | Only if known false-positive |
| File too large | Yes | No | No |
| Suspicious filename | Usually sanitize; reject if dangerous | Optional | If safely normalized |
| Malware detected | Yes | Yes | No |
| AV scanner unavailable | Depends policy | Yes | Usually no |
| XML contains DTD/external entity | Yes | Optional | No |
| Zip bomb suspected | Yes | Yes | No |
| Parser crash | No direct user retry loop | Optional | No |
| User unauthorized | Yes | No | No |

Untuk sistem regulatory atau multi-tenant, default yang lebih aman adalah **reject or quarantine**, bukan “best effort parse”.

---

## 5. Filename & Path Traversal

### 5.1 Masalah

Filename dari client tidak boleh dipercaya.

Contoh berbahaya:

```text id="gqc41n"
../../etc/passwd
..\..\windows\system32\drivers\etc\hosts
report.pdf%00.exe
invoice.pdf.exe
CON
aux.txt
file
file.
```

### 5.2 Invariant

```text id="g60z6m"
Client filename boleh disimpan sebagai display metadata.
Client filename tidak boleh menjadi filesystem path.
Storage key harus digenerate server.
```

### 5.3 Safe filename sanitizer

```java id="gjv6s3"
package com.company.files.security;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.text.Normalizer;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.UUID;

public final class SafeFilename {

    private SafeFilename() {}

    public static String sanitizeForDisplay(String input) {
        if (input == null || input.isBlank()) {
            return "upload-" + UUID.randomUUID();
        }

        String baseName = Path.of(input).getFileName().toString();

        String normalized = Normalizer.normalize(baseName, Normalizer.Form.NFKC);

        String sanitized = normalized
                .replaceAll("[\\r\\n\\t]", "_")
                .replaceAll("[^a-zA-Z0-9._ -]", "_")
                .replaceAll("\\s+", " ")
                .trim();

        if (sanitized.isBlank() || sanitized.equals(".") || sanitized.equals("..")) {
            return "upload-" + UUID.randomUUID();
        }

        if (sanitized.length() > 120) {
            sanitized = sanitized.substring(0, 120);
        }

        return sanitized;
    }

    public static String extensionOf(String sanitizedFilename) {
        int index = sanitizedFilename.lastIndexOf('.');
        if (index < 0 || index == sanitizedFilename.length() - 1) {
            return "";
        }

        return sanitizedFilename.substring(index + 1).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }
}
```

### 5.4 Storage key

```java id="mh69yc"
public final class StorageKeyFactory {

    public String originalFileKey(
            String tenantId,
            String fileId,
            String yyyy,
            String mm,
            String dd
    ) {
        return "tenant/%s/files/%s/%s/%s/%s/original"
                .formatted(tenantId, yyyy, mm, dd, fileId);
    }
}
```

Jangan:

```java id="fcw11z"
Path target = uploadDir.resolve(originalFilename); // dangerous
```

Gunakan:

```java id="yeagqv"
Path target = uploadDir.resolve(generatedFileId + ".bin").normalize();

if (!target.startsWith(uploadDir)) {
    throw new SecurityException("Invalid storage path");
}
```

---

## 6. Extension Allowlist

OWASP menyarankan allowlist extension yang memang dibutuhkan secara bisnis, bukan blocklist. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Contoh policy

```java id="mpe1t1"
public record FileTypePolicy(
        String importType,
        Set<String> allowedExtensions,
        Set<String> allowedDetectedMimeTypes,
        long maxSizeBytes,
        boolean requiresAvScan
) {
    public static FileTypePolicy customerImport() {
        return new FileTypePolicy(
                "CUSTOMER_IMPORT",
                Set.of("csv", "xlsx"),
                Set.of(
                        "text/csv",
                        "text/plain",
                        "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet"
                ),
                50L * 1024 * 1024,
                true
        );
    }

    public static FileTypePolicy legalDocumentUpload() {
        return new FileTypePolicy(
                "LEGAL_DOCUMENT",
                Set.of("pdf", "docx"),
                Set.of(
                        "application/pdf",
                        "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document"
                ),
                100L * 1024 * 1024,
                true
        );
    }
}
```

### Double extension rule

| Filename | Decision |
|---|---|
| `customers.csv` | Candidate valid |
| `customers.csv.exe` | Reject |
| `invoice.pdf` | Candidate valid |
| `invoice.pdf.scr` | Reject |
| `report.final.xlsx` | Candidate valid, extension `xlsx` |
| `file` | Reject kalau extension required |

---

## 7. MIME Detection & Signature Validation

`Content-Type` dari client hanya hint. OWASP secara eksplisit memperingatkan bahwa header ini bisa di-spoof. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Recommended logic

```text id="rqow04"
declared Content-Type from client
        │
        ▼
detected MIME from server-side detector
        │
        ▼
extension allowlist
        │
        ▼
format-specific parse smoke test
        │
        ▼
accepted / rejected / quarantined
```

### Example

```java id="ihodtw"
public FileTypeDecision decide(
        String extension,
        String declaredContentType,
        String detectedContentType,
        FileTypePolicy policy
) {
    if (!policy.allowedExtensions().contains(extension)) {
        return FileTypeDecision.reject("UNSUPPORTED_EXTENSION");
    }

    if (!policy.allowedDetectedMimeTypes().contains(detectedContentType)) {
        return FileTypeDecision.reject("UNSUPPORTED_DETECTED_MIME");
    }

    if (declaredContentType != null
            && !declaredContentType.isBlank()
            && !declaredContentType.equalsIgnoreCase(detectedContentType)) {
        return FileTypeDecision.review("DECLARED_MIME_MISMATCH");
    }

    return FileTypeDecision.accept();
}
```

Catatan: CSV sering terdeteksi sebagai `text/plain`, sehingga jangan hanya mengandalkan MIME. Lanjutkan dengan header/schema validation pada seri CSV.

---

## 8. Antivirus / Malware Scanning

Antivirus scanning bukan pengganti validation. Ia adalah **lapisan tambahan**.

```text id="yqpvqm"
File staged
   │
   ▼
AV scan
   │
   ├─ CLEAN        -> continue
   ├─ INFECTED     -> reject/quarantine
   ├─ ERROR        -> fail closed or quarantine
   └─ TIMEOUT      -> fail closed or quarantine
```

ClamAV menyediakan `clamd`, daemon multi-threaded yang memakai `libclamav` untuk scan file; dokumentasi ClamAV menyebut daemon dapat listen melalui Unix socket atau TCP dan memerlukan signature database yang diperbarui, biasanya via `freshclam`. ([docs.clamav.net](https://docs.clamav.net/manual/Usage/Scanning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk remote TCP scan, dokumentasi ClamAV menjelaskan bahwa `clamdscan` memakai `INSTREAM`, sehingga file content dikirim sebagai stream, bukan meminta daemon remote membuka local path. ([docs.clamav.net](https://docs.clamav.net/manual/Usage/ClamdProtocol.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 8.1 AV scanner interface

```java id="qiuqd6"
package com.company.files.security;

import java.nio.file.Path;

public interface MalwareScanner {
    MalwareScanResult scan(Path file);
}
```

```java id="q57m2z"
public record MalwareScanResult(
        MalwareScanStatus status,
        String engine,
        String signature,
        String detail
) {}

public enum MalwareScanStatus {
    CLEAN,
    INFECTED,
    ERROR,
    TIMEOUT
}
```

### 8.2 Fail-open vs fail-closed

| Policy | Arti | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| Fail-open | AV error, file tetap lanjut | Low-risk internal batch |
| Fail-closed | AV error, file ditolak | Public upload, PII, regulatory |
| Quarantine | File disimpan tapi tidak diproses | Enterprise default yang aman |

Untuk perusahaan enterprise, default yang masuk akal:

```text id="8cotuy"
AV unavailable -> QUARANTINED
AV infected    -> REJECTED_SECURITY_RISK
AV clean       -> ACCEPTED_FOR_PROCESSING
```

---

## 9. XML Security: XXE & Entity Expansion

XXE terjadi saat parser XML yang lemah memproses external entity. OWASP menjelaskan dampaknya bisa berupa disclosure data rahasia, denial-of-service, SSRF, port scanning dari perspektif server, dan dampak lain. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/www-community/vulnerabilities/XML_External_Entity_%28XXE%29_Processing?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Rekomendasi OWASP yang paling aman adalah menonaktifkan DTD/external entities sepenuhnya jika tidak diperlukan. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/XML_External_Entity_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 9.1 Unsafe XML

```xml id="fz8v89"
<?xml version="1.0"?>
<!DOCTYPE data [
  <!ENTITY xxe SYSTEM "file:///etc/passwd">
]>
<customer>
  <name>&xxe;</name>
</customer>
```

### 9.2 Secure DocumentBuilderFactory

```java id="rta6an"
package com.company.files.security.xml;

import javax.xml.XMLConstants;
import javax.xml.parsers.DocumentBuilderFactory;
import javax.xml.parsers.ParserConfigurationException;

public final class SecureXmlFactories {

    private SecureXmlFactories() {}

    public static DocumentBuilderFactory secureDocumentBuilderFactory()
            throws ParserConfigurationException {

        DocumentBuilderFactory factory = DocumentBuilderFactory.newInstance();

        factory.setFeature("http://apache.org/xml/features/disallow-doctype-decl", true);
        factory.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/external-general-entities", false);
        factory.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/external-parameter-entities", false);
        factory.setFeature("http://apache.org/xml/features/nonvalidating/load-external-dtd", false);

        factory.setXIncludeAware(false);
        factory.setExpandEntityReferences(false);

        factory.setFeature(XMLConstants.FEATURE_SECURE_PROCESSING, true);
        factory.setAttribute(XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_DTD, "");
        factory.setAttribute(XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_SCHEMA, "");

        return factory;
    }
}
```

### 9.3 Secure SAXParserFactory

```java id="brq27w"
import javax.xml.XMLConstants;
import javax.xml.parsers.SAXParserFactory;

public static SAXParserFactory secureSaxParserFactory() throws Exception {
    SAXParserFactory factory = SAXParserFactory.newInstance();

    factory.setFeature("http://apache.org/xml/features/disallow-doctype-decl", true);
    factory.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/external-general-entities", false);
    factory.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/external-parameter-entities", false);
    factory.setFeature("http://apache.org/xml/features/nonvalidating/load-external-dtd", false);

    factory.setFeature(XMLConstants.FEATURE_SECURE_PROCESSING, true);
    factory.setXIncludeAware(false);
    factory.setNamespaceAware(true);

    return factory;
}
```

### 9.4 Secure StAX

```java id="bwaslu"
import javax.xml.XMLConstants;
import javax.xml.stream.XMLInputFactory;

public static XMLInputFactory secureXmlInputFactory() {
    XMLInputFactory factory = XMLInputFactory.newFactory();

    factory.setProperty(XMLInputFactory.SUPPORT_DTD, false);
    factory.setProperty("javax.xml.stream.isSupportingExternalEntities", false);
    factory.setProperty(XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_DTD, "");
    factory.setProperty(XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_SCHEMA, "");

    return factory;
}
```

Catatan package: di Java SE, XML API masih tersedia melalui `javax.xml.*`. Ini berbeda dari Jakarta REST yang memakai `jakarta.ws.rs.*`.

---

## 10. Zip Bomb & Archive Bomb

Banyak format Office modern seperti `.xlsx`, `.docx`, `.pptx` sebenarnya adalah ZIP container. Jadi Excel/Word processing juga harus diperlakukan sebagai archive processing.

### 10.1 Attack pattern

```text id="oz6som"
small compressed file
   │
   ▼
parser decompresses
   │
   ▼
huge expanded content
   │
   ├─ memory spike
   ├─ disk exhaustion
   ├─ CPU spike
   └─ worker starvation
```

Apache POI memiliki `ZipSecureFile` untuk proteksi zip bomb; API-nya menyediakan `setMinInflateRatio(double)` untuk rasio compressed-vs-inflated, default 1% atau `0.01`, dan parsing akan gagal jika compression terlalu ekstrem. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/apidocs/dev/org/apache/poi/openxml4j/util/ZipSecureFile.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) API POI juga menyediakan limit seperti maximum single zip entry size untuk membatasi konsumsi memori. ([javadoc.io](https://javadoc.io/doc/org.apache.poi/poi-ooxml/5.2.4/org/apache/poi/openxml4j/util/ZipSecureFile.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 10.2 POI guardrail

```java id="xg3uh8"
import org.apache.poi.openxml4j.util.ZipSecureFile;

public final class PoiSecurityConfig {

    private PoiSecurityConfig() {}

    public static void configureZipBombProtection() {
        // Default POI min inflate ratio umumnya 0.01.
        // Jangan disable dengan nilai negatif kecuali ada alasan sangat kuat.
        ZipSecureFile.setMinInflateRatio(0.01d);

        // Sesuaikan dengan policy aplikasi.
        ZipSecureFile.setMaxEntrySize(100L * 1024 * 1024); // 100 MB per entry
        ZipSecureFile.setMaxTextSize(20L * 1024 * 1024);  // 20 MB extracted text
    }
}
```

### 10.3 Jangan lakukan ini

```java id="8p89px"
ZipSecureFile.setMinInflateRatio(-1.0d); // disables protection; dangerous
```

False positive bisa terjadi pada file legitimate yang sangat terkompresi, tetapi solusi enterprise bukan langsung disable. Lebih baik buat policy per file type, size limit, AV scan, quarantine, dan manual review.

---

## 11. Safe Archive Extraction

Kalau sistem mengekstrak ZIP/TAR, wajib cegah Zip Slip/path traversal.

### 11.1 Unsafe

```java id="l67e7f"
Path output = targetDir.resolve(zipEntry.getName());
Files.copy(zipInputStream, output);
```

Jika entry bernama `../../app/config.yml`, ini bisa menulis keluar dari target directory.

### 11.2 Safe extraction

```java id="rnswtb"
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.zip.ZipInputStream;

public class SafeZipExtractor {

    public void extract(InputStream input, Path targetDir) throws Exception {
        Path normalizedTargetDir = targetDir.toAbsolutePath().normalize();
        Files.createDirectories(normalizedTargetDir);

        try (ZipInputStream zip = new ZipInputStream(input)) {
            var entry = zip.getNextEntry();

            while (entry != null) {
                Path outputPath = normalizedTargetDir
                        .resolve(entry.getName())
                        .normalize();

                if (!outputPath.startsWith(normalizedTargetDir)) {
                    throw new SecurityException("Zip entry escapes target directory: " + entry.getName());
                }

                if (entry.isDirectory()) {
                    Files.createDirectories(outputPath);
                } else {
                    Files.createDirectories(outputPath.getParent());
                    Files.copy(zip, outputPath);
                }

                zip.closeEntry();
                entry = zip.getNextEntry();
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### 11.3 Tambahkan limits

```text id="0a3otb"
[ ] max number of entries
[ ] max total uncompressed bytes
[ ] max single entry size
[ ] max nesting depth
[ ] allowed entry extensions
[ ] reject absolute path
[ ] reject symlink/hardlink where applicable
[ ] timeout extraction
```

---

## 12. Apache Tika Security Notes

Apache Tika berguna untuk content detection dan extraction lintas format, tetapi jangan menjalankan extraction tanpa limit. Dokumentasi Tika tentang limits menyebut `EmbeddedLimits` mengontrol kedalaman nested dan jumlah embedded document yang diproses, yang penting untuk proteksi terhadap zip bomb-style attack. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/docs/4.0.0-SNAPSHOT/advanced/setting-limits.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Dokumentasi Tika juga memiliki halaman security yang mencatat CVE/vulnerability dan security model, sehingga dependency Tika perlu diperlakukan sebagai komponen security-sensitive yang harus rutin diupdate. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/security.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Practical rule

| Use case | Recommended |
|---|---|
| MIME detection ringan | Boleh di API/intake service |
| Full text extraction PDF/Office | Jalankan di worker isolated |
| Recursive embedded extraction | Batasi depth/count/size |
| Untrusted public upload | AV scan + sandbox/worker limit |
| Huge document extraction | Async job + timeout |

---

## 13. PDF Security

PDF parser bisa menjadi target resource exhaustion atau parser bug. Untuk PDF upload:

| Risk | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| Encrypted PDF | Reject atau minta password flow terpisah |
| Scanned image PDF besar | Page/image limit |
| Embedded file | Reject atau strip sesuai policy |
| JavaScript/action dalam PDF | Jangan execute; scan/strip jika perlu |
| Huge page count | Max page count |
| Huge text extraction | Max extracted chars |
| Parser crash | Worker isolation + retry cap |
| Sensitive content | Encrypt storage + strict download authz |

### PDF decision example

```text id="hl9uhv"
PDF upload accepted only if:
- detected MIME = application/pdf
- size <= limit
- AV clean
- page count <= policy
- not encrypted, unless supported
- no unsupported embedded files
- extraction within timeout
```

---

## 14. Office Document Security

DOCX/XLSX adalah ZIP container. XLSM/DOCM bisa mengandung macro.

| Format | Risk | Default policy |
|---|---|---|
| `.xlsx` | Zip bomb, formula injection, huge workbook | Allow with POI limits |
| `.xls` | Legacy binary parser risk | Allow only if business requires |
| `.xlsm` | Macro-enabled workbook | Usually reject |
| `.docx` | Embedded objects, zip bomb | Allow with limits |
| `.docm` | Macro-enabled document | Usually reject |
| `.pptx` | Embedded media/object | Allow only if needed |
| `.csv` | Formula injection if exported to Excel | Escape dangerous leading chars |

### Formula injection concern

CSV/Excel export can become dangerous when opened in spreadsheet tools if cell value starts with formula characters.

Risky prefixes:

```text id="oplhvh"
=
+
-
@
\t
\r
```

Mitigation for user-controlled values exported to CSV/Excel:

```java id="9p9p1s"
public static String escapeForSpreadsheet(String value) {
    if (value == null || value.isEmpty()) {
        return value;
    }

    char first = value.charAt(0);

    if (first == '=' || first == '+' || first == '-' || first == '@'
            || first == '\t' || first == '\r') {
        return "'" + value;
    }

    return value;
}
```

---

## 15. JSON Deserialization Security

Untuk JSON, risiko utamanya bukan hanya malformed JSON, tapi unsafe polymorphic deserialization.

### Jangan

```java id="vfi3ed"
// Jangan aktifkan default typing secara luas untuk untrusted JSON.
objectMapper.activateDefaultTyping(...);
```

### Better

```text id="pt2xt3"
[ ] Map untrusted JSON ke DTO eksplisit
[ ] Disable unknown fields jika kontrak harus strict
[ ] Batasi max request size
[ ] Batasi nesting depth jika parser mendukung
[ ] Jangan polymorphic deserialize tanpa allowlist
[ ] Jangan deserialize Java native object stream dari upload
```

### DTO boundary

```java id="0zchzo"
public record ImportMetadataRequest(
        String sourceSystem,
        String schemaVersion,
        boolean dryRun
) {}
```

---

## 16. XML / JSON / YAML / Java Serialization Rule

Untuk file dari user:

| Format | Rule |
|---|---|
| XML | Secure parser, DTD disabled, external entity disabled |
| JSON | DTO explicit mapping, no unsafe polymorphism |
| YAML | Avoid for untrusted upload unless strictly configured |
| Java native serialization | Reject |
| Binary serialized object | Reject |
| Unknown compressed archive | Reject or quarantine |

Java native serialization dari upload hampir selalu tidak boleh diterima di microservice modern karena attack surface-nya terlalu besar.

---

## 17. Quarantine Model

Jangan hanya `delete` semua suspicious file kalau perusahaan butuh audit/security investigation.

```text id="7m5wib"
Uploaded file
  │
  ├─ clean      -> processing storage
  ├─ invalid    -> reject, optional short retention
  ├─ suspicious -> quarantine storage
  └─ infected   -> quarantine/reject according to policy
```

### Metadata quarantine

```sql id="u6rc56"
CREATE TABLE file_quarantine (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    file_object_id       UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
    reason_code          TEXT NOT NULL,
    detected_content_type TEXT,
    scanner_engine       TEXT,
    scanner_signature    TEXT,
    quarantined_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    reviewed_by          TEXT,
    reviewed_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    review_decision      TEXT,
    notes                TEXT
);
```

### Quarantine rules

| Rule | Explanation |
|---|---|
| Quarantine files are never processed automatically | Prevent accidental execution/parsing |
| Access is security/admin-only | Avoid leaking malware/PII |
| Storage is isolated | Separate bucket/prefix/permissions |
| Retention is explicit | Avoid indefinite unsafe storage |
| Review action is audited | Who approved/rejected and why |

---

## 18. Safe Storage Policy

| Storage concern | Recommended policy |
|---|---|
| Public access | Off by default |
| Filename | Server-generated storage key |
| Original filename | Metadata only |
| Tenant isolation | Prefix + IAM/policy + DB authorization |
| Encryption | At rest and in transit |
| Sensitive files | Short-lived signed URL or proxy download with authz |
| Download cache | `Cache-Control: no-store` |
| Access log | Required |
| Deletion | Soft-delete/retention according to domain |

### Never expose object storage key directly as authorization

Bad:

```text id="okrfyk"
GET /files/tenant/acme/files/2026/07/13/file123/original
```

Better:

```text id="0h273s"
GET /v1/files/{fileId}/content
  -> service checks tenant/user/domain authorization
  -> service streams file or issues short-lived signed URL
```

---

## 19. Worker Isolation

Parsing untrusted files should happen in a worker with controlled resources, not in the main API request.

```text id="shs9gz"
API Pod
  ├─ receive upload
  ├─ stage
  ├─ AV scan or enqueue scan
  └─ create job

Worker Pod
  ├─ parse file
  ├─ enforce parser timeout
  ├─ memory/cpu limits
  ├─ update job status
  └─ generate report
```

### Kubernetes separation

| Component | Resource profile |
|---|---|
| API pod | Low latency, small memory, no heavy parsing |
| File worker | Higher memory/CPU, controlled concurrency |
| AV scanner | Separate service/daemon |
| Object storage | Durable original file retention |
| PostgreSQL | Metadata, job state, audit |

---

## 20. Timeout & Resource Limits

| Operation | Limit |
|---|---|
| Upload request | HTTP/gateway timeout |
| Staging copy | Max size + copy timeout |
| MIME detection | Time bounded |
| AV scan | Time bounded |
| PDF extraction | Max pages + timeout |
| Excel parsing | Max sheets/rows/cells |
| XML parsing | Max size/depth/entity disabled |
| Archive extraction | Max entries/expanded size/depth |
| Export generation | Streaming + max rows or async |

Example parser budget:

```java id="0g9qqu"
public record ParserBudget(
        long maxFileSizeBytes,
        int maxRows,
        int maxColumns,
        int maxSheets,
        int maxPages,
        long maxExtractedTextChars,
        int timeoutSeconds
) {}
```

---

## 21. Domain Authorization

File security bukan hanya content security. User harus authorized untuk **jenis file dan domain object**.

| Scenario | Required check |
|---|---|
| Upload customer import | User can import customer data for tenant |
| Upload order attachment | User can modify/read order |
| Download generated report | User can access report scope |
| Download original file | User can access upload job and tenant |
| Retry failed import | User can execute import action |
| View error report | User can see row-level data |

Jangan hanya cek “user logged in”. Cek action + tenant + domain scope.

---

## 22. Secure Error Handling

Jangan bocorkan detail internal parser/security ke user.

### Internal log

```json id="4g1p80"
{
  "event": "file_rejected",
  "reason": "XXE_DOCTYPE_DETECTED",
  "fileId": "file-123",
  "tenantId": "tenant-1",
  "detectedContentType": "application/xml",
  "parser": "DocumentBuilderFactory",
  "correlationId": "corr-456"
}
```

### User response

```json id="x4dz61"
{
  "error": {
    "code": "UNSUPPORTED_OR_UNSAFE_FILE",
    "message": "The uploaded file could not be accepted because it failed security validation.",
    "correlationId": "corr-456"
  }
}
```

Jangan tampilkan:

```text id="q7kr81"
file:///etc/passwd
internal storage path
parser stack trace
AV internal socket address
bucket name
SQL error
```

---

## 23. Secure File Upload Checklist

```text id="jz3p88"
Authentication & authorization
[ ] User authenticated
[ ] User authorized for upload action
[ ] Tenant/domain scope checked
[ ] Download authorization checked separately

Request boundary
[ ] Multipart field name validated
[ ] Metadata schema validated
[ ] Size limit enforced at gateway and app
[ ] Rate limit / concurrency limit applied

Filename & path
[ ] Original filename never used as storage path
[ ] Filename sanitized for display
[ ] Generated storage key used
[ ] Path normalized and checked
[ ] No direct public storage path exposure

Type validation
[ ] Extension allowlist
[ ] Server-side MIME detection
[ ] Client Content-Type treated as hint only
[ ] Format-specific smoke validation

Malware/security scan
[ ] AV scan integrated where required
[ ] AV timeout handled
[ ] AV unavailable policy defined
[ ] Infected file rejected/quarantined
[ ] Quarantine storage isolated

Parser hardening
[ ] XML DTD disabled
[ ] XML external entities disabled
[ ] Secure processing enabled
[ ] ZIP/OOXML zip bomb limits enabled
[ ] Archive extraction prevents path traversal
[ ] Parser timeout enforced
[ ] Worker resource limits configured

Storage & audit
[ ] Original file retention policy defined
[ ] Checksum stored
[ ] Metadata stored
[ ] Audit actor/timestamp recorded
[ ] Access logs enabled
[ ] Sensitive download uses no-store

Observability
[ ] Rejection reason metrics
[ ] Scan duration metrics
[ ] Parser failure metrics
[ ] Quarantine count metrics
[ ] Correlation ID present
```

---

## 24. Senior-Level Design Invariants

| Invariant | Why it matters |
|---|---|
| File metadata from client is untrusted | Prevent spoofing |
| Original filename is display-only | Prevent path traversal |
| Storage key is server-generated | Prevent overwrite and traversal |
| Unsupported file type is rejected before parser | Reduce attack surface |
| XML parser must be hardened by default | Prevent XXE/SSRF/data leak |
| OOXML parser must keep zip bomb protection enabled | Prevent resource exhaustion |
| AV scanner failure must not silently allow risky file | Prevent fail-open exposure |
| Quarantine files are never auto-processed | Prevent delayed exploit |
| Parsing untrusted files belongs in worker isolation | Protect API latency and memory |
| Error response must not leak internals | Reduce attacker feedback |

---

## 25. Kesimpulan Seri 3

File security harus diperlakukan sebagai **policy + pipeline**, bukan satu validasi sederhana.

Desain aman:

```text id="zog4ox"
Upload
  -> authz
  -> size limit
  -> filename sanitize
  -> extension allowlist
  -> MIME detection
  -> checksum
  -> malware scan
  -> secure staging
  -> parser hardening
  -> worker isolation
  -> audit
```

Poin paling penting:

```text id="5mxuhe"
Never trust filename.
Never trust Content-Type.
Never parse XML with DTD/external entity enabled.
Never disable zip bomb protection casually.
Never process quarantined files automatically.
Never expose original file without domain authorization.
```

---

Rekomendasi lanjut: **Seri 4 - CSV/TSV Processing: Parsing, Header Mapping, Encoding, Validation, Error Report, dan Safe Export untuk Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice**.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:19:52 PM

# Seri 4/20 - CSV/TSV Processing: Parsing, Header Mapping, Encoding, Validation, Error Report & Safe Export

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada **CSV/TSV processing** di Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice:

```text
CSV / TSV Upload
   │
   ▼
Intake Pipeline
   │
   ▼
CSV Parser
   │
   ├─ detect charset / delimiter policy
   ├─ validate header
   ├─ parse row by row
   ├─ map raw record → DTO/domain command
   ├─ validate row
   ├─ collect row-level errors
   ├─ persist valid rows
   └─ generate error report
```

CSV terlihat sederhana, tetapi di production sering menjadi sumber bug karena delimiter, quote, newline dalam field, encoding, header mismatch, formula injection, duplicate row, dan partial failure. RFC 4180 mendokumentasikan format umum CSV dan mendaftarkan MIME type `text/csv`, tetapi dalam praktik banyak sistem memakai variasi delimiter, newline, quote, dan encoding sendiri. ([IETF](https://www.ietf.org/rfc/rfc4180.txt?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text
CSV/TSV Processing
│
├─ 1. File Contract
│   ├─ expected extension: .csv / .tsv
│   ├─ expected delimiter
│   ├─ required headers
│   ├─ optional headers
│   ├─ schema version
│   └─ max row/column limit
│
├─ 2. Parsing
│   ├─ charset handling
│   ├─ BOM handling
│   ├─ delimiter
│   ├─ quote char
│   ├─ escape char
│   ├─ null/blank handling
│   └─ malformed row handling
│
├─ 3. Mapping
│   ├─ raw record
│   ├─ typed DTO
│   ├─ normalized command
│   └─ domain entity/update
│
├─ 4. Validation
│   ├─ required field
│   ├─ type conversion
│   ├─ enum validation
│   ├─ cross-field validation
│   ├─ duplicate detection
│   └─ reference lookup
│
├─ 5. Persistence
│   ├─ staging table
│   ├─ batch insert
│   ├─ upsert
│   ├─ transaction boundary
│   └─ partial success policy
│
├─ 6. Error Reporting
│   ├─ row number
│   ├─ column name
│   ├─ raw value
│   ├─ error code
│   └─ suggested fix
│
└─ 7. Export
    ├─ safe CSV writing
    ├─ formula injection protection
    ├─ streaming response
    ├─ Content-Disposition
    └─ no full-memory buffering
```

---

## 3. Library Pilihan

| Library | Cocok untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| **Apache Commons CSV** | Parsing/writing CSV eksplisit dan predictable | Menyediakan `CSVFormat`, `CSVParser`, `CSVRecord`, `CSVPrinter`; dokumentasi resminya menyebut Commons CSV membaca dan menulis variasi format CSV dengan format umum yang sudah didefinisikan di `CSVFormat`. ([commons.apache.org](https://commons.apache.org/proper/commons-csv/apidocs/index.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| **OpenCSV** | Mapping CSV ke bean dengan annotation | Dokumentasi OpenCSV menyebutnya sebagai library parser CSV Java yang easy-to-use. ([opencsv.sourceforge.net](https://opencsv.sourceforge.net/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| **Jackson CSV** | CSV sebagai bagian dari ekosistem Jackson DTO mapping | `CsvMapper` menyediakan schema untuk POJO dan mapping dataformat CSV. ([fasterxml.github.io](https://fasterxml.github.io/jackson-dataformat-csv/javadoc/2.8/com/fasterxml/jackson/dataformat/csv/CsvMapper.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| **Manual parser** | Hampir tidak disarankan | Mudah salah untuk quote, newline, escape, dan edge case RFC-style CSV |

Rekomendasi praktis untuk microservice enterprise: mulai dengan **Apache Commons CSV** untuk kontrol eksplisit. Gunakan OpenCSV/Jackson CSV kalau project memang banyak memakai annotation-based binding atau sudah distandarkan pada Jackson untuk semua format.

---

## 4. Maven Dependency

```xml
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.apache.commons</groupId>
    <artifactId>commons-csv</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Kalau project memakai BOM/dependency management internal, ikuti versi yang sudah dikurasi. Hindari hardcode versi sembarangan di module service.

---

## 5. CSV Contract: Jangan Biarkan “Bebas Format”

Sebelum menulis parser, definisikan contract.

```text
Customer Import CSV Contract
│
├─ encoding: UTF-8
├─ delimiter: comma
├─ quote: double quote
├─ header required: customer_id, name, email, status
├─ header optional: phone, birth_date
├─ schemaVersion: 2026-07
├─ max rows: 100_000
├─ max columns: 50
└─ duplicate key: customer_id
```

### Contoh header

```csv
customer_id,name,email,status,phone,birth_date
C001,Alice,alice@example.com,ACTIVE,+628123456789,1990-01-01
C002,Bob,bob@example.com,INACTIVE,,1988-04-12
```

### Untuk TSV

```tsv
customer_id	name	email	status
C001	Alice	alice@example.com	ACTIVE
```

CSV/TSV jangan diperlakukan sebagai “file bebas”. Untuk import enterprise, CSV adalah **external contract**.

---

## 6. Data Model

### 6.1 Raw record

Raw record menyimpan nilai asli sebelum normalisasi.

```java
public record RawCustomerCsvRecord(
        long rowNumber,
        String customerId,
        String name,
        String email,
        String status,
        String phone,
        String birthDate
) {}
```

### 6.2 Typed DTO

DTO berisi hasil parsing dan type conversion.

```java
import java.time.LocalDate;

public record CustomerImportRow(
        long rowNumber,
        String customerId,
        String name,
        String email,
        CustomerStatus status,
        String phone,
        LocalDate birthDate
) {}

public enum CustomerStatus {
    ACTIVE,
    INACTIVE
}
```

### 6.3 Domain command

Command adalah bentuk yang dikirim ke application/domain service.

```java
public record UpsertCustomerCommand(
        String customerId,
        String name,
        String email,
        CustomerStatus status,
        String phone,
        LocalDate birthDate,
        String sourceFileId,
        long sourceRowNumber
) {}
```

Kenapa dipisah?

| Layer | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| Raw record | Audit nilai original |
| DTO | Parsing dan type conversion |
| Domain command | Operasi bisnis |
| Entity | Persistence internal |

Jangan langsung map CSV row ke entity database. Itu membuat format eksternal bocor ke model internal.

---

## 7. Error Model

### 7.1 Row error

```java
public record RowError(
        long rowNumber,
        String columnName,
        String rawValue,
        String errorCode,
        String message
) {}
```

### 7.2 Import result

```java
import java.util.List;

public record CsvImportResult(
        String jobId,
        int totalRows,
        int acceptedRows,
        int rejectedRows,
        List<RowError> errors
) {
    public boolean hasErrors() {
        return rejectedRows > 0;
    }
}
```

### 7.3 Error contoh

```json
{
  "rowNumber": 15,
  "columnName": "email",
  "rawValue": "not-an-email",
  "errorCode": "INVALID_EMAIL",
  "message": "Email is not valid"
}
```

Error yang baik harus bisa dijawab oleh user bisnis: **row mana, kolom mana, nilai apa, salahnya apa, harus diperbaiki bagaimana**.

## 8. CSV Parser dengan Apache Commons CSV

### 8.1 Format definition

```java
import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVFormat;

public final class CsvFormats {

    private CsvFormats() {}

    public static CSVFormat customerImportFormat() {
        return CSVFormat.DEFAULT.builder()
                .setHeader()
                .setSkipHeaderRecord(true)
                .setIgnoreEmptyLines(true)
                .setTrim(true)
                .setAllowMissingColumnNames(false)
                .build();
    }

    public static CSVFormat tsvFormat() {
        return CSVFormat.TDF.builder()
                .setHeader()
                .setSkipHeaderRecord(true)
                .setIgnoreEmptyLines(true)
                .setTrim(true)
                .setAllowMissingColumnNames(false)
                .build();
    }
}
```

`CSVFormat` adalah pusat konfigurasi Commons CSV untuk delimiter, quote, header, trim, dan variasi format; ini lebih aman daripada parser manual karena quote/newline/escape ditangani library. ([commons.apache.org](https://commons.apache.org/proper/commons-csv/apidocs/index.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

### 8.2 Parser service

```java
import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVParser;
import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVRecord;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.Charset;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class CustomerCsvParser {

    private static final List<String> REQUIRED_HEADERS = List.of(
            "customer_id",
            "name",
            "email",
            "status"
    );

    public ParsedCsv<RawCustomerCsvRecord> parse(
            Path csvFile,
            Charset charset,
            int maxRows
    ) {
        List<RawCustomerCsvRecord> records = new ArrayList<>();
        List<RowError> errors = new ArrayList<>();

        try (BufferedReader reader = Files.newBufferedReader(csvFile, charset);
             CSVParser parser = CsvFormats.customerImportFormat().parse(reader)) {

            validateHeaders(parser, errors);

            for (CSVRecord record : parser) {
                if (record.getRecordNumber() > maxRows) {
                    errors.add(new RowError(
                            record.getRecordNumber(),
                            null,
                            null,
                            "MAX_ROWS_EXCEEDED",
                            "CSV file exceeds maximum row limit: " + maxRows
                    ));
                    break;
                }

                records.add(toRawRecord(record));
            }

        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new CsvProcessingException("Failed to parse CSV file", e);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new CsvProcessingException("Malformed CSV file or invalid header", e);
        }

        return new ParsedCsv<>(records, errors);
    }

    private void validateHeaders(CSVParser parser, List<RowError> errors) {
        var headerMap = parser.getHeaderMap();

        for (String required : REQUIRED_HEADERS) {
            if (!headerMap.containsKey(required)) {
                errors.add(new RowError(
                        1,
                        required,
                        null,
                        "MISSING_REQUIRED_HEADER",
                        "Required header is missing: " + required
                ));
            }
        }
    }

    private RawCustomerCsvRecord toRawRecord(CSVRecord record) {
        return new RawCustomerCsvRecord(
                record.getRecordNumber(),
                get(record, "customer_id"),
                get(record, "name"),
                get(record, "email"),
                get(record, "status"),
                get(record, "phone"),
                get(record, "birth_date")
        );
    }

    private String get(CSVRecord record, String column) {
        return record.isMapped(column) ? record.get(column) : null;
    }
}
```

```java
import java.util.List;

public record ParsedCsv<T>(
        List<T> records,
        List<RowError> fileErrors
) {}
```

```java
public class CsvProcessingException extends RuntimeException {
    public CsvProcessingException(String message, Throwable cause) {
        super(message, cause);
    }
}
```

---

## 9. Header Validation

Header validation harus eksplisit.

| Check | Contoh masalah | Action |
|---|---|---|
| Required header missing | Tidak ada `customer_id` | Reject file |
| Duplicate header | `email,email` | Reject file |
| Unknown header | `customer_type` tidak dikenal | Warn atau reject sesuai policy |
| Header case mismatch | `Customer_ID` | Bisa normalize atau reject |
| Header whitespace | `" email "` | Trim header kalau policy mengizinkan |
| Wrong delimiter | Semua header terbaca sebagai satu kolom | Reject dengan message jelas |

### Strict vs lenient

| Mode | Perilaku | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| Strict | Unknown header ditolak | Regulatory/system import |
| Lenient | Unknown header diabaikan | User-facing import template |
| Compatibility | Header alias didukung | Migrasi template lama |

### Header alias example

```java
import java.util.Map;

public final class HeaderAliases {

    private static final Map<String, String> ALIASES = Map.of(
            "customer id", "customer_id",
            "customerId", "customer_id",
            "email_address", "email"
    );

    public static String canonicalize(String header) {
        if (header == null) {
            return null;
        }

        String normalized = header.trim();
        return ALIASES.getOrDefault(normalized, normalized);
    }
}
```

---

## 10. Encoding & BOM Handling

CSV adalah text format, sehingga encoding sangat penting. RFC 4180 mendaftarkan `text/csv`, tetapi interpretasi karakter tetap bergantung pada encoding/charset yang digunakan. ([IETF](https://www.ietf.org/rfc/rfc4180.txt?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Recommended policy

```text
Default encoding: UTF-8
Accepted encoding: UTF-8, optionally UTF-8 with BOM
Reject or quarantine: unknown binary-looking text
Avoid: platform default charset
```

### Jangan

```java
Files.newBufferedReader(path); // memakai default UTF-8 di modern JDK, tapi tetap lebih baik eksplisit
```

### Lebih baik

```java
Files.newBufferedReader(path, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
```

Kalau banyak file dari Excel Windows, pertimbangkan BOM handling dengan Apache Commons IO `BOMInputStream`, tetapi tetap simpan policy bahwa canonical import format adalah UTF-8.

---

## 11. Row Mapping & Type Conversion

### 11.1 Mapper

```java
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.format.DateTimeParseException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class CustomerCsvRowMapper {

    public MappingResult<CustomerImportRow> map(RawCustomerCsvRecord raw) {
        List<RowError> errors = new ArrayList<>();

        String customerId = normalizeBlank(raw.customerId());
        String name = normalizeBlank(raw.name());
        String email = normalizeBlank(raw.email());
        CustomerStatus status = parseStatus(raw.status(), raw.rowNumber(), errors);
        LocalDate birthDate = parseDate(raw.birthDate(), raw.rowNumber(), "birth_date", errors);

        if (customerId == null) {
            errors.add(required(raw.rowNumber(), "customer_id"));
        }

        if (name == null) {
            errors.add(required(raw.rowNumber(), "name"));
        }

        if (email == null) {
            errors.add(required(raw.rowNumber(), "email"));
        } else if (!isValidEmail(email)) {
            errors.add(new RowError(
                    raw.rowNumber(),
                    "email",
                    raw.email(),
                    "INVALID_EMAIL",
                    "Email format is invalid"
            ));
        }

        if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
            return MappingResult.invalid(errors);
        }

        return MappingResult.valid(new CustomerImportRow(
                raw.rowNumber(),
                customerId,
                name,
                email,
                status,
                normalizeBlank(raw.phone()),
                birthDate
        ));
    }

    private String normalizeBlank(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }
        return value.trim();
    }

    private CustomerStatus parseStatus(String value, long rowNumber, List<RowError> errors) {
        String normalized = normalizeBlank(value);

        if (normalized == null) {
            errors.add(required(rowNumber, "status"));
            return null;
        }

        try {
            return CustomerStatus.valueOf(normalized.toUpperCase());
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            errors.add(new RowError(
                    rowNumber,
                    "status",
                    value,
                    "INVALID_STATUS",
                    "Status must be ACTIVE or INACTIVE"
            ));
            return null;
        }
    }

    private LocalDate parseDate(
            String value,
            long rowNumber,
            String column,
            List<RowError> errors
    ) {
        String normalized = normalizeBlank(value);

        if (normalized == null) {
            return null;
        }

        try {
            return LocalDate.parse(normalized);
        } catch (DateTimeParseException e) {
            errors.add(new RowError(
                    rowNumber,
                    column,
                    value,
                    "INVALID_DATE",
                    "Date must use ISO format yyyy-MM-dd"
            ));
            return null;
        }
    }

    private boolean isValidEmail(String email) {
        return email.contains("@") && email.indexOf("@") > 0;
    }

    private RowError required(long rowNumber, String column) {
        return new RowError(
                rowNumber,
                column,
                null,
                "REQUIRED_FIELD",
                "Required field is missing"
        );
    }
}
```

```java
import java.util.List;

public record MappingResult<T>(
        T value,
        List<RowError> errors
) {
    public static <T> MappingResult<T> valid(T value) {
        return new MappingResult<>(value, List.of());
    }

    public static <T> MappingResult<T> invalid(List<RowError> errors) {
        return new MappingResult<>(null, errors);
    }

    public boolean isValid() {
        return errors == null || errors.isEmpty();
    }
}
```

### Prinsip conversion

| Field type | Rule |
|---|---|
| String | Trim, normalize blank to null kalau sesuai policy |
| Number | Parse eksplisit, jangan pakai locale default diam-diam |
| Date | Gunakan ISO `yyyy-MM-dd` atau contract eksplisit |
| Enum | Normalize case hanya jika policy mengizinkan |
| Boolean | Tentukan accepted values: `true/false`, `Y/N`, `1/0` |
| Money | Gunakan `BigDecimal`, bukan `double` |
| ID | Treat as string, bukan numeric, agar leading zero aman |

---

## 12. Validation Layers

```text
Validation
│
├─ File-level validation
│   ├─ extension
│   ├─ MIME
│   ├─ size
│   ├─ charset
│   └─ header
│
├─ Row-level validation
│   ├─ required field
│   ├─ type conversion
│   ├─ enum
│   ├─ format
│   └─ field length
│
├─ Cross-field validation
│   ├─ start_date <= end_date
│   ├─ status compatible with reason
│   └─ amount/currency pair
│
├─ Cross-row validation
│   ├─ duplicate key inside file
│   ├─ conflicting values
│   └─ sequence/order issue
│
└─ Database/domain validation
    ├─ reference exists
    ├─ unique business key
    ├─ tenant ownership
    └─ state transition allowed
```

### Duplicate detection inside file

```java
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Set;

public class CustomerCsvCrossRowValidator {

    public List<RowError> validateDuplicates(List<CustomerImportRow> rows) {
        Set<String> seenCustomerIds = new HashSet<>();
        List<RowError> errors = new ArrayList<>();

        for (CustomerImportRow row : rows) {
            if (!seenCustomerIds.add(row.customerId())) {
                errors.add(new RowError(
                        row.rowNumber(),
                        "customer_id",
                        row.customerId(),
                        "DUPLICATE_CUSTOMER_ID_IN_FILE",
                        "Customer ID appears more than once in the uploaded file"
                ));
            }
        }

        return errors;
    }
}
```

---

## 13. Processing Policy: Fail-Fast vs Partial Success

| Policy | Arti | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| Fail-fast | Satu error membuat seluruh file ditolak | System-to-system import, regulatory payload |
| Partial success | Row valid tetap diproses, row invalid masuk error report | User-facing bulk import |
| Dry run | Hanya validasi, tidak persist | Pre-check sebelum import |
| Staging first | Semua row masuk staging table, lalu validasi DB | Import besar, audit-heavy |

### Recommended untuk user-facing CSV import

```text
1. Parse semua row
2. Validasi semua row
3. Jika file-level error → reject file
4. Jika row-level error → partial success atau reject sesuai policy
5. Generate error report
6. Persist row valid dalam batch
```

### Recommended untuk regulatory/system import

```text
1. Parse semua row
2. Validasi semua row
3. Jika ada error → reject seluruh file
4. Tidak ada partial mutation
5. Simpan error report dan original file
```

---

### 14.2 MyBatis XML

```xml
<insert id="insertStagingRows">
    INSERT INTO customer_import_staging (
        job_id,
        row_number,
        customer_id,
        name,
        email,
        status,
        phone,
        birth_date,
        row_status,
        error_json,
        created_at
    )
    VALUES
    <foreach collection="list" item="row" separator=",">
        (
            #{row.jobId},
            #{row.rowNumber},
            #{row.customerId},
            #{row.name},
            #{row.email},
            #{row.status},
            #{row.phone},
            #{row.birthDate},
            #{row.rowStatus},
            #{row.errorJson}::jsonb,
            now()
        )
    </foreach>
</insert>
```

### 14.3 Staging table

```sql
CREATE TABLE customer_import_staging (
    job_id        TEXT NOT NULL,
    row_number    BIGINT NOT NULL,
    customer_id   TEXT,
    name          TEXT,
    email         TEXT,
    status        TEXT,
    phone         TEXT,
    birth_date    TEXT,
    row_status    TEXT NOT NULL,
    error_json    JSONB,
    created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (job_id, row_number)
);

CREATE INDEX idx_customer_import_staging_job_status
ON customer_import_staging (job_id, row_status);
```

Staging table berguna untuk audit, retry, reconciliation, dan error analysis. Untuk file kecil, bisa langsung batch insert ke target table, tetapi staging lebih defensible untuk proses enterprise.

---

## 15. Chunking untuk File Besar

Jangan simpan semua row ke memory kalau file besar.

```text
CSV parser stream
   │
   ├─ parse row
   ├─ map row
   ├─ validate row
   ├─ buffer up to batch size
   ├─ flush batch to DB/staging
   └─ continue
```

### Batch processor pattern

```java
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

public class BatchBuffer<T> {

    private final int batchSize;
    private final Consumer<List<T>> flusher;
    private final List<T> buffer = new ArrayList<>();

    public BatchBuffer(int batchSize, Consumer<List<T>> flusher) {
        this.batchSize = batchSize;
        this.flusher = flusher;
    }

    public void add(T item) {
        buffer.add(item);

        if (buffer.size() >= batchSize) {
            flush();
        }
    }

    public void flush() {
        if (!buffer.isEmpty()) {
            flusher.accept(List.copyOf(buffer));
            buffer.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

Recommended initial batch size:

| Operation | Batch size awal |
|---|---:|
| DB staging insert | 500-2.000 rows |
| Domain upsert berat | 100-500 rows |
| Error report collection | Stream to file jika sangat besar |
| In-memory validation | Batasi sesuai memory budget |

---

## 16. Error Report CSV

### 16.1 Error report format

```csv
row_number,column_name,raw_value,error_code,message
15,email,not-an-email,INVALID_EMAIL,Email format is invalid
20,status,UNKNOWN,INVALID_STATUS,Status must be ACTIVE or INACTIVE
```

### 16.2 Writer

```java
import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVFormat;
import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVPrinter;

import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;

public class CsvErrorReportWriter {

    public void write(Path target, List<RowError> errors) {
        CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.builder()
                .setHeader("row_number", "column_name", "raw_value", "error_code", "message")
                .build();

        try (BufferedWriter writer = Files.newBufferedWriter(target, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
             CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format)) {

            for (RowError error : errors) {
                printer.printRecord(
                        error.rowNumber(),
                        error.columnName(),
                        error.rawValue(),
                        error.errorCode(),
                        error.message()
                );
            }

        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new CsvProcessingException("Failed to write CSV error report", e);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 17. Safe CSV Export

CSV export bisa rentan terhadap CSV/formula injection. OWASP menjelaskan bahwa CSV Injection, juga dikenal sebagai Formula Injection, terjadi saat aplikasi memasukkan input tidak terpercaya ke file CSV dan spreadsheet menginterpretasikan cell tertentu sebagai formula. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/www-community/attacks/CSV_Injection?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 17.1 Nilai berisiko

Nilai cell yang dimulai dengan karakter ini harus dianggap berisiko saat diekspor ke spreadsheet-compatible CSV:

```text
=
+
-
@
tab
carriage return
line feed
```

### 17.2 Escaper

```java
public final class CsvExportSafety {

    private CsvExportSafety() {}

    public static String escapeSpreadsheetFormula(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isEmpty()) {
            return value;
        }

        char first = value.charAt(0);

        if (first == '='
                || first == '+'
                || first == '-'
                || first == '@'
                || first == '\t'
                || first == '\r'
                || first == '\n') {
            return "'" + value;
        }

        return value;
    }
}
```

### 17.3 Export endpoint

```java
@GET
@Path("/customers.csv")
@Produces("text/csv")
public Response exportCustomers() {
    StreamingOutput stream = output -> {
        try (BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(
                new OutputStreamWriter(output, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
             CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(
                     writer,
                     CSVFormat.DEFAULT.builder()
                             .setHeader("customer_id", "name", "email", "status")
                             .build()
             )) {

            customerQueryService.streamCustomers(customer -> {
                try {
                    printer.printRecord(
                            customer.customerId(),
                            CsvExportSafety.escapeSpreadsheetFormula(customer.name()),
                            CsvExportSafety.escapeSpreadsheetFormula(customer.email()),
                            customer.status()
                    );
                } catch (IOException e) {
                    throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
                }
            });
        }
    };

    return Response.ok(stream, "text/csv")
            .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"customers.csv\"")
            .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            .build();
}
```

Untuk data sensitif, gunakan `Cache-Control: no-store` dan authorization check sebelum streaming response.

## 18. Import Job Flow

```text
POST /v1/customer-imports
        │
        ▼
File Intake
        │
        ▼
Create job: PENDING
        │
        ▼
Worker starts
        │
        ▼
CSV file-level validation
        │
        ├─ failed header/format → FAILED
        │
        ▼
Row parsing + validation
        │
        ├─ row valid → staging/target table
        ├─ row invalid → error report
        │
        ▼
Finalize
        │
        ├─ no errors → SUCCESS
        ├─ some errors → PARTIAL_SUCCESS
        └─ fatal parser error → FAILED
```

### Status response

```json
{
  "jobId": "job_001",
  "status": "PARTIAL_SUCCESS",
  "totalRows": 1000,
  "acceptedRows": 970,
  "rejectedRows": 30,
  "links": {
    "errorReport": "/v1/customer-imports/job_001/errors.csv"
  }
}
```

## 19. Observability

Metrics:

| Metric | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `csv_import_started_total` | Counter | Jumlah job CSV dimulai |
| `csv_import_completed_total{status}` | Counter | Job selesai by status |
| `csv_import_rows_total{result}` | Counter | Valid/invalid rows |
| `csv_import_duration_seconds` | Histogram | Durasi processing |
| `csv_import_parse_error_total{code}` | Counter | Parser/file-level errors |
| `csv_import_row_error_total{code}` | Counter | Row validation errors |
| `csv_export_duration_seconds` | Histogram | Durasi export |
| `csv_export_rows_total` | Counter | Jumlah row diekspor |

Structured log:

```json
{
  "event": "csv_import_completed",
  "jobId": "job_001",
  "fileId": "file_001",
  "tenantId": "tenant_001",
  "totalRows": 1000,
  "acceptedRows": 970,
  "rejectedRows": 30,
  "status": "PARTIAL_SUCCESS",
  "durationMs": 8123,
  "correlationId": "corr_001"
}
```

Jangan log full row jika mengandung PII. Log cukup row number, error code, dan job/file id.

## 20. Testing Strategy

```text
CSV test fixtures
│
├─ valid
│   ├─ normal.csv
│   ├─ quoted-values.csv
│   ├─ multiline-field.csv
│   └─ utf8-bom.csv
│
├─ invalid-header
│   ├─ missing-required-header.csv
│   ├─ duplicate-header.csv
│   └─ wrong-delimiter.csv
│
├─ invalid-row
│   ├─ invalid-email.csv
│   ├─ invalid-date.csv
│   ├─ invalid-enum.csv
│   └─ duplicate-key.csv
│
├─ security
│   ├─ formula-injection.csv
│   ├─ huge-row.csv
│   └─ too-many-columns.csv
│
└─ export-golden
    ├─ expected-customers.csv
    └─ expected-errors.csv
```

### Unit test example

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class CsvExportSafetyTest {

    @Test
    void shouldEscapeFormulaLikeValues() {
        assertEquals("'=cmd()", CsvExportSafety.escapeSpreadsheetFormula("=cmd()"));
        assertEquals("'+SUM(A1:A2)", CsvExportSafety.escapeSpreadsheetFormula("+SUM(A1:A2)"));
        assertEquals("'@evil", CsvExportSafety.escapeSpreadsheetFormula("@evil"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldNotEscapeNormalValues() {
        assertEquals("Alice", CsvExportSafety.escapeSpreadsheetFormula("Alice"));
        assertEquals("alice@example.com", CsvExportSafety.escapeSpreadsheetFormula("alice@example.com"));
    }
}
```

---

## 21. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Parser manual pakai `String.split(",")` | Gagal untuk quoted comma, escaped quote, multiline field |
| Mapping by column index | Header berubah sedikit langsung salah mapping |
| Tidak validasi header | File salah format masuk domain |
| Pakai platform default charset | Hasil beda antar environment |
| Load semua row ke memory | OOM untuk file besar |
| Fail seluruh file tanpa error report | User tidak tahu cara memperbaiki |
| Langsung insert ke target table | Sulit rollback/audit partial failure |
| Tidak escape formula saat export | CSV injection risk |
| Tidak punya row number | Error report tidak actionable |
| Tidak punya schema version | Template evolution kacau |
| Tidak batasi row/column/field length | Resource exhaustion |

---

## 22. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| CSV adalah external contract | Harus punya schema/header/version |
| Parser harus streaming-friendly | Jangan load file besar ke memory |
| Header mapping lebih aman daripada index mapping | Format lebih tahan perubahan |
| Raw value harus tersedia untuk error report | User perlu tahu nilai yang salah |
| Row number harus berasal dari parser | Error harus bisa ditelusuri |
| DTO/domain command dipisah dari raw CSV | Format eksternal tidak bocor ke domain |
| File-level error berbeda dari row-level error | Contract status lebih jelas |
| Export harus formula-safe | CSV yang dibuka di Excel/LibreOffice bisa execute formula-like cell |
| Import harus idempotent | Retry tidak boleh duplicate mutation |
| Staging table berguna untuk audit | Terutama untuk regulatory/bulk import |

---

## 23. Production Checklist

```text
File contract
[ ] Required headers didefinisikan
[ ] Optional headers didefinisikan
[ ] Delimiter jelas
[ ] Encoding jelas
[ ] Schema version jelas
[ ] Max rows/columns/field length jelas

Parsing
[ ] Tidak pakai String.split(",")
[ ] Parser library digunakan
[ ] Header validation ada
[ ] Charset eksplisit
[ ] Empty line policy jelas
[ ] Blank/null policy jelas
[ ] Malformed row ditangani

Mapping & validation
[ ] Raw record dipisah dari DTO
[ ] DTO dipisah dari domain command
[ ] Required field validation
[ ] Type conversion validation
[ ] Enum validation
[ ] Cross-field validation
[ ] Duplicate-in-file validation
[ ] DB/reference validation

Persistence
[ ] Batch size dikontrol
[ ] Transaction boundary jelas
[ ] Staging table dipertimbangkan
[ ] Idempotency key/checksum digunakan
[ ] Partial success policy jelas

Error report
[ ] Row number ada
[ ] Column name ada
[ ] Raw value ada
[ ] Error code ada
[ ] Human-readable message ada
[ ] Error report downloadable

Export
[ ] Streaming response
[ ] Content-Type text/csv
[ ] Content-Disposition attachment
[ ] Cache-Control sesuai sensitivity
[ ] Formula injection escaping
[ ] Tidak full buffering di memory

Observability
[ ] Metrics row valid/invalid
[ ] Metrics duration
[ ] Structured log
[ ] Correlation id
[ ] Tidak log PII/full row
```

---

## 24. Kesimpulan Seri 4

CSV/TSV processing yang production-ready harus diperlakukan sebagai **data contract + streaming pipeline + validation engine**, bukan sekadar “baca file baris per baris”.

Pola yang sehat:

```text
CSV file
  -> intake security
  -> header validation
  -> streaming parse
  -> raw record
  -> DTO conversion
  -> validation
  -> staging/persistence
  -> error report
  -> audit + metrics
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text
Never parse CSV with String.split(",").
Never map by index if header is available.
Never trust CSV as clean data.
Never export user-controlled values without formula injection protection.
Never process large CSV fully in memory.
```

# Seri 5/20 - Excel Processing: XLS/XLSX Import, Apache POI, Streaming, Formula/Date Handling & Large Workbook Safety

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **Excel import processing** di Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice:

```text id="dcz2sk"
Excel Upload (.xls / .xlsx)
   │
   ▼
File Intake Pipeline
   │
   ├─ size limit
   ├─ MIME detection
   ├─ checksum
   ├─ AV scan
   └─ zip bomb guardrail
   │
   ▼
Excel Parser
   │
   ├─ workbook detection
   ├─ sheet selection
   ├─ header validation
   ├─ row iteration
   ├─ cell type conversion
   ├─ formula/date handling
   ├─ row-level validation
   └─ error report
   │
   ▼
Domain Processing / Staging Table / MyBatis Batch
```

Excel berbeda dari CSV karena Excel adalah **workbook model**, bukan plain text. Di dalamnya ada sheet, row, cell, style, formula, date serial, merged cells, hidden rows, data validation, shared strings, dan untuk `.xlsx` ada ZIP container. Apache POI menyediakan API Java untuk format Microsoft Office, termasuk HSSF untuk `.xls`, XSSF untuk `.xlsx`, dan SXSSF untuk streaming write `.xlsx` besar. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/components/spreadsheet/how-to.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="ch2f55"
Excel Processing
│
├─ 1. File-Level Contract
│   ├─ allowed extension: xlsx, maybe xls
│   ├─ allowed sheets
│   ├─ required sheet name
│   ├─ required header row index
│   ├─ max rows
│   ├─ max columns
│   └─ template version
│
├─ 2. Workbook Opening
│   ├─ WorkbookFactory
│   ├─ HSSFWorkbook for XLS
│   ├─ XSSFWorkbook for XLSX
│   ├─ encrypted/corrupt file handling
│   └─ zip bomb protection
│
├─ 3. Sheet Processing
│   ├─ select by sheet name
│   ├─ ignore hidden sheets?
│   ├─ validate header row
│   ├─ iterate physical/logical rows
│   └─ stop at max row limit
│
├─ 4. Cell Reading
│   ├─ string
│   ├─ numeric
│   ├─ date
│   ├─ boolean
│   ├─ blank
│   ├─ formula
│   └─ error cell
│
├─ 5. Mapping & Validation
│   ├─ raw Excel row
│   ├─ typed DTO
│   ├─ domain command
│   ├─ row errors
│   └─ cross-row validation
│
├─ 6. Persistence
│   ├─ staging table
│   ├─ MyBatis batch insert
│   ├─ upsert
│   └─ partial success policy
│
└─ 7. Safety
    ├─ memory limit
    ├─ zip bomb guard
    ├─ formula policy
    ├─ macro policy
    ├─ parser timeout
    └─ worker isolation
```

## 3. XLS vs XLSX vs XLSM

| Format | POI model | Karakteristik | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|---|---|
| `.xls` | HSSF | Format Excel lama berbasis binary OLE2 | Support hanya jika bisnis masih butuh legacy upload |
| `.xlsx` | XSSF | Format Office Open XML berbasis ZIP container | Default modern import/export |
| `.xlsm` | XSSF-compatible, macro-enabled | Bisa mengandung macro VBA | Biasanya reject untuk upload user biasa |
| `.xlsb` | Limited/different support | Binary workbook modern | Jangan support kecuali ada kebutuhan eksplisit |

Apache POI `WorkbookFactory` dapat membuat workbook yang sesuai, baik HSSFWorkbook maupun XSSFWorkbook, dengan auto-detection dari input. Ini berguna untuk code path import yang menerima `.xls` dan `.xlsx`, tetapi tetap lakukan extension/MIME allowlist sebelum parser. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/apidocs/5.0/org/apache/poi/ss/usermodel/WorkbookFactory.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Maven Dependency

Untuk Excel `.xls` dan `.xlsx`:

```xml id="u87zlv"
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.apache.poi</groupId>
    <artifactId>poi</artifactId>
</dependency>

<dependency>
    <groupId>org.apache.poi</groupId>
    <artifactId>poi-ooxml</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Gunakan versi dari dependency management/BOM project. Untuk aplikasi enterprise, penting untuk rutin patch POI karena parser file adalah attack surface.

---

## 5. File Contract untuk Excel Import

Jangan biarkan user upload workbook bebas tanpa kontrak.

```text id="n9qvhb"
Customer Import Excel Contract
│
├─ file type: .xlsx only
├─ sheet name: Customers
├─ header row: row 1
├─ data starts: row 2
├─ required columns:
│   ├─ Customer ID
│   ├─ Name
│   ├─ Email
│   └─ Status
├─ optional columns:
│   ├─ Phone
│   └─ Birth Date
├─ max rows: 100,000
├─ max columns: 50
├─ formula cells: reject or evaluate? explicit policy
├─ merged cells: reject in data area
└─ hidden rows/columns: reject or ignore? explicit policy
```

### Header contoh

| Customer ID | Name | Email | Status | Phone | Birth Date |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C001 | Alice | alice@example.com | ACTIVE | +628123456789 | 1990-01-01 |
| C002 | Bob | bob@example.com | INACTIVE |  | 1988-04-12 |

Excel import harus lebih ketat daripada Excel manual bisnis. Karena workbook punya fitur visual, user sering mengira format valid karena “kelihatan benar”, padahal cell type/header/formula bisa berbeda.

---

## 6. Excel Parser Strategy

Ada tiga pendekatan utama.

| Strategy | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| Usermodel `Workbook`/`Sheet`/`Row`/`Cell` | File kecil-menengah | API mudah | Bisa boros memory |
| SAX/event-based `.xlsx` reader | File `.xlsx` besar | Memory lebih hemat | Implementasi lebih kompleks |
| Convert to CSV first | Pipeline sederhana | Reuse CSV processor | Hilang style/formula/sheet semantics, butuh conversion aman |

Untuk onboarding dan microservice biasa, mulai dari **WorkbookFactory + clear limits**. Untuk file besar, pindahkan ke worker dan pertimbangkan SAX/event model.

---

## 7. Zip Bomb Protection untuk XLSX/DOCX

`.xlsx` adalah ZIP container. Jangan disable proteksi zip bomb. Apache POI menyediakan `ZipSecureFile`; dokumentasi API menyebut `setMinInflateRatio` mengatur rasio inflated-vs-deflated bytes untuk mendeteksi zip bomb, dengan default 1% atau `0.01d`. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/apidocs/dev/org/apache/poi/openxml4j/util/ZipSecureFile.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Dokumentasi konfigurasi POI juga menyediakan konfigurasi seperti max single zip entry size untuk membatasi memory consumption saat dokumen dari user diproses. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/components/configuration.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="er74hh"
import org.apache.poi.openxml4j.util.ZipSecureFile;

public final class ExcelSecurityConfig {

    private ExcelSecurityConfig() {}

    public static void apply() {
        ZipSecureFile.setMinInflateRatio(0.01d);
        ZipSecureFile.setMaxEntrySize(100L * 1024 * 1024); // 100 MB per ZIP entry
        ZipSecureFile.setMaxTextSize(20L * 1024 * 1024);  // 20 MB extracted text
    }
}
```

Jangan lakukan ini tanpa alasan kuat dan security review:

```java id="2n01ey"
ZipSecureFile.setMinInflateRatio(-1.0d); // dangerous: disables protection pattern
```
