## 9. Workbook Opening Pattern

```java id="6qf8wn"
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.Workbook;
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.WorkbookFactory;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Path;

public class ExcelWorkbookLoader {

    public Workbook open(Path excelFile) {
        ExcelSecurityConfig.apply();

        try {
            return WorkbookFactory.create(excelFile.toFile(), null, true);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new ExcelProcessingException("Failed to open Excel workbook", e);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            throw new ExcelProcessingException("Excel workbook is corrupt or unsupported", e);
        }
    }
}
```

`WorkbookFactory` berguna karena auto-detect workbook type, tetapi Anda tetap perlu close workbook setelah selesai. Dokumentasi factory POI menjelaskan factory ini membuat workbook yang tepat, HSSFWorkbook atau XSSFWorkbook, dengan auto-detection dari input. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/apidocs/5.0/org/apache/poi/ss/usermodel/WorkbookFactory.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="xmummg"
try (Workbook workbook = workbookLoader.open(path)) {
    // process workbook
}
```

---

## 10. Sheet Selection Policy

Jangan parse semua sheet secara default kecuali memang contract-nya multi-sheet.

```java id="ysfev7"
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.Sheet;
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.Workbook;

public class ExcelSheetSelector {

    public Sheet requireSheet(Workbook workbook, String expectedSheetName) {
        Sheet sheet = workbook.getSheet(expectedSheetName);

        if (sheet == null) {
            throw new ExcelRejectedException(
                    "MISSING_REQUIRED_SHEET",
                    "Required sheet is missing: " + expectedSheetName
            );
        }

        return sheet;
    }
}
```

### Sheet policy

| Policy | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Required sheet name | Gunakan untuk template bisnis |
| Sheet by index | Hindari kecuali file selalu generated |
| Multiple sheets | Definisikan contract per sheet |
| Hidden sheet | Biasanya ignore atau reject jika data area tersembunyi |
| Extra sheet | Ignore atau warn sesuai policy |

---

## 11. Header Mapping

Excel header harus dinormalisasi, tetapi jangan terlalu permisif.

```java id="6k3pub"
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.Row;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public class ExcelHeaderMapper {

    public Map<String, Integer> mapHeaders(Row headerRow) {
        Map<String, Integer> result = new HashMap<>();

        for (var cell : headerRow) {
            String header = ExcelCellReader.asDisplayString(cell);
            String canonical = canonicalizeHeader(header);

            if (canonical != null && !canonical.isBlank()) {
                if (result.containsKey(canonical)) {
                    throw new ExcelRejectedException(
                            "DUPLICATE_HEADER",
                            "Duplicate header: " + canonical
                    );
                }

                result.put(canonical, cell.getColumnIndex());
            }
        }

        require(result, "customer_id");
        require(result, "name");
        require(result, "email");
        require(result, "status");

        return result;
    }

    private void require(Map<String, Integer> headers, String name) {
        if (!headers.containsKey(name)) {
            throw new ExcelRejectedException(
                    "MISSING_REQUIRED_HEADER",
                    "Required header is missing: " + name
            );
        }
    }

    private String canonicalizeHeader(String header) {
        if (header == null) {
            return null;
        }

        String normalized = header.trim().toLowerCase();

        return switch (normalized) {
            case "customer id", "customer_id", "customerid" -> "customer_id";
            case "name", "customer name" -> "name";
            case "email", "email address" -> "email";
            case "status" -> "status";
            case "phone" -> "phone";
            case "birth date", "birth_date" -> "birth_date";
            default -> normalized;
        };
    }
}
```

---

## 12. Cell Reading: Jangan Pakai `toString()` Mentah

Excel cell punya type. `cell.toString()` sering menghasilkan output tidak sesuai contract: numeric ID bisa berubah menjadi scientific notation, date bisa menjadi serial number, formula bisa menjadi string formula, dan blank cell bisa ambigu.

### Cell types yang perlu dipahami

| Cell type | Contoh | Policy |
|---|---|---|
| String | `"Alice"` | Trim/normalize |
| Numeric | `123`, date serial | Bedakan number vs date |
| Boolean | `TRUE` | Convert eksplisit |
| Formula | `=A1+B1` | Reject atau evaluate sesuai policy |
| Blank | empty cell | Treat as null |
| Error | `#N/A`, `#VALUE!` | Reject row/cell |

### Utility reader

```java id="m8az0q"
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.*;
import org.apache.poi.ss.util.CellAddress;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.ZoneId;

public final class ExcelCellReader {

    private static final DataFormatter FORMATTER = new DataFormatter();

    private ExcelCellReader() {}

    public static String asDisplayString(Cell cell) {
        if (cell == null) {
            return null;
        }

        return switch (cell.getCellType()) {
            case STRING -> normalize(cell.getStringCellValue());
            case NUMERIC -> {
                if (DateUtil.isCellDateFormatted(cell)) {
                    yield normalize(FORMATTER.formatCellValue(cell));
                }
                yield normalize(FORMATTER.formatCellValue(cell));
            }
            case BOOLEAN -> String.valueOf(cell.getBooleanCellValue());
            case FORMULA -> throw formulaRejected(cell);
            case BLANK -> null;
            case ERROR -> throw new ExcelCellRejectedException(
                    cell.getAddress(),
                    "CELL_ERROR",
                    "Cell contains Excel error value"
            );
            default -> normalize(FORMATTER.formatCellValue(cell));
        };
    }

    public static LocalDate asLocalDate(Cell cell) {
        if (cell == null || cell.getCellType() == CellType.BLANK) {
            return null;
        }

        if (cell.getCellType() == CellType.FORMULA) {
            throw formulaRejected(cell);
        }

        if (cell.getCellType() == CellType.NUMERIC && DateUtil.isCellDateFormatted(cell)) {
            return cell.getDateCellValue()
                    .toInstant()
                    .atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault())
                    .toLocalDate();
        }

        String value = asDisplayString(cell);
        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }

        return LocalDate.parse(value);
    }

    private static String normalize(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }
        return value.trim();
    }

    private static ExcelCellRejectedException formulaRejected(Cell cell) {
        return new ExcelCellRejectedException(
                cell.getAddress(),
                "FORMULA_NOT_ALLOWED",
                "Formula cells are not allowed in this import"
        );
    }
}
```

```java id="c5dva6"
import org.apache.poi.ss.util.CellAddress;

public class ExcelCellRejectedException extends RuntimeException {

    private final CellAddress cellAddress;
    private final String code;

    public ExcelCellRejectedException(CellAddress cellAddress, String code, String message) {
        super(message);
        this.cellAddress = cellAddress;
        this.code = code;
    }

    public CellAddress cellAddress() {
        return cellAddress;
    }

    public String code() {
        return code;
    }
}
```

---

## 13. Formula Handling Policy

Formula di file import harus ditentukan secara eksplisit.

| Policy | Arti | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| Reject formula | Jika ada formula, row/cell ditolak | Import data operasional |
| Use cached value | Pakai value terakhir yang disimpan Excel | Template-generated trusted file |
| Evaluate formula | POI menghitung formula | Sangat hati-hati; bisa mahal/kompleks |
| Strip formula | Ambil output dan buang formula | Controlled internal workflow |

Untuk import dari user, default saya sarankan: **reject formula cells di data area**. Formula bisa menyembunyikan nilai, menghasilkan hasil berbeda antar environment, dan memperumit audit.

---

## 14. Date Handling

Excel date sering bermasalah karena Excel menyimpan date sebagai numeric serial dengan formatting. Karena itu `1990-01-01` bisa terbaca sebagai numeric, bukan string.

### Recommended date policy

```text id="62r6j2"
Accepted:
- Excel date cell formatted as date
- ISO string yyyy-MM-dd

Rejected:
- ambiguous localized string like 01/02/03
- formula date unless formula policy allows
```

### Date parser

```java id="sjh9br"
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.Cell;
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.DateUtil;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.format.DateTimeParseException;

public class ExcelDateParser {

    public LocalDate parse(Cell cell, String columnName, int rowNumber) {
        if (cell == null) {
            return null;
        }

        if (cell.getCellType() == CellType.NUMERIC && DateUtil.isCellDateFormatted(cell)) {
            return cell.getDateCellValue()
                    .toInstant()
                    .atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault())
                    .toLocalDate();
        }

        String value = ExcelCellReader.asDisplayString(cell);

        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }

        try {
            return LocalDate.parse(value);
        } catch (DateTimeParseException e) {
            throw new ExcelValueRejectedException(
                    rowNumber,
                    columnName,
                    cell.getAddress().formatAsString(),
                    value,
                    "INVALID_DATE",
                    "Date must be a real Excel date cell or ISO date yyyy-MM-dd"
            );
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan teknis: hindari localized date string seperti `01/02/2026` karena ambigu. Paksa ISO format jika user mengetik tanggal sebagai text.

---

## 15. Row Parser

```java id="jldmi0"
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.Row;
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.Sheet;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public class CustomerExcelParser {

    private static final int HEADER_ROW_INDEX = 0;
    private static final int DATA_START_ROW_INDEX = 1;

    private final ExcelHeaderMapper headerMapper = new ExcelHeaderMapper();

    public ParsedExcel<RawCustomerExcelRow> parse(Sheet sheet, int maxRows) {
        Row headerRow = sheet.getRow(HEADER_ROW_INDEX);

        if (headerRow == null) {
            throw new ExcelRejectedException("MISSING_HEADER_ROW", "Header row is missing");
        }

        Map<String, Integer> headers = headerMapper.mapHeaders(headerRow);

        List<RawCustomerExcelRow> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        List<ExcelRowError> errors = new ArrayList<>();

        int lastRowNum = sheet.getLastRowNum();
        int processedRows = 0;

        for (int rowIndex = DATA_START_ROW_INDEX; rowIndex <= lastRowNum; rowIndex++) {
            if (++processedRows > maxRows) {
                errors.add(new ExcelRowError(
                        sheet.getSheetName(),
                        rowIndex + 1,
                        null,
                        null,
                        null,
                        "MAX_ROWS_EXCEEDED",
                        "Excel file exceeds maximum row limit: " + maxRows
                ));
                break;
            }

            Row row = sheet.getRow(rowIndex);

            if (isEmptyRow(row)) {
                continue;
            }

            try {
                rows.add(toRawRow(sheet, row, headers));
            } catch (ExcelCellRejectedException e) {
                errors.add(new ExcelRowError(
                        sheet.getSheetName(),
                        rowIndex + 1,
                        null,
                        e.cellAddress().formatAsString(),
                        null,
                        e.code(),
                        e.getMessage()
                ));
            }
        }

        return new ParsedExcel<>(rows, errors);
    }

    private RawCustomerExcelRow toRawRow(
            Sheet sheet,
            Row row,
            Map<String, Integer> headers
    ) {
        return new RawCustomerExcelRow(
                sheet.getWorkbook().getSheetIndex(sheet),
                sheet.getSheetName(),
                row.getRowNum() + 1,
                read(row, headers, "customer_id"),
                read(row, headers, "name"),
                read(row, headers, "email"),
                read(row, headers, "status"),
                read(row, headers, "phone"),
                read(row, headers, "birth_date")
        );
    }

    private String read(Row row, Map<String, Integer> headers, String column) {
        Integer index = headers.get(column);
        if (index == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return ExcelCellReader.asDisplayString(row.getCell(index));
    }

    private boolean isEmptyRow(Row row) {
        if (row == null) {
            return true;
        }

        for (var cell : row) {
            if (ExcelCellReader.asDisplayString(cell) != null) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```java id="wfvoww"
import java.util.List;

public record ParsedExcel<T>(
        List<T> rows,
        List<ExcelRowError> fileErrors
) {}
```

```java id="uhdlqj"
public class ExcelRejectedException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String code;

    public ExcelRejectedException(String code, String message) {
        super(message);
        this.code = code;
    }

    public String code() {
        return code;
    }
}
```

## 16. Mapping & Validation

Gunakan pola yang sama dengan CSV: raw row → typed row → domain command.

```java id="6aw022"
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.format.DateTimeParseException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class CustomerExcelRowMapper {

    public MappingResult<CustomerExcelImportRow> map(RawCustomerExcelRow raw) {
        List<ExcelRowError> errors = new ArrayList<>();

        String customerId = normalize(raw.customerId());
        String name = normalize(raw.name());
        String email = normalize(raw.email());
        CustomerStatus status = parseStatus(raw, errors);
        LocalDate birthDate = parseBirthDate(raw, errors);

        required(raw, errors, "Customer ID", customerId, raw.customerId());
        required(raw, errors, "Name", name, raw.name());
        required(raw, errors, "Email", email, raw.email());

        if (email != null && !email.contains("@")) {
            errors.add(error(raw, "Email", raw.email(), "INVALID_EMAIL", "Email is invalid"));
        }

        if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
            return MappingResult.invalid(errors);
        }

        return MappingResult.valid(new CustomerExcelImportRow(
                raw.rowNumber(),
                customerId,
                name,
                email,
                status,
                normalize(raw.phone()),
                birthDate
        ));
    }

    private CustomerStatus parseStatus(RawCustomerExcelRow raw, List<ExcelRowError> errors) {
        String value = normalize(raw.status());

        if (value == null) {
            errors.add(error(raw, "Status", raw.status(), "REQUIRED_FIELD", "Status is required"));
            return null;
        }

        try {
            return CustomerStatus.valueOf(value.toUpperCase());
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            errors.add(error(raw, "Status", raw.status(), "INVALID_STATUS", "Status must be ACTIVE or INACTIVE"));
            return null;
        }
    }

    private LocalDate parseBirthDate(RawCustomerExcelRow raw, List<ExcelRowError> errors) {
        String value = normalize(raw.birthDate());

        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }

        try {
            return LocalDate.parse(value);
        } catch (DateTimeParseException e) {
            errors.add(error(raw, "Birth Date", raw.birthDate(), "INVALID_DATE", "Birth Date must use yyyy-MM-dd"));
            return null;
        }
    }

    private void required(
            RawCustomerExcelRow raw,
            List<ExcelRowError> errors,
            String column,
            String normalizedValue,
            String rawValue
    ) {
        if (normalizedValue == null) {
            errors.add(error(raw, column, rawValue, "REQUIRED_FIELD", column + " is required"));
        }
    }

    private ExcelRowError error(
            RawCustomerExcelRow raw,
            String column,
            String rawValue,
            String code,
            String message
    ) {
        return new ExcelRowError(
                raw.sheetName(),
                raw.rowNumber(),
                column,
                null,
                rawValue,
                code,
                message
        );
    }

    private String normalize(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : value.trim();
    }
}
```

```java id="45he2n"
import java.util.List;

public record MappingResult<T>(
        T value,
        List<ExcelRowError> errors
) {
    public static <T> MappingResult<T> valid(T value) {
        return new MappingResult<>(value, List.of());
    }

    public static <T> MappingResult<T> invalid(List<ExcelRowError> errors) {
        return new MappingResult<>(null, errors);
    }

    public boolean isValid() {
        return errors == null || errors.isEmpty();
    }
}
```

---

## 17. Memory & Large Workbook Safety

### Import

`WorkbookFactory`/XSSF usermodel nyaman, tetapi untuk workbook besar bisa boros memory karena model workbook dimuat. Untuk file besar, gunakan worker pod dengan memory limit jelas, atau gunakan event-based streaming reader untuk `.xlsx`.

### Export

Untuk menghasilkan `.xlsx` besar, gunakan SXSSF. Dokumentasi POI menjelaskan SXSSF adalah streaming extension dari XSSF untuk spreadsheet sangat besar saat heap terbatas; SXSSF menjaga low memory footprint dengan sliding window, dan row lama yang keluar dari window ditulis ke disk sehingga tidak lagi bisa diakses. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/components/spreadsheet/how-to.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) API `SXSSFWorkbook` juga dideskripsikan sebagai streaming version dari XSSFWorkbook yang memungkinkan file sangat besar dibuat tanpa kehabisan memory. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/apidocs/4.0/org/apache/poi/xssf/streaming/SXSSFWorkbook.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="6amxwk"
import org.apache.poi.xssf.streaming.SXSSFWorkbook;

try (SXSSFWorkbook workbook = new SXSSFWorkbook(100)) {
    workbook.setCompressTempFiles(true);

    // create sheet, rows, cells

    // write to OutputStream
} finally {
    // In POI SXSSF, dispose temp files when applicable.
}
```

Untuk import besar, SXSSF tidak membantu karena SXSSF adalah terutama untuk **write/export**, bukan membaca file besar.

---

## 18. Excel Limits

Excel punya batas format. Apache POI menyediakan enum `SpreadsheetVersion`; dokumentasi POI mencatat untuk Excel 97 format lama tersedia 64k rows dan 256 columns. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/apidocs/4.1/org/apache/poi/ss/SpreadsheetVersion.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk `.xlsx`, limit Excel modern jauh lebih besar daripada `.xls`, tetapi tetap tidak berarti service Anda boleh menerima workbook tanpa batas. Tetapkan limit aplikasi sendiri.

| Limit | Harus ditentukan di aplikasi |
|---|---|
| Max file size | Ya |
| Max sheets | Ya |
| Max rows per sheet | Ya |
| Max columns per sheet | Ya |
| Max cell text length | Ya |
| Max formula cells | Biasanya zero untuk import |
| Max merged regions | Ya jika diproses |
| Max processing time | Ya |
| Max memory per worker | Ya |

---

## 19. Hidden Rows, Merged Cells, Empty Rows

Excel punya fitur visual yang bisa membingungkan parser.

| Feature | Risiko | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|---|
| Hidden row | Data tersembunyi ikut terproses | Reject atau ignore eksplisit |
| Hidden column | Kolom penting tersembunyi | Reject untuk template import |
| Merged cell | Nilai hanya ada di cell kiri-atas | Reject di data area |
| Empty row di tengah | Bisa berarti gap atau akhir data | Tentukan policy |
| Formatted empty rows | `lastRowNum` tinggi padahal data kosong | Gunakan empty-row detection |
| Filtered rows | Row tidak terlihat tapi tetap ada | Jangan bergantung pada tampilan Excel |

Contoh check hidden row:

```java id="5x3n2u"
if (row.getZeroHeight()) {
    // reject or skip according to policy
}
```

---

## 20. Error Report Excel

Untuk user bisnis, error report Excel sering lebih enak daripada CSV.

### Error report columns

| Sheet | Row | Cell | Column | Raw Value | Error Code | Message |
|---|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| Customers | 15 | C15 | Email | not-an-email | INVALID_EMAIL | Email is invalid |
| Customers | 20 | D20 | Status | UNKNOWN | INVALID_STATUS | Status must be ACTIVE or INACTIVE |

### Error workbook writer

```java id="8eh6ya"
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.Row;
import org.apache.poi.xssf.streaming.SXSSFWorkbook;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.util.List;

public class ExcelErrorReportWriter {

    public void write(OutputStream output, List<ExcelRowError> errors) {
        try (SXSSFWorkbook workbook = new SXSSFWorkbook(100)) {
            workbook.setCompressTempFiles(true);

            var sheet = workbook.createSheet("Errors");

            Row header = sheet.createRow(0);
            header.createCell(0).setCellValue("Sheet");
            header.createCell(1).setCellValue("Row");
            header.createCell(2).setCellValue("Cell");
            header.createCell(3).setCellValue("Column");
            header.createCell(4).setCellValue("Raw Value");
            header.createCell(5).setCellValue("Error Code");
            header.createCell(6).setCellValue("Message");

            int rowIndex = 1;

            for (ExcelRowError error : errors) {
                Row row = sheet.createRow(rowIndex++);
                row.createCell(0).setCellValue(nullToBlank(error.sheetName()));
                row.createCell(1).setCellValue(error.rowNumber());
                row.createCell(2).setCellValue(nullToBlank(error.cellAddress()));
                row.createCell(3).setCellValue(nullToBlank(error.columnName()));
                row.createCell(4).setCellValue(nullToBlank(error.rawValue()));
                row.createCell(5).setCellValue(nullToBlank(error.errorCode()));
                row.createCell(6).setCellValue(nullToBlank(error.message()));
            }

            workbook.write(output);
            workbook.dispose();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new ExcelProcessingException("Failed to write Excel error report", e);
        }
    }

    private String nullToBlank(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value;
    }
}
```

---

## 21. Jersey Download untuk Error Report

```java id="fpsxqx"
@GET
@Path("/customer-imports/{jobId}/errors.xlsx")
@Produces("application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet")
public Response downloadExcelErrors(@PathParam("jobId") String jobId) {
    authorizeJobAccess(jobId);

    StreamingOutput stream = output -> {
        List<ExcelRowError> errors = importJobService.getExcelErrors(jobId);
        excelErrorReportWriter.write(output, errors);
    };

    return Response.ok(stream)
            .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"%s_errors.xlsx\"".formatted(jobId))
            .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            .build();
}
```

Jangan membuat `byte[]` besar di memory untuk error report. Stream langsung ke response atau generate ke object storage lalu stream dari storage.

## 23. Transaction Boundary

Untuk Excel import, hindari satu transaksi besar untuk seluruh workbook jika file besar.

| Strategy | Cocok untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Single transaction | File kecil, all-or-nothing | Lock lama, rollback mahal |
| Batch transaction | File besar dengan staging | Perlu finalization logic |
| Staging + final apply | Enterprise import | Lebih kompleks tapi audit-friendly |
| Dry run | Validasi sebelum apply | Perlu jalur status jelas |

Recommended:

```text id="oxl7gf"
1. Intake original file
2. Create job
3. Parse Excel
4. Insert staging rows in batches
5. Validate staging/domain references
6. Apply valid rows to target tables
7. Generate error report
8. Mark job SUCCESS / PARTIAL_SUCCESS / FAILED
```

```text id="7fadkc"
├─ invalid-row
│   ├─ invalid-email.xlsx
│   ├─ invalid-status.xlsx
│   ├─ invalid-date.xlsx
│   └─ duplicate-customer-id.xlsx
│
├─ edge-case
│   ├─ formula-cells.xlsx
│   ├─ hidden-rows.xlsx
│   ├─ merged-cells.xlsx
│   ├─ formatted-empty-rows.xlsx
│   └─ xls-legacy.xls
│
└─ security
    ├─ xlsm-macro.xlsm
    ├─ corrupt.xlsx
    └─ zip-bomb-like.xlsx
```

### Unit test arah

```java id="m4d4rv"
@Test
void shouldRejectMissingRequiredSheet() {
    ExcelRejectedException ex = assertThrows(
            ExcelRejectedException.class,
            () -> parser.parse(workbookWithoutCustomersSheet)
    );

    assertEquals("MISSING_REQUIRED_SHEET", ex.code());
}
```

---

## 26. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Menganggap Excel sama seperti CSV | Sheet/cell/formula/date behavior terabaikan |
| Memakai `cell.toString()` mentah | Numeric/date/formula bisa salah |
| Tidak membedakan `.xls` dan `.xlsx` | Legacy limit/performance/security beda |
| Import formula cell tanpa policy | Audit dan consistency lemah |
| Tidak set max rows/columns | Resource exhaustion |
| Tidak handle formatted empty rows | Parser mengira ada banyak data |
| Tidak validasi sheet/header | File salah template tetap masuk |
| Load workbook besar di API pod | OOM/latency spike |
| Disable zip bomb protection | Security risk |
| Tidak close workbook | Resource leak |
| Tidak generate error report | User tidak bisa memperbaiki file |
| Tidak simpan original file | Audit/reprocess sulit |

---

## 27. Production Checklist

```text id="42gu8q"
File policy
[ ] Allowed extension jelas: xlsx/xls?
[ ] XLSM/macro policy jelas
[ ] MIME detection server-side
[ ] File size limit
[ ] AV scan jika required
[ ] ZipSecureFile protection tidak dimatikan

Workbook contract
[ ] Required sheet name
[ ] Header row index
[ ] Data start row index
[ ] Required headers
[ ] Optional headers
[ ] Max rows
[ ] Max columns
[ ] Hidden row/column policy
[ ] Merged cell policy
[ ] Formula policy

Parsing
[ ] Workbook ditutup
[ ] Sheet dipilih eksplisit
[ ] Header divalidasi
[ ] Empty row handling
[ ] Cell type handling
[ ] Date handling
[ ] Formula handling
[ ] Error cell handling

Validation
[ ] Raw row dipisah dari typed DTO
[ ] Row-level error model
[ ] Cross-row duplicate validation
[ ] Domain/reference validation
[ ] Partial success policy

Persistence
[ ] Staging table dipertimbangkan
[ ] Batch insert size dikontrol
[ ] Transaction boundary jelas
[ ] Idempotency/checksum digunakan

Runtime
[ ] Worker terpisah dari API untuk file besar
[ ] Memory limit jelas
[ ] Parser timeout
[ ] Metrics dan structured log
[ ] Original file retention
[ ] Error report downloadable
```

---

## 28. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| Excel workbook adalah external contract | Harus ada sheet/header/template policy |
| Cell display tidak sama dengan cell value | Type conversion harus eksplisit |
| Formula harus punya policy | Default aman: reject di import data |
| Date harus dinormalisasi | Hindari localized ambiguous date |
| `.xlsx` adalah ZIP container | Zip bomb protection wajib |
| Large workbook tidak boleh diproses di API pod | Gunakan async worker |
| Error report harus pakai row/cell address | User perlu lokasi fix yang presisi |
| Original file harus retained | Audit dan reprocess |
| Staging table meningkatkan defensibility | Cocok untuk enterprise/regulatory |
| Export besar pakai SXSSF, bukan XSSFWorkbook biasa | Memory lebih aman |

---

## 29. Kesimpulan Seri 5

Excel import yang production-ready harus diperlakukan sebagai **template-driven data ingestion**, bukan free-form spreadsheet upload.

Pola sehat:

```text id="fxfv02"
Excel file
  -> intake/security
  -> workbook open with guardrails
  -> required sheet selection
  -> header validation
  -> typed cell reading
  -> row mapping
  -> row/domain validation
  -> staging/persistence
  -> error report
  -> audit + metrics
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="fxg1fb"
Never trust Excel visual appearance.
Never use cell.toString() as your only conversion strategy.
Never allow formula cells without explicit policy.
Never disable zip bomb protection casually.
Never process large workbooks inside the API request path.
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:24:06 PM

Lanjut **Seri 6 dari 20**. Sekarang fokusnya bukan import Excel, tetapi **membangun report/export Excel yang aman, hemat memory, dan nyaman dipakai user bisnis**.

# Seri 6/20 - Excel Report Generation: Template, Styling, SXSSF Streaming, Multi-Sheet Report, Large Export & Safe Formula Handling

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada **Excel generation/export**, bukan import.

Dalam Jersey/JAX-RS microservice, Excel export biasanya dipakai untuk:

```text
Business report
Reconciliation result
Error report
Audit extract
Operational dashboard export
Bulk data download
Regulatory evidence file
Template file for future import
```

Mental model-nya:

```text
HTTP Request
   │
   ▼
Authorize export access
   │
   ▼
Prepare export job or stream directly
   │
   ▼
Query data incrementally
   │
   ▼
Generate XLSX workbook
   │
   ├─ sheet
   ├─ header
   ├─ style
   ├─ row
   ├─ cell
   ├─ formula policy
   └─ metadata
   │
   ▼
Stream XLSX response / store generated file
```

Untuk report besar, **Apache POI SXSSF** adalah pilihan penting karena POI mendokumentasikan SXSSF sebagai streaming extension dari XSSF untuk membuat spreadsheet besar saat heap terbatas; SXSSF membatasi akses hanya pada row dalam sliding window dan menulis row lama ke disk. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/components/spreadsheet/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

```text
├─ 1. API Boundary
```

---

## 3. Generation Mode

| Mode | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| `XSSFWorkbook` langsung | Report kecil | API lengkap, mudah styling | Memory besar kalau banyak row |
| `SXSSFWorkbook` streaming | Export besar | Memory lebih stabil | Row lama tidak bisa diedit lagi setelah keluar window |
| Template-based XSSF | Report bisnis dengan layout tetap | Format lebih rapi dan familiar | Perlu hati-hati saat clone/style/formula |
| Async generated file | Report berat/lama | Tidak kena HTTP timeout | Perlu job status + storage |
| Direct streaming response | Report cepat/sedang | Simpler UX | Request tetap hidup sampai selesai |

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text
< 10k rows        → XSSFWorkbook masih masuk akal
10k-1M rows       → SXSSFWorkbook atau async export
> 1M rows         → pertimbangkan CSV/Parquet, bukan Excel
multi-minute job  → async export
PII-heavy export  → audit + authorization + no-store + retention policy
```

Excel cocok untuk konsumsi manusia. Untuk data volume sangat besar atau integrasi analytics, gunakan CSV atau Parquet.

## 5. API Contract untuk Excel Export

### 5.1 Direct download

```http
GET /v1/customer-reports/monthly.xlsx?period=2026-07
Accept: application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet
```

Response:

```http
200 OK
Content-Type: application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet
Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="customer-report-2026-07.xlsx"
Cache-Control: no-store
```

### 5.2 Async export

```http
POST /v1/exports
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json
{
  "type": "CUSTOMER_REPORT",
  "period": "2026-07",
  "format": "XLSX",
  "includeDetails": true
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "exportJobId": "exp_20260713_001",
  "status": "PENDING",
  "links": {
    "status": "/v1/exports/exp_20260713_001",
    "download": "/v1/exports/exp_20260713_001/file"
  }
}
```

Gunakan direct streaming untuk report ringan. Gunakan async job kalau report butuh query berat, banyak sheet, banyak row, atau perlu approval/audit sebelum download.

## 7. Basic Jersey Download Endpoint

```java
@Path("/customer-reports")
@Produces("application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet")
public class CustomerReportResource {

    private final CustomerReportExportService exportService;

    public CustomerReportResource(CustomerReportExportService exportService) {
        this.exportService = exportService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/monthly.xlsx")
    public Response downloadMonthlyReport(@QueryParam("period") String period) {
        authorizeReportAccess(period);

        StreamingOutput stream = output -> {
            exportService.writeMonthlyReport(period, output);
        };

        String filename = "customer-report-" + period + ".xlsx";

        return Response.ok(stream)
                .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"" + filename + "\"")
                .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
                .build();
    }

    private void authorizeReportAccess(String period) {
        // Check tenant, role, report privilege, PII privilege if needed.
    }
}
```

Untuk file sensitif, `Cache-Control: no-store` adalah default yang lebih aman agar browser/proxy tidak menyimpan export berisi data bisnis/PII.

## 8. Report Data Model

Gunakan model report terpisah dari entity DB.

```java
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;

public record CustomerReportRow(
        String customerId,
        String customerName,
        String email,
        String status,
        LocalDate registeredDate,
        BigDecimal lifetimeValue,
        int openOrders,
        String riskFlag
) {}
```

```java
import java.time.Instant;

public record CustomerReportMetadata(
        String reportName,
        String period,
        String tenantId,
        String generatedBy,
        Instant generatedAt,
        long totalRows
) {}
```

Jangan kirim entity database langsung ke workbook writer. Report model membuat boundary lebih jelas dan mencegah field internal ikut terekspor tanpa sengaja.

---

## 9. SXSSFWorkbook Writer Pattern

### 9.1 Workbook writer

```java
import org.apache.poi.xssf.streaming.SXSSFWorkbook;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.OutputStream;

public class CustomerReportWorkbookWriter {

    private static final int ROW_WINDOW_SIZE = 100;

    private final CustomerReportSheetWriter sheetWriter;

    public CustomerReportWorkbookWriter(CustomerReportSheetWriter sheetWriter) {
        this.sheetWriter = sheetWriter;
    }

    public void write(
            CustomerReportMetadata metadata,
            Iterable<CustomerReportRow> rows,
            OutputStream output
    ) {
        SXSSFWorkbook workbook = new SXSSFWorkbook(ROW_WINDOW_SIZE);
        workbook.setCompressTempFiles(true);

        try {
            WorkbookStyles styles = WorkbookStyles.create(workbook);

            sheetWriter.writeSummarySheet(workbook, styles, metadata);
            sheetWriter.writeDetailSheet(workbook, styles, rows);

            workbook.write(output);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new ExcelReportGenerationException("Failed to write Excel report", e);
        } finally {
            try {
                workbook.close();
            } catch (IOException ignored) {
                // log if needed
            }

            workbook.dispose(); // delete temporary files created by SXSSF
        }
    }
}
```

SXSSF menggunakan temp files di disk untuk menjaga memory tetap rendah, sehingga cleanup temp files harus eksplisit diperhatikan; dokumentasi POI menjelaskan bahwa SXSSF menulis row lama ke disk ketika keluar dari sliding window. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/components/spreadsheet/how-to.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. Sheet Writer

```java
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.Row;
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.Sheet;
import org.apache.poi.xssf.streaming.SXSSFWorkbook;

import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;

public class CustomerReportSheetWriter {

    public void writeSummarySheet(
            SXSSFWorkbook workbook,
            WorkbookStyles styles,
            CustomerReportMetadata metadata
    ) {
        Sheet sheet = workbook.createSheet("Summary");

        int rowIndex = 0;

        Row title = sheet.createRow(rowIndex++);
        title.createCell(0).setCellValue(metadata.reportName());
        title.getCell(0).setCellStyle(styles.title());

        Row period = sheet.createRow(rowIndex++);
        period.createCell(0).setCellValue("Period");
        period.createCell(1).setCellValue(metadata.period());

        Row generatedBy = sheet.createRow(rowIndex++);
        generatedBy.createCell(0).setCellValue("Generated By");
        generatedBy.createCell(1).setCellValue(metadata.generatedBy());

        Row generatedAt = sheet.createRow(rowIndex++);
        generatedAt.createCell(0).setCellValue("Generated At");
        generatedAt.createCell(1).setCellValue(
                metadata.generatedAt()
                        .atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault())
                        .toLocalDateTime()
        );
        generatedAt.getCell(1).setCellStyle(styles.dateTime());

        Row totalRows = sheet.createRow(rowIndex);
        totalRows.createCell(0).setCellValue("Total Rows");
        totalRows.createCell(1).setCellValue(metadata.totalRows());
        totalRows.getCell(1).setCellStyle(styles.integer());
    }

    public void writeDetailSheet(
            SXSSFWorkbook workbook,
            WorkbookStyles styles,
            Iterable<CustomerReportRow> rows
    ) {
        Sheet sheet = workbook.createSheet("Customers");

        int rowIndex = 0;
        writeHeader(sheet.createRow(rowIndex++), styles);

        for (CustomerReportRow reportRow : rows) {
            Row row = sheet.createRow(rowIndex++);

            int col = 0;
            ExcelValueWriter.writeString(row, col++, reportRow.customerId(), styles.text());
            ExcelValueWriter.writeString(row, col++, reportRow.customerName(), styles.text());
            ExcelValueWriter.writeString(row, col++, reportRow.email(), styles.text());
            ExcelValueWriter.writeString(row, col++, reportRow.status(), styles.text());
            ExcelValueWriter.writeDate(row, col++, reportRow.registeredDate(), styles.date());
            ExcelValueWriter.writeBigDecimal(row, col++, reportRow.lifetimeValue(), styles.currency());
            ExcelValueWriter.writeInteger(row, col++, reportRow.openOrders(), styles.integer());
            ExcelValueWriter.writeString(row, col, reportRow.riskFlag(), styles.text());
        }
    }

    private void writeHeader(Row row, WorkbookStyles styles) {
        String[] headers = {
                "Customer ID",
                "Customer Name",
                "Email",
                "Status",
                "Registered Date",
                "Lifetime Value",
                "Open Orders",
                "Risk Flag"
        };

        for (int i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) {
            row.createCell(i).setCellValue(headers[i]);
            row.getCell(i).setCellStyle(styles.header());
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 11. Style Factory: Jangan Buat Style Per Cell

Apache POI menyediakan `Workbook#createCellStyle()` dan `Workbook#createDataFormat()` untuk membuat style dan data format pada workbook. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/apidocs/5.0/org/apache/poi/ss/usermodel/Workbook.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Namun style jangan dibuat per cell, karena workbook punya batas style dan pembuatan style berulang membuat file membengkak dan performa turun.

### 11.1 Style container

```java
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.CellStyle;

public record WorkbookStyles(
        CellStyle title,
        CellStyle header,
        CellStyle text,
        CellStyle date,
        CellStyle dateTime,
        CellStyle integer,
        CellStyle currency
) {
    public static WorkbookStyles create(org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.Workbook workbook) {
        return WorkbookStyleFactory.create(workbook);
    }
}
```

### 11.2 Style factory

```java
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.*;

public final class WorkbookStyleFactory {

    private WorkbookStyleFactory() {}

    public static WorkbookStyles create(Workbook workbook) {
        Font titleFont = workbook.createFont();
        titleFont.setBold(true);
        titleFont.setFontHeightInPoints((short) 14);

        Font headerFont = workbook.createFont();
        headerFont.setBold(true);

        DataFormat dataFormat = workbook.createDataFormat();

        CellStyle title = workbook.createCellStyle();
        title.setFont(titleFont);

        CellStyle header = workbook.createCellStyle();
        header.setFont(headerFont);
        header.setWrapText(true);

        CellStyle text = workbook.createCellStyle();
        text.setWrapText(false);

        CellStyle date = workbook.createCellStyle();
        date.setDataFormat(dataFormat.getFormat("yyyy-mm-dd"));

        CellStyle dateTime = workbook.createCellStyle();
        dateTime.setDataFormat(dataFormat.getFormat("yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss"));

        CellStyle integer = workbook.createCellStyle();
        integer.setDataFormat(dataFormat.getFormat("#,##0"));

        CellStyle currency = workbook.createCellStyle();
        currency.setDataFormat(dataFormat.getFormat("#,##0.00"));

        return new WorkbookStyles(
                title,
                header,
                text,
                date,
                dateTime,
                integer,
                currency
        );
    }
}
```

POI `CellStyle` mendukung properti seperti wrap text, alignment, fill, border, dan data format; gunakan style sebagai reusable object, bukan object baru untuk setiap cell. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/apidocs/dev/org/apache/poi/ss/usermodel/CellStyle.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Value Writer: Type-Safe Cell Output

```java
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.CellStyle;
import org.apache.poi.ss.usermodel.Row;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.Date;

public final class ExcelValueWriter {

    private ExcelValueWriter() {}

    public static void writeString(Row row, int column, String value, CellStyle style) {
        var cell = row.createCell(column);
        cell.setCellValue(value == null ? "" : sanitizeText(value));
        cell.setCellStyle(style);
    }

    public static void writeInteger(Row row, int column, Integer value, CellStyle style) {
        var cell = row.createCell(column);
        if (value != null) {
            cell.setCellValue(value);
        }
        cell.setCellStyle(style);
    }

    public static void writeBigDecimal(Row row, int column, BigDecimal value, CellStyle style) {
        var cell = row.createCell(column);
        if (value != null) {
            cell.setCellValue(value.doubleValue());
        }
        cell.setCellStyle(style);
    }

    public static void writeDate(Row row, int column, LocalDate value, CellStyle style) {
        var cell = row.createCell(column);
        if (value != null) {
            Date date = Date.from(value.atStartOfDay(ZoneId.systemDefault()).toInstant());
            cell.setCellValue(date);
        }
        cell.setCellStyle(style);
    }

    private static String sanitizeText(String value) {
        return value
                .replace('\u0000', ' ')
                .replace('\r', ' ')
                .replace('\n', ' ')
                .trim();
    }
}
```

Catatan penting: untuk monetary value, `BigDecimal` adalah model domain yang tepat, tetapi Apache POI numeric cell memakai `double`. Untuk report manusia ini biasanya cukup, tetapi untuk financial/regulatory output yang membutuhkan presisi absolut, pertimbangkan menulis nilai sebagai formatted string atau pastikan aturan rounding difinalisasi sebelum export.

---

## 13. Formula Handling: Jangan Masukkan Input User ke Formula

Untuk `.xlsx`, formula biasanya hanya dibuat jika aplikasi memanggil `setCellFormula(...)`. Karena itu aturan aman:

```text
Never call setCellFormula() with untrusted input.
Never build formula from user-controlled field name/value.
Prefer pre-defined formulas owned by application.
For untrusted text, write as string cell.
```

OWASP menjelaskan formula/spreadsheet injection terjadi saat aplikasi memasukkan input tidak terpercaya ke file yang dibuka spreadsheet dan nilai tertentu diinterpretasikan sebagai formula; risiko ini paling terkenal pada CSV, tetapi prinsip amannya tetap relevan untuk spreadsheet-compatible export. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/www-community/attacks/CSV_Injection?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 13.1 Safe formula policy

```java
public final class ExcelFormulaPolicy {

    private ExcelFormulaPolicy() {}

    public static String trustedSumFormula(String startCell, String endCell) {
        if (!isSafeCellRef(startCell) || !isSafeCellRef(endCell)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsafe cell reference");
        }

        return "SUM(" + startCell + ":" + endCell + ")";
    }

    private static boolean isSafeCellRef(String value) {
        return value != null && value.matches("[A-Z]{1,3}[1-9][0-9]{0,6}");
    }
}
```

### 13.2 Safe application-owned formula

```java
var totalCell = summaryRow.createCell(1);
totalCell.setCellFormula(ExcelFormulaPolicy.trustedSumFormula("F2", "F1001"));
totalCell.setCellStyle(styles.currency());
```

### 13.3 Unsafe

```java
cell.setCellFormula(userProvidedFormula); // unsafe
```

---

## 14. Excel vs CSV Formula Injection

| Format | Risk mode | Practical mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| CSV | Spreadsheet app may interpret text beginning with `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, tab, CR/LF as formula | Prefix/escape dangerous values before export |
| XLSX string cell | String cell is not the same as formula cell | Use `setCellValue(String)`, not `setCellFormula` |
| XLSX formula cell | Formula executes/recalculates in spreadsheet | Only use application-owned formulas |
| Template workbook | Existing formulas may reference generated cells | Keep formulas deterministic and reviewed |

Untuk XLSX, jangan overcorrect dengan menambahkan apostrophe ke semua text jika tidak diperlukan. Namun untuk field yang nanti mungkin diekspor ulang ke CSV oleh downstream user, formula-like string tetap bisa diberi guard sesuai policy organisasi.

---

## 15. Multi-Sheet Report Design

```text
Workbook
│
├─ Summary
│   ├─ report title
│   ├─ generated at
│   ├─ period
│   ├─ totals
│   └─ caveats
│
├─ Details
│   ├─ raw/detail rows
│   ├─ normalized values
│   └─ row-level status
│
├─ Exceptions
│   ├─ failed rows
│   ├─ error code
│   └─ remediation message
│
├─ Metadata
│   ├─ source query
│   ├─ filters
│   ├─ job id
│   ├─ checksum
│   └─ data version
│
└─ Lookup / Reference
    ├─ allowed statuses
    ├─ business definitions
    └─ code descriptions
```

### Sheet naming rules

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Keep sheet names short | Excel UI readability |
| Avoid user-controlled sheet names | Prevent confusing/malicious names |
| Use stable names | Downstream macros/scripts may depend on them |
| Put metadata in separate sheet | Audit and reproducibility |
| Include caveats | Prevent misinterpretation |

---

## 16. Template-Based Report

Template cocok kalau user bisnis sudah punya layout yang disepakati.

```text
template/customer-report-template.xlsx
   │
   ├─ pre-built styles
   ├─ company header
   ├─ formulas
   ├─ print layout
   └─ placeholder cells
```

Pattern:

```text
Load template workbook
   │
   ├─ fill metadata cells
   ├─ fill detail rows
   ├─ preserve styles
   ├─ update formulas if needed
   └─ write output
```

Untuk template kecil-menengah, `XSSFWorkbook` lebih mudah karena bisa membaca dan memodifikasi workbook existing. Untuk dataset besar, gunakan template hanya untuk style/layout dasar lalu generate detail sheet dengan SXSSF; desain ini lebih kompleks karena SXSSF tidak cocok untuk banyak operasi random-access.

### Placeholder strategy

| Placeholder | Value |
|---|---|
| `${report.period}` | `2026-07` |
| `${generated.at}` | timestamp |
| `${generated.by}` | user |
| `${total.rows}` | row count |

Hindari placeholder replacement berbasis search global yang sembarangan. Lebih aman isi cell tertentu berdasarkan named range atau coordinate yang dikelola template.

---

## 17. Large Export Data Source Strategy

Masalah besar export Excel bukan hanya workbook generation, tapi juga query data.

```text
Bad:
query all rows into List
  -> generate workbook

Better:
query page/cursor
  -> write rows incrementally
  -> flush workbook window
```

### 17.1 MyBatis cursor idea

```java
import org.apache.ibatis.cursor.Cursor;

public interface CustomerReportMapper {
    Cursor<CustomerReportRow> streamCustomerReportRows(CustomerReportFilter filter);
}
```

```java
public void writeMonthlyReport(String period, OutputStream output) {
    CustomerReportFilter filter = new CustomerReportFilter(period);

    try (Cursor<CustomerReportRow> cursor = mapper.streamCustomerReportRows(filter)) {
        CustomerReportMetadata metadata = metadataService.build(period);
        workbookWriter.write(metadata, cursor, output);
    } catch (IOException e) {
        throw new ExcelReportGenerationException("Failed to stream report data", e);
    }
}
```

### 17.2 Pagination fallback

```text
page = 0
size = 1000

while true:
    rows = query page
    if rows empty: break
    write rows
    page++
```

Cursor lebih streaming-friendly, tetapi pagination lebih mudah dikontrol dan di-retry. Untuk dataset yang berubah selama export, gunakan snapshot/filter yang stabil agar row tidak lompat/duplikat.

---

## 18. Direct Streaming vs Generate-to-Storage

| Strategy | Alur | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| Direct streaming | Request → generate → response | Report kecil/sedang dan cepat |
| Generate temp file | Request/job → write temp → stream | Perlu tahu `Content-Length` atau reuse |
| Generate object storage | Async job → object storage → download | Report besar, audit, retry, approval |
| Precomputed report | Scheduled generation | WBR/MBR/QBR/report rutin |

Untuk direct streaming, begitu response mulai dikirim, error handling menjadi terbatas. Kalau query/generation bisa gagal di tengah, async generation ke storage lebih aman karena file baru diberikan ke user setelah lengkap.

---

## 19. Export Job Model

```sql
CREATE TABLE export_job (
    id                  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
    requested_by         TEXT NOT NULL,

    export_type          TEXT NOT NULL,
    export_format        TEXT NOT NULL,
    filter_json          JSONB NOT NULL,

    status               TEXT NOT NULL,
    total_rows           BIGINT,
    file_object_id       UUID,
    failure_code         TEXT,
    failure_message      TEXT,

    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    started_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    expires_at           TIMESTAMPTZ
);
```

Status:

```text
PENDING
RUNNING
SUCCESS
FAILED
EXPIRED
CANCELLED
```

Async export memberi audit trail yang lebih baik: siapa meminta report, parameter apa, kapan selesai, ukuran file berapa, dan file mana yang di-download.

---

## 20. Row Limits & Excel Constraints

Tetapkan limit aplikasi sendiri walaupun Excel mendukung worksheet besar. Jangan menganggap “kalau Excel bisa, service juga harus bisa”.

| Limit | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Max rows per sheet | Tentukan per report |
| Max columns | Hindari report terlalu lebar |
| Max sheets | Batasi agar file tetap usable |
| Max generated file size | Lindungi storage/download |
| Max duration | Gunakan async jika melebihi budget |
| Max query window | Hindari export seluruh database tanpa filter |
| Max PII rows | Butuh privilege khusus atau masking |

Untuk export terlalu besar, lebih baik response jujur:

```json
{
  "error": {
    "code": "EXPORT_TOO_LARGE",
    "message": "The requested export is too large for XLSX. Please narrow the filter or use CSV/Parquet export."
  }
}
```

---

## 21. Styling Rules yang Praktis

| Area | Style |
|---|---|
| Title | Bold, larger font |
| Header | Bold, wrap text |
| Date | `yyyy-mm-dd` |
| Date-time | `yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss` |
| Integer | `#,##0` |
| Decimal/currency | `#,##0.00` |
| Text ID | Text cell, not numeric |
| Status | Optional style, but do not over-style |
| Error rows | Separate sheet usually better than heavy coloring |

Hindari styling berlebihan. Report enterprise yang baik lebih mengutamakan konsistensi, filterability, dan auditability daripada warna banyak.

---

## 22. Auto Size Column Concern

`autoSizeColumn` terlihat nyaman, tetapi untuk sheet besar bisa mahal karena harus mengukur isi cell. Untuk SXSSF large export, lebih aman:

```text
- set fixed width per column
- auto-size hanya untuk small report
- batasi sample rows kalau perlu
```

Contoh fixed width:

```java
sheet.setColumnWidth(0, 18 * 256); // Customer ID
sheet.setColumnWidth(1, 30 * 256); // Name
sheet.setColumnWidth(2, 35 * 256); // Email
sheet.setColumnWidth(3, 15 * 256); // Status
```

---

## 23. Freeze Pane & Filter

Untuk report manusia, ini meningkatkan usability.

```java
sheet.createFreezePane(0, 1);
sheet.setAutoFilter(new org.apache.poi.ss.util.CellRangeAddress(
        0,
        0,
        0,
        7
));
```

Use case:

| Feature | Use |
|---|---|
| Freeze header row | Header tetap terlihat saat scroll |
| Auto filter | User bisa filter status/error |
| Separate summary sheet | User dapat overview cepat |
| Metadata sheet | Audit dan reproducibility |
| Hidden technical sheet | Lookup/reference internal, jika perlu |

---

## 24. Error Report Excel Pattern

Error report sebaiknya punya sheet yang actionable.

```text
Errors sheet
│
├─ Sheet Name
├─ Row Number
├─ Cell Address
├─ Column Name
├─ Raw Value
├─ Error Code
├─ Message
└─ Suggested Fix
```

Contoh writer:

```java
public void writeErrorsSheet(
        SXSSFWorkbook workbook,
        WorkbookStyles styles,
        Iterable<ExcelRowError> errors
) {
    Sheet sheet = workbook.createSheet("Errors");

    Row header = sheet.createRow(0);
    String[] headers = {
            "Sheet",
            "Row",
            "Cell",
            "Column",
            "Raw Value",
            "Error Code",
            "Message",
            "Suggested Fix"
    };

    for (int i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) {
        header.createCell(i).setCellValue(headers[i]);
        header.getCell(i).setCellStyle(styles.header());
    }

    int rowIndex = 1;

    for (ExcelRowError error : errors) {
        Row row = sheet.createRow(rowIndex++);

        ExcelValueWriter.writeString(row, 0, error.sheetName(), styles.text());
        ExcelValueWriter.writeInteger(row, 1, error.rowNumber(), styles.integer());
        ExcelValueWriter.writeString(row, 2, error.cellAddress(), styles.text());
        ExcelValueWriter.writeString(row, 3, error.columnName(), styles.text());
        ExcelValueWriter.writeString(row, 4, error.rawValue(), styles.text());
        ExcelValueWriter.writeString(row, 5, error.errorCode(), styles.text());
        ExcelValueWriter.writeString(row, 6, error.message(), styles.text());
        ExcelValueWriter.writeString(row, 7, suggestFix(error.errorCode()), styles.text());
    }

    sheet.createFreezePane(0, 1);
}
```

Error report bukan hanya “log untuk developer”. Ia harus membantu user bisnis memperbaiki file.

---

## 25. PII & Sensitive Data Policy

Excel export sering bocor karena terlalu mudah di-download dan dibagikan.

| Risk | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| User export data di luar scope | Check tenant/domain/role |
| Export seluruh customer base | Require special privilege |
| Email/phone/address leak | Masking atau explicit PII permission |
| Browser/proxy cache | `Cache-Control: no-store` |
| File URL dibagikan | Short-lived signed URL atau proxy download with authz |
| Report terlalu lama disimpan | `expires_at` dan cleanup job |
| Tidak ada audit | Log export request + download event |

### Masking example

```java
public final class PiiMasking {

    private PiiMasking() {}

    public static String maskEmail(String email) {
        if (email == null || !email.contains("@")) {
            return email;
        }

        String[] parts = email.split("@", 2);
        String local = parts[0];

        if (local.length() <= 2) {
            return "**@" + parts[1];
        }

        return local.charAt(0) + "***" + local.charAt(local.length() - 1) + "@" + parts[1];
    }
}
```

---

## 26. Failure Modes

| Failure mode | Contoh | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| OOM | Report dibuat dengan `XSSFWorkbook` untuk 500k rows | SXSSF, async, row limit |
| Temp disk penuh | SXSSF temp files memenuhi `/tmp` | Staging dir eksplisit, ephemeral storage limit, cleanup |
| Response gagal di tengah | Client disconnect / query error | Async generate-to-storage untuk export berat |
| Style explosion | Buat style baru per cell | Style cache |
| Formula injection | `setCellFormula` dari input user | Formula allowlist |
| Wrong type | ID `00123` ditulis numeric jadi `123` | Write ID as string |
| Huge query | Full table export tanpa filter | Required filter + max row limit |
| Sensitive leak | Report include PII tanpa privilege | Column-level policy |
| Excel unusable | Terlalu banyak sheet/columns | Report design review |
| Corrupt file | Exception saat workbook write | Generate to temp then publish only after success |

---

## 27. Observability

Metrics:

| Metric | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `excel_export_started_total` | Counter | Jumlah export dimulai |
| `excel_export_completed_total{status}` | Counter | Export selesai by status |
| `excel_export_duration_seconds` | Histogram | Durasi generation |
| `excel_export_rows_total{report_type}` | Counter | Jumlah row diekspor |
| `excel_export_file_size_bytes` | Histogram | Ukuran file output |
| `excel_export_failed_total{reason}` | Counter | Alasan gagal |
| `excel_export_pii_columns_total` | Gauge/Counter | Indikasi export sensitive |
| `excel_export_temp_cleanup_failed_total` | Counter | Cleanup SXSSF/temp gagal |

Structured log:

```json
{
  "event": "excel_export_completed",
  "exportJobId": "exp_20260713_001",
  "tenantId": "tenant_001",
  "requestedBy": "user_001",
  "reportType": "CUSTOMER_REPORT",
  "format": "XLSX",
  "rowCount": 125000,
  "sheetCount": 3,
  "fileSizeBytes": 18392012,
  "durationMs": 42150,
  "status": "SUCCESS",
  "correlationId": "corr_001"
}
```

Jangan log isi report. Untuk PII-heavy export, log metadata export dan event download, bukan data cell.

---

## 28. Testing Strategy

```text
excel-export-tests
│
├─ unit
│   ├─ style factory creates reusable styles
│   ├─ value writer preserves string IDs
│   ├─ formula policy rejects unsafe reference
│   └─ PII masking works
│
├─ golden-file
│   ├─ small-report.xlsx
│   ├─ error-report.xlsx
│   └─ metadata-sheet.xlsx
│
├─ integration
│   ├─ MyBatis cursor export
│   ├─ Jersey download endpoint
│   └─ async export job
│
├─ performance
│   ├─ 100k rows
│   ├─ 500k rows
│   └─ temp file cleanup
│
└─ security
    ├─ untrusted formula-like text
    ├─ unauthorized download
    ├─ PII masking
    └─ export too large
```

### Golden-file test idea

Buka generated workbook dengan POI lalu assert:

```text
- sheet exists
- header exists
- row count expected
- key cells have expected values
- date cell style exists
- no unexpected formula cells
```

Example:

```java
@Test
void generatedWorkbookShouldContainSummaryAndCustomersSheet() {
    ByteArrayOutputStream output = new ByteArrayOutputStream();

    writer.write(metadata, List.of(sampleRow), output);

    try (Workbook workbook = WorkbookFactory.create(
            new ByteArrayInputStream(output.toByteArray())
    )) {
        assertNotNull(workbook.getSheet("Summary"));
        assertNotNull(workbook.getSheet("Customers"));

        Sheet customers = workbook.getSheet("Customers");
        assertEquals("Customer ID", customers.getRow(0).getCell(0).getStringCellValue());
        assertEquals("C001", customers.getRow(1).getCell(0).getStringCellValue());
    }
}
```

---

## 29. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Pakai `XSSFWorkbook` untuk export ratusan ribu row | OOM |
| Membuat `CellStyle` per cell | File membengkak, performa buruk |
| Menulis ID sebagai numeric | Leading zero hilang |
| Menulis money tanpa rounding policy | Angka terlihat tidak konsisten |
| Auto-size semua column untuk report besar | Export lambat |
| Generate report langsung dari resource method | Resource terlalu gemuk |
| Tidak check authorization download | Data leak |
| Tidak audit export | Sulit investigasi |
| Menyimpan generated file selamanya | Storage leak |
| Tidak cleanup SXSSF temp files | Disk leak |
| Formula dari input user | Security risk |
| Direct streaming untuk report sangat berat | Error di tengah response sulit ditangani |
| Tidak punya metadata sheet | Report sulit diaudit |

---

## 30. Production Checklist

```text
API boundary
[ ] Endpoint export jelas dan domain-specific
[ ] Authorization tenant/domain/report checked
[ ] PII privilege checked jika ada sensitive columns
[ ] Content-Type XLSX benar
[ ] Content-Disposition attachment benar
[ ] Cache-Control no-store untuk sensitive report

Workbook strategy
[ ] XSSFWorkbook hanya untuk small/template report
[ ] SXSSFWorkbook untuk large export
[ ] Async export untuk long-running report
[ ] Row/sheet/size limit jelas
[ ] Temp file cleanup dilakukan
[ ] Generated file retention policy jelas

Data source
[ ] Query tidak load semua row ke memory
[ ] Cursor/pagination digunakan untuk export besar
[ ] Filter wajib untuk report besar
[ ] Snapshot/consistency strategy jelas
[ ] MyBatis query optimized

Styling
[ ] Style dibuat sekali dan reused
[ ] Date/number/currency format eksplisit
[ ] ID ditulis sebagai string
[ ] Header freeze/filter jika perlu
[ ] Auto-size dibatasi

Safety
[ ] Tidak ada setCellFormula dari input user
[ ] Formula hanya application-owned
[ ] Formula-like text policy jelas
[ ] PII masking/column suppression tersedia
[ ] No internal fields accidentally exported

Observability
[ ] Duration metric
[ ] Row count metric
[ ] File size metric
[ ] Failure reason metric
[ ] Structured log
[ ] Export audit event
[ ] Download audit event

Testing
[ ] Golden workbook tests
[ ] Large export test
[ ] Unauthorized download test
[ ] PII masking test
[ ] Temp cleanup test
```

---

## 31. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| Excel export adalah data disclosure event | Harus diaudit dan diauthorize |
| Report model harus terpisah dari DB entity | Hindari field internal bocor |
| Large export harus streaming-friendly | Jangan load all rows/all workbook ke memory |
| Style harus reusable | Jangan style per cell |
| ID bisnis harus string | Jangan hilangkan leading zero |
| Formula harus application-owned | Jangan formula dari input user |
| Sensitive report harus `no-store` | Kurangi risiko cache leak |
| Async export lebih aman untuk heavy report | Hindari response gagal di tengah |
| Generated file harus punya retention policy | Hindari storage leak |
| Metadata sheet meningkatkan defensibility | Report bisa dijelaskan ulang |

---

## 32. Kesimpulan Seri 6

Excel report generation yang production-ready bukan hanya “buat workbook lalu download”. Ia adalah kombinasi dari:

```text
authorization
+ report model
+ streaming data source
+ workbook generation
+ style reuse
+ formula safety
+ PII policy
+ audit trail
+ runtime limits
```

Pola sehat:

```text
Request export
  -> authorize
  -> validate filter
  -> query incrementally
  -> write workbook with SXSSF if large
  -> reuse styles
  -> avoid untrusted formulas
  -> stream or store generated file
  -> audit + metrics
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text
Never generate large Excel fully in memory.
Never create styles per cell.
Never write business IDs as numeric cells if leading zero matters.
Never create formulas from user input.
Never export sensitive data without explicit authorization and audit.
```
