## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:28:29 PM

Lanjut **Seri 7 dari 20**. Fokusnya JSON sebagai kontrak API/event: bukan hanya `ObjectMapper`, tapi boundary DTO, compatibility, security deserialization, schema evolution, dan konsistensi Java time.

# Seri 7/20 - JSON Serialization/Deserialization: Jackson, JSON-B, DTO Boundary, Java Time, Unknown Fields, Schema Versioning & API/Event Payload Safety

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas JSON di Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice sebagai **kontrak sistem**, bukan sekadar format payload.

```text id="61vh0o"
HTTP Request / Event Payload / File JSON
        │
        ▼
JSON Boundary
        │
        ├─ deserialize JSON → request DTO
        ├─ validate request DTO
        ├─ map DTO → domain command
        ├─ execute application logic
        ├─ map domain result → response DTO
        └─ serialize response DTO → JSON
```

Di Jakarta REST/JAX-RS, konversi body HTTP ke Java object dan Java object ke response body dilakukan oleh entity providers seperti `MessageBodyReader` dan `MessageBodyWriter`. Ini berarti JSON handling sebaiknya dipandang sebagai bagian dari **provider + DTO contract + validation layer**, bukan otomatis “langsung masuk domain/entity”. ([jakarta.ee](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Jackson adalah pilihan umum di ekosistem Java karena menyediakan databinding, tree model, dan streaming API untuk JSON processing. Jakarta JSON-B adalah standar Jakarta untuk binding Java object ke/dari JSON document. ([GitHub](https://github.com/FasterXML/jackson-databind?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="y2ovlb"
JSON Processing in Jersey/JAX-RS
│
├─ 1. API Boundary
│   ├─ @Consumes(application/json)
│   ├─ @Produces(application/json)
│   ├─ MessageBodyReader
│   ├─ MessageBodyWriter
│   └─ error response contract
│
├─ 2. JSON Provider
│   ├─ Jackson
│   ├─ JSON-B
│   ├─ JSON-P
│   └─ custom provider
│
├─ 3. DTO Boundary
│   ├─ request DTO
│   ├─ response DTO
│   ├─ event DTO
│   ├─ file DTO
│   └─ internal domain model
│
├─ 4. Deserialization Policy
│   ├─ unknown fields
│   ├─ null handling
│   ├─ enum handling
│   ├─ Java time handling
│   ├─ number precision
│   └─ polymorphism restriction
│
├─ 5. Validation
│   ├─ JSON shape validation
│   ├─ Bean Validation
│   ├─ cross-field validation
│   ├─ domain validation
│   └─ authorization at property level
│
├─ 6. Serialization Policy
│   ├─ field naming
│   ├─ date format
│   ├─ null output
│   ├─ sensitive fields
│   ├─ response envelope
│   └─ backward compatibility
│
├─ 7. Schema & Versioning
│   ├─ JSON Schema
│   ├─ OpenAPI schema
│   ├─ event schema
│   ├─ additive changes
│   └─ deprecated fields
│
└─ 8. Runtime Safety
    ├─ payload size limit
    ├─ nesting depth
    ├─ huge string/number limit
    ├─ streaming parse
    ├─ logging redaction
    └─ security tests
```

---

## 3. Jackson vs JSON-B vs JSON-P

| Option | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Jackson** | Microservice yang butuh konfigurasi detail, Java time, custom serializer, tree model, streaming, event payload | Sangat fleksibel, ekosistem luas | Perlu konfigurasi disiplin agar aman dan konsisten |
| **Jakarta JSON-B** | Aplikasi yang ingin standar Jakarta EE untuk object ↔ JSON binding | Standar Jakarta, portable secara konsep | Fitur custom biasanya tidak seluas Jackson |
| **Jakarta JSON-P** | Parsing/generating JSON secara programmatic/tree/streaming level | Cocok untuk manipulasi JSON low-level | Bukan object mapping utama |
| **Manual parsing** | Hampir tidak disarankan untuk API normal | Kontrol penuh | Mudah salah dan verbose |

Jersey mendokumentasikan bahwa JSON support tersedia melalui extension modules; untuk Jackson 2.x provider, Jersey menggunakan module `jersey-media-json-jackson`, dan `ObjectMapper` dapat dikustomisasi melalui provider. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/media.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Rekomendasi praktis untuk stack Anda: **pakai Jackson sebagai default JSON provider**, kecuali perusahaan sudah menetapkan JSON-B sebagai standar Jakarta EE-wide.

---

## 4. Maven Dependency

Untuk Jersey + Jackson:

```xml id="2qum2w"
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.media</groupId>
    <artifactId>jersey-media-json-jackson</artifactId>
</dependency>

<dependency>
    <groupId>com.fasterxml.jackson.core</groupId>
    <artifactId>jackson-databind</artifactId>
</dependency>

<dependency>
    <groupId>com.fasterxml.jackson.datatype</groupId>
    <artifactId>jackson-datatype-jsr310</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Gunakan versi dari BOM/dependency management project. Jangan mencampur versi Jackson/Jersey secara ad-hoc. Untuk Jersey 3.x/Jakarta namespace, pastikan seluruh stack memakai `jakarta.*`, bukan sebagian `javax.*`.

---

## 5. Register Jackson di Jersey

```java id="ev7grd"
package com.company.api.config;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ContextResolver;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

@Provider
public class ObjectMapperProvider implements ContextResolver<ObjectMapper> {

    private final ObjectMapper mapper;

    public ObjectMapperProvider() {
        this.mapper = ObjectMapperFactory.create();
    }

    @Override
    public ObjectMapper getContext(Class<?> type) {
        return mapper;
    }
}
```

```java id="v1c9kk"
package com.company.api.config;

import org.glassfish.jersey.jackson.JacksonFeature;
import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public class ApiApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public ApiApplication() {
        packages("com.company");

        register(JacksonFeature.class);
        register(ObjectMapperProvider.class);
    }
}
```

Jersey documentation menunjukkan pola register `JacksonFeature` dan custom `ObjectMapper` provider untuk mengontrol JSON serialization/deserialization behavior. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/user-guide.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. ObjectMapper Baseline

```java id="13o78z"
package com.company.api.config;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonParser;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.StreamReadConstraints;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.DeserializationFeature;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.MapperFeature;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.SerializationFeature;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.json.JsonMapper;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.datatype.jsr310.JavaTimeModule;

public final class ObjectMapperFactory {

    private ObjectMapperFactory() {}

    public static ObjectMapper create() {
        return JsonMapper.builder()
                .addModule(new JavaTimeModule())

                // Date/time as ISO-8601 string, not numeric timestamp.
                .disable(SerializationFeature.WRITE_DATES_AS_TIMESTAMPS)

                // Avoid silently accepting invalid enum values.
                .disable(DeserializationFeature.READ_UNKNOWN_ENUM_VALUES_AS_NULL)

                // Recommended for request DTO strictness.
                .enable(DeserializationFeature.FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES)

                // Avoid leaking nulls unless API contract explicitly includes them.
                .serializationInclusion(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)

                // Keep safer default: no broad polymorphic/default typing.
                .disable(MapperFeature.DEFAULT_VIEW_INCLUSION)

                // Jackson 2.15+ supports stream read constraints.
                .streamReadConstraints(StreamReadConstraints.builder()
                        .maxNestingDepth(100)
                        .maxStringLength(5_000_000)
                        .maxNumberLength(1_000)
                        .build())

                .build();
    }
}
```

Jersey notes that Jackson 2.15 introduced default parsing limitations for text length, number length, and nesting depth, and Jersey keeps Jackson defaults while allowing configuration override. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/migration.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. DTO Boundary: Jangan Bind JSON Langsung ke Entity

### Jangan

```java id="plkjfw"
@POST
@Path("/customers")
public Response create(CustomerEntity entity) {
    customerMapper.insert(entity);
    return Response.ok().build();
}
```

Risikonya:

| Risiko | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Mass assignment | Client mengirim `isAdmin`, `tenantId`, `status`, `approvedBy` |
| Internal field leak | Field DB/internal ikut terekspos |
| Coupling API ↔ database | Refactor DB memecahkan API |
| Validation kabur | Tidak jelas mana field input legal |
| Security lemah | Property-level authorization sulit |

OWASP API Security 2023 menggabungkan excessive data exposure dan mass assignment ke dalam kategori Broken Object Property Level Authorization, dengan akar masalah validasi authorization yang kurang pada level properti object. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/API-Security/editions/2023/en/0x11-t10/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Lebih baik

```java id="frgztk"
public record CreateCustomerRequest(
        String customerId,
        String name,
        String email,
        String phone
) {}
```

```java id="7n4svc"
public record CustomerResponse(
        String customerId,
        String name,
        String email,
        String status
) {}
```

```java id="0x2ttc"
public record CreateCustomerCommand(
        String tenantId,
        String customerId,
        String name,
        String email,
        String phone,
        String requestedBy
) {}
```

```text id="64xy41"
JSON Request
  -> CreateCustomerRequest
  -> validate
  -> CreateCustomerCommand
  -> domain service
  -> CustomerResponse
  -> JSON Response
```

---

## 8. Resource Method Pattern

```java id="e44w67"
package com.company.customer.api;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Consumes;
import jakarta.ws.rs.POST;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Produces;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

@Path("/customers")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CustomerResource {

    private final CustomerApplicationService customerService;

    public CustomerResource(CustomerApplicationService customerService) {
        this.customerService = customerService;
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(@Valid CreateCustomerRequest request) {
        String tenantId = CurrentTenant.required();
        String userId = CurrentUser.required();

        CreateCustomerCommand command = new CreateCustomerCommand(
                tenantId,
                request.customerId(),
                request.name(),
                request.email(),
                request.phone(),
                userId
        );

        CustomerResult result = customerService.create(command);

        CustomerResponse response = new CustomerResponse(
                result.customerId(),
                result.name(),
                result.email(),
                result.status().name()
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.CREATED)
                .entity(response)
                .build();
    }
}
```

Resource menerima DTO, bukan entity. Tenant/user tidak boleh diambil dari JSON body jika itu adalah security context; ambil dari auth/session/token/server context.

---

## 9. Request DTO Validation

```java id="8mhss8"
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;

public record CreateCustomerRequest(

        @NotBlank
        @Size(max = 64)
        String customerId,

        @NotBlank
        @Size(max = 200)
        String name,

        @NotBlank
        @Email
        @Size(max = 320)
        String email,

        @Size(max = 32)
        String phone
) {}
```

Bean Validation menangani field-level validation, tetapi tidak cukup untuk domain rules.

```text id="5mriwj"
Bean Validation:
- required
- length
- format
- simple pattern

Domain Validation:
- customer id unique per tenant
- status transition allowed
- actor has permission
- referenced object belongs to tenant
```

---

## 10. Unknown Fields Policy

Ada dua mode:

| Mode | ObjectMapper setting | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| Strict | `FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES = true` | Public API, command payload, regulatory operation |
| Lenient | `FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES = false` | Event consumers yang butuh forward compatibility |

### API request default

Untuk API command seperti `POST /customers`, saya sarankan **strict**.

```json id="jrzrqx"
{
  "customerId": "C001",
  "name": "Alice",
  "email": "alice@example.com",
  "isAdmin": true
}
```

Response sebaiknya:

```json id="572j9b"
{
  "error": {
    "code": "UNKNOWN_JSON_FIELD",
    "message": "Request contains unsupported field: isAdmin"
  }
}
```

### Event consumer default

Untuk event dari service lain, lebih aman **lenient** agar consumer lama tidak mati ketika producer menambah field baru.

```java id="c8sgqq"
ObjectReader eventReader = mapper.readerFor(CustomerCreatedEvent.class)
        .without(DeserializationFeature.FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES);
```

Prinsip:

```text id="h628k6"
Command API  -> strict by default
Event input   -> lenient for additive compatibility
Audit payload -> strict if regulatory contract
```

---

## 11. Null, Missing, and Empty String

JSON membedakan:

```json id="t5us11"
{}
```

```json id="8zmtg3"
{ "email": null }
```

```json id="c2f388"
{ "email": "" }
```

Ketiganya harus punya makna yang jelas.

| Case | Makna umum | Contoh policy |
|---|---|---|
| Missing field | Client tidak mengirim field | PATCH: tidak berubah |
| Explicit `null` | Client ingin menghapus value | PATCH: clear field jika allowed |
| Empty string | Value kosong | Biasanya invalid untuk required field |
| Blank string | `"   "` | Normalize atau reject |

### Create request

```text id="ei5mff"
missing required field -> invalid
null required field    -> invalid
empty required field   -> invalid
```

### Patch request

Gunakan DTO khusus. Jangan pakai create DTO.

```java id="4fnr0m"
import java.util.Optional;

public record PatchCustomerRequest(
        Optional<String> name,
        Optional<String> email,
        Optional<String> phone
) {}
```

Catatan: `Optional` dalam DTO perlu konfigurasi Jackson JDK8 module pada Jackson 2.x jika digunakan luas. Alternatif enterprise yang lebih eksplisit adalah menggunakan JSON Merge Patch/JSON Patch atau custom `PatchField<T>`.

---

## 12. Java Time Handling

### Recommended types

| Use case | Type |
|---|---|
| Timestamp event/audit | `Instant` |
| API timestamp dengan offset | `OffsetDateTime` |
| Local business date | `LocalDate` |
| Local wall-clock time | `LocalTime` |
| Duration | `Duration` |
| Jangan default | `java.util.Date`, `LocalDateTime` untuk cross-system timestamp |

### Recommended JSON shape

```json id="kuwcwy"
{
  "createdAt": "2026-07-13T14:30:00Z",
  "effectiveDate": "2026-07-13"
}
```

### DTO example

```java id="2ecirr"
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;

public record CustomerResponse(
        String customerId,
        String name,
        String status,
        Instant createdAt,
        LocalDate effectiveDate
) {}
```

### Rule

```text id="kz0ha7"
Instant       -> absolute machine time
LocalDate     -> business date without timezone
OffsetDateTime -> external API timestamp if offset matters
LocalDateTime -> avoid for distributed systems unless context is explicit
```

---

## 13. Number and Precision

JSON number tidak punya tipe decimal eksplisit. Di Java, pilih tipe berdasarkan domain.

| Domain | Java type | Jangan |
|---|---|---|
| Money | `BigDecimal` | `double` |
| Count | `long` / `int` | `String` kecuali external contract |
| Percentage | `BigDecimal` | Ambiguous `float` |
| ID numeric-looking | `String` | `long`, karena leading zero bisa hilang |
| Large external ID | `String` | JavaScript clients bisa kehilangan precision |

### Money DTO

```java id="o11fpx"
import java.math.BigDecimal;

public record ChargeRequest(
        String accountId,
        BigDecimal amount,
        String currency
) {}
```

### BigDecimal validation

```java id="ernb17"
import jakarta.validation.constraints.DecimalMin;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Digits;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;

public record ChargeRequest(
        @NotNull String accountId,

        @NotNull
        @DecimalMin(value = "0.00", inclusive = false)
        @Digits(integer = 18, fraction = 2)
        BigDecimal amount,

        @NotNull String currency
) {}
```

---

## 14. Enum Handling

### Default DTO

```java id="7ovmvv"
public enum CustomerStatus {
    ACTIVE,
    INACTIVE,
    SUSPENDED
}
```

JSON:

```json id="jfj2a0"
{
  "status": "ACTIVE"
}
```

### Problem

Jika client mengirim:

```json id="2dkgjr"
{
  "status": "active"
}
```

Keputusan harus eksplisit:

| Policy | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Case-sensitive | Cocok untuk strict system API |
| Case-insensitive | Cocok untuk user-facing API |
| Unknown enum as null | Hindari untuk command API |
| Unknown enum as `UNKNOWN` | Cocok untuk event consumer compatibility |
| Enum as code object | Cocok untuk long-lived enterprise contract |

### Stable code pattern

```java id="88902m"
public enum CustomerStatus {
    ACTIVE("ACTIVE"),
    INACTIVE("INACTIVE"),
    SUSPENDED("SUSPENDED");

    private final String code;

    CustomerStatus(String code) {
        this.code = code;
    }

    public String code() {
        return code;
    }
}
```

Untuk long-lived public API, hati-hati rename enum Java karena bisa memecahkan JSON contract.

---

## 15. JSON Schema & OpenAPI

JSON Schema Draft 2020-12 adalah update komprehensif dari draft sebelumnya dan membawa fitur seperti vocabulary/dynamic references serta perbaikan untuk validasi schema modern. ([json-schema.org](https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Gunakan schema untuk:

| Use case | Schema |
|---|---|
| Public API | OpenAPI schema |
| File JSON upload | JSON Schema |
| Event payload | JSON Schema / AsyncAPI / Avro/Protobuf jika event-heavy |
| Contract test | Schema validation |
| Compatibility check | Backward/forward compatibility rules |

### Example JSON Schema

```json id="bl7ffc"
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "https://company.example/schemas/customer-created-event.schema.json",
  "type": "object",
  "required": ["eventId", "eventType", "occurredAt", "customer"],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "eventId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "eventType": {
      "const": "CustomerCreated"
    },
    "occurredAt": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    },
    "customer": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["customerId", "name", "email"],
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "properties": {
        "customerId": { "type": "string" },
        "name": { "type": "string" },
        "email": { "type": "string", "format": "email" }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

Untuk API command yang strict, `additionalProperties: false` berguna. Untuk event yang perlu forward compatibility, gunakan dengan hati-hati karena consumer lama bisa gagal saat producer menambah field.

---

## 16. API Versioning & Compatibility

### Change matrix

| Perubahan | Compatibility |
|---|---|
| Tambah optional response field | Biasanya backward compatible |
| Tambah required request field | Breaking |
| Hapus response field | Breaking untuk client yang bergantung |
| Rename field | Breaking |
| Ubah tipe field | Breaking |
| Tambah enum value | Bisa breaking untuk strict client |
| Ubah date format | Breaking |
| Ubah null/missing behavior | Breaking |
| Tambah optional event field | Biasanya forward compatible |
| Hapus event field | Breaking |

### Recommended versioning

```text id="bk1xun"
API URL:
  /v1/customers

Event type:
  CustomerCreated.v1
  CustomerCreated.v2

Schema:
  customer-created-event-v1.schema.json
  customer-created-event-v2.schema.json
```

### Deprecation pattern

```json id="ythaob"
{
  "customerId": "C001",
  "name": "Alice",
  "email": "alice@example.com",
  "status": "ACTIVE",
  "deprecatedLegacyCode": "L-001"
}
```

Jangan hapus field sampai client/event consumer yang masih memakai field tersebut sudah dimigrasikan.

---

## 17. Event Payload JSON

Untuk Kafka/RabbitMQ/event bus, JSON event sebaiknya punya envelope.

```json id="j6zvj7"
{
  "eventId": "evt_001",
  "eventType": "CustomerCreated",
  "eventVersion": 1,
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-13T14:30:00Z",
  "producer": "customer-service",
  "correlationId": "corr_001",
  "tenantId": "tenant_001",
  "payload": {
    "customerId": "C001",
    "name": "Alice",
    "email": "alice@example.com"
  }
}
```

### Event DTO

```java id="n819yt"
import java.time.Instant;

public record DomainEvent<T>(
        String eventId,
        String eventType,
        int eventVersion,
        Instant occurredAt,
        String producer,
        String correlationId,
        String tenantId,
        T payload
) {}
```

```java id="ck7n4x"
public record CustomerCreatedPayload(
        String customerId,
        String name,
        String email
) {}
```

### Event invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| Event payload immutable | Jangan edit event lama |
| Event punya version | Schema evolution bisa dikontrol |
| Event punya occurredAt | Timeline domain jelas |
| Event punya correlationId | Trace lintas service |
| Event punya tenantId jika multi-tenant | Consumer bisa enforce isolation |
| Event consumer lenient untuk field tambahan | Forward compatibility |

---

## 18. File JSON Processing

Untuk JSON file upload, jangan langsung deserialize seluruh file jika ukurannya besar.

| File shape | Strategy |
|---|---|
| Small object | `readValue` ke DTO |
| Array besar | Streaming parser |
| NDJSON | Read line by line + per-line deserialize |
| Event dump | Streaming + schema validation |
| Unknown shape | Reject atau detect schema first |

### Large JSON array example

```json id="jakvwn"
[
  { "customerId": "C001", "name": "Alice" },
  { "customerId": "C002", "name": "Bob" }
]
```

### Streaming parser

```java id="dt9v3f"
import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonFactory;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonToken;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import java.io.InputStream;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

public class CustomerJsonArrayImporter {

    private final ObjectMapper mapper;

    public CustomerJsonArrayImporter(ObjectMapper mapper) {
        this.mapper = mapper;
    }

    public void parseArray(InputStream input, Consumer<CreateCustomerRequest> consumer) {
        JsonFactory factory = mapper.getFactory();

        try (var parser = factory.createParser(input)) {
            if (parser.nextToken() != JsonToken.START_ARRAY) {
                throw new JsonProcessingRejectedException(
                        "EXPECTED_JSON_ARRAY",
                        "Expected root JSON array"
                );
            }

            while (parser.nextToken() == JsonToken.START_OBJECT) {
                CreateCustomerRequest row = mapper.readValue(parser, CreateCustomerRequest.class);
                consumer.accept(row);
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new JsonProcessingException("Failed to parse JSON array", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Untuk NDJSON, setiap baris adalah satu JSON object. Ini sering lebih scalable untuk logs/events/imports.

---

## 19. Security: Deserialization and Mass Assignment

### Jangan aktifkan default typing luas

```java id="0gk752"
// Hindari untuk untrusted JSON.
objectMapper.activateDefaultTyping(...);
```

### Jangan deserialize ke `Object`

```java id="cxn9jn"
Map<String, Object> body = mapper.readValue(json, Map.class);
// Lalu apply langsung ke entity -> rawan mass assignment.
```

### Lebih aman

```text id="q7ymwn"
untrusted JSON
  -> explicit DTO class
  -> Bean Validation
  -> domain command
  -> authorization
  -> controlled mutation
```

### Property-level authorization

```java id="2mmola"
public record UpdateCustomerAdminRequest(
        String status,
        String riskFlag
) {}
```

Endpoint admin harus terpisah dari endpoint user biasa:

```text id="tjvfn2"
PATCH /v1/customers/{id}
PATCH /v1/admin/customers/{id}/status
```

Jangan satu DTO menerima semua field lalu berharap service “ingat” menyaring.

---

## 20. Sensitive Field Serialization

### Jangan

```java id="u5t03y"
public record UserResponse(
        String userId,
        String email,
        String passwordHash,
        String resetToken
) {}
```

### Lebih baik

```java id="eg4pil"
public record UserResponse(
        String userId,
        String email,
        String displayName
) {}
```

`@JsonIgnore` bisa membantu, tetapi jangan bergantung padanya sebagai satu-satunya security boundary. Lebih baik response DTO memang tidak punya field sensitif.

```java id="6m16o6"
import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonIgnore;

public class UserInternalModel {
    private String userId;
    private String email;

    @JsonIgnore
    private String passwordHash;
}
```

Rule senior:

```text id="ebcrxd"
Sensitive data should be absent from response DTO,
not merely hidden by serializer annotation.
```

---

## 21. Error Response Contract

### Standard error DTO

```java id="ojjepc"
import java.util.List;

public record ApiErrorResponse(
        ApiError error
) {}

public record ApiError(
        String code,
        String message,
        String correlationId,
        List<ApiErrorDetail> details
) {}

public record ApiErrorDetail(
        String field,
        String reason,
        String rejectedValue
) {}
```

### Example JSON

```json id="96hg64"
{
  "error": {
    "code": "VALIDATION_FAILED",
    "message": "Request validation failed",
    "correlationId": "corr_001",
    "details": [
      {
        "field": "email",
        "reason": "must be a well-formed email address",
        "rejectedValue": "not-an-email"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

### Exception mapper

```java id="v2frul"
import jakarta.validation.ConstraintViolationException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.util.List;

@Provider
public class ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<ConstraintViolationException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(ConstraintViolationException exception) {
        List<ApiErrorDetail> details = exception.getConstraintViolations()
                .stream()
                .map(v -> new ApiErrorDetail(
                        v.getPropertyPath().toString(),
                        v.getMessage(),
                        sanitizeRejectedValue(v.getInvalidValue())
                ))
                .toList();

        ApiError error = new ApiError(
                "VALIDATION_FAILED",
                "Request validation failed",
                CorrelationId.current(),
                details
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
                .entity(new ApiErrorResponse(error))
                .build();
    }

    private String sanitizeRejectedValue(Object value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }

        String text = String.valueOf(value);
        return text.length() > 200 ? text.substring(0, 200) + "..." : text;
    }
}
```

Jangan echo full payload kalau bisa mengandung PII/secrets.

---

## 22. JSON Logging & Redaction

### Jangan log raw JSON body

```text id="i1t1y5"
BAD:
log.info("request={}", rawJsonBody)
```

Risiko:

| Risiko | Contoh |
|---|---|
| PII leak | email, phone, address |
| Secret leak | token, password, API key |
| Payload besar | log storage bengkak |
| Compliance issue | data sensitif tersebar ke log |

### Log metadata

```json id="62iyab"
{
  "event": "api_request_processed",
  "method": "POST",
  "path": "/v1/customers",
  "tenantId": "tenant_001",
  "actorId": "user_001",
  "status": 201,
  "durationMs": 42,
  "correlationId": "corr_001"
}
```

### Redaction helper

```java id="c8rq5o"
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

public final class JsonLogRedactor {

    private static final Set<String> SENSITIVE_KEYS = Set.of(
            "password",
            "passwordHash",
            "token",
            "accessToken",
            "refreshToken",
            "apiKey",
            "secret",
            "authorization"
    );

    private JsonLogRedactor() {}

    public static Map<String, Object> redact(Map<String, Object> input) {
        // Simplified example. Real implementation should recurse safely.
        return input.entrySet()
                .stream()
                .collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.toMap(
                        Map.Entry::getKey,
                        e -> SENSITIVE_KEYS.contains(e.getKey()) ? "***REDACTED***" : e.getValue()
                ));
    }
}
```

---

## 23. JSON Patch vs Merge Patch vs Custom Patch DTO

| Strategy | Format | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| Custom patch DTO | Domain-specific JSON | Paling mudah dikontrol |
| JSON Merge Patch | RFC-style partial object merge | Simple field replacement/null-as-delete |
| JSON Patch | Operation list: add/remove/replace | Advanced patch, array manipulation |
| Full update PUT | Full representation | Resource replacement |

Untuk enterprise domain, custom patch DTO sering paling aman karena Anda bisa enforce property-level authorization dengan jelas.

### Custom patch DTO

```java id="9m0c0y"
public record PatchCustomerRequest(
        String name,
        String email,
        String phone
) {}
```

Problem: tidak bisa bedakan missing vs explicit null dengan record sederhana. Kalau butuh semantics itu, buat type eksplisit:

```java id="x190bw"
public record PatchField<T>(
        boolean present,
        T value
) {}
```

Atau gunakan library JSON Patch/Merge Patch dengan policy yang jelas.

---

## 24. JSON with PostgreSQL JSONB

PostgreSQL JSONB berguna untuk:

| Use case | Cocok? |
|---|---|
| Audit snapshot | Ya |
| Raw event payload | Ya |
| Flexible metadata | Ya |
| Primary transactional model | Hati-hati |
| Search/query domain core | Lebih baik relational kalau struktur stabil |

### Table example

```sql id="1cj25i"
CREATE TABLE inbound_event (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    event_type      TEXT NOT NULL,
    event_version   INTEGER NOT NULL,
    correlation_id  TEXT,
    tenant_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload         JSONB NOT NULL,
    received_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_type_version
ON inbound_event (event_type, event_version);

CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_payload_gin
ON inbound_event USING GIN (payload);
```

### MyBatis insert

```xml id="0h4hu6"
<insert id="insertInboundEvent">
    INSERT INTO inbound_event (
        id,
        event_type,
        event_version,
        correlation_id,
        tenant_id,
        payload,
        received_at
    )
    VALUES (
        #{id},
        #{eventType},
        #{eventVersion},
        #{correlationId},
        #{tenantId},
        #{payloadJson}::jsonb,
        now()
    )
</insert>
```

Rule:

```text id="1vqx3l"
Use JSONB for flexible/audit payload.
Use relational columns for stable query-critical fields.
```

---

## 25. JSON File Generation

Untuk generate JSON besar, jangan build giant object/list di memory.

### Streaming JSON array

```java id="i6eag3"
import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonGenerator;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import java.io.OutputStream;

public class CustomerJsonExporter {

    private final ObjectMapper mapper;
    private final CustomerQueryService queryService;

    public CustomerJsonExporter(ObjectMapper mapper, CustomerQueryService queryService) {
        this.mapper = mapper;
        this.queryService = queryService;
    }

    public void writeCustomers(OutputStream output) {
        try (JsonGenerator generator = mapper.getFactory().createGenerator(output)) {
            generator.writeStartArray();

            queryService.streamCustomers(customer -> {
                try {
                    mapper.writeValue(generator, new CustomerResponse(
                            customer.customerId(),
                            customer.name(),
                            customer.email(),
                            customer.status().name()
                    ));
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    throw new RuntimeException(e);
                }
            });

            generator.writeEndArray();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new JsonProcessingException("Failed to export customers as JSON", e);
        }
    }
}
```

### Jersey endpoint

```java id="mdh4ka"
@GET
@Path("/customers.json")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public Response exportCustomersJson() {
    authorizeExport();

    StreamingOutput stream = output -> customerJsonExporter.writeCustomers(output);

    return Response.ok(stream)
            .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"customers.json\"")
            .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            .build();
}
```

### Serialization golden test

```java id="2p0xvl"
@Test
void customerResponseShouldSerializeStableJson() throws Exception {
    ObjectMapper mapper = ObjectMapperFactory.create();

    CustomerResponse response = new CustomerResponse(
            "C001",
            "Alice",
            "alice@example.com",
            "ACTIVE"
    );

    String json = mapper.writeValueAsString(response);

    assertThat(json).contains("\"customerId\":\"C001\"");
    assertThat(json).doesNotContain("password");
    assertThat(json).doesNotContain("internal");
}
```

### Unknown field test

```java id="6gsf2n"
@Test
void createCustomerRequestShouldRejectUnknownField() {
    ObjectMapper mapper = ObjectMapperFactory.create();

    String json = """
            {
              "customerId": "C001",
              "name": "Alice",
              "email": "alice@example.com",
              "isAdmin": true
            }
            """;

    assertThrows(Exception.class, () ->
            mapper.readValue(json, CreateCustomerRequest.class)
    );
}
```

Jangan log seluruh request body untuk error JSON.

---

## 28. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Bind JSON langsung ke entity | Mass assignment, internal field leak |
| Satu DTO untuk create/update/patch/response | Semantics kacau |
| Unknown fields silently ignored untuk command API | Client salah payload tapi tetap dianggap sukses |
| Unknown fields rejected untuk semua event consumer | Consumer rapuh terhadap additive changes |
| Pakai `LocalDateTime` untuk timestamp distributed | Ambiguous timezone |
| Money pakai `double` | Precision bug |
| ID numeric-looking pakai number | Leading zero hilang |
| Broad polymorphic deserialization | Security risk |
| Log raw JSON body | PII/secret leak |
| `@JsonIgnore` sebagai satu-satunya security control | Field masih ada di model internal |
| Tidak punya schema/version | Compatibility sulit |
| Tidak test JSON shape | Breaking change tidak terdeteksi |
| Response DTO expose terlalu banyak field | Excessive data exposure |
| Tidak batasi payload size/depth | Resource exhaustion |

---

## 29. Production Checklist

```text id="8c664x"
Provider/config
[ ] Jersey Jackson provider registered
[ ] Single shared ObjectMapper policy
[ ] JavaTimeModule registered
[ ] Dates serialized as ISO string
[ ] Stream read constraints configured
[ ] Dependency versions controlled by BOM

DTO boundary
[ ] Request DTO separate from entity
[ ] Response DTO separate from entity
[ ] Event DTO separate from API DTO where needed
[ ] Create/update/patch DTO separated
[ ] Tenant/user/security fields not accepted from JSON body

Deserialization
[ ] Unknown field policy defined
[ ] Enum policy defined
[ ] Null/missing/empty policy defined
[ ] Number precision policy defined
[ ] No unsafe default typing
[ ] No untrusted JSON to raw Object/entity mutation

Validation
[ ] Bean Validation on request DTO
[ ] Cross-field validation
[ ] Domain validation
[ ] Property-level authorization
[ ] Error response contract

Serialization
[ ] Sensitive fields absent from response DTO
[ ] Null field output policy
[ ] Date/time format stable
[ ] Field naming stable
[ ] Backward compatibility reviewed

Schema/versioning
[ ] OpenAPI schema maintained
[ ] Event schema maintained
[ ] JSON Schema for file/event where useful
[ ] Compatibility rules documented
[ ] Deprecated fields tracked

Runtime/security
[ ] Payload size limit
[ ] Nesting/string/number constraints
[ ] Raw JSON not logged
[ ] PII redaction
[ ] Deserialization failure metrics
[ ] Contract tests
```

---

## 30. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| JSON is a contract, not just a map | Perubahan field adalah perubahan API/event |
| Request DTO must not be persistence entity | Mencegah mass assignment |
| Response DTO must not be domain/entity dump | Mencegah excessive data exposure |
| Security context must not come from body | Tenant/user/role berasal dari auth context |
| Unknown field policy depends on boundary | Strict untuk command API, lenient untuk event consumer |
| Time representation must be explicit | `Instant`/`OffsetDateTime`/`LocalDate` sesuai makna |
| Money must not use floating point | Gunakan `BigDecimal` |
| Polymorphic deserialization must be allowlisted | Jangan default typing luas |
| Raw JSON logs are dangerous | Log metadata, bukan payload |
| Schema evolution must be designed | Jangan ubah JSON contract diam-diam |

---

## 31. Kesimpulan Seri 7

JSON processing yang production-ready di Jersey/JAX-RS adalah kombinasi dari:

```text id="xd853a"
JAX-RS provider
+ ObjectMapper policy
+ DTO boundary
+ validation
+ versioning
+ serialization safety
+ deserialization security
+ observability
```

Pola sehat:

```text id="f3uobr"
JSON input
  -> MessageBodyReader/Jackson
  -> request DTO
  -> validation
  -> domain command
  -> service
  -> response DTO
  -> MessageBodyWriter/Jackson
  -> JSON output
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="eywgec"
Never bind external JSON directly to persistence entities.
Never trust client-provided security fields.
Never silently accept unknown command fields unless explicitly intended.
Never serialize sensitive fields by accident.
Never use unsafe polymorphic deserialization for untrusted JSON.
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:32:08 PM

Lanjut **Seri 8 dari 20**. Fokusnya XML: kontrak legacy/enterprise, namespace/XSD, secure parser, dan streaming supaya file besar tidak membuat service boros memory.

# Seri 8/20 - XML Processing: JAXB, StAX/SAX, XSD, Namespace, Secure Parsing, XXE Prevention & Large XML Streaming

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **XML processing** di Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice:

```text id="66pl5a"
XML Request / XML File / Legacy Integration Payload
        │
        ▼
XML Boundary
        │
        ├─ content-type validation
        ├─ secure parser configuration
        ├─ namespace handling
        ├─ XSD/schema validation
        ├─ JAXB binding or StAX streaming
        ├─ DTO/domain mapping
        ├─ error reporting
        └─ audit/observability
```

XML masih sering muncul di enterprise integration: regulatory payload, legacy system, SOAP-adjacent integration, payment/telecom/order systems, document metadata, dan batch file exchange. Jakarta XML Binding menyediakan API dan tools untuk mapping otomatis antara XML document dan Java object. Jakarta REST/JAX-RS sendiri menggunakan entity providers seperti `MessageBodyReader` dan `MessageBodyWriter` untuk mapping HTTP body ke Java type dan sebaliknya, sehingga XML di REST service juga sebaiknya diperlakukan sebagai **provider + DTO + validation boundary**, bukan langsung masuk entity/domain. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/xml-binding/4.0/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="l1zjvm"
XML Processing in Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice
│
├─ 1. API / File Boundary
│   ├─ application/xml
│   ├─ text/xml
│   ├─ multipart XML upload
│   ├─ XML download/export
│   └─ error response contract
│
├─ 2. Parser Choice
│   ├─ JAXB / Jakarta XML Binding
│   ├─ StAX cursor/event parser
│   ├─ SAX event parser
│   ├─ DOM tree parser
│   └─ XSLT / XPath if required
│
├─ 3. Security Hardening
│   ├─ disable DTD
│   ├─ disable external entities
│   ├─ restrict external schema/DTD access
│   ├─ prevent XXE
│   ├─ prevent entity expansion
│   └─ parser size/depth/time limits
│
├─ 4. Contract Governance
│   ├─ namespace
│   ├─ XSD
│   ├─ schema version
│   ├─ backward compatibility
│   └─ canonical model
│
├─ 5. Mapping
│   ├─ XML DTO
│   ├─ raw XML metadata
│   ├─ domain command
│   ├─ entity mapping
│   └─ validation result
│
├─ 6. Large XML Strategy
│   ├─ streaming parse
│   ├─ record-by-record processing
│   ├─ batching
│   ├─ checkpointing
│   └─ async worker
│
└─ 7. Operational Concerns
    ├─ structured errors
    ├─ audit trail
    ├─ original XML retention
    ├─ metrics
    └─ redacted logging
```

---

## 3. Parser / Processing Options

| Option | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko / catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| **JAXB / Jakarta XML Binding** | XML dengan schema jelas dan ukuran kecil-menengah | Object mapping rapi, cocok contract-first | Kurang cocok untuk XML sangat besar |
| **StAX** | XML besar, record-by-record processing | Streaming, pull-based, memory rendah | Mapping manual lebih banyak |
| **SAX** | XML besar/event-driven | Streaming dan cepat | Callback style lebih sulit dibaca |
| **DOM** | XML kecil yang perlu random access tree | Mudah XPath/tree traversal | Load seluruh XML ke memory |
| **XPath** | Cari node tertentu di XML kecil/sedang | Ekspresif | Bisa mahal; hati-hati namespace |
| **XSLT** | Transform XML → XML/HTML/text | Cocok legacy transform | Perlu hardening external access |

StAX mendefinisikan API streaming untuk XML; dokumentasi Java menyebut StAX punya cursor API untuk read/write XML secara efisien dan event iterator API yang event-based dan extensible. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/api/java.xml/javax/xml/stream/package-summary.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Kapan Pakai JAXB vs StAX?

| Use case | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| REST endpoint menerima XML kecil dengan schema stabil | JAXB |
| Generate XML response dari DTO | JAXB |
| File XML berisi ribuan/jutaan item | StAX |
| Perlu validasi XSD lalu map object | JAXB + schema validation |
| Perlu membaca sebagian node saja | StAX |
| XML legacy dengan namespace kompleks | JAXB jika schema kuat; StAX kalau mapping terlalu kustom |
| Regulatory batch XML | StAX + XSD + audit/error report |
| XML yang harus dipertahankan original structure-nya | StAX/DOM tergantung ukuran |

Rule praktis:

```text id="6dk938"
Small structured XML    -> JAXB
Large repeated XML      -> StAX
Need full tree access   -> DOM, only if size bounded
Need strict contract    -> XSD validation
Untrusted XML           -> secure parser always
```

---

## 5. Maven Dependencies

Untuk Jakarta XML Binding API dan runtime implementation:

```xml id="3q4rik"
<dependency>
    <groupId>jakarta.xml.bind</groupId>
    <artifactId>jakarta.xml.bind-api</artifactId>
</dependency>

<dependency>
    <groupId>org.glassfish.jaxb</groupId>
    <artifactId>jaxb-runtime</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Untuk Java 17+, JAXB tidak lagi diasumsikan selalu tersedia sebagai bagian default JDK runtime aplikasi; biasanya explicit dependency lebih aman untuk microservice. Gunakan versi dari BOM/dependency management project.

---

## 6. XML Contract Example

Contoh XML customer import:

```xml id="cs0vdx"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<customerImport xmlns="https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1"
                schemaVersion="1.0">
    <sourceSystem>CRM</sourceSystem>
    <customers>
        <customer>
            <customerId>C001</customerId>
            <name>Alice</name>
            <email>alice@example.com</email>
            <status>ACTIVE</status>
        </customer>
        <customer>
            <customerId>C002</customerId>
            <name>Bob</name>
            <email>bob@example.com</email>
            <status>INACTIVE</status>
        </customer>
    </customers>
</customerImport>
```

XML contract harus menjawab:

| Aspek | Contoh keputusan |
|---|---|
| Root element | `customerImport` |
| Namespace | `https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1` |
| Schema version | `1.0` |
| Encoding | UTF-8 |
| Required element | `customerId`, `name`, `email`, `status` |
| Optional element | `phone`, `birthDate` |
| Max records | 100.000 |
| Duplicate key | `customerId` |
| Unknown element | Reject untuk strict import |
| Empty element | Treat as null atau invalid sesuai field |

---

## 7. XSD Contract Example

```xml id="dg9dgf"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<xs:schema
    xmlns:xs="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema"
    targetNamespace="https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1"
    xmlns="https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1"
    elementFormDefault="qualified">

    <xs:element name="customerImport">
        <xs:complexType>
            <xs:sequence>
                <xs:element name="sourceSystem" type="xs:string"/>
                <xs:element name="customers">
                    <xs:complexType>
                        <xs:sequence>
                            <xs:element name="customer" type="CustomerType"
                                        minOccurs="1" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>
                        </xs:sequence>
                    </xs:complexType>
                </xs:element>
            </xs:sequence>
            <xs:attribute name="schemaVersion" type="xs:string" use="required"/>
        </xs:complexType>
    </xs:element>

    <xs:complexType name="CustomerType">
        <xs:sequence>
            <xs:element name="customerId" type="xs:string"/>
            <xs:element name="name" type="xs:string"/>
            <xs:element name="email" type="xs:string"/>
            <xs:element name="status" type="CustomerStatusType"/>
            <xs:element name="phone" type="xs:string" minOccurs="0"/>
            <xs:element name="birthDate" type="xs:date" minOccurs="0"/>
        </xs:sequence>
    </xs:complexType>

    <xs:simpleType name="CustomerStatusType">
        <xs:restriction base="xs:string">
            <xs:enumeration value="ACTIVE"/>
            <xs:enumeration value="INACTIVE"/>
        </xs:restriction>
    </xs:simpleType>
</xs:schema>
```

XSD berguna untuk **shape validation**, tetapi tidak menggantikan domain validation. XSD bisa memvalidasi struktur dan tipe dasar; domain service tetap harus memvalidasi tenant, duplicate, state transition, reference ownership, dan authorization.

---

## 8. JAXB DTO Model

Jakarta XML Binding memakai package `jakarta.xml.bind.*`. Jakarta XML Binding 4.0 adalah bagian dari Jakarta EE 10 dan menyediakan API/tools untuk mapping XML document ↔ Java object. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/xml-binding/4.0/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="n9ogzr"
package com.company.customer.xml;

import jakarta.xml.bind.annotation.XmlAccessType;
import jakarta.xml.bind.annotation.XmlAccessorType;
import jakarta.xml.bind.annotation.XmlAttribute;
import jakarta.xml.bind.annotation.XmlElement;
import jakarta.xml.bind.annotation.XmlRootElement;

import java.util.List;

@XmlRootElement(
        name = "customerImport",
        namespace = "https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1"
)
@XmlAccessorType(XmlAccessType.FIELD)
public class CustomerImportXml {

    @XmlAttribute(name = "schemaVersion", required = true)
    private String schemaVersion;

    @XmlElement(name = "sourceSystem", required = true)
    private String sourceSystem;

    @XmlElement(name = "customers", required = true)
    private CustomersXml customers;

    public String getSchemaVersion() {
        return schemaVersion;
    }

    public String getSourceSystem() {
        return sourceSystem;
    }

    public CustomersXml getCustomers() {
        return customers;
    }

    @XmlAccessorType(XmlAccessType.FIELD)
    public static class CustomersXml {

        @XmlElement(name = "customer", required = true)
        private List<CustomerXml> customer;

        public List<CustomerXml> getCustomer() {
            return customer;
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="htval4"
package com.company.customer.xml;

import jakarta.xml.bind.annotation.XmlAccessType;
import jakarta.xml.bind.annotation.XmlAccessorType;
import jakarta.xml.bind.annotation.XmlElement;

@XmlAccessorType(XmlAccessType.FIELD)
public class CustomerXml {

    @XmlElement(name = "customerId", required = true)
    private String customerId;

    @XmlElement(name = "name", required = true)
    private String name;

    @XmlElement(name = "email", required = true)
    private String email;

    @XmlElement(name = "status", required = true)
    private String status;

    @XmlElement(name = "phone")
    private String phone;

    @XmlElement(name = "birthDate")
    private String birthDate;

    public String getCustomerId() {
        return customerId;
    }

    public String getName() {
        return name;
    }

    public String getEmail() {
        return email;
    }

    public String getStatus() {
        return status;
    }

    public String getPhone() {
        return phone;
    }

    public String getBirthDate() {
        return birthDate;
    }
}
```

Catatan: untuk DTO XML, JavaBean-style class sering lebih mudah daripada record, terutama saat memakai JAXB runtime yang mengharapkan no-arg constructor dan field/property access.

---

## 9. JAXB Unmarshal dengan Secure Parser

Jangan langsung unmarshal dari raw `InputStream` tanpa secure XML parser policy. OWASP menyarankan menonaktifkan DTD/external entities untuk mencegah XXE; untuk StAX `XMLInputFactory`, OWASP secara eksplisit menunjukkan `XMLInputFactory.SUPPORT_DTD` diset `false` dan external entities dinonaktifkan. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/XML_External_Entity_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="2we83x"
package com.company.customer.xml;

import jakarta.xml.bind.JAXBContext;
import jakarta.xml.bind.JAXBException;
import jakarta.xml.bind.Unmarshaller;

import javax.xml.XMLConstants;
import javax.xml.stream.XMLInputFactory;
import javax.xml.stream.XMLStreamReader;
import javax.xml.validation.Schema;
import java.io.InputStream;

public class SecureJaxbXmlReader<T> {

    private final Class<T> type;
    private final Schema schema;

    public SecureJaxbXmlReader(Class<T> type, Schema schema) {
        this.type = type;
        this.schema = schema;
    }

    public T read(InputStream input) {
        try {
            JAXBContext context = JAXBContext.newInstance(type);
            Unmarshaller unmarshaller = context.createUnmarshaller();

            if (schema != null) {
                unmarshaller.setSchema(schema);
            }

            XMLInputFactory factory = secureXmlInputFactory();
            XMLStreamReader reader = factory.createXMLStreamReader(input);

            return type.cast(unmarshaller.unmarshal(reader));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new XmlProcessingException("Failed to parse XML", e);
        }
    }

    private XMLInputFactory secureXmlInputFactory() {
        XMLInputFactory factory = XMLInputFactory.newFactory();

        factory.setProperty(XMLInputFactory.SUPPORT_DTD, false);
        factory.setProperty("javax.xml.stream.isSupportingExternalEntities", false);

        // Supported by JAXP 1.5+ implementations.
        factory.setProperty(XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_DTD, "");
        factory.setProperty(XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_SCHEMA, "");

        return factory;
    }
}
```

Dokumentasi Java untuk `XMLInputFactory` menyebut implementasi JAXP 1.5+ wajib mendukung property `XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_DTD`, dan `setProperty` digunakan untuk mengatur fitur/properti parser. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/javase/8/docs/api/javax/xml/stream/XMLInputFactory.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. Secure XML Factory Patterns

### 10.1 Secure StAX

```java id="361epd"
import javax.xml.XMLConstants;
import javax.xml.stream.XMLInputFactory;

public final class SecureStaxFactory {

    private SecureStaxFactory() {}

    public static XMLInputFactory create() {
        XMLInputFactory factory = XMLInputFactory.newFactory();

        factory.setProperty(XMLInputFactory.SUPPORT_DTD, false);
        factory.setProperty("javax.xml.stream.isSupportingExternalEntities", false);
        factory.setProperty(XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_DTD, "");
        factory.setProperty(XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_SCHEMA, "");

        return factory;
    }
}
```

### 10.2 Secure DOM

```java id="rb0nqm"
import javax.xml.XMLConstants;
import javax.xml.parsers.DocumentBuilderFactory;

public final class SecureDomFactory {

    private SecureDomFactory() {}

    public static DocumentBuilderFactory create() {
        try {
            DocumentBuilderFactory factory = DocumentBuilderFactory.newInstance();

            factory.setFeature("http://apache.org/xml/features/disallow-doctype-decl", true);
            factory.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/external-general-entities", false);
            factory.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/external-parameter-entities", false);
            factory.setFeature("http://apache.org/xml/features/nonvalidating/load-external-dtd", false);

            factory.setXIncludeAware(false);
            factory.setExpandEntityReferences(false);
            factory.setFeature(XMLConstants.FEATURE_SECURE_PROCESSING, true);
            factory.setAttribute(XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_DTD, "");
            factory.setAttribute(XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_SCHEMA, "");

            return factory;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to configure secure DOM parser", e);
        }
    }
}
```

### 10.3 Secure SAX

```java id="r0572i"
import javax.xml.XMLConstants;
import javax.xml.parsers.SAXParserFactory;

public final class SecureSaxFactory {

    private SecureSaxFactory() {}

    public static SAXParserFactory create() {
        try {
            SAXParserFactory factory = SAXParserFactory.newInstance();

            factory.setNamespaceAware(true);
            factory.setXIncludeAware(false);

            factory.setFeature("http://apache.org/xml/features/disallow-doctype-decl", true);
            factory.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/external-general-entities", false);
            factory.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/external-parameter-entities", false);
            factory.setFeature("http://apache.org/xml/features/nonvalidating/load-external-dtd", false);
            factory.setFeature(XMLConstants.FEATURE_SECURE_PROCESSING, true);

            return factory;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to configure secure SAX parser", e);
        }
    }
}
```

OWASP menjelaskan XXE dapat menyebabkan disclosure file internal, SSRF, port scanning dari sisi server, dan denial-of-service; rekomendasi umumnya adalah menonaktifkan DTD/external entities jika tidak diperlukan. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/XML_External_Entity_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11. XSD Validation

### 11.1 Load schema securely

```java id="tzppn6"
import javax.xml.XMLConstants;
import javax.xml.transform.stream.StreamSource;
import javax.xml.validation.Schema;
import javax.xml.validation.SchemaFactory;
import java.io.InputStream;

public class XmlSchemaLoader {

    public Schema loadSchema(InputStream xsdStream) {
        try {
            SchemaFactory factory = SchemaFactory.newInstance(XMLConstants.W3C_XML_SCHEMA_NS_URI);

            factory.setProperty(XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_DTD, "");
            factory.setProperty(XMLConstants.ACCESS_EXTERNAL_SCHEMA, "");
            factory.setFeature(XMLConstants.FEATURE_SECURE_PROCESSING, true);

            return factory.newSchema(new StreamSource(xsdStream));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new XmlProcessingException("Failed to load XML schema", e);
        }
    }
}
```

### 11.2 Validation error handling

```java id="q6r0rg"
import org.xml.sax.ErrorHandler;
import org.xml.sax.SAXParseException;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class CollectingXmlErrorHandler implements ErrorHandler {

    private final List<XmlValidationError> errors = new ArrayList<>();

    @Override
    public void warning(SAXParseException exception) {
        errors.add(toError("WARNING", exception));
    }

    @Override
    public void error(SAXParseException exception) {
        errors.add(toError("ERROR", exception));
    }

    @Override
    public void fatalError(SAXParseException exception) {
        errors.add(toError("FATAL", exception));
    }

    public List<XmlValidationError> errors() {
        return List.copyOf(errors);
    }

    private XmlValidationError toError(String severity, SAXParseException e) {
        return new XmlValidationError(
                severity,
                e.getLineNumber(),
                e.getColumnNumber(),
                e.getMessage()
        );
    }
}
```

```java id="1h8pkv"
public record XmlValidationError(
        String severity,
        int lineNumber,
        int columnNumber,
        String message
) {}
```

### 11.3 Exception response

```json id="sv8pwn"
{
  "error": {
    "code": "XML_SCHEMA_VALIDATION_FAILED",
    "message": "XML does not match the expected schema.",
    "correlationId": "corr_001",
    "details": [
      {
        "line": 24,
        "column": 17,
        "reason": "Element 'email' is required."
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

## 12. Namespace Handling

XML namespace adalah sumber error paling umum di enterprise integration.

### 12.1 Namespace-aware XML

```xml id="rkexqi"
<customerImport xmlns="https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1">
    <customers>
        <customer>
            <customerId>C001</customerId>
        </customer>
    </customers>
</customerImport>
```

### 12.2 Secara visual sama, tapi namespace beda

```xml id="wnhvkk"
<customerImport>
    <customers>
        <customer>
            <customerId>C001</customerId>
        </customer>
    </customers>
</customerImport>
```

Bagi parser namespace-aware, dua XML di atas **berbeda**. Yang kedua tidak berada di namespace `https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1`.

### 12.3 Namespace policy

| Policy | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Namespace wajib | Ya untuk kontrak enterprise |
| Namespace versioned | Ya untuk breaking changes |
| Prefix bebas | Jangan bergantung pada prefix |
| URI stabil | Jangan ubah sembarangan |
| Default namespace | Boleh, tapi DTO/XSD harus konsisten |
| Unknown namespace | Reject untuk strict import |

Contoh: prefix boleh beda tapi namespace URI sama.

```xml id="vdc0mj"
<ci:customerImport xmlns:ci="https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1">
    ...
</ci:customerImport>
```

Parser harus melihat namespace URI, bukan prefix `ci`.

---

## 13. JAXB Namespace Mapping

```java id="ao0xq0"
@XmlRootElement(
        name = "customerImport",
        namespace = "https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1"
)
public class CustomerImportXml {
    ...
}
```

Untuk package-level namespace, gunakan `package-info.java`:

```java id="q06vve"
@jakarta.xml.bind.annotation.XmlSchema(
        namespace = "https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1",
        elementFormDefault = jakarta.xml.bind.annotation.XmlNsForm.QUALIFIED
)
package com.company.customer.xml;
```

Package-level namespace membantu menghindari annotation namespace berulang di setiap field/class.

---

## 14. StAX Large XML Streaming

Untuk XML besar seperti ini:

```xml id="k05wm0"
<customerImport xmlns="https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1">
    <customers>
        <customer>...</customer>
        <customer>...</customer>
        <customer>...</customer>
        <!-- hundreds of thousands -->
    </customers>
</customerImport>
```

Jangan load seluruh XML ke JAXB object list besar. Gunakan StAX untuk membaca item satu per satu.

### 14.1 StAX parser skeleton

```java id="81jvnd"
import javax.xml.stream.XMLInputFactory;
import javax.xml.stream.XMLStreamConstants;
import javax.xml.stream.XMLStreamReader;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

public class CustomerXmlStreamParser {

    private static final String NS = "https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1";

    public void parse(InputStream input, Consumer<RawCustomerXmlRecord> consumer) {
        XMLInputFactory factory = SecureStaxFactory.create();

        try {
            XMLStreamReader reader = factory.createXMLStreamReader(input);

            long rowNumber = 0;

            while (reader.hasNext()) {
                int event = reader.next();

                if (event == XMLStreamConstants.START_ELEMENT
                        && isElement(reader, "customer")) {

                    rowNumber++;
                    RawCustomerXmlRecord record = readCustomer(reader, rowNumber);
                    consumer.accept(record);
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new XmlProcessingException("Failed to stream customer XML", e);
        }
    }

    private RawCustomerXmlRecord readCustomer(
            XMLStreamReader reader,
            long rowNumber
    ) throws Exception {
        String customerId = null;
        String name = null;
        String email = null;
        String status = null;
        String phone = null;
        String birthDate = null;

        while (reader.hasNext()) {
            int event = reader.next();

            if (event == XMLStreamConstants.START_ELEMENT) {
                String localName = reader.getLocalName();

                switch (localName) {
                    case "customerId" -> customerId = readText(reader);
                    case "name" -> name = readText(reader);
                    case "email" -> email = readText(reader);
                    case "status" -> status = readText(reader);
                    case "phone" -> phone = readText(reader);
                    case "birthDate" -> birthDate = readText(reader);
                    default -> skipElement(reader);
                }
            }

            if (event == XMLStreamConstants.END_ELEMENT
                    && isElement(reader, "customer")) {
                break;
            }
        }

        return new RawCustomerXmlRecord(
                rowNumber,
                customerId,
                name,
                email,
                status,
                phone,
                birthDate
        );
    }

    private String readText(XMLStreamReader reader) throws Exception {
        return reader.getElementText();
    }

    private boolean isElement(XMLStreamReader reader, String localName) {
        return localName.equals(reader.getLocalName())
                && NS.equals(reader.getNamespaceURI());
    }

    private void skipElement(XMLStreamReader reader) throws Exception {
        int depth = 1;

        while (reader.hasNext() && depth > 0) {
            int event = reader.next();

            if (event == XMLStreamConstants.START_ELEMENT) {
                depth++;
            } else if (event == XMLStreamConstants.END_ELEMENT) {
                depth--;
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="n7gtm9"
public record RawCustomerXmlRecord(
        long recordNumber,
        String customerId,
        String name,
        String email,
        String status,
        String phone,
        String birthDate
) {}
```

StAX pull parser memudahkan aplikasi mengontrol kapan membaca node berikutnya. Ini cocok untuk batch XML besar karena memory bisa tetap stabil.

---

## 15. Batch Processing dari XML Stream

```text id="due8w0"
StAX reader
   │
   ├─ read <customer>
   ├─ map raw XML record
   ├─ validate
   ├─ add to batch
   ├─ flush batch to staging table
   └─ continue
```

```java id="zcru2k"
public class CustomerXmlImportWorker {

    private final CustomerXmlStreamParser parser;
    private final CustomerXmlRowMapper rowMapper;
    private final CustomerImportMapper importMapper;

    public void process(InputStream xmlInput, String jobId) {
        BatchBuffer<CustomerImportStagingRow> batch = new BatchBuffer<>(
                1000,
                rows -> importMapper.insertStagingRows(rows)
        );

        parser.parse(xmlInput, raw -> {
            MappingResult<CustomerImportStagingRow> result = rowMapper.map(raw, jobId);

            batch.add(result.value());
        });

        batch.flush();
    }
}
```

Batch processing penting agar XML besar tidak menjadi `List` raksasa di heap.

---

## 16. XML DTO → Domain Command

```java id="dokhnq"
import java.time.LocalDate;

public record CustomerImportCommand(
        String tenantId,
        String customerId,
        String name,
        String email,
        CustomerStatus status,
        String phone,
        LocalDate birthDate,
        String sourceFileId,
        long sourceRecordNumber
) {}
```

Mapper:

```java id="ket6zy"
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.format.DateTimeParseException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class CustomerXmlCommandMapper {

    public MappingResult<CustomerImportCommand> map(
            RawCustomerXmlRecord raw,
            String tenantId,
            String sourceFileId
    ) {
        List<XmlRecordError> errors = new ArrayList<>();

        String customerId = normalize(raw.customerId());
        String name = normalize(raw.name());
        String email = normalize(raw.email());
        CustomerStatus status = parseStatus(raw.status(), raw.recordNumber(), errors);
        LocalDate birthDate = parseDate(raw.birthDate(), raw.recordNumber(), errors);

        if (customerId == null) {
            errors.add(error(raw.recordNumber(), "customerId", "REQUIRED_FIELD", "customerId is required"));
        }

        if (name == null) {
            errors.add(error(raw.recordNumber(), "name", "REQUIRED_FIELD", "name is required"));
        }

        if (email == null || !email.contains("@")) {
            errors.add(error(raw.recordNumber(), "email", "INVALID_EMAIL", "email is invalid"));
        }

        if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
            return MappingResult.invalid(errors);
        }

        return MappingResult.valid(new CustomerImportCommand(
                tenantId,
                customerId,
                name,
                email,
                status,
                normalize(raw.phone()),
                birthDate,
                sourceFileId,
                raw.recordNumber()
        ));
    }

    private CustomerStatus parseStatus(
            String value,
            long recordNumber,
            List<XmlRecordError> errors
    ) {
        String normalized = normalize(value);

        if (normalized == null) {
            errors.add(error(recordNumber, "status", "REQUIRED_FIELD", "status is required"));
            return null;
        }

        try {
            return CustomerStatus.valueOf(normalized);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            errors.add(error(recordNumber, "status", "INVALID_STATUS", "status must be ACTIVE or INACTIVE"));
            return null;
        }
    }

    private LocalDate parseDate(
            String value,
            long recordNumber,
            List<XmlRecordError> errors
    ) {
        String normalized = normalize(value);

        if (normalized == null) {
            return null;
        }

        try {
            return LocalDate.parse(normalized);
        } catch (DateTimeParseException e) {
            errors.add(error(recordNumber, "birthDate", "INVALID_DATE", "birthDate must use yyyy-MM-dd"));
            return null;
        }
    }

    private XmlRecordError error(long recordNumber, String path, String code, String message) {
        return new XmlRecordError(recordNumber, path, code, message);
    }

    private String normalize(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : value.trim();
    }
}
```

```java id="z0s500"
public record XmlRecordError(
        long recordNumber,
        String path,
        String errorCode,
        String message
) {}
```

## 17. Jersey XML Endpoint

### 17.1 JAXB request body endpoint

```java id="4wexjg"
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Consumes;
import jakarta.ws.rs.POST;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Produces;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

@Path("/customer-imports")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_XML)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CustomerXmlImportResource {

    private final CustomerXmlImportService service;

    public CustomerXmlImportResource(CustomerXmlImportService service) {
        this.service = service;
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/xml")
    public Response importXml(@Valid CustomerImportXml request) {
        String tenantId = CurrentTenant.required();
        ImportJobResult result = service.importSmallXml(tenantId, request);

        return Response.accepted(result).build();
    }
}
```

Ini cocok untuk XML kecil/sedang. Untuk XML besar, jangan bind request body langsung ke object graph besar.

### 17.2 Multipart XML upload untuk async processing

```java id="2i8a17"
@POST
@Path("/xml-files")
@Consumes(MediaType.MULTIPART_FORM_DATA)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public Response uploadXmlFile(
        @FormDataParam("file") InputStream fileStream,
        @FormDataParam("file") FormDataContentDisposition fileMeta
) {
    StagedFile staged = fileIntakeService.stageXml(
            fileStream,
            fileMeta.getFileName()
    );

    ImportJob job = importJobService.createXmlImportJob(staged);

    return Response.accepted(new UploadAcceptedResponse(
            job.id(),
            staged.originalFilename(),
            "ACCEPTED_FOR_PROCESSING"
    )).build();
}
```

## 18. XML Response / Generation with JAXB

```java id="x1kpeg"
@XmlRootElement(name = "customer")

}
```

```java id="dtq6u2"
@GET
@Path("/{customerId}.xml")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_XML)
public Response getCustomerXml(@PathParam("customerId") String customerId) {
    CustomerResult customer = customerService.get(customerId);

    CustomerXmlResponse response = new CustomerXmlResponse(
            customer.customerId(),
            customer.name(),
            customer.status().name()
    );

    return Response.ok(response).build();
```

Untuk XML besar, gunakan `XMLStreamWriter` dan `StreamingOutput`, bukan membangun object list besar.
