## 19. Streaming XML Export

```java id="9ckttf"
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.StreamingOutput;

import javax.xml.stream.XMLOutputFactory;
import javax.xml.stream.XMLStreamWriter;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

@GET
@Path("/customers.xml")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_XML)
public Response exportCustomersXml() {
    authorizeExport();

    StreamingOutput stream = output -> {
        XMLOutputFactory factory = XMLOutputFactory.newFactory();
        XMLStreamWriter writer = factory.createXMLStreamWriter(output, StandardCharsets.UTF_8.name());

        writer.writeStartDocument("UTF-8", "1.0");
        writer.writeStartElement("customers");

        customerQueryService.streamCustomers(customer -> {
            try {
                writer.writeStartElement("customer");

                writer.writeStartElement("customerId");
                writer.writeCharacters(customer.customerId());
                writer.writeEndElement();

                writer.writeStartElement("name");
                writer.writeCharacters(customer.name());
                writer.writeEndElement();

                writer.writeStartElement("status");
                writer.writeCharacters(customer.status().name());
                writer.writeEndElement();

                writer.writeEndElement();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                throw new RuntimeException(e);
            }
        });

        writer.writeEndElement();
        writer.writeEndDocument();
        writer.flush();
        writer.close();
    };

    return Response.ok(stream)
            .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"customers.xml\"")
            .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            .build();
}
```

`XMLStreamWriter.writeCharacters(...)` lebih aman daripada manual string concatenation karena writer menangani escaping karakter XML.

---

## 20. XML Escaping

Jangan generate XML dengan string concatenation.

### Jangan

```java id="eivh2i"
String xml = "<name>" + customerName + "</name>";
```

Kalau `customerName` berisi `Alice & Bob <test>`, XML menjadi invalid atau berbahaya.

### Gunakan

```java id="v3glzu"
writer.writeStartElement("name");
writer.writeCharacters(customerName);
writer.writeEndElement();
```

Karakter penting XML:

| Character | Escaped form |
|---|---|
| `<` | `&lt;` |
| `>` | `&gt;` |
| `&` | `&amp;` |
| `"` | `&quot;` dalam attribute |
| `'` | `&apos;` dalam attribute |

---

## 21. XML Error Reporting

Untuk XML file import, error bisa terjadi di beberapa level.

| Error level | Contoh | Response/job error |
|---|---|---|
| File-level | Not XML, malformed XML | `XML_MALFORMED` |
| Security-level | DTD/XXE detected | `UNSAFE_XML_REJECTED` |
| Schema-level | XSD validation failed | `XML_SCHEMA_VALIDATION_FAILED` |
| Record-level | Customer email invalid | Row/record error report |
| Domain-level | Customer already locked | Domain error |
| Persistence-level | DB constraint violation | Job failed/partial |

Error report untuk large XML:

```csv id="rozxai"
record_number,path,error_code,message
15,/customerImport/customers/customer[15]/email,INVALID_EMAIL,email is invalid
20,/customerImport/customers/customer[20]/status,INVALID_STATUS,status must be ACTIVE or INACTIVE
```

Untuk XML, `lineNumber`/`columnNumber` dari parser berguna untuk malformed/schema error, sementara `recordNumber`/path lebih berguna untuk domain validation.

---

## 22. XML Catalog

Untuk enterprise XML yang memakai XSD include/import, hindari mengambil schema dari internet saat runtime. Java 17 punya XML Catalog API yang membantu konfigurasi local catalog untuk XML processors; dokumentasi Java menjelaskan XML Catalog API memudahkan konfigurasi processor untuk memakai local catalog. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/core/xml-catalog-api1.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Use case:

```text id="63lx04"
External schema URI:
  https://example.company/schemas/common-types.xsd

Runtime local mapping:
  classpath:/schemas/common-types.xsd
```

Keuntungan:

| Keuntungan | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Tidak bergantung internet | Lebih stabil |
| Menghindari SSRF/external fetch | Lebih aman |
| Schema version terkendali | Reproducible |
| Deployable bersama service | Mudah audit |

---

## 23. XML Schema Versioning

Versioning XML biasanya lewat namespace, attribute, atau keduanya.

### Namespace versioning

```xml id="7h13z2"
<customerImport xmlns="https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1">
```

Breaking change:

```xml id="7r1deg"
<customerImport xmlns="https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v2">
```

### Attribute versioning

```xml id="6z1y7z"
<customerImport xmlns="https://example.company/schemas/customer-import"
                schemaVersion="1.0">
```

### Recommendation

| Change type | Versioning action |
|---|---|
| Add optional element | Same major version usually OK |
| Add required element | New version |
| Rename element | New version |
| Change type | New version |
| Remove element | New version |
| Add enum value | Compatibility review |
| Change namespace | Breaking |

Untuk long-lived enterprise XML, namespace versioning lebih eksplisit dan membantu routing parser.

---

## 24. XML + MyBatis/PostgreSQL Integration

Staging table untuk XML import:

```sql id="h3om8x"
CREATE TABLE customer_xml_import_staging (
    job_id          TEXT NOT NULL,
    record_number   BIGINT NOT NULL,

    customer_id     TEXT,
    name            TEXT,
    email           TEXT,
    status          TEXT,
    phone           TEXT,
    birth_date      TEXT,

    row_status      TEXT NOT NULL,
    error_json      JSONB,

    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (job_id, record_number)
);

CREATE INDEX idx_customer_xml_import_staging_job_status
ON customer_xml_import_staging (job_id, row_status);
```

MyBatis batch insert:

```xml id="wbe40h"
<insert id="insertStagingRows">
    INSERT INTO customer_xml_import_staging (
        job_id,
        record_number,
        customer_id,
        name,
        email,
        status,
        phone,
        birth_date,
        row_status,
        error_json,
        created_at
    )
    VALUES
    <foreach collection="list" item="row" separator=",">
        (
            #{row.jobId},
            #{row.recordNumber},
            #{row.customerId},
            #{row.name},
            #{row.email},
            #{row.status},
            #{row.phone},
            #{row.birthDate},
            #{row.rowStatus},
            #{row.errorJson}::jsonb,
            now()
        )
    </foreach>
</insert>
```

Rule:

```text id="n31u75"
XML shape belongs in XML DTO/staging.
Domain mutation belongs in application service.
Persistence entity should not depend on XML structure.
```

---

## 25. Security Failure Modes

| Failure mode | Contoh | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| XXE | XML membaca `/etc/passwd` atau URL internal | Disable DTD/external entities |
| Billion laughs/entity expansion | DoS via entity expansion | Disable DTD + secure processing |
| External schema fetch | Parser mengambil XSD dari internet/internal URL | `ACCESS_EXTERNAL_SCHEMA=""`, XML Catalog |
| Huge XML | Memory/CPU exhaustion | StAX streaming, size limit, record limit |
| Deep nesting | Stack/CPU exhaustion | Parser constraints, max depth |
| XPath injection | User input masuk XPath expression | Parameterize/allowlist, avoid dynamic XPath |
| XSLT external access | Transform fetch file/network | Restrict external access |
| XML signature wrapping | Signed XML/SAML misuse | Use mature security libraries and strict validation |
| Raw XML logging | PII/secrets leak | Redact/log metadata only |
| Namespace confusion | Wrong element accepted | Namespace-aware parsing |

---

## 26. Parser Runtime Limits

Untuk XML file besar, tetapkan budget eksplisit.

```java id="r70bki"
public record XmlProcessingBudget(
        long maxFileSizeBytes,
        long maxRecords,
        int maxElementDepth,
        long maxTextLengthPerElement,
        int timeoutSeconds,
        int batchSize
) {}
```

Contoh policy:

| File type | Max size | Max records | Mode |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| XML API request | 1-5 MB | N/A | JAXB |
| XML customer import | 50-200 MB | 100k-1M | StAX worker |
| XML regulatory batch | Domain-specific | Domain-specific | StAX + XSD + audit |
| XML export | Streamed | Query-limited | XMLStreamWriter |

---

## 27. Observability

Metrics:

| Metric | Type |
|---|---|
| `xml_import_started_total` | Counter |
| `xml_import_completed_total{status}` | Counter |
| `xml_parse_failed_total{reason}` | Counter |
| `xml_schema_validation_failed_total` | Counter |
| `xml_xxe_rejected_total` | Counter |
| `xml_records_processed_total{result}` | Counter |
| `xml_processing_duration_seconds` | Histogram |
| `xml_file_size_bytes` | Histogram |
| `xml_export_duration_seconds` | Histogram |

Structured log:

```json id="0yqq1m"
{
  "event": "xml_import_completed",
  "jobId": "job_001",
  "fileId": "file_001",
  "tenantId": "tenant_001",
  "schemaVersion": "1.0",
  "namespace": "https://example.company/schemas/customer-import/v1",
  "recordsProcessed": 100000,
  "recordsRejected": 37,
  "status": "PARTIAL_SUCCESS",
  "durationMs": 52500,
  "correlationId": "corr_001"
}
```

Jangan log raw XML body. XML sering berisi PII, credential, account data, atau regulated content.

### XXE rejection test

```java id="ubh33n"
@Test
void shouldRejectXmlWithDoctype() {
    String xml = """
            <?xml version="1.0"?>
            <!DOCTYPE data [
              <!ENTITY xxe SYSTEM "file:///etc/passwd">
            ]>
            <customerImport>&xxe;</customerImport>
            """;

    assertThrows(XmlProcessingException.class, () ->
            secureReader.read(new ByteArrayInputStream(xml.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)))
    );
}
```

### XML escaping test

```java id="jtbeba"
@Test
void shouldEscapeXmlCharactersOnExport() {
    String name = "Alice & Bob <Partners>";

    String xml = xmlExporter.exportOneCustomer("C001", name);

    assertThat(xml).contains("Alice &amp; Bob &lt;Partners&gt;");
}
```

## 29. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| XML parser default tanpa hardening | XXE/SSRF/file disclosure |
| Disable validation karena “ribet” | Bad data masuk domain |
| Mengabaikan namespace | XML salah bisa dianggap benar/atau sebaliknya |
| JAXB untuk file XML sangat besar | OOM |
| DOM untuk semua XML | Memory boros |
| Generate XML dengan string concatenation | Invalid XML/injection |
| Runtime fetch external XSD/DTD | SSRF, dependency network |
| Log raw XML | PII/secrets leak |
| Tidak simpan original XML | Audit/reprocess sulit |
| Satu DTO untuk XML/API/domain/entity | Coupling dan security risk |
| Treat XSD as domain validation | Domain rules tetap bocor |
| Tidak punya versioning | Breaking change sulit dikelola |

---

## 30. Production Checklist

```text id="eb5rba"
Boundary
[ ] Content-Type application/xml/text/xml jelas
[ ] File size limit
[ ] XML upload masuk intake pipeline
[ ] Original XML retained jika audit required
[ ] Correlation id ada

Security
[ ] DTD disabled
[ ] External entities disabled
[ ] ACCESS_EXTERNAL_DTD disabled
[ ] ACCESS_EXTERNAL_SCHEMA disabled
[ ] Secure processing enabled
[ ] XML Catalog digunakan jika schema import/include perlu local mapping
[ ] No raw XML logging
[ ] Parser timeout/limits
[ ] Worker isolation untuk XML besar

Contract
[ ] Namespace jelas
[ ] Schema version jelas
[ ] XSD tersedia jika strict contract
[ ] Compatibility rules terdokumentasi
[ ] Unknown element policy jelas
[ ] Required/optional element jelas

Parsing
[ ] JAXB untuk XML kecil/sedang
[ ] StAX/SAX untuk XML besar
[ ] DOM hanya untuk size-bounded XML
[ ] Namespace-aware parsing
[ ] Error line/column captured
[ ] Record number/path captured untuk batch

Mapping
[ ] XML DTO tidak sama dengan entity
[ ] XML DTO → domain command
[ ] Bean/schema validation + domain validation
[ ] Cross-record validation jika perlu
[ ] Batch persistence

Export
[ ] XMLStreamWriter/JAXB digunakan
[ ] Tidak string concatenation
[ ] Escaping aman
[ ] Content-Disposition benar jika download
[ ] Cache-Control no-store untuk sensitive XML

Observability
[ ] Parse duration metric
[ ] Schema validation failure metric
[ ] XXE rejection metric
[ ] Record processed metric
[ ] Structured log tanpa raw XML
```

---

## 31. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| XML parser must be secure by default | Jangan mengandalkan default parser |
| Namespace URI matters, prefix does not | Prefix XML bisa berubah |
| XSD validates shape, not business truth | Domain validation tetap wajib |
| JAXB is not for unbounded XML | Gunakan StAX/SAX untuk file besar |
| External DTD/schema access should be denied | Cegah XXE/SSRF/network dependency |
| Original XML is audit evidence | Simpan checksum + original jika regulatory |
| XML DTO must not be persistence entity | Hindari coupling dan mass assignment |
| XML generation must use writer/marshaller | Jangan string concatenation |
| Raw XML should not be logged | PII/secrets risk |
| Versioning must be explicit | Namespace/schemaVersion harus dikelola |

---

## 32. Kesimpulan Seri 8

XML processing yang production-ready di Jersey/JAX-RS adalah kombinasi dari:

```text id="fbo97t"
secure parser
+ namespace-aware contract
+ XSD validation
+ JAXB/StAX choice
+ DTO/domain boundary
+ large-file streaming
+ audit
+ observability
```

Pola sehat:

```text id="25o3y5"
XML input
  -> intake/security
  -> secure parser
  -> namespace/schema validation
  -> JAXB for small XML or StAX for large XML
  -> XML DTO/raw record
  -> domain command
  -> persistence/job result
  -> structured error report
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="h5q4j5"
Never parse untrusted XML with default insecure settings.
Never allow external DTD/schema access by default.
Never ignore namespace in enterprise XML.
Never use DOM/JAXB for unbounded large XML.
Never generate XML by string concatenation.
```

Lanjut **Seri 9 dari 20**. Fokusnya PDF extraction: membedakan text-based PDF vs scanned PDF, mengambil text/metadata/page-level content secara aman, dan membangun pipeline yang tidak membebani API pod.

# Seri 9/20 - PDF Extraction: Text, Metadata, Page-Level Extraction, Scanned PDF Limitation, PDFBox, Tika & Safe Extraction Pipeline

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **PDF extraction** di Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice:

```text id="73r8aq"
PDF Upload
   │
   ▼
File Intake Pipeline
   │
   ├─ size limit
   ├─ MIME detection
   ├─ checksum
   ├─ AV scan / quarantine
   └─ original file retention
   │
   ▼
PDF Extraction Worker
   │
   ├─ open PDF safely
   ├─ inspect encryption / permissions
   ├─ extract metadata
   ├─ extract page count
   ├─ extract text per page
   ├─ classify text-based vs scanned
   ├─ optionally route to OCR
   └─ persist extraction result
```

PDF harus dipahami sebagai **document container**, bukan sekadar text file. Ada PDF yang punya text layer, ada yang hanya gambar hasil scan, ada yang encrypted/password-protected, ada yang punya embedded files, forms, metadata, signature, JavaScript/action, dan struktur internal kompleks.

Apache PDFBox adalah library Java untuk bekerja dengan PDF; fitur resminya mencakup extract Unicode text, split/merge, form filling, PDF/A validation, save as image, create PDFs, dan digital signing. ([pdfbox.apache.org](https://pdfbox.apache.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Apache Tika dapat mendeteksi dan mengekstrak metadata serta text dari banyak tipe file, termasuk PDF, melalui satu interface. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

PDF Extraction
│
├─ 1. Intake & Security
│   ├─ allowed extension: pdf

## 3. PDF Extraction Use Cases

| Use case | Extraction target | Output |
|---|---|---|
| Search indexing | Text + metadata | Search document/index |
| Document validation | Page count, metadata, required text | Validation result |
| Regulatory evidence | Original file + checksum + extracted text snapshot | Audit package |
| Form processing | AcroForm fields | Structured field map |
| Attachment classification | Metadata + first pages text | Classification label |
| Legal/contract review | Text by page | Clause extraction pipeline |
| OCR routing | Detect scanned PDF | OCR job |
| Preview | Page images or page text | Preview resource |
| Duplicate detection | Checksum + metadata | Duplicate flag |
| Data extraction | Known phrase/table/field | Domain-specific extraction result |

---

## 4. Library Pilihan

| Library | Cocok untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| **Apache PDFBox** | Direct PDF operations: open, inspect, text extraction, metadata, page count, forms, rendering | Pilihan utama jika butuh kontrol PDF-level |
| **Apache Tika** | Generic content extraction across file types | Cocok untuk unified extraction pipeline lintas PDF/Word/Excel |
| **Tesseract/OCR engine** | Scanned PDF / image-only PDF | Bukan pengganti PDF parser; digunakan jika tidak ada text layer |
| **Commercial OCR/document AI** | High accuracy forms/invoices/ID documents | Cocok untuk production OCR berat, tapi governance/cost perlu dihitung |
| **Custom PDF parser** | Tidak disarankan | PDF format kompleks dan rawan bug |

PDFBox `PDFTextStripper` secara resmi adalah class untuk mengambil text dari PDF dan mengabaikan formatting; dokumentasinya juga mengingatkan bahwa client bertanggung jawab memverifikasi permission extraction pada dokumen. ([javadoc.io](https://javadoc.io/doc/org.apache.pdfbox/pdfbox/2.0.34/org/apache/pdfbox/text/PDFTextStripper.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Tika PDF parser juga dapat memproses encrypted PDF jika password yang dibutuhkan diberikan lewat metadata input; jika tidak ada password, parser mencoba empty password yang sering dipakai pada beberapa PDF. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/3.2.1/api/org/apache/tika/parser/pdf/PDFParser.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Gunakan dependency management/BOM project. PDF parser adalah attack surface, jadi dependency seperti PDFBox/Tika harus masuk daftar library yang rutin dipatch.

## 6. PDF Contract untuk Microservice

Sebelum extract, buat policy.

```text id="gaz70w"
PDF Extraction Policy
│
├─ allowed MIME: application/pdf
├─ max file size: e.g. 50 MB
├─ max pages: e.g. 300
├─ encrypted PDF: reject or password flow?
├─ scanned PDF: OCR route or reject?
├─ embedded files: reject or ignore?
├─ forms: extract or ignore?
├─ max extracted text chars: e.g. 5M
├─ max extraction time: e.g. 60s
├─ retention: original + extracted snapshot
└─ audit: checksum + actor + timestamp + parser version
```

Policy harus domain-specific. PDF identitas, kontrak legal, invoice, dan report internal mungkin butuh aturan berbeda.

---

## 7. PDF Extraction State Machine

```text id="vlpn2m"
UPLOADED
   │
   ▼
STAGED
   │
   ▼
PDF_VALIDATION
   │
   ├─ not PDF ───────────────► REJECTED_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE
   ├─ too large ─────────────► REJECTED_TOO_LARGE
   ├─ malware/suspicious ────► QUARANTINED
   └─ accepted
   │
   ▼
PDF_INSPECTION
   │
   ├─ encrypted unsupported ─► REJECTED_ENCRYPTED
   ├─ too many pages ────────► REJECTED_TOO_MANY_PAGES
   └─ accepted
   │
   ▼
TEXT_EXTRACTION
   │
   ├─ text found ────────────► TEXT_EXTRACTED
   ├─ no text layer ─────────► OCR_REQUIRED
   └─ parser failure ────────► EXTRACTION_FAILED
   │
   ▼
COMPLETED / PARTIAL / FAILED
```

State eksplisit membantu debugging, audit, dan retry.

## 9. Basic PDFBox Text Extraction

```java id="kcb5z1"
import org.apache.pdfbox.Loader;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDDocument;
import org.apache.pdfbox.text.PDFTextStripper;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Path;

public class PdfTextExtractionService {

    public String extractAllText(Path pdfPath) {
        try (PDDocument document = Loader.loadPDF(pdfPath.toFile())) {
            if (document.isEncrypted()) {
                throw new PdfRejectedException(
                        "ENCRYPTED_PDF",
                        "Encrypted PDF is not supported by this extraction flow"
                );
            }

            PDFTextStripper stripper = new PDFTextStripper();
            stripper.setSortByPosition(true);

            return stripper.getText(document);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new PdfExtractionException("Failed to extract text from PDF", e);
        }
    }
}
```

`PDFTextStripper` mengambil text dari PDF dan mengabaikan banyak aspek formatting/layout; ini cukup untuk indexing/basic extraction, tetapi tidak cukup jika Anda butuh layout-preserving extraction seperti table atau column-aware legal analysis. ([javadoc.io](https://javadoc.io/doc/org.apache.pdfbox/pdfbox/2.0.34/org/apache/pdfbox/text/PDFTextStripper.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. Page-Level Extraction

Untuk audit dan troubleshooting, page-level extraction lebih berguna daripada satu string besar.

```java id="q4e53y"
import org.apache.pdfbox.Loader;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDDocument;
import org.apache.pdfbox.text.PDFTextStripper;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class PdfPageTextExtractor {

    public List<PdfPageText> extractByPage(Path pdfPath, PdfExtractionPolicy policy) {
        try (PDDocument document = Loader.loadPDF(pdfPath.toFile())) {
            int pageCount = document.getNumberOfPages();

            if (pageCount > policy.maxPages()) {
                throw new PdfRejectedException(
                        "TOO_MANY_PAGES",
                        "PDF exceeds maximum page limit: " + policy.maxPages()
                );
            }

            PDFTextStripper stripper = new PDFTextStripper();
            stripper.setSortByPosition(true);

            List<PdfPageText> pages = new ArrayList<>();
            long totalChars = 0;

            for (int page = 1; page <= pageCount; page++) {
                stripper.setStartPage(page);
                stripper.setEndPage(page);

                String text = stripper.getText(document);
                String normalized = normalizeText(text);

                totalChars += normalized.length();

                if (totalChars > policy.maxExtractedChars()) {
                    throw new PdfRejectedException(
                            "EXTRACTED_TEXT_TOO_LARGE",
                            "Extracted text exceeds maximum character limit"
                    );
                }

                boolean likelyScanned = normalized.isBlank();
                double qualityScore = estimateTextQuality(normalized);

                pages.add(new PdfPageText(
                        page,
                        normalized,
                        normalized.length(),
                        likelyScanned,
                        qualityScore
                ));
            }

            return pages;
        } catch (PdfRejectedException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new PdfExtractionException("Failed to extract PDF page text", e);
        }
    }

    private String normalizeText(String text) {
        if (text == null) {
            return "";
        }

        return text
                .replace("\u0000", "")
                .replaceAll("[ \\t\\x0B\\f\\r]+", " ")
                .replaceAll("\\n{3,}", "\n\n")
                .trim();
    }

    private double estimateTextQuality(String text) {
        if (text == null || text.isBlank()) {
            return 0.0d;
        }

        long lettersOrDigits = text.chars()
                .filter(Character::isLetterOrDigit)
                .count();

        return (double) lettersOrDigits / Math.max(1, text.length());
    }
}
```

Page-level extraction membantu menjawab:

```text id="3uxl22"
- halaman mana yang kosong/scanned?
- halaman mana yang gagal extraction?
- berapa text per halaman?
- apakah extraction result masuk akal?
- apakah OCR hanya perlu untuk halaman tertentu?
```

---

## 11. PDF Metadata Extraction

PDF punya metadata seperti title, author, creator, producer, creation date, modification date.

```java id="fij5p5"
import org.apache.pdfbox.Loader;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDDocument;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDDocumentInformation;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Calendar;

public class PdfMetadataExtractor {

    public PdfDocumentMetadata extractMetadata(Path pdfPath) {
        try (PDDocument document = Loader.loadPDF(pdfPath.toFile())) {
            PDDocumentInformation info = document.getDocumentInformation();

            return new PdfDocumentMetadata(
                    info.getTitle(),
                    info.getAuthor(),
                    info.getSubject(),
                    info.getCreator(),
                    info.getProducer(),
                    toInstant(info.getCreationDate()),
                    toInstant(info.getModificationDate()),
                    String.valueOf(document.getVersion())
            );
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new PdfExtractionException("Failed to extract PDF metadata", e);
        }
    }

    private Instant toInstant(Calendar calendar) {
        return calendar == null ? null : calendar.toInstant();
    }
}
```

Metadata dari PDF **tidak boleh dipercaya sebagai fakta domain**. `author`, `creator`, dan tanggal bisa kosong, salah, atau dimanipulasi. Simpan sebagai document metadata, bukan sebagai bukti tunggal.

---

## 12. Text-Based vs Scanned PDF

### 12.1 Perbedaan utama

| Jenis PDF | Isi utama | PDFBox text extraction | Butuh OCR? |
|---|---|---|---|
| Text-based PDF | Text layer + fonts | Bisa extract text | Tidak biasanya |
| Scanned PDF | Image per page | Text kosong/minimal | Ya |
| Searchable scanned PDF | Image + OCR text layer | Bisa extract text, kualitas bervariasi | Mungkin tidak |
| Mixed PDF | Sebagian text, sebagian scan | Partial extraction | OCR page tertentu |
| Form PDF | Fields + visible text | Perlu extract form fields juga | Tergantung |
| Image-heavy PDF | Banyak image, sedikit text | Text minimal | Mungkin |

### 12.2 Simple scanned heuristic

```java id="rdx0qa"
public class PdfScanHeuristic {

    public boolean likelyScanned(List<PdfPageText> pages) {
        if (pages.isEmpty()) {
            return true;
        }

        long blankPages = pages.stream()
                .filter(page -> page.text() == null || page.text().isBlank())
                .count();

        double blankRatio = (double) blankPages / pages.size();

        return blankRatio >= 0.8d;
    }

    public boolean pageNeedsOcr(PdfPageText page) {
        return page.text() == null
                || page.text().isBlank()
                || page.extractionQualityScore() < 0.25d;
    }
}
```

Heuristic ini tidak sempurna. PDF dengan decorative image atau OCR text layer buruk bisa salah klasifikasi. Untuk production, gabungkan dengan image count/page render check dan hasil OCR confidence.

---

## 13. OCR Routing

PDFBox/Tika extraction bukan OCR engine. Jika PDF hanya berupa gambar scan, perlu OCR.

```text id="7h7cbu"
PDF page
  -> render page to image
  -> OCR engine
  -> text + confidence
  -> merge with page-level result
```

Apache Tika menyediakan toolkit untuk deteksi dan extraction text/metadata lintas format; Tika PDF parser juga menangani PDF dan embedded documents, tetapi OCR memerlukan setup tambahan seperti engine OCR eksternal. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### OCR decision

| Kondisi | Action |
|---|---|
| Text layer cukup | Tidak OCR |
| Semua halaman kosong | Route seluruh PDF ke OCR |
| Sebagian halaman kosong | OCR halaman kosong saja |
| PDF terlalu besar | Reject atau async OCR |
| Bahasa tidak didukung OCR | Manual review / unsupported |
| OCR confidence rendah | Manual review / partial result |

### OCR harus async

OCR biasanya CPU-heavy dan lambat. Jangan jalankan OCR di request HTTP langsung.

```text id="j46x0o"
API upload
   -> stage file
   -> create job
   -> text extraction worker
   -> if OCR required: OCR worker
   -> result available via status endpoint
```

---

## 14. Tika-Based Generic Extraction

Kalau service perlu unified extraction untuk PDF, Word, Excel, PPT, dan format lain, Tika bisa menjadi abstraction.

```java id="2zr1oj"
import org.apache.tika.Tika;
import org.apache.tika.metadata.Metadata;

import java.io.InputStream;

public class TikaTextExtractionService {

    private final Tika tika = new Tika();

    public TikaExtractionResult extract(InputStream input) {
        try {
            Metadata metadata = new Metadata();
            String text = tika.parseToString(input, metadata);

            return new TikaExtractionResult(
                    text,
                    metadata.names().length
            );
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new PdfExtractionException("Failed to extract text with Tika", e);
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="51pw7g"
public record TikaExtractionResult(
        String text,
        int metadataFieldCount
) {}
```

Tika cocok sebagai **generic extraction layer**, tetapi kalau Anda butuh page-level PDF extraction, permissions, forms, rendering, atau granular PDF inspection, PDFBox langsung lebih tepat.

---

## 15. PDF Forms Extraction

Banyak PDF enterprise berisi AcroForm fields.

```java id="liunj7"
import org.apache.pdfbox.Loader;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDDocument;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.interactive.form.PDAcroForm;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.interactive.form.PDField;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public class PdfFormExtractor {

    public Map<String, String> extractFormFields(Path pdfPath) {
        try (PDDocument document = Loader.loadPDF(pdfPath.toFile())) {
            PDAcroForm acroForm = document.getDocumentCatalog().getAcroForm();

            Map<String, String> fields = new LinkedHashMap<>();

            if (acroForm == null) {
                return fields;
            }

            for (PDField field : acroForm.getFieldTree()) {
                fields.put(field.getFullyQualifiedName(), field.getValueAsString());
            }

            return fields;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new PdfExtractionException("Failed to extract PDF form fields", e);
        }
    }
}
```

PDFBox resmi menyebut dapat mengekstrak data dari PDF forms atau mengisi PDF forms. ([pdfbox.apache.org](https://pdfbox.apache.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 16. Encryption & Permissions

PDF bisa punya user password dan owner password. PDFBox documentation menjelaskan PDF encryption memakai dua password: user password untuk membuka/view dengan restricted permissions, dan owner password untuk akses penuh. ([pdfbox.apache.org](https://pdfbox.apache.org/2.0/cookbook/encryption.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Policy choices

| Policy | Perilaku |
|---|---|
| Reject encrypted PDF | Paling sederhana dan aman |
| Allow empty-password encrypted PDF | Bisa untuk beberapa PDF yang teknis encrypted tapi bisa dibuka |
| Password flow | User submit password secara aman |
| Owner password only | Untuk internal controlled process |
| Ignore permissions | Tidak disarankan tanpa legal/security review |

### Basic check

```java id="6ry6oc"
try (PDDocument document = Loader.loadPDF(pdfPath.toFile())) {
    if (document.isEncrypted()) {
        throw new PdfRejectedException(
                "ENCRYPTED_PDF",
                "Encrypted PDF is not supported"
        );
    }
}
```

Catatan: ekstraksi text dari PDF yang memiliki permission restriction perlu policy legal/enterprise yang jelas.

---

## 17. Safe Extraction Policy

PDF extraction harus dibatasi.

```java id="1cz1b9"
public record PdfExtractionPolicy(
        long maxFileSizeBytes,
        int maxPages,
        long maxExtractedChars,
        int maxPageTextChars,
        int extractionTimeoutSeconds,
        boolean allowEncrypted,
        boolean extractForms,
        boolean allowEmbeddedFiles,
        boolean routeScannedToOcr
) {
    public static PdfExtractionPolicy defaultPolicy() {
        return new PdfExtractionPolicy(
                50L * 1024 * 1024,
                300,
                5_000_000,
                100_000,
                60,
                false,
                true,
                false,
                true
        );
    }
}
```

### Recommended default

| Concern | Default |
|---|---|
| Encrypted PDF | Reject |
| Embedded files | Reject or ignore |
| JavaScript/action | Flag suspicious |
| Max pages | Domain-specific |
| Max text chars | Limit |
| OCR | Async only |
| Parser timeout | Required |
| Original file | Retain with checksum |
| Raw extracted text logging | Never |

---

## 18. Worker Isolation

Jangan lakukan PDF extraction berat di API pod.

```text id="7kglew"
API Pod
  ├─ receive upload
  ├─ intake validation
  ├─ stage original file
  └─ create extraction job

PDF Worker Pod
  ├─ load PDF
  ├─ inspect
  ├─ extract text/metadata/forms
  ├─ persist result
  └─ update job

OCR Worker Pod
  ├─ render page
  ├─ OCR
  ├─ persist OCR result
  └─ update job
```

### Kenapa dipisah?

| Alasan | Dampak |
|---|---|
| PDF parser bisa CPU/memory-heavy | API latency tetap stabil |
| OCR jauh lebih berat | Bisa scale worker terpisah |
| Timeout HTTP terbatas | Async job lebih aman |
| Parser bug bisa crash worker | Blast radius lebih kecil |
| Resource limit lebih spesifik | K8s scheduling lebih baik |

---

## 19. Timeout Pattern

Java tidak bisa selalu menghentikan parsing library secara aman dari thread interrupt. Praktisnya gunakan worker process/pod/job dengan timeout dan resource limit.

```text id="ow3xgt"
Extraction job budget:
- max file size
- max pages
- max chars
- max duration
- max retry
- max memory
```

Dalam Kubernetes:

```yaml id="cj8ekg"
resources:
  requests:
    cpu: "500m"
    memory: "1Gi"
    ephemeral-storage: "1Gi"
  limits:
    cpu: "2"
    memory: "2Gi"
    ephemeral-storage: "5Gi"
```

Untuk workload OCR, pisahkan resource profile dari PDF text extraction biasa.

---

## 20. Persistence Model

### 20.1 Extraction job

```sql id="go1hq0"
CREATE TABLE pdf_extraction_job (
    id                  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_object_id       UUID NOT NULL,
    requested_by         TEXT NOT NULL,

    status               TEXT NOT NULL,
    page_count           INTEGER,
    encrypted            BOOLEAN,
    text_layer_detected  BOOLEAN,
    extracted_char_count BIGINT,

    failure_code         TEXT,
    failure_message      TEXT,

    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    started_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at         TIMESTAMPTZ
);
```

### 20.2 Page text

```sql id="4wdv37"
CREATE TABLE pdf_page_text (
    job_id          TEXT NOT NULL,
    page_number     INTEGER NOT NULL,
    text_content    TEXT,
    char_count      INTEGER NOT NULL,
    likely_scanned  BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    quality_score   NUMERIC(5,4),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (job_id, page_number)
);

CREATE INDEX idx_pdf_page_text_job_scanned
ON pdf_page_text (job_id, likely_scanned);
```

### 20.3 Metadata

```sql id="9i9b65"
CREATE TABLE pdf_document_metadata (
    job_id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    title               TEXT,
    author              TEXT,
    subject             TEXT,
    creator             TEXT,
    producer            TEXT,
    creation_date        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    modification_date    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    pdf_version          TEXT,
    metadata_json        JSONB
);
```

## 22. Search Indexing Pattern

Jika extracted text dipakai untuk search, jangan langsung query full text dari table utama kalau kebutuhan search kompleks.

```text id="klhm52"
PDF extraction
   │
   ├─ store original file metadata
   ├─ store page-level text
   ├─ publish DocumentTextExtracted event
   │
   ▼
Search indexer
   ├─ normalize text
   ├─ redact if needed
   ├─ index by tenant/document id
   └─ update search status
```

Event contoh:

```json id="d2x230"
{
  "eventId": "evt_001",
  "eventType": "DocumentTextExtracted",
  "eventVersion": 1,
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-13T14:30:00Z",
  "tenantId": "tenant_001",
  "payload": {
    "fileId": "file_001",
    "extractionJobId": "pdf_job_001",
    "pageCount": 12,
    "textLayerDetected": true,
    "extractedCharCount": 54321
  }
}
```

Jangan return stack trace parser ke client.

---

## 24. Security Concern Khusus PDF

| Risiko | Contoh | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| Malicious PDF | Exploit parser/viewer | AV scan, sandboxed worker, patch dependencies |
| Encrypted PDF | Tidak bisa inspect / permission ambiguity | Reject/password flow |
| Embedded files | PDF sebagai container file lain | Reject/extract only with strict policy |
| JavaScript/action | Potential malicious behavior in viewer | Flag/reject for high-risk workflows |
| Huge page count | CPU/memory exhaustion | Max page limit |
| Huge text extraction | DB/log/storage bloat | Max chars limit |
| Scanned image huge | OCR cost explosion | OCR budget |
| Raw text PII | Data leakage | Redaction/access control |
| Unauthorized download | Sensitive document leak | Domain authorization |
| Metadata spoofing | Fake author/date | Treat as untrusted metadata |

---

## 25. PDF Extraction Quality Caveats

PDF text extraction sering tidak sempurna.

| PDF characteristic | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Multi-column layout | Text order bisa campur |
| Tables | Struktur table bisa hilang |
| Header/footer | Ikut masuk text |
| Hyphenation | Kata bisa terpecah |
| Ligatures/font encoding | Karakter bisa salah |
| Scanned image | Tidak ada text |
| OCR text layer buruk | Text ada tapi banyak noise |
| Rotated text | Bisa hilang/salah urut |
| Watermark | Bisa ikut extraction |
| Form fields | Mungkin perlu extraction terpisah |

Jangan menganggap extracted text sebagai representasi legal sempurna dari PDF. Untuk regulatory/legal evidence, simpan original PDF dan checksum; extracted text adalah derivative artifact.

---

## 26. Tika vs PDFBox Decision Matrix

| Requirement | Pilihan |
|---|---|
| Extract PDF text per page | PDFBox |
| Extract PDF metadata directly | PDFBox |
| Extract AcroForm fields | PDFBox |
| Render PDF page to image | PDFBox |
| Unified extraction for many formats | Tika |
| Simple indexing pipeline | Tika |
| Need embedded document handling | Tika with explicit embedded policy |
| Need precise page-level control | PDFBox |
| Need OCR route | Tika/OCR integration or custom OCR worker |
| Need document type detection | Tika |

PDFBox dan Tika bukan saling menggantikan sepenuhnya. Tika sering menjadi façade extraction lintas format, sementara PDFBox memberi kontrol PDF-level.

---

### Unit test examples

```java id="jszoe2"
@Test
void shouldExtractTextFromTextBasedPdf() {
    List<PdfPageText> pages = extractor.extractByPage(
            Path.of("fixtures/simple-text.pdf"),
            PdfExtractionPolicy.defaultPolicy()
    );

    assertFalse(pages.isEmpty());
    assertTrue(pages.get(0).charCount() > 0);
}
```

```java id="zlz04d"
@Test
void shouldClassifyBlankTextPagesAsLikelyScanned() {
    PdfPageText page = new PdfPageText(
            1,
            "",
            0,
            true,
            0.0
    );

    assertTrue(new PdfScanHeuristic().pageNeedsOcr(page));
}
```

```java id="y9oxxn"
@Test
void shouldRejectTooManyPages() {
    PdfExtractionPolicy policy = new PdfExtractionPolicy(
            50L * 1024 * 1024,
            1,
            5_000_000,
            100_000,
            60,
            false,
            true,
            false,
            true
    );

    assertThrows(PdfRejectedException.class, () ->
            extractor.extractByPage(Path.of("fixtures/multi-page.pdf"), policy)
    );
}
```

Metrics:

| Metric | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `pdf_extraction_started_total` | Counter | Jumlah job extraction |
| `pdf_extraction_completed_total{status}` | Counter | Completion by status |
| `pdf_extraction_duration_seconds` | Histogram | Lama extraction |
| `pdf_extraction_pages_total` | Histogram | Page count |
| `pdf_extraction_chars_total` | Histogram | Extracted chars |
| `pdf_extraction_failed_total{reason}` | Counter | Failure reason |
| `pdf_extraction_ocr_required_total` | Counter | Scanned/no text layer |
| `pdf_extraction_encrypted_rejected_total` | Counter | Encrypted PDF rejected |
| `pdf_extraction_parser_error_total` | Counter | Parser failures |

Structured log:

{

Jangan log extracted text secara raw. Text PDF bisa berisi PII, credential, legal content, account number, dan regulated data.

## 29. Production Checklist

```text id="1l2d60"
Intake
[ ] Extension allowlist: pdf
[ ] MIME detection: application/pdf
[ ] Max file size
[ ] Checksum SHA-256
[ ] AV scan/quarantine policy
[ ] Original file retained if audit required

PDF inspection
[ ] PDF can be opened
[ ] Encrypted policy checked
[ ] Page count extracted
[ ] Max page limit enforced
[ ] Metadata extracted
[ ] Embedded file policy checked if needed
[ ] Form extraction policy checked

Text extraction
[ ] Page-level extraction
[ ] Max extracted chars
[ ] Text normalization
[ ] Scanned/no-text heuristic
[ ] OCR routing decision
[ ] Parser failure handled

Runtime
[ ] Extraction runs in worker, not API request
[ ] Worker CPU/memory limits
[ ] Timeout/budget
[ ] Retry cap
[ ] Dependency versions patched
[ ] Temporary files cleaned

Persistence
[ ] Job status table
[ ] Page text table or object storage
[ ] Metadata table
[ ] Original file checksum
[ ] Parser version recorded if regulatory

Security
[ ] Raw PDF/text not logged
[ ] Download requires authorization
[ ] Cache-Control no-store for sensitive output
[ ] PII handling policy
[ ] Quarantine storage isolated

Observability
[ ] Duration metric
[ ] Page count metric
[ ] OCR required metric
[ ] Failure reason metric
[ ] Structured log
[ ] Correlation id
```

---

## 30. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Menganggap semua PDF punya text | Scanned PDF menghasilkan text kosong |
| Extract seluruh PDF di API request | Timeout dan latency spike |
| Tidak batasi page count | Resource exhaustion |
| Tidak batasi extracted chars | DB/storage/log bloat |
| Tidak handle encrypted PDF | Parser error tidak jelas |
| Tidak simpan original file | Audit dan reprocess sulit |
| Log extracted text | PII/legal data leak |
| Pakai metadata PDF sebagai fakta | Metadata bisa palsu/kosong |
| Menganggap text order pasti benar | Multi-column/table layout bisa kacau |
| Tidak punya OCR route | Scanned PDF gagal diproses |
| OCR synchronous | CPU spike dan HTTP timeout |
| Tidak patch PDFBox/Tika | Parser attack surface |
| Tidak test corrupt/malicious PDFs | Production surprise |

---

## 31. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| PDF extraction result is derivative, original PDF remains source of truth | Simpan original + checksum |
| Text-based PDF and scanned PDF are different processing paths | Jangan paksa satu parser |
| PDF extraction belongs in worker, not request path | Stabilitas API |
| Page-level extraction is better for audit/debugging | Traceability |
| PDF metadata is untrusted | Jangan jadikan fakta domain tunggal |
| OCR must be explicitly budgeted | OCR mahal dan noisy |
| Extracted text must be treated as sensitive | Jangan log/ekspos sembarangan |
| Parser limits are mandatory | Page/size/char/time limits |
| Encrypted PDF requires explicit policy | Legal/security ambiguity |
| PDFBox and Tika serve different abstraction levels | Pilih sesuai kebutuhan |

---

## 32. Kesimpulan Seri 9

PDF extraction yang production-ready bukan hanya:

```text id="96p0s8"
PDFTextStripper.getText(document)
```

Pola sehat:

```text id="g0y7lg"
PDF upload
  -> intake/security
  -> stage original
  -> create extraction job
  -> PDF worker
  -> inspect encryption/page count/metadata
  -> extract text per page
  -> detect scanned/no-text pages
  -> optionally route OCR
  -> persist result
  -> audit + metrics
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="2ya8o7"
Never assume a PDF has extractable text.
Never run heavy PDF/OCR work in the API request path.
Never trust PDF metadata as domain truth.
Never log raw extracted text.
Never discard original PDF if audit/reprocessing matters.
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:38:26 PM

Lanjut **Seri 10 dari 20**. Sekarang kita masuk ke PDF generation: membuat dokumen final seperti report, invoice, certificate, surat, atau regulatory evidence dengan layout, font, pagination, metadata, dan download contract yang benar.

# Seri 10/20 - PDF Generation: Document Layout, Report, Invoice, Font Embedding, Pagination, Digital Signature, PDF/A Concern & Streaming Download

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **PDF generation** dalam Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice:

```text id="3xhx84"
Business Data / Domain Result
        │
        ▼
PDF Generation Pipeline
        │
        ├─ choose generation strategy
        ├─ prepare document model
        ├─ layout pages
        ├─ embed fonts
        ├─ add images/barcodes/tables
        ├─ paginate
        ├─ add metadata
        ├─ optionally sign / PDF-A validate
        └─ stream or store generated PDF
```

PDF generation berbeda dari CSV/Excel export. PDF biasanya adalah **final-form document**: tampilannya dianggap representasi resmi, sulit diedit, sering dipakai untuk invoice, agreement, approval letter, certificate, case document, audit evidence, atau regulatory report.

Apache PDFBox adalah open-source Java library untuk membuat, memanipulasi, dan mengekstrak konten PDF; situs resminya juga mencatat kemampuan seperti PDF creation, manipulation, extraction, digital signing, dan PDF/A-1b validation. Per Juli 2026, situs PDFBox menampilkan rilis PDFBox 3.0.7 pada 9 Maret 2026 dan 2.0.36 pada 15 Maret 2026, jadi untuk project baru Java 17+ sebaiknya evaluasi PDFBox 3.x kecuali dependency internal masih menahan di 2.x. ([pdfbox.apache.org](https://pdfbox.apache.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="03juqg"
PDF Generation
│
├─ 1. Document Use Case
│   ├─ report
│   ├─ invoice
│   ├─ certificate
│   ├─ legal letter
│   ├─ regulatory evidence
│   └─ generated attachment
│
├─ 2. Generation Strategy
│   ├─ programmatic PDF
│   ├─ HTML → PDF
│   ├─ DOCX/template → PDF
│   ├─ existing PDF stamping
│   └─ generated PDF + signature
│
├─ 3. Layout Model
│   ├─ page size
│   ├─ margin
│   ├─ header/footer
│   ├─ table
│   ├─ paragraph
│   ├─ image/logo
│   ├─ page break
│   └─ pagination
│
├─ 4. Typography
│   ├─ font embedding
│   ├─ Unicode support
│   ├─ fallback font
│   ├─ line wrapping
│   ├─ text measurement
│   └─ right-to-left / CJK if needed
│
├─ 5. Compliance
│   ├─ metadata
│   ├─ checksum
│   ├─ PDF/A
│   ├─ digital signature
│   ├─ timestamping
│   └─ audit trail
│
├─ 6. Runtime
│   ├─ direct streaming
│   ├─ async generation
│   ├─ object storage
│   ├─ worker isolation
│   ├─ memory limit
│   └─ rendering verification
│
└─ 7. Security
    ├─ authorization
    ├─ PII handling
    ├─ no-store download
    ├─ template injection control
    ├─ no untrusted file path
    └─ signed URL / proxy download
```

---

## 3. Pilihan Library / Strategy

| Strategy | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko / catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Apache PDFBox** | Programmatic PDF, stamping existing PDF, metadata, signing, low-level control | Open-source Apache License, kuat untuk kontrol PDF-level | Layout high-level seperti table/pagination harus banyak dibangun sendiri |
| **iText Core** | Enterprise-grade PDF generation, PDF/A, PDF/UA, signatures, complex document workflows | Fitur komersial kuat dan lengkap | iText memakai dual-license commercial dan AGPL; harus dicek legal sebelum dipakai di produk tertutup. ([iTextpdf](https://itextpdf.com/how-buy?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| **OpenPDF** | Alternative open-source PDF generation/editing/encryption | LGPL/MPL, successor-style dari iText lama, dapat membuat/mengedit/render/encrypt PDF dan HTML-to-PDF | Evaluasi maturity, feature fit, dan compliance internal. ([GitHub](https://github.com/librepdf/openpdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| **HTML → PDF** | Layout report yang mirip web/template | Designer-friendly, CSS-based | Rendering engine harus stabil; CSS support tidak selalu sama dengan browser |
| **DOCX → PDF** | Surat, memo, kontrak, dokumen bisnis panjang | Template mudah dikelola user bisnis | Conversion fidelity harus diuji |
| **Existing PDF stamping** | Form/surat/template fixed layout | Cocok untuk menaruh text/signature/QR di koordinat tertentu | Coordinate system rawan salah; perlu visual regression |

Rekomendasi praktis untuk stack Java/Jersey:

```text id="plpwc1"
Simple generated PDF / stamping       -> PDFBox
Complex enterprise PDF/PDF-A/signing  -> evaluate iText with license review
Open-source high-level generation     -> evaluate OpenPDF carefully
Business letter template              -> DOCX template -> PDF conversion
Web-like report                       -> HTML template -> PDF renderer
Fixed official form                   -> existing PDF stamping
```

---

## 4. PDF Generation Mode

| Mode | Alur | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| Direct streaming | Request → generate PDF → response | PDF kecil/cepat |
| Generate to temp file | Request/job → temp PDF → stream | Perlu verify/size/checksum sebelum response |
| Async generation | POST job → worker generate → object storage → download | PDF berat, report banyak halaman, signature/PDF-A |
| Pre-generated document | Domain event → generate PDF → attach to case/order | Workflow-driven document |
| Template stamping | Load template PDF → stamp fields/QR/signature → save | Official form/certificate |

Untuk dokumen yang menjadi evidence/audit, lebih baik **generate ke storage dulu**, hitung checksum, simpan metadata, baru expose download endpoint. Direct streaming cocok untuk report ringan yang bisa diregenerasi.

---

## 5. Maven Dependency

Untuk PDFBox 3.x:

```xml id="j4i5o5"
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.apache.pdfbox</groupId>
    <artifactId>pdfbox</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Untuk signing dengan CMS/PKCS#7 biasanya Anda akan membutuhkan library crypto seperti Bouncy Castle, tergantung implementasi signing internal. PDFBox menyediakan model signature PDF, tetapi integrasi certificate chain, TSA, HSM/KMS, dan trust validation tetap perlu desain security tersendiri. PDFBox `PDSignature` merepresentasikan digital signature yang dapat dilampirkan ke dokumen. ([javadoc.io](https://javadoc.io/doc/org.apache.pdfbox/pdfbox/3.0.5/org/apache/pdfbox/pdmodel/interactive/digitalsignature/PDSignature.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. PDF Generation Contract

Sebelum code, definisikan contract:

```text id="2dncr8"
PDF Document Contract
│
├─ document type: INVOICE / CASE_SUMMARY / APPROVAL_LETTER
├─ version: v1
├─ page size: A4 / Letter
├─ orientation: portrait / landscape
├─ language / locale
├─ timezone
├─ font policy
├─ header/footer
├─ page numbering
├─ metadata fields
├─ retention policy
├─ signature required?
├─ PDF/A required?
├─ watermark required?
└─ download authorization scope
```

PDF generation tidak boleh bergantung pada “kebetulan layout kelihatan bagus di sample”. Harus ada contract supaya perubahan template, font, page break, dan metadata bisa dikontrol.

---

import java.math.BigDecimal;

Jangan generate PDF langsung dari entity database. Gunakan **PDF document model** yang stabil.

## 8. Basic PDFBox PDF Generation

Contoh minimal membuat PDF dengan text:

```java id="bsp78f"
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDDocument;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDPage;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDPageContentStream;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.common.PDRectangle;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.font.Standard14Fonts;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.font.PDType1Font;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.OutputStream;

public class SimplePdfWriter {

    public void write(OutputStream output) {
        try (PDDocument document = new PDDocument()) {
            PDPage page = new PDPage(PDRectangle.A4);
            document.addPage(page);

            try (PDPageContentStream content = new PDPageContentStream(document, page)) {
                PDType1Font font = new PDType1Font(Standard14Fonts.FontName.HELVETICA_BOLD);

                content.beginText();
                content.setFont(font, 16);
                content.newLineAtOffset(50, 780);
                content.showText("Invoice");
                content.endText();
            }

            document.save(output);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new PdfGenerationException("Failed to generate PDF", e);
        }
    }
}
```

PDFBox memberi kontrol rendah atas PDF content stream. Ini fleksibel, tetapi Anda harus mengelola sendiri coordinate, line wrapping, page break, table rendering, dan font handling.

---

## 9. Coordinate System PDF

PDF menggunakan coordinate system berbasis point.

```text id="kcy7sa"
A4 portrait:
width  ≈ 595 pt
height ≈ 842 pt

Origin:
(0, 0) ada di kiri bawah halaman

Y naik ke atas.
```

Contoh:

```text id="dnq1gy"
(50, 780)  -> dekat kiri atas
(50, 50)   -> dekat kiri bawah
```

Kesalahan umum: developer berpikir origin ada di kiri atas seperti HTML/canvas biasa. Di PDF, default origin ada di bawah kiri.

---

## 10. Font Embedding

Untuk production PDF, terutama yang mengandung Unicode, bahasa Indonesia dengan karakter khusus, simbol mata uang, atau kemungkinan nama orang multi-bahasa, gunakan embedded TrueType/OpenType font.

PDFBox `PDType0Font.load(...)` dapat memuat TTF untuk di-embed ke document sebagai Type 0 font, dan parameter `embedSubset` dapat digunakan untuk subset embedding. ([pdfbox.apache.org](https://pdfbox.apache.org/docs/2.0.9/javadocs/org/apache/pdfbox/pdmodel/font/PDType0Font.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="3il9fz"
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDDocument;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.font.PDType0Font;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;

public class PdfFontRegistry {

    private final PDDocument document;

    public PdfFontRegistry(PDDocument document) {
        this.document = document;
    }

    public PDType0Font loadFont(String classpathLocation) {
        try (InputStream input = getClass().getResourceAsStream(classpathLocation)) {
            if (input == null) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Font not found: " + classpathLocation);
            }

            return PDType0Font.load(document, input, true);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new PdfGenerationException("Failed to load PDF font", e);
        }
    }
}
```

### Font policy

| Concern | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Unicode support | Use embedded Type0 font |
| Corporate font | Pastikan lisensi font mengizinkan embedding |
| PDF/A | Font embedding biasanya wajib |
| File size | Gunakan subset embedding |
| Missing glyph | Test dengan fixture multi-locale |
| Font files | Jangan expose/share font file ke user |

Catatan penting: font adalah asset berlisensi. Jangan menganggap semua corporate font boleh di-embed ke PDF.

---

## 11. Text Wrapping

PDFBox tidak otomatis wrap text seperti HTML. Anda perlu mengukur text width.

```java id="65esgu"
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.font.PDFont;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class PdfTextWrapper {

    public List<String> wrap(
            String text,
            PDFont font,
            float fontSize,
            float maxWidth
    ) {
        List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>();

        if (text == null || text.isBlank()) {
            return lines;
        }

        StringBuilder current = new StringBuilder();

        for (String word : text.split("\\s+")) {
            String candidate = current.isEmpty()
                    ? word
                    : current + " " + word;

            if (textWidth(candidate, font, fontSize) <= maxWidth) {
                current.setLength(0);
                current.append(candidate);
            } else {
                if (!current.isEmpty()) {
                    lines.add(current.toString());
                }
                current.setLength(0);
                current.append(word);
            }
        }

        if (!current.isEmpty()) {
            lines.add(current.toString());
        }

        return lines;
    }

    private float textWidth(String text, PDFont font, float fontSize) {
        try {
            return font.getStringWidth(text) / 1000f * fontSize;
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new PdfGenerationException("Failed to measure text", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Masalah nyata yang harus diuji:

| Edge case | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Long word tanpa spasi | Overflow |
| Nama panjang | Overlap |
| Unicode/CJK | Width/glyph issue |
| Newline manual | Layout pecah |
| RTL text | Butuh shaping/bidi support |
| Emoji/symbol | Missing glyph |

---

## 12. Layout Engine Sederhana

Buat abstraction sendiri agar content stream tidak tersebar di banyak service.

```java id="ckz1jf"
public record PdfPageLayout(
        float pageWidth,
        float pageHeight,
        float marginLeft,
        float marginRight,
        float marginTop,
        float marginBottom
) {
    public float contentWidth() {
        return pageWidth - marginLeft - marginRight;
    }

    public float startY() {
        return pageHeight - marginTop;
    }

    public float bottomY() {
        return marginBottom;
    }
}
```

```java id="ktkqlr"
public class PdfCursor {

    private float x;
    private float y;

    public PdfCursor(float x, float y) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
    }

    public float x() {
        return x;
    }

    public float y() {
        return y;
    }

    public void moveDown(float amount) {
        this.y -= amount;
    }

    public void moveTo(float x, float y) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
    }
}
```

Dengan cursor seperti ini, Anda bisa mengelola line break dan page break lebih eksplisit.

---

## 13. Pagination Pattern

```text id="z0bgz9"
Before writing block:
    estimate block height

If cursorY - blockHeight < bottomMargin:
    close current page content stream
    create new page
    draw header/footer
    reset cursor

Write block
Move cursor down
```

Contoh helper:

```java id="8qrz0k"
public class PdfPaginator {

    private final PdfPageLayout layout;

    public PdfPaginator(PdfPageLayout layout) {
        this.layout = layout;
    }

    public boolean needsNewPage(PdfCursor cursor, float requiredHeight) {
        return cursor.y() - requiredHeight < layout.bottomY();
    }
}
```

Senior-level rule:

```text id="b5kv1b"
Never write content before checking whether it fits.
Never assume table row height is constant if text can wrap.
Never add footer/page number only at the end unless page count is known.
```

---

## 14. Table Rendering

PDFBox tidak punya high-level table layout seperti spreadsheet. Anda perlu membuat renderer atau memakai library/strategy lain.

### Table model

```java id="2rufkb"
import java.util.List;

public record PdfTable(
        List<String> headers,
        List<List<String>> rows,
        List<Float> columnWidths
) {}
```

### Table renderer concept

```java id="3mr72j"
public class PdfTableRenderer {

    private static final float ROW_PADDING = 4f;
    private static final float LINE_HEIGHT = 12f;

    public float estimateRowHeight(List<String> cells) {
        // Simplified. Real implementation should wrap by column width.
        return LINE_HEIGHT + (ROW_PADDING * 2);
    }
}
```

Untuk invoice/report production, jangan reimplement full table engine secara buru-buru jika tabel kompleks. Pertimbangkan HTML-to-PDF atau iText/OpenPDF jika requirement table/layout berat.

---

## 15. Header, Footer, Page Number

```java id="oh9eh5"
public record PdfHeaderFooter(
        String documentTitle,
        String tenantName,
        String generatedAtText
) {}
```

Pattern:

```text id="1o4lm2"
For each page:
- draw header
- draw footer
- draw page number
- draw confidentiality label if required
```

Page total problem:

| Requirement | Strategy |
|---|---|
| “Page 1” only | Easy during page generation |
| “Page 1 of N” | Need second pass/stamping after all pages known |
| Dynamic TOC | More complex; use DOCX/template pipeline or layout engine |

Untuk “Page X of Y”, Anda bisa generate PDF dulu, hitung page count, lalu reopen/stamp footer. Atau simpan page count ketika document object selesai sebelum save, tergantung arsitektur renderer.

---

## 16. Images, Logo, QR Code, Barcode

### Logo/image

```java id="tbaq8d"
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.graphics.image.PDImageXObject;

PDImageXObject logo = PDImageXObject.createFromFileByContent(logoFile, document);

content.drawImage(logo, 50, 760, 100, 40);
```

### QR code pattern

```text id="vxhrip"
domain verification URL / document id
        │
        ▼
QR generation library
        │
        ▼
PNG/image bytes
        │
        ▼
PDImageXObject
        │
        ▼
drawImage into PDF
```

QR content jangan berisi secrets. Gunakan document verification URL atau opaque token yang bisa divalidasi server-side.

---

## 17. Metadata & Document Information

PDF metadata berguna untuk audit dan search, tetapi jangan masukkan data sensitif berlebihan.

```java id="g9y0ms"
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDDocument;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDDocumentInformation;

public class PdfMetadataWriter {

    public void apply(PDDocument document, InvoicePdfModel model) {
        PDDocumentInformation info = new PDDocumentInformation();

        info.setTitle("Invoice " + model.invoiceNumber());
        info.setSubject("Generated invoice");
        info.setAuthor(model.sellerName());
        info.setCreator("billing-service");
        info.setProducer("company-pdf-generator");

        document.setDocumentInformation(info);
    }
}
```

### Metadata policy

| Field | Isi |
|---|---|
| Title | Human-readable document title |
| Subject | Business purpose |
| Author | Organization/service, bukan user private jika tidak perlu |
| Creator | Service name/version |
| Producer | Generator library/service |
| Keywords | Hati-hati PII |
| Custom metadata | Simpan di DB lebih aman |

Audit metadata utama sebaiknya tetap di PostgreSQL, bukan hanya PDF metadata.

---

## 18. Watermark

Use case:

| Watermark | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Draft | `DRAFT` |
| Confidential | `CONFIDENTIAL` |
| Duplicate copy | `COPY` |
| Canceled | `CANCELED` |
| Environment | `NON-PRODUCTION` |

Watermark bisa diterapkan saat generation atau stamping pass. Jangan mengandalkan watermark sebagai security control; itu hanya visual indicator.

---

## 19. PDF/A Concern

PDF/A adalah subset PDF untuk long-term archiving. Secara konsep, PDF/A bertujuan menjaga visual appearance dokumen dalam jangka panjang secara independen dari tools/systems yang membuat atau menyimpan file. ([arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/0906.0867?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

PDFBox menyebut kemampuan validasi PDF/A-1b; iText juga memosisikan produknya untuk PDF/A, PDF/UA, dan PDF 2.0 workflows. ([pdfbox.apache.org](https://pdfbox.apache.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### PDF/A biasanya mensyaratkan perhatian pada:

| Concern | Implikasi |
|---|---|
| Font embedding | Font harus tersedia di dokumen |
| Color profile | Output intent/ICC profile |
| Metadata | XMP metadata |
| No encryption | PDF/A tidak mengizinkan encryption untuk arsip |
| No external dependency | Dokumen harus self-contained |
| Validation | Harus divalidasi, bukan diasumsikan |
| Version target | PDF/A-1, PDF/A-2, PDF/A-3 punya aturan berbeda |

### Practical rule

```text id="rmyhcu"
If PDF/A is a compliance requirement:
    use a library/workflow that explicitly supports PDF/A creation
    validate generated PDF/A with a validator
    store validation result with generated document metadata
```

Jangan klaim “PDF/A compliant” hanya karena extension file `.pdf`.

---

## 20. Digital Signature

Digital signature PDF bertujuan memberikan:

| Property | Makna |
|---|---|
| Integrity | Dokumen tidak berubah setelah ditandatangani |
| Authenticity | Signer dapat diverifikasi |
| Non-repudiation concern | Tergantung legal framework, certificate, process |
| Timestamp | Membuktikan waktu signing via TSA jika digunakan |
| Long-term validation | Butuh chain/OCSP/CRL/TSA policy |

iText memasarkan solusi tanda tangan PDF untuk non-repudiation, integrity, dan authenticity protection, sementara PDFBox menyediakan API signature PDF level. ([iTextpdf](https://itextpdf.com/solutions/electronic-signatures-pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Architecture

```text id="4swmbb"
Generated PDF
   │
   ▼
Canonical saved file
   │
   ▼
Signing service
   │
   ├─ private key / HSM / KMS
   ├─ certificate chain
   ├─ timestamp authority
   ├─ signature appearance
   └─ audit event
   │
   ▼
Signed PDF
   │
   ▼
Store + checksum + signature metadata
```

### Jangan

```text id="dbtja3"
- Simpan private key di source code
- Simpan keystore password di config plain text
- Sign file yang masih bisa berubah setelah signing
- Tambahkan watermark/stamp setelah signing tanpa incremental-signing design
- Menganggap gambar tanda tangan = digital signature
```

Gambar tanda tangan/stamp hanya visual. Digital signature adalah cryptographic signature.

---

## 21. Visual Signature vs Digital Signature

| Jenis | Contoh | Efek |
|---|---|---|
| Visual stamp | Gambar tanda tangan, QR, seal | Hanya tampilan |
| Digital signature invisible | Cryptographic signature tanpa appearance | Integrity/authenticity |
| Digital signature visible | Cryptographic signature + appearance | Integrity/authenticity + tampilan |
| Certification signature | Membatasi perubahan berikutnya | Lebih kompleks |
| Timestamp | Waktu signing dari trusted TSA | LTV/compliance concern |

Untuk regulatory workflow, bedakan jelas antara **approval stamp** dan **digital signature**.

---

## 22. PDF Download Endpoint

### Direct streaming

```java id="9netuq"
@GET
@Path("/invoices/{invoiceId}.pdf")
@Produces("application/pdf")
public Response downloadInvoicePdf(@PathParam("invoiceId") String invoiceId) {
    authorizeInvoiceAccess(invoiceId);

    StreamingOutput stream = output -> {
        GenerateInvoicePdfCommand command = new GenerateInvoicePdfCommand(
                CurrentTenant.required(),
                invoiceId,
                CurrentUser.required(),
                Locale.forLanguageTag("id-ID"),
                "Asia/Jakarta",
                false,
                false,
                Instant.now()
        );

        invoicePdfService.writeInvoice(command, output);
    };

    return Response.ok(stream)
            .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"invoice-" + invoiceId + ".pdf\"")
            .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            .build();
}
```

Untuk dokumen sensitif seperti invoice, case summary, legal notice, dan report customer, gunakan authorization check dan `Cache-Control: no-store`.

---

## 23. Async Generation Endpoint

```text id="k5tjii"
POST /v1/documents
GET  /v1/documents/{documentId}
GET  /v1/documents/{documentId}/content
```

### Request

```json id="72kkug"
{
  "documentType": "INVOICE_PDF",
  "resourceId": "INV-001",
  "options": {
    "requireDigitalSignature": true,
    "requirePdfA": false
  }
}
```

### Response

```json id="8gvkvf"
{
  "documentJobId": "docjob_001",
  "status": "PENDING",
  "links": {
    "status": "/v1/documents/docjob_001",
    "content": "/v1/documents/docjob_001/content"
  }
}
```

Async generation cocok untuk:

| Kondisi | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| PDF > beberapa detik generate | Hindari HTTP timeout |
| Perlu digital signature | Signing bisa lambat/bergantung HSM |
| Perlu PDF/A validation | Ada step validasi |
| Perlu visual QA/render verification | Tidak cocok direct response |
| Dokumen menjadi audit artifact | Harus disimpan dengan checksum |

## 24. Document Job Table

```sql id="jc3g0q"
CREATE TABLE generated_document_job (
    id                  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
    requested_by         TEXT NOT NULL,

    document_type        TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_type        TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_id          TEXT NOT NULL,
    document_version     TEXT NOT NULL,

    status               TEXT NOT NULL,

    file_object_id       UUID,
    sha256_hex           TEXT,
    size_bytes           BIGINT,
    page_count           INTEGER,

    require_signature    BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    signature_status     TEXT,
    signer_subject       TEXT,
    signed_at            TIMESTAMPTZ,

    require_pdfa         BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    pdfa_status          TEXT,
    pdfa_validation_json JSONB,

    failure_code         TEXT,
    failure_message      TEXT,

    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    started_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    expires_at           TIMESTAMPTZ
);
```

Status:

```text id="adjeh3"
PENDING
GENERATING
GENERATED
SIGNING
SIGNED
VALIDATING
READY
FAILED
EXPIRED
```

---

## 25. Storage Strategy

```text id="uv8g1j"
generated-documents/
  tenant/{tenantId}/documents/{yyyy}/{mm}/{documentJobId}/document.pdf
  tenant/{tenantId}/documents/{yyyy}/{mm}/{documentJobId}/document-signed.pdf
  tenant/{tenantId}/documents/{yyyy}/{mm}/{documentJobId}/metadata.json
  tenant/{tenantId}/documents/{yyyy}/{mm}/{documentJobId}/render-preview/page-1.png
```

### Rule

| Artifact | Simpan? |
|---|---|
| Generated PDF unsigned | Ya jika audit butuh pre-sign artifact |
| Signed PDF | Ya |
| PDF/A validation result | Ya jika compliance |
| Render preview | Optional |
| Input data snapshot | Ya untuk reproducibility |
| Template version | Ya |
| Generator service version | Ya |

Untuk dokumen resmi, simpan **document model snapshot** atau setidaknya business data version supaya PDF bisa dijelaskan ulang.

---

## 26. Template Strategy

### 26.1 Programmatic PDF

Cocok untuk:

```text id="p2oioe"
- invoice sederhana
- receipt
- short certificate
- compact report
```

### 26.2 HTML template → PDF

Cocok untuk:

```text id="3r1yv2"
- layout mirip web
- table medium
- branding rich
- designer-friendly template
```

### 26.3 DOCX template → PDF

Cocok untuk:

```text id="k0xyg8"
- surat formal
- legal memo
- kontrak
- dokumen panjang dengan heading/table/list
```

### 26.4 Existing PDF stamping

Cocok untuk:

```text id="oo6m19"
- government/official form
- certificate fixed coordinate
- pre-approved template PDF
```

Rule praktis:

```text id="hrvdyg"
If layout is document-like and editable by business: DOCX template.
If layout is web-like and CSS-driven: HTML template.
If layout is fixed official form: PDF stamping.
If layout is small and fully controlled by code: PDFBox programmatic.
```

---

## 27. Rendering Verification

PDF “berhasil dibuat” belum tentu benar secara visual.

Untuk dokumen penting, lakukan render verification:

```text id="ln61x1"
Generate PDF
   │
   ▼
Render page(s) to image
   │
   ▼
Check:
- no clipped text
- no overlap
- expected page count
- expected header/footer
- barcode/QR visible
- signature area visible
- font rendered correctly
```

Untuk CI/regression, gunakan golden fixture dan render diff. PDF text assertion saja tidak cukup karena layout bisa rusak walau text ada.

---

## 28. Security: Template Injection & Data Leakage

| Risiko | Contoh | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| Template injection | User input masuk HTML/template expression | Escape user data, no arbitrary template execution |
| Path traversal | Logo/template path dari user | Template id allowlist |
| SSRF | HTML-to-PDF renderer fetch external URL | Disable external fetch or allowlist |
| PII leak | Response/cache/log berisi PDF sensitive | Authorization + no-store + no raw content logs |
| Unauthorized document generation | User generate PDF resource orang lain | Domain authorization |
| QR token leak | QR berisi secret/token panjang | Opaque verification id, short-lived token if needed |
| Font license issue | Embed font tanpa hak | Font license review |
| Post-sign mutation | Tambah content setelah signing | Finalize before signing |
| Object storage exposure | Public bucket | Private storage + proxy/signed URL |

## 31. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Menganggap PDFBox punya layout engine otomatis | Table/page break kacau |
| Tidak embed font | Karakter hilang/berubah di viewer lain |
| Tidak test Unicode | Nama/alamat rusak |
| Page break setelah content ditulis | Text terpotong |
| Tidak hitung row height | Table overlap |
| Membuat PDF di API path untuk report berat | Timeout |
| Tidak simpan checksum | Audit/integrity lemah |
| Gambar tanda tangan dianggap digital signature | False assurance |
| Mutasi PDF setelah signing | Signature invalid |
| Klaim PDF/A tanpa validasi | Compliance risk |
| Template path dari request user | Path traversal |
| HTML-to-PDF fetch resource eksternal | SSRF |
| Tidak punya retention policy | Storage bloat |
| Tidak audit download | Data leak sulit diinvestigasi |

---

## 32. Production Checklist

```text id="c5g8qy"
Document contract
[ ] Document type jelas
[ ] Version jelas
[ ] Page size/orientation jelas
[ ] Locale/timezone jelas
[ ] Header/footer policy
[ ] Page numbering policy
[ ] Metadata policy

Generation
[ ] Strategy dipilih: programmatic/HTML/DOCX/stamping

[ ] Text wrapping
[ ] Pagination
[ ] Table layout tested
[ ] Image/QR/barcode tested

Security
[ ] Authorization sebelum generate/download
[ ] PII policy
[ ] Cache-Control no-store
[ ] Template id allowlist
[ ] No user-controlled file path
[ ] No external fetch unless allowlisted
[ ] No raw PDF/content logs

Signature/compliance
[ ] Visual stamp dibedakan dari digital signature
[ ] Private key/HSM/KMS policy
[ ] Certificate chain/TSA policy
[ ] No mutation after signing
[ ] PDF/A generated only through compliant workflow
[ ] PDF/A validation result stored

Runtime
[ ] Direct streaming only for fast PDFs
[ ] Async generation for heavy PDFs
[ ] Object storage for official documents
[ ] Checksum stored
[ ] File size/page count stored
[ ] Worker resource limit
[ ] Temp cleanup

Testing
[ ] Unit tests for layout helpers
[ ] PDF text assertion
[ ] Render regression
[ ] Unicode/font test
[ ] Long content test
[ ] Multi-page test
[ ] Unauthorized access test
```

---

## 33. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| PDF is final-form output | Layout correctness matters as much as data correctness |
| PDF document model must be explicit | Jangan generate langsung dari entity |
| Font embedding is production concern | Viewer/environment tidak boleh mengubah tampilan |
| Pagination must be deterministic | Jangan biarkan content terpotong |
| PDF/A must be validated | Tidak cukup “save as PDF” |
| Digital signature is cryptographic, not visual | Stamp bukan signature |
| Final PDF should not change after signing | Mutation invalidates integrity |
| Official PDFs need checksum and metadata | Audit/reproducibility |
| Heavy PDF generation belongs in worker | API latency tetap aman |
| Sensitive PDF download is data disclosure event | Authorization + audit |

---

## 34. Kesimpulan Seri 10

PDF generation production-ready adalah gabungan dari:

```text id="7hsunq"
document contract
+ generation strategy
+ layout engine
+ font embedding
+ pagination
+ metadata
+ signature/compliance policy
+ storage/checksum
+ secure download
+ visual verification
```

Pola sehat:

```text id="s9ae4u"
Request document
  -> authorize
  -> build document model
  -> generate PDF
  -> verify page count/layout where needed
  -> sign / validate PDF-A if required
  -> store PDF + checksum + metadata
  -> expose authorized download
  -> audit generation and download
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="kwpjnn"
Never treat PDF generation as just writing text to a file.
Never rely on non-embedded fonts for official documents.
Never confuse visual signature with digital signature.
Never mutate a PDF after signing unless your signature workflow supports it.
Never claim PDF/A or compliance without validation evidence.
```

# Seri 11/20 - Word DOC/DOCX Processing: Template, Placeholder Replacement, Table Generation, Image/Signature Insertion, DOCX-to-PDF Strategy & Style Preservation

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **Word document processing** dalam Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice:

```text id="7ccuba"
Business Data / Case Data / Workflow Data
        │
        ▼
DOCX Processing Pipeline
        │
        ├─ load template DOCX
        ├─ validate template version
        ├─ replace placeholders
        ├─ generate paragraphs/tables
        ├─ insert image / QR / signature stamp
        ├─ preserve styles
        ├─ save DOCX
        ├─ optionally convert to PDF
        └─ store + audit + expose download
```

DOCX biasanya dipakai untuk **dokumen bisnis yang masih perlu layout editable**: surat, memo, contract draft, approval letter, notice, case summary, template regulatory response, dan dokumen yang perlu direview manusia sebelum difinalisasi ke PDF.

Untuk Java, pilihan umum adalah **Apache POI XWPF** dan **docx4j**. Apache POI XWPF adalah API untuk membaca/menulis `.docx`; dokumentasi POI menyatakan XWPF memiliki core API yang cukup stabil untuk akses bagian utama Word `.docx`, tetapi belum lengkap untuk seluruh fitur format Word. `XWPFDocument` sendiri dideskripsikan sebagai high-level class untuk bekerja dengan `.docx`, tetapi beberapa kompleksitas OOXML masih bisa muncul. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/components/document/quick-guide-xwpf.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

│
├─ 1. Document Use Case
│   ├─ editable business document

├─ 2. File Format
