## 3. DOC vs DOCX vs DOCM

| Format | Karakteristik | Java handling | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|---|---|
| `.docx` | Modern WordprocessingML / Office Open XML, ZIP-based | Apache POI XWPF, docx4j | Default untuk project modern |
| `.doc` | Legacy binary Word | Apache POI HWPF, terbatas | Support hanya jika legacy wajib |
| `.docm` | Macro-enabled DOCX | Bisa mengandung macro | Biasanya reject untuk upload user biasa |
| `.dotx` | Word template tanpa macro | Bisa jadi source template | Boleh untuk internal controlled template |
| `.dotm` | Template macro-enabled | Macro risk | Hindari kecuali controlled internal workflow |

Apache POI memisahkan dukungan Word lama dan baru: HWPF untuk `.doc` berada di `poi-scratchpad`, sedangkan XWPF untuk `.docx` berada di `poi-ooxml`. ([poi.apache.org](https://poi.apache.org/components/document/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Rekomendasi praktis: **gunakan `.docx` sebagai format utama**. Reject `.docm`/`.dotm` kecuali ada requirement internal yang sangat jelas dan security review.

---

## 4. Library Decision Matrix

| Library / strategy | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Batasan |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Apache POI XWPF** | Template sederhana, paragraph/table/image manipulation | Sudah umum, cukup untuk DOCX dasar | Tidak semua fitur Word high-level tersedia; placeholder bisa terpecah antar run |
| **docx4j** | Need control WordprocessingML, complex document manipulation | Open-source ASLv2; memakai JAXB object model; lebih dekat ke struktur OOXML | Perlu memahami Open XML/JAXB; learning curve lebih tinggi |
| **Template engine + DOCX unzip XML manipulation** | Tidak direkomendasikan kecuali controlled | Bisa sangat spesifik | Rawan merusak DOCX |
| **DOCX → PDF via LibreOffice/headless service** | Conversion fidelity lebih mirip Word/Office | Praktis untuk template bisnis | Perlu service/runtime terpisah, font/install management |
| **DOCX → PDF via library** | Embedded conversion | Lebih mudah deploy dalam Java | Fidelity harus diuji; fitur Word kompleks bisa tidak sama |

docx4j mendeskripsikan dirinya sebagai open-source Java library untuk membuat dan memanipulasi Microsoft Open XML Word `.docx`, PowerPoint `.pptx`, dan Excel `.xlsx`; ia memakai JAXB untuk representasi object di memory dan menekankan fleksibilitas tinggi jika Anda memahami struktur Open XML. ([docx4java.org](https://www.docx4java.org/trac/docx4j?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

Untuk `.doc` legacy:

Gunakan versi dari dependency management/BOM project. DOCX adalah ZIP-based OOXML, jadi POI tetap perlu diperlakukan sebagai library security-sensitive.

## 6. Word Document Use Case

| Use case | Output utama | Strategi |
|---|---|---|
| Surat formal | DOCX + PDF final | DOCX template → fill → review → PDF |
| Contract draft | DOCX editable | DOCX template dengan placeholder/clauses |
| Case summary | DOCX/PDF | Generate table + narrative |
| Approval memo | DOCX/PDF | Template + signature block |
| Regulatory response | DOCX final draft + PDF archive | Template versioning + audit |
| Error report human-readable | DOCX jarang, lebih umum Excel/PDF | Gunakan hanya jika narrative-heavy |
| Certificate/notice | PDF biasanya final | DOCX template jika business user maintain layout |

Rule praktis:

```text id="b5dn9c"
If user needs to edit/review text: generate DOCX.
If document is final-form/audit artifact: generate PDF.
If both are needed: generate DOCX first, then final PDF from approved DOCX.
```

---

## 7. Template Contract

Sebelum membuat generator, definisikan template contract.

```text id="h27mq2"
Template: customer-notice-v1.docx
│
├─ documentType: CUSTOMER_NOTICE
├─ templateVersion: v1
├─ placeholders:
│   ├─ {{customer.name}}
│   ├─ {{customer.id}}
│   ├─ {{notice.date}}
│   ├─ {{case.number}}
│   └─ {{generated.by}}
├─ repeating sections:
│   └─ {{#violations}} ... {{/violations}}
├─ tables:
│   └─ violation table row template
├─ image placeholders:
│   ├─ {{company.logo}}
│   └─ {{signature.image}}
├─ conditional sections:
│   ├─ {{#hasPenalty}}
│   └─ {{#hasAppealRight}}
└─ output:
    ├─ DOCX
    └─ PDF optional
```

Template harus punya version. Jangan biarkan file template diganti manual tanpa versioning karena output legal/regulatory bisa berubah diam-diam.

---

## 8. Placeholder Design

### Good placeholder

```text id="f79lnz"
{{customer.name}}
{{customer.id}}
{{case.number}}
{{notice.date}}
{{generated.at}}
{{officer.displayName}}
```

### Bad placeholder

```text id="uo00y0"
{name}
<name>
CUSTOMER_NAME
[customer]
```

Kenapa?

| Rule | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Gunakan delimiter unik seperti `{{...}}` | Mengurangi false replacement |
| Gunakan nama domain path | Lebih jelas dan scalable |
| Hindari placeholder terlalu pendek | Bisa tabrakan dengan text biasa |
| Jangan pakai placeholder dari input user | Template contract harus controlled |
| Dokumentasikan semua placeholder | Agar template bisa divalidasi |

---

## 9. Masalah Besar: Placeholder Bisa Terpecah Antar Run

Di WordprocessingML, paragraph berisi **runs**. Microsoft menjelaskan run adalah container untuk region text dengan property yang sama, dan satu paragraph bisa berisi banyak run. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/office/open-xml/word/working-with-runs?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh visual di Word:

```text id="30ltwv"
{{customer.name}}
```

Bisa tersimpan sebagai:

```text id="rt66bb"
Run 1: "{{customer"
Run 2: "."
Run 3: "name}}"
```

Akibatnya replacement sederhana per run akan gagal.

### Jangan hanya begini

```java id="a170a0"
for (XWPFRun run : paragraph.getRuns()) {
    String text = run.getText(0);
    if (text != null) {
        run.setText(text.replace("{{customer.name}}", "Alice"), 0);
    }
}
```

Ini hanya berhasil kalau placeholder berada dalam satu run.

---

## 10. Apache POI Basic DOCX Generation

```java id="0yzql4"
import org.apache.poi.xwpf.usermodel.*;

import java.io.OutputStream;

public class SimpleDocxWriter {

    public void write(OutputStream output) {
        try (XWPFDocument document = new XWPFDocument()) {
            XWPFParagraph title = document.createParagraph();
            title.setAlignment(ParagraphAlignment.CENTER);

            XWPFRun titleRun = title.createRun();
            titleRun.setBold(true);
            titleRun.setFontSize(16);
            titleRun.setText("Customer Notice");

            XWPFParagraph paragraph = document.createParagraph();
            XWPFRun run = paragraph.createRun();
            run.setText("This document was generated by the case management system.");

            document.write(output);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new DocxGenerationException("Failed to generate DOCX", e);
        }
    }
}
```

POI XWPF cocok untuk pembuatan dokumen sederhana, tetapi untuk layout Word kompleks, lebih aman menggunakan template Word dan mengisi bagian tertentu.

---

## 11. Load Template DOCX

```java id="7b387m"
import org.apache.poi.xwpf.usermodel.XWPFDocument;

import java.io.InputStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;

public class DocxTemplateLoader {

    public XWPFDocument load(Path templatePath) {
        try (InputStream input = Files.newInputStream(templatePath)) {
            return new XWPFDocument(input);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new DocxGenerationException("Failed to load DOCX template", e);
        }
    }
}
```

### Template storage rule

| Concern | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Template path | Server-side allowlist, jangan dari user input |
| Template version | Simpan metadata version |
| Template checksum | Simpan untuk audit |
| Template update | Review + test + release |
| Template access | Read-only for service runtime |
| Template macro | Reject `.dotm`/`.docm` unless controlled |

---

## 12. Simple Placeholder Replacement

Untuk placeholder yang dipastikan berada dalam satu run:

```java id="1l87nv"
import org.apache.poi.xwpf.usermodel.*;

import java.util.Map;

public class SimpleDocxPlaceholderReplacer {

    public void replace(XWPFDocument document, Map<String, String> values) {
        for (XWPFParagraph paragraph : document.getParagraphs()) {
            replaceInParagraph(paragraph, values);
        }

        for (XWPFTable table : document.getTables()) {
            replaceInTable(table, values);
        }
    }

    private void replaceInTable(XWPFTable table, Map<String, String> values) {
        for (XWPFTableRow row : table.getRows()) {
            for (XWPFTableCell cell : row.getTableCells()) {
                for (XWPFParagraph paragraph : cell.getParagraphs()) {
                    replaceInParagraph(paragraph, values);
                }
                for (XWPFTable nestedTable : cell.getTables()) {
                    replaceInTable(nestedTable, values);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private void replaceInParagraph(XWPFParagraph paragraph, Map<String, String> values) {
        for (XWPFRun run : paragraph.getRuns()) {
            String text = run.getText(0);

            if (text == null) {
                continue;
            }

            String replaced = text;

            for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : values.entrySet()) {
                replaced = replaced.replace(entry.getKey(), safeValue(entry.getValue()));
            }

            if (!replaced.equals(text)) {
                run.setText(replaced, 0);
            }
        }
    }

    private String safeValue(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value;
    }
}
```

Ini cukup untuk template yang dibuat disiplin: placeholder diketik sekali tanpa styling berbeda di tengah placeholder. Namun production template sering tidak sebersih itu.

---

## 13. Run-Aware Placeholder Replacement

Untuk placeholder yang bisa terpecah antar run, ada dua pendekatan:

| Pendekatan | Cara kerja | Trade-off |
|---|---|---|
| Merge paragraph text lalu rebuild runs | Mudah mengganti placeholder lintas run | Bisa menghilangkan style granular |
| Detect placeholder span across runs | Lebih preserve style | Implementasi lebih kompleks |
| Gunakan content controls / bookmarks | Lebih robust | Template authoring lebih disiplin |
| Gunakan docx4j/OpenXML-level manipulation | Lebih powerful | Learning curve lebih tinggi |

### Practical enterprise rule

```text id="yr830o"
Untuk dokumen resmi:
- jangan bergantung pada arbitrary placeholder replacement di runs
- gunakan template authoring guideline
- gunakan content controls/bookmarks untuk area dinamis kompleks
- test generated DOCX dari template aktual
```

### Paragraph rebuild simple version

```java id="c3cmux"
import org.apache.poi.xwpf.usermodel.*;

import java.util.Map;

public class ParagraphTextReplacer {

    public void replaceWholeParagraphText(XWPFParagraph paragraph, Map<String, String> values) {
        String originalText = paragraph.getText();

        if (originalText == null || originalText.isBlank()) {
            return;
        }

        String replaced = originalText;

        for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : values.entrySet()) {
            replaced = replaced.replace(entry.getKey(), entry.getValue() == null ? "" : entry.getValue());
        }

        if (replaced.equals(originalText)) {
            return;
        }

        XWPFRun styleSource = paragraph.getRuns().isEmpty()
                ? null
                : paragraph.getRuns().get(0);

        int runCount = paragraph.getRuns().size();
        for (int i = runCount - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            paragraph.removeRun(i);
        }

        XWPFRun newRun = paragraph.createRun();

        if (styleSource != null) {
            copyBasicRunStyle(styleSource, newRun);
        }

        newRun.setText(replaced);
    }

    private void copyBasicRunStyle(XWPFRun source, XWPFRun target) {
        target.setBold(source.isBold());
        target.setItalic(source.isItalic());
        target.setUnderline(source.getUnderline());
        target.setFontFamily(source.getFontFamily());
        if (source.getFontSize() > 0) {
            target.setFontSize(source.getFontSize());
        }
        target.setColor(source.getColor());
    }
}
```

Kelemahan: kalau paragraph berisi text dengan banyak style berbeda, style bisa hilang. Untuk template official, gunakan pattern yang lebih terkontrol.

---

## 14. Template Authoring Guidelines

Agar placeholder replacement stabil, minta template author mengikuti aturan ini:

```text id="t0ovxv"
[ ] Placeholder diketik utuh dalam satu langkah
[ ] Jangan ubah style sebagian placeholder
[ ] Jangan letakkan placeholder di textbox/shape kecuali library support diuji
[ ] Jangan pakai track changes di template final
[ ] Jangan pakai comments sebagai placeholder
[ ] Jangan gunakan macro
[ ] Gunakan table row khusus untuk repeating data
[ ] Simpan template sebagai .docx
[ ] Jalankan automated template validation sebelum release
```

Template Word yang kelihatan sederhana bisa punya struktur internal rumit: runs, bookmarks, fields, numbering, comments, revisions, headers, footers, footnotes, text boxes, dan embedded objects.

---

## 15. Table Generation

### 15.1 Create table from scratch

```java id="350io1"
import org.apache.poi.xwpf.usermodel.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;

public class InvoiceDocxTableWriter {

    public void writeInvoiceLines(XWPFDocument document, List<InvoiceLinePdfModel> lines) {
        XWPFTable table = document.createTable();

        XWPFTableRow header = table.getRow(0);
        header.getCell(0).setText("Description");
        header.addNewTableCell().setText("Qty");
        header.addNewTableCell().setText("Unit Price");
        header.addNewTableCell().setText("Total");

        for (InvoiceLinePdfModel line : lines) {
            XWPFTableRow row = table.createRow();
            row.getCell(0).setText(nullToBlank(line.description()));
            row.getCell(1).setText(String.valueOf(line.quantity()));
            row.getCell(2).setText(formatMoney(line.unitPrice()));
            row.getCell(3).setText(formatMoney(line.lineTotal()));
        }
    }

    private String formatMoney(BigDecimal value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.toPlainString();
    }

    private String nullToBlank(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value;
    }
}
```

### 15.2 Table from template row

Lebih baik untuk style preservation: buat satu row template di Word lalu clone/isi row.

```text id="6zmuiz"
Table:
| Description | Qty | Unit Price | Total |
| {{line.description}} | {{line.qty}} | {{line.unitPrice}} | {{line.total}} |
```

Processing:

```text id="re136f"
1. Find table by marker or position
2. Identify template row
3. Clone row for each item
4. Replace placeholders inside cloned row
5. Remove original template row
```

Apache POI bisa memanipulasi `XWPFTable`, `XWPFTableRow`, dan `XWPFTableCell`, tetapi cloning row dengan style kompleks sering membutuhkan copy underlying CT objects. Test sangat penting.

---

## 16. Table Row Clone Pattern

```java id="lrogtv"
import org.apache.poi.xwpf.usermodel.*;
import org.openxmlformats.schemas.wordprocessingml.x2006.main.CTRow;

public class XwpfTableRowCloner {

    public XWPFTableRow cloneRow(XWPFTable table, XWPFTableRow sourceRow, int targetIndex) {
        CTRow copiedCtRow = (CTRow) sourceRow.getCtRow().copy();
        XWPFTableRow newRow = new XWPFTableRow(copiedCtRow, table);

        table.addRow(newRow, targetIndex);

        return newRow;
    }
}
```

Catatan: menggunakan underlying CT classes memberi kontrol lebih kuat tetapi mengikat code ke struktur OOXML/POI internals. Ini normal untuk advanced XWPF, tetapi harus ditest dengan template nyata.

---

## 17. Fill Row Placeholders

```java id="pr42jo"
import org.apache.poi.xwpf.usermodel.*;

import java.util.Map;

public class XwpfTableRowPlaceholderFiller {

    private final SimpleDocxPlaceholderReplacer replacer = new SimpleDocxPlaceholderReplacer();

    public void fillRow(XWPFTableRow row, Map<String, String> values) {
        for (XWPFTableCell cell : row.getTableCells()) {
            for (XWPFParagraph paragraph : cell.getParagraphs()) {
                replaceInParagraph(paragraph, values);
            }
        }
    }

    private void replaceInParagraph(XWPFParagraph paragraph, Map<String, String> values) {
        for (XWPFRun run : paragraph.getRuns()) {
            String text = run.getText(0);

            if (text == null) {
                continue;
            }

            String replaced = text;
            for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : values.entrySet()) {
                replaced = replaced.replace(entry.getKey(), entry.getValue() == null ? "" : entry.getValue());
            }

            run.setText(replaced, 0);
        }
    }
}
```

Untuk repeated table row, pastikan placeholder dalam row template tidak split antar run. Kalau split, gunakan run-aware replacer atau content control approach.

---

## 18. Image Insertion

Use case:

```text id="frw9i2"
- company logo
- QR verification code
- visual signature stamp
- profile/document image
- generated chart snapshot
```

Apache POI XWPF menyediakan `XWPFRun.addPicture(...)` untuk memasukkan gambar ke run.

```java id="i23odu"
import org.apache.poi.util.Units;
import org.apache.poi.xwpf.usermodel.Document;
import org.apache.poi.xwpf.usermodel.XWPFRun;

import java.io.InputStream;

public class DocxImageInserter {

    public void insertPng(
            XWPFRun run,
            InputStream imageInput,
            String filename,
            int widthPx,
            int heightPx
    ) {
        try {
            run.addPicture(
                    imageInput,
                    Document.PICTURE_TYPE_PNG,
                    filename,
                    Units.toEMU(widthPx),
                    Units.toEMU(heightPx)
            );
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new DocxGenerationException("Failed to insert image into DOCX", e);
        }
    }
}
```

### Image policy

| Concern | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Logo | From server-side trusted asset |
| Signature stamp | Treat as visual only, not digital signature |
| QR content | No secrets; use document verification id |
| User-uploaded image | Validate MIME/size, scan, strip metadata if needed |
| Dimensions | Explicit width/height |
| Format | PNG/JPEG preferred |

---

## 19. Visual Signature vs Digital Signature

Dalam DOCX workflow, sering ada “signature image”.

| Jenis | Arti | Security effect |
|---|---|---|
| Signature image | Gambar tanda tangan/stamp | Visual only |
| Approval metadata | DB record bahwa user approved | Audit/system truth |
| Digital signature DOCX/PDF | Cryptographic signature | Integrity/authenticity |
| PDF final signed | Common final artifact | Better for final-form documents |

Rule:

```text id="w8rp0w"
Jangan menyebut image tanda tangan sebagai digital signature.
Untuk legal/regulatory final artifact, simpan approval event di DB dan finalisasi ke signed PDF jika diperlukan.
```

---

## 20. Headers, Footers, Footnotes, Textboxes

Placeholder tidak hanya ada di body.

| Area | Perlu diproses? |
|---|---|
| Body paragraphs | Ya |
| Tables | Ya |
| Header | Sering ya |
| Footer | Sering ya |
| Footnotes/endnotes | Tergantung dokumen legal |
| Textboxes/shapes | Hati-hati, POI support bisa terbatas |
| Comments | Biasanya reject/remove |
| Track changes | Reject template final |
| Fields | Perlu update/rendering di Word/LibreOffice |

Minimal template processor harus scan body + tables + headers + footers.

### Header/footer traversal concept

```java id="z9tj1y"
for (XWPFHeader header : document.getHeaderList()) {
    for (XWPFParagraph paragraph : header.getParagraphs()) {
        // replace placeholders
    }
    for (XWPFTable table : header.getTables()) {
        // replace inside table
    }
}

for (XWPFFooter footer : document.getFooterList()) {
    for (XWPFParagraph paragraph : footer.getParagraphs()) {
        // replace placeholders
    }
    for (XWPFTable table : footer.getTables()) {
        // replace inside table
    }
}
```

---

## 21. Style Preservation

Style preservation adalah alasan utama memakai template DOCX.

| Element | Yang perlu dijaga |
|---|---|
| Paragraph | alignment, spacing, style id |
| Run | bold, italic, font, size, color |
| Table | borders, width, cell shading |
| Numbering | list numbering, indentation |
| Header/footer | page number, logo, confidentiality |
| Section | margins, page orientation |
| Page break | lokasi break |
| Fields | TOC/date/page ref |

Microsoft Open XML docs menunjukkan style paragraph diterapkan lewat style id di paragraph properties, sehingga menjaga `pPr`/style penting untuk mempertahankan tampilan. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/office/open-xml/word/how-to-apply-a-style-to-a-paragraph-in-a-word-processing-document?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical rule:

```text id="s7b9yg"
Lebih baik clone styled template element lalu isi text,
daripada membuat semua style dari code.
```

---

## 22. Conditional Sections

Contoh:

```text id="lu5u52"
{{#hasPenalty}}
Penalty amount: {{penalty.amount}}
Penalty due date: {{penalty.dueDate}}
{{/hasPenalty}}
```

Implementasi conditional section di DOCX tidak mudah jika hanya berdasarkan paragraph text, karena marker bisa ada dalam paragraph/run/table.

Strategi yang lebih robust:

| Strategy | Cara |
|---|---|
| Paragraph markers | Remove paragraph range between start/end markers |
| Table row markers | Remove row if condition false |
| Content controls | Tag section dengan metadata |
| Multiple templates | Template berbeda per condition besar |
| docx4j | Manipulasi OpenXML lebih fleksibel |

Untuk senior design, jangan terlalu memaksakan satu template dengan banyak conditional kompleks. Kadang lebih maintainable punya beberapa template version/variant.

---

## 23. Content Controls / Structured Document Tags

Untuk template Word yang lebih robust, pertimbangkan **content controls** atau structured document tags, terutama jika business user nyaman membuat template dengan Developer tab Word.

Keuntungan:

| Keuntungan | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Placeholder lebih structured | Tidak mudah split run seperti plain text |
| Bisa diberi tag | Program bisa cari berdasarkan tag |
| Cocok untuk block/repeating section | Lebih kuat dari text marker |
| Lebih dekat ke Word template authoring | Template lebih maintainable |

Trade-off: implementasi API lebih advanced, terutama dengan POI. docx4j sering lebih nyaman untuk manipulasi WordprocessingML-level.

---

## 24. DOCX-to-PDF Strategy

DOCX-to-PDF adalah area yang sering bermasalah karena fidelity.

| Strategy | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| LibreOffice headless conversion service | Fidelity relatif baik untuk dokumen Word umum | Perlu runtime/service, font management, concurrency control |
| docx4j PDF conversion | Java-native | Fidelity tergantung fitur dokumen; perlu testing |
| Commercial converter | Fidelity/support lebih kuat | Cost/license |
| Generate PDF langsung dari data | Deterministic jika layout sederhana | Harus maintain dua renderer jika DOCX juga perlu |
| HTML intermediate | Layout controlled via HTML/CSS | Word fidelity hilang |

docx4j memiliki forum/dokumentasi komunitas untuk conversion PDF via XSL-FO, tetapi conversion fidelity perlu diuji pada template nyata karena Word layout engine sangat kompleks. ([docx4java.org](https://www.docx4java.org/forums/docx-java-f6/how-to-convert-the-html-sample-to-a-pdf-file-t1361.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Recommended enterprise flow

```text id="2phpvg"
DOCX template
   │
   ▼
Generate DOCX draft
   │
   ▼
Human review / workflow approval
   │
   ▼
Convert approved DOCX to PDF
   │
   ▼
Store final PDF + checksum
   │
   ▼
Optional digital signing
```

Untuk final legal/regulatory document, PDF yang disimpan harus dianggap artifact final. DOCX adalah editable source/draft.

---

## 25. Conversion Service Architecture

```text id="aqso5b"
Document Worker
   │
   ├─ generate DOCX
   ├─ store DOCX
   ├─ call conversion service
   │      ├─ LibreOffice/headless pod
   │      ├─ controlled fonts
   │      ├─ timeout
   │      └─ isolated temp dir
   ├─ receive PDF
   ├─ validate page count / render smoke test
   ├─ store PDF
   └─ update document job
```

### Conversion controls

| Control | Reason |
|---|---|
| Timeout | Conversion can hang |
| Worker isolation | LibreOffice can be heavy |
| Font installation | Layout depends on fonts |
| Temp cleanup | Conversion creates temp files |
| No external links | Avoid SSRF/local file access |
| Max pages/file size | Prevent resource abuse |
| Render smoke test | Detect broken output |
| Version pinning | Reproducibility |

## 27. Storage Strategy

```text id="5j3e5d"
tenant/{tenantId}/documents/{yyyy}/{mm}/{jobId}/source-template.docx

tenant/{tenantId}/documents/{yyyy}/{mm}/{jobId}/generated.pdf
tenant/{tenantId}/documents/{yyyy}/{mm}/{jobId}/metadata.json
```

| Artifact | Simpan? | Alasan |
|---|---:|---|
| Template id/version/checksum | Ya | Reproducibility |
| Generated DOCX | Ya jika editable source penting |
| Generated PDF | Ya jika final artifact |
| Input data snapshot | Ya untuk audit-heavy documents |
| Conversion logs | Optional, redacted |
| Render preview | Optional |
| Approval metadata | Ya |

## 28. Jersey Download Endpoint

```java id="l7l1ty"
@GET
@Path("/documents/{documentJobId}/docx")
@Produces("application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document")
public Response downloadDocx(@PathParam("documentJobId") String documentJobId) {
    authorizeDocumentAccess(documentJobId);

    StreamingOutput stream = output -> {
        documentStorageService.streamGeneratedDocx(documentJobId, output);
    };

    return Response.ok(stream)
            .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"document-" + documentJobId + ".docx\"")
            .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            .build();
}
```

```java id="uurli9"
@GET
@Path("/documents/{documentJobId}/pdf")
@Produces("application/pdf")
public Response downloadPdf(@PathParam("documentJobId") String documentJobId) {
    authorizeDocumentAccess(documentJobId);

    StreamingOutput stream = output -> {
        documentStorageService.streamGeneratedPdf(documentJobId, output);
    };

    return Response.ok(stream)
            .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"document-" + documentJobId + ".pdf\"")
            .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            .build();
}
```

DOCX dan PDF adalah data disclosure events. Download harus diaudit, terutama untuk dokumen case/regulatory/customer.

---

## 29. Security Concerns

| Risiko | Contoh | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| Macro-enabled file | `.docm` upload/generation | Reject macro-enabled formats |
| Template path traversal | User request `../../template.docx` | Template id allowlist |
| Placeholder injection | Input mengandung marker/template syntax | Treat input as text only |
| XML/OOXML bomb | DOCX sebagai ZIP container | File size, zip protection, worker isolation |
| Embedded object | DOCX berisi object/file | Reject or strip if untrusted |
| External links | Template references external URL/file | Disable/strip/validate |
| PII leak | Generated DOCX includes unauthorized data | Domain authorization + field policy |
| Raw content logging | Log full generated text/document | Log metadata only |
| Conversion SSRF/local file | Converter loads external resources | Isolated conversion, no network, allowlist |
| Font issue | Missing font changes layout | Controlled font package |

---

## 30. Template Validation

Sebelum template dipakai production, jalankan validator.

```text id="j4o5wv"
Template validation checks:
[ ] file extension .docx
[ ] no macros
[ ] template id/version exists
[ ] expected placeholders exist
[ ] no unknown placeholders
[ ] no track changes
[ ] no comments if not allowed
[ ] no external links if not allowed
[ ] no unsupported text boxes/placeholders
[ ] required tables/markers exist
[ ] sample generation succeeds
[ ] PDF conversion succeeds if required
```

Contoh unknown placeholder detection:

```java id="q6np7p"
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

public class PlaceholderValidator {

    private static final Pattern PLACEHOLDER = Pattern.compile("\\{\\{[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+}}");

    public void validatePlaceholders(String documentText, Set<String> allowedPlaceholders) {
        var matcher = PLACEHOLDER.matcher(documentText);

        while (matcher.find()) {
            String found = matcher.group();

            if (!allowedPlaceholders.contains(found)) {
                throw new TemplateValidationException(
                        "UNKNOWN_PLACEHOLDER",
                        "Template contains unsupported placeholder: " + found
                );
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### Test generated DOCX text

```java id="y4g06m"
@Test
void generatedDocxShouldContainCustomerName() throws Exception {
    ByteArrayOutputStream output = new ByteArrayOutputStream();

    generator.generate(sampleModel(), output);

    try (XWPFDocument doc = new XWPFDocument(
            new ByteArrayInputStream(output.toByteArray())
    )) {
        String text = doc.getParagraphs()
                .stream()
                .map(XWPFParagraph::getText)
                .collect(Collectors.joining("\n"));

        assertTrue(text.contains("Alice"));
        assertFalse(text.contains("{{customer.name}}"));
    }
}
```

### Test no unresolved placeholders

```java id="3t0swq"
@Test
void generatedDocxShouldNotContainUnresolvedPlaceholders() throws Exception {
    String text = extractAllText(generatedDocxBytes);

    assertFalse(text.matches("(?s).*\\{\\{[^}]+}}.*"));
}
```

## 33. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Placeholder replacement per run saja | Gagal kalau placeholder split antar run |
| Generate semua style dari code | Output tidak konsisten dan sulit maintain |
| Tidak proses header/footer | Placeholder masih tersisa |
| Tidak proses table cells | Placeholder table tidak terganti |
| Menganggap signature image = digital signature | False assurance |
| Tidak versioning template | Output berubah tanpa audit |
| Mengizinkan `.docm` | Macro/security risk |
| Template path dari user input | Path traversal |
| Tidak test DOCX-to-PDF fidelity | PDF final rusak |
| Tidak install font yang sama di converter | Layout berubah |
| Tidak simpan template checksum | Sulit reproduksi |
| Log full document content | PII/legal leak |
| Tidak audit download | Data disclosure tidak terlacak |
| Terlalu banyak conditional dalam satu template | Template rapuh |

---

```text id="5osflb"
Template governance
[ ] Template id/version jelas
[ ] Template checksum disimpan
[ ] Template path allowlisted
[ ] Template validation automated
[ ] No macro-enabled template unless approved
[ ] No track changes/comments in release template

Generation

Conversion

[ ] Font package controlled
[ ] Conversion worker isolated

Security
[ ] Authorization before generate/download
[ ] PII field policy
[ ] Cache-Control no-store
[ ] No raw document content logs
[ ] No user-controlled template path
[ ] External links/object policy

Persistence/audit
[ ] Document job persisted
[ ] Template id/version/checksum recorded

[ ] Generated PDF file id recorded if applicable
[ ] SHA-256 checksum stored
[ ] Generated by/at recorded
[ ] Download audit event

Testing
[ ] Template fixture tests
[ ] Placeholder split-run test
[ ] Table row generation test
[ ] Header/footer replacement test
[ ] Conversion fidelity test
[ ] Unauthorized access test
```

## 35. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| DOCX is editable source, PDF is usually final artifact | Jangan campur lifecycle |
| Template version must be auditable | Dokumen resmi harus reproducible |
| Placeholder replacement is run-sensitive | Visual text ≠ internal structure |
| Style should come from template | Code-generated styling sulit maintain |
| Generated document model must be explicit | Jangan dump entity ke DOCX |
| Header/footer/table must be traversed | Placeholder bisa ada di mana saja |
| Signature image is not digital signature | Approval truth harus di DB/signature cryptographic |
| DOCX-to-PDF conversion must be tested visually | Text test saja tidak cukup |
| Font availability affects output | Converter/runtime harus controlled |
| Document download is data disclosure | Authorization + audit wajib |

---

## 36. Kesimpulan Seri 11

DOCX processing production-ready adalah kombinasi dari:

```text id="6smuo8"
template governance
+ placeholder strategy
+ style preservation
+ table/image insertion
+ conversion pipeline
+ audit trail
+ secure download
```

Pola sehat:

```text id="tjk67w"
Request document
  -> authorize
  -> load versioned template
  -> validate template
  -> build document model
  -> replace placeholders
  -> generate tables/images/signature blocks
  -> check unresolved placeholders
  -> save DOCX
  -> optionally convert to PDF
  -> store checksum + metadata
  -> expose authorized download
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="z2fvxo"
Never assume Word visual text maps to one run.
Never use unversioned templates for official documents.
Never treat DOCX signature images as digital signatures.
Never skip DOCX-to-PDF fidelity testing.
Never expose generated documents without authorization and audit.
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:43:41 PM

Lanjut **Seri 12 dari 20**. Fokusnya Apache Tika sebagai façade extraction lintas format: kapan berguna, kapan tidak cukup, bagaimana memberi limit, dan kenapa extraction sebaiknya berjalan di worker terisolasi.

# Seri 12/20 - Apache Tika & Generic Document Extraction: Unified Extraction across PDF, Word, Excel, PowerPoint, Metadata, Embedded Files, Limits & Worker Isolation

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **Apache Tika** sebagai layer generic extraction untuk berbagai format file:

```text
PDF / DOCX / XLSX / PPTX / HTML / RTF / TXT / Images / Archives
        │
        ▼
Apache Tika
        │
        ├─ detect content type
        ├─ extract metadata
        ├─ extract structured/plain text
        ├─ discover embedded documents
        ├─ normalize extraction output
        └─ handoff to indexing / classification / validation pipeline
```

Apache Tika adalah toolkit untuk mendeteksi dan mengekstrak metadata serta text dari lebih dari seribu tipe file, termasuk PPT, XLS, dan PDF, melalui satu interface. Per Juli 2026, halaman download Apache Tika menampilkan **Apache Tika 3.3.1** sebagai latest stable release, dengan **4.0.0-beta-1** sebagai preview line 4.x. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Tika cocok saat sistem butuh **generic document extraction**, misalnya search indexing, content classification, document preview, deduplication enrichment, atau metadata harvesting lintas PDF/Word/Excel/PowerPoint.

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text
Generic Document Extraction
│
├─ 1. Intake Layer
│   ├─ upload file
│   ├─ size limit
│   ├─ checksum
│   ├─ MIME detection
│   ├─ AV scan
│   └─ staging storage
│
├─ 2. Tika Detection
│   ├─ filename hint
│   ├─ content magic bytes
│   ├─ metadata hint
│   ├─ media type
│   └─ parser selection
│
├─ 3. Tika Parsing
│   ├─ parse document
│   ├─ extract text
│   ├─ extract metadata
│   ├─ optionally parse embedded docs
│   └─ capture warnings/errors
│
├─ 4. Normalization
│   ├─ clean text
│   ├─ truncate by limit
│   ├─ classify format
│   ├─ detect empty/scanned docs
│   └─ produce canonical result
│
├─ 5. Persistence / Handoff
│   ├─ store extraction result
│   ├─ store metadata JSON
│   ├─ publish extracted event
│   ├─ index in search engine
│   └─ route OCR / manual review
│
└─ 6. Runtime Governance
    ├─ worker isolation
    ├─ parser timeout
    ├─ embedded document limits
    ├─ max chars
    ├─ max depth
    ├─ dependency patching
    └─ observability
```

---

## 3. Kapan Tika Cocok dan Kapan Tidak?

| Use case | Tika cocok? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Detect file type lintas format | Ya | Sangat cocok sebagai generic detector |
| Extract text dari PDF/Word/Excel/PPT | Ya | Cocok untuk indexing/search |
| Extract metadata umum | Ya | Metadata tidak boleh dianggap fakta domain |
| Build unified search pipeline | Ya | Satu interface lintas format |
| Extract page-level PDF text | Sebagian | Lebih baik PDFBox langsung untuk page-level detail |
| Extract Excel rows untuk import bisnis | Tidak utama | Gunakan Apache POI + schema/header validation |
| Extract Word template placeholders | Tidak utama | Gunakan POI/docx4j |
| Extract table structure akurat dari PDF | Tidak cukup | Perlu parser/layout/table-specific logic |
| OCR scanned PDF | Tidak otomatis sebagai magic solution | Perlu OCR engine/config pipeline |
| Parse untrusted huge files di API request | Tidak | Jalankan di worker isolated |

Tika adalah **extraction façade**, bukan pengganti semua parser spesifik. Ia mempermudah generic extraction, tetapi untuk business import/export yang punya contract ketat, parser khusus tetap lebih tepat.

---

## 4. Library / Module Overview

Untuk Tika 3.x, dokumentasi “Getting Started” menjelaskan komponen seperti `tika-core`, parser packages, dan `tika-app`; parser package berisi class parser berdasarkan parser library eksternal, sedangkan standard parser package mencakup parser umum yang sering dipakai. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/3.2.3/gettingstarted.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Maven dependency umum

```xml
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.apache.tika</groupId>
    <artifactId>tika-core</artifactId>
</dependency>

<dependency>
    <groupId>org.apache.tika</groupId>
    <artifactId>tika-parsers-standard-package</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Untuk dependency management, ikuti BOM/internal parent project. Jangan hardcode versi sembarangan di satu module, karena Tika membawa parser libraries lain seperti PDFBox/POI yang juga harus dipatch secara konsisten.

---

## 5. Posisi Tika dalam Arsitektur Microservice

```text
Jersey Upload Endpoint
        │
        ▼
File Intake Service
        │
        ├─ validate size
        ├─ detect MIME initial
        ├─ checksum SHA-256
        ├─ AV scan
        ├─ stage original file
        └─ create extraction job
        │
        ▼
Document Extraction Worker
        │
        ├─ Tika detect
        ├─ Tika parse
        ├─ normalize text
        ├─ persist metadata/text
        ├─ publish event
        └─ route OCR/manual review if needed
```

Tika sebaiknya berada di **worker**, bukan langsung di JAX-RS resource method, karena parsing dokumen tidak terpercaya bisa lambat, boros memory, atau crash parser dependency.

## 6. Data Model Canonical Extraction

### 6.1 Extraction result

```java
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public record DocumentExtractionResult(
        String extractionJobId,
        String fileId,
        String tenantId,
        String detectedContentType,
        String parserName,
        String status,
        String extractedText,
        long extractedTextLength,
        Map<String, List<String>> metadata,
        List<EmbeddedDocumentSummary> embeddedDocuments,
        List<ExtractionWarning> warnings,
        Instant extractedAt
) {}
```

### 6.2 Embedded document summary

```java
public record EmbeddedDocumentSummary(
        String embeddedId,
        String embeddedPath,
        String detectedContentType,
        String resourceName,
        long extractedTextLength,
        String status
) {}
```

### 6.3 Warning

```java
public record ExtractionWarning(
        String code,
        String message,
        String scope
) {}
```

Canonical result ini menjaga agar output Tika tidak langsung bocor ke domain model. Tika metadata bisa sangat bervariasi antar format dan parser.

---

## 7. Basic Tika Detection

```java
import org.apache.tika.Tika;

import java.nio.file.Path;

public class TikaDetectionService {

    private final Tika tika = new Tika();

    public String detect(Path file) {
        try {
            return tika.detect(file);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new DocumentExtractionException("Failed to detect document type", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Apache Tika mendukung content detection dengan sinyal seperti byte content, filename, dan metadata; karena itu hasil Tika lebih baik daripada hanya percaya extension atau client-provided `Content-Type`. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/2.1.0/detection.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Tetap ingat: detection bukan proof absolut. Untuk import bisnis yang strict, tetap lakukan parser-specific validation.

---

## 8. Basic Text Extraction

```java
import org.apache.tika.Tika;
import org.apache.tika.metadata.Metadata;

import java.io.InputStream;

public class BasicTikaExtractionService {

    private final Tika tika = new Tika();

    public TikaTextResult extract(InputStream input, int maxChars) {
        try {
            Metadata metadata = new Metadata();

            String text = tika.parseToString(input, metadata, maxChars);

            return new TikaTextResult(
                    normalize(text),
                    TikaMetadataMapper.toMap(metadata)
            );
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new DocumentExtractionException("Failed to extract text with Tika", e);
        }
    }

    private String normalize(String text) {
        if (text == null) {
            return "";
        }

        return text
                .replace("\u0000", "")
                .replaceAll("[ \\t\\x0B\\f\\r]+", " ")
                .replaceAll("\\n{3,}", "\n\n")
                .trim();
    }
}
```

```java
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public record TikaTextResult(
        String text,
        Map<String, List<String>> metadata
) {}
```

`parseToString` berguna untuk simple extraction, tetapi jangan jadikan ini satu-satunya pattern untuk file besar atau dokumen embedded kompleks.

---

## 9. Metadata Mapping

Tika metadata bisa punya multiple values untuk satu key.

```java
import org.apache.tika.metadata.Metadata;

import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.TreeMap;

public final class TikaMetadataMapper {

    private TikaMetadataMapper() {}

    public static Map<String, List<String>> toMap(Metadata metadata) {
        Map<String, List<String>> result = new TreeMap<>();

        for (String name : metadata.names()) {
            result.put(name, Arrays.asList(metadata.getValues(name)));
        }

        return result;
    }
}
```

Metadata dari dokumen tidak boleh dipercaya sebagai fakta final. `author`, `created`, `modified`, `title`, `creator`, atau `producer` bisa kosong, salah, atau dimanipulasi.

---

## 10. AutoDetectParser Pattern

Untuk kontrol lebih besar daripada `new Tika().parseToString(...)`, gunakan parser API.

```java
import org.apache.tika.exception.TikaException;
import org.apache.tika.metadata.Metadata;
import org.apache.tika.parser.AutoDetectParser;
import org.apache.tika.parser.ParseContext;
import org.apache.tika.sax.BodyContentHandler;
import org.xml.sax.SAXException;

import java.io.InputStream;

public class TikaAutoDetectExtractionService {

    private final AutoDetectParser parser = new AutoDetectParser();

    public TikaTextResult extract(InputStream input, int maxChars) {
        Metadata metadata = new Metadata();
        ParseContext context = new ParseContext();

        BodyContentHandler handler = new BodyContentHandler(maxChars);

        try {
            parser.parse(input, handler, metadata, context);

            return new TikaTextResult(
                    handler.toString(),
                    TikaMetadataMapper.toMap(metadata)
            );
        } catch (SAXException e) {
            throw new DocumentExtractionException("Tika extraction exceeded limits or failed", e);
        } catch (TikaException e) {
            throw new DocumentExtractionException("Tika parser failed", e);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new DocumentExtractionException("Document extraction failed", e);
        }
    }
}
```

`AutoDetectParser` memilih parser berdasarkan detection dan konfigurasi parser yang tersedia. Gunakan ini saat butuh `Metadata`, `ParseContext`, parser config, atau embedded document behavior.

---

## 11. BodyContentHandler Limit

Salah satu guardrail paling penting adalah membatasi jumlah karakter extracted text.

```java
BodyContentHandler handler = new BodyContentHandler(5_000_000);
```

Kenapa?

| Risiko | Contoh |
|---|---|
| DB membengkak | Dokumen menghasilkan puluhan MB text |
| Memory spike | Handler menampung text besar |
| Search indexing overload | Index terlalu besar |
| Log/debug leak | Text besar tidak sengaja ter-log |
| Parser abuse | Dokumen kecil menghasilkan extracted text besar |

Untuk search indexing, sering kali cukup menyimpan sebagian text, misalnya 1-5 juta karakter, tergantung kebutuhan.

---

## 12. Embedded Documents

Dokumen Office/PDF/archive bisa mengandung embedded document. Tika menyediakan `RecursiveParserWrapper` untuk recursive parsing dan mengumpulkan metadata tiap embedded resource; dokumentasi API menjelaskan bahwa item pertama adalah metadata outer container, sementara item berikutnya adalah metadata embedded resource, dan wrapper menangani embedded path calculation. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/2.0.0/api/org/apache/tika/parser/RecursiveParserWrapper.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh embedded:

```text
DOCX
 ├─ body text
 ├─ image
 ├─ embedded Excel
 └─ embedded PDF

PPTX
 ├─ slide text
 ├─ images
 └─ embedded media

ZIP
 ├─ document.pdf
 ├─ spreadsheet.xlsx
 └─ notes.txt
```

### Policy wajib

| Policy | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Parse embedded docs? | Off atau limited by default |
| Max embedded count | Wajib |
| Max nesting depth | Wajib |
| Max embedded extracted chars | Wajib |
| Embedded file type allowlist | Wajib untuk high-risk workflows |
| Store embedded text? | Hanya jika dibutuhkan |
| Quarantine suspicious embedded | Ya |

---

## 13. Recursive Extraction Concept

```java
import org.apache.tika.metadata.Metadata;
import org.apache.tika.parser.AutoDetectParser;
import org.apache.tika.parser.RecursiveParserWrapper;
import org.apache.tika.parser.RecursiveParserWrapperHandler;
import org.apache.tika.sax.BasicContentHandlerFactory;

import java.io.InputStream;
import java.util.List;

public class RecursiveTikaExtractionService {

    public List<Metadata> extractRecursive(InputStream input, int maxCharsPerDocument) {
        try {
            AutoDetectParser baseParser = new AutoDetectParser();
            RecursiveParserWrapper parser = new RecursiveParserWrapper(baseParser);

            BasicContentHandlerFactory handlerFactory =
                    new BasicContentHandlerFactory(
                            BasicContentHandlerFactory.HANDLER_TYPE.TEXT,
                            maxCharsPerDocument
                    );

            RecursiveParserWrapperHandler handler =
                    new RecursiveParserWrapperHandler(handlerFactory, -1);

            parser.parse(input, handler, new Metadata(), new org.apache.tika.parser.ParseContext());

            return handler.getMetadataList();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new DocumentExtractionException("Recursive Tika extraction failed", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan: API detail bisa berbeda antar versi Tika. Bungkus penggunaan Tika di adapter internal agar upgrade Tika tidak menyebar ke seluruh codebase.

---

## 14. Embedded Metadata Fields

Dokumentasi Tika advanced embedded documents menjelaskan metadata seperti `X-TIKA:embedded_id` dan `X-TIKA:embedded_id_path` yang membantu mengidentifikasi embedded document dalam satu parse operation. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/docs/4.0.0-SNAPSHOT/advanced/embedded-documents.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Gunakan metadata ini untuk:

```text
- tracing embedded resource
- reporting extraction result
- indexing nested document
- debugging parser output
- preventing duplicate embedded processing
```

Contoh canonical embedded output:

```json
{
  "embeddedId": "3",
  "embeddedPath": "/2/3",
  "resourceName": "attachment.xlsx",
  "detectedContentType": "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
  "status": "EXTRACTED",
  "extractedTextLength": 12450
}
```

---

## 15. Tika Config

Tika dapat dikonfigurasi untuk parser, detector, translator, service loader behavior, dan komponen lain; dokumentasi konfigurasi Tika menjelaskan bahwa konfigurasi XML dapat mengatur komponen seperti Parsers dan Detectors secara fine-grained. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/2.9.3/configuring.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Contoh konseptual `tika-config.xml`

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<properties>
    <parsers>
        <parser class="org.apache.tika.parser.DefaultParser">
            <!-- parser-specific excludes/configs can go here depending on version -->
        </parser>
    </parsers>
</properties>
```

Praktiknya: simpan config Tika sebagai resource versioned dalam service atau worker image, bukan config ad-hoc yang berubah tanpa audit.

---

## 16. Format Coverage: Apa yang Diekstrak?

| Format | Tika output umum | Caveat |
|---|---|---|
| PDF | Text + metadata | Page-level detail terbatas dibanding PDFBox langsung |
| DOCX | Paragraph/table text + metadata | Placeholder/template handling bukan tujuan Tika |
| XLSX | Sheet text/metadata | Tidak cocok untuk business import row-level validation |
| PPTX | Slide text/metadata | Layout/slide structure terbatas |
| HTML | Text + title/meta | Sanitization tetap perlu jika HTML ditampilkan |
| TXT | Text | Encoding detection tetap concern |
| RTF | Text | Formatting hilang |
| ZIP | Bisa discover embedded docs | Harus limit depth/count |
| Images | Metadata, kadang OCR if configured separately | Tika bukan OCR engine default yang cukup |
| Audio/video | Metadata | Text/transcript butuh speech-to-text pipeline |

Tika bagus untuk “apa isi dokumen ini secara umum?”, bukan “tolong pahami business schema file ini dengan presisi”.

---

## 17. Canonical Extraction Pipeline

```text
DocumentExtractionWorker
│
├─ load file from storage
├─ verify checksum
├─ run Tika detection
├─ check detected type against policy
├─ parse with limits
├─ normalize text
├─ map metadata
├─ inspect embedded documents
├─ classify extraction result
├─ persist result
├─ publish event
└─ cleanup temp files
```

### Worker skeleton

```java
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.time.Instant;

public class DocumentExtractionWorker {

    private final FileStorage storage;
    private final TikaAutoDetectExtractionService tikaExtractionService;
    private final DocumentExtractionRepository repository;
    private final DocumentEventPublisher eventPublisher;

    public void process(DocumentExtractionJob job) {
        repository.markStarted(job.jobId());

        try (InputStream input = storage.open(job.fileObjectId())) {
            TikaTextResult result = tikaExtractionService.extract(
                    input,
                    job.policy().maxExtractedChars()
            );

            DocumentExtractionResult extractionResult = new DocumentExtractionResult(
                    job.jobId(),
                    job.fileObjectId(),
                    job.tenantId(),
                    job.detectedContentType(),
                    "apache-tika",
                    "EXTRACTED",
                    result.text(),
                    result.text().length(),
                    result.metadata(),
                    List.of(),
                    classifyWarnings(result),
                    Instant.now()
            );

            repository.saveResult(extractionResult);
            repository.markCompleted(job.jobId(), "EXTRACTED");

            eventPublisher.publishDocumentExtracted(extractionResult);

        } catch (Exception e) {
            repository.markFailed(job.jobId(), "EXTRACTION_FAILED", safeMessage(e));
        }
    }

    private List<ExtractionWarning> classifyWarnings(TikaTextResult result) {
        if (result.text() == null || result.text().isBlank()) {
            return List.of(new ExtractionWarning(
                    "NO_TEXT_EXTRACTED",
                    "No text was extracted. Document may be scanned, image-only, or unsupported.",
                    "document"
            ));
        }

        return List.of();
    }

    private String safeMessage(Exception e) {
        return e.getClass().getSimpleName();
    }
}
```

---

## 18. Policy Model

```java
import java.util.Set;

public record DocumentExtractionPolicy(
        Set<String> allowedContentTypes,
        long maxFileSizeBytes,
        int maxExtractedChars,
        int maxEmbeddedDocuments,
        int maxEmbeddingDepth,
        int parseTimeoutSeconds,
        boolean parseEmbeddedDocuments,
        boolean routeEmptyTextToOcr
) {
    public static DocumentExtractionPolicy defaultSearchIndexingPolicy() {
        return new DocumentExtractionPolicy(
                Set.of(
                        "application/pdf",
                        "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
                        "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
                        "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation",
                        "text/plain",
                        "text/html"
                ),
                50L * 1024 * 1024,
                2_000_000,
                25,
                3,
                60,
                false,
                true
        );
    }
}
```

Policy harus per use case. Extraction untuk search indexing, legal evidence, malware analysis, dan preview tidak selalu sama.

---

## 19. Worker Isolation & Forked Process

Tika Pipes documentation untuk 4.x snapshot menjelaskan model parse dalam isolated forked process dengan timeout dan memory limit yang configurable; jika parse hang atau crash, hanya forked process yang terdampak dan pipeline dapat lanjut. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/docs/4.0.0-SNAPSHOT/pipes/getting-started.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Walaupun Anda tidak langsung memakai Tika Pipes, prinsip arsitekturnya relevan:

```text
API pod
  -> create extraction job

Extraction worker
  -> optionally fork parser process
  -> enforce timeout
  -> enforce memory limit
  -> kill failed parse
  -> record failure
```

Untuk dokumen tidak terpercaya, isolation bukan kemewahan; ini cara mengurangi blast radius parser bug, malicious document, atau resource exhaustion.

---

## 20. Timeout Strategy

Java thread interrupt sering tidak cukup untuk menghentikan parser yang hang di native/library call. Gunakan desain yang lebih kuat:

| Level | Control |
|---|---|
| HTTP layer | Jangan parse di request path |
| Worker job | Job timeout |
| Process level | Forked process timeout |
| Kubernetes | CPU/memory limit |
| Parser handler | Max extracted chars |
| Embedded parser | Max depth/count |
| Retry | Retry cap + dead letter |

### Simple executor timeout

```java
import java.util.concurrent.*;

public class TimeoutBoundExtractionRunner {

    private final ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(4);

    public <T> T runWithTimeout(Callable<T> task, int timeoutSeconds) {
        Future<T> future = executor.submit(task);

        try {
            return future.get(timeoutSeconds, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (TimeoutException e) {
            future.cancel(true);
            throw new DocumentExtractionException("Document extraction timed out", e);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new DocumentExtractionException("Document extraction failed", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Ini membantu, tetapi untuk parser yang tidak kooperatif terhadap interrupt, process isolation lebih kuat.

---

## 21. Persisting Extraction Result

### 21.1 Job table

```sql
CREATE TABLE document_extraction_job (
    id                  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_object_id       UUID NOT NULL,
    requested_by         TEXT NOT NULL,

    status               TEXT NOT NULL,
    detected_content_type TEXT,
    parser_name          TEXT,

    extracted_text_length BIGINT,
    embedded_count        INTEGER,

    failure_code         TEXT,
    failure_message      TEXT,

    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    started_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at         TIMESTAMPTZ
);
```

### 21.2 Result table

```sql
CREATE TABLE document_extraction_result (
    job_id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_object_id       UUID NOT NULL,

    extracted_text       TEXT,
    metadata_json        JSONB NOT NULL,
    warnings_json        JSONB,

    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### 21.3 Embedded table

```sql
CREATE TABLE document_embedded_extraction_result (
    job_id                  TEXT NOT NULL,
    embedded_id              TEXT NOT NULL,
    embedded_path            TEXT,
    resource_name            TEXT,
    detected_content_type    TEXT,
    extracted_text_length    BIGINT,
    status                   TEXT NOT NULL,
    metadata_json            JSONB,

    PRIMARY KEY (job_id, embedded_id)
);
```

Untuk extracted text besar, pertimbangkan simpan di object storage/search index dan metadata di PostgreSQL.

## 22. MyBatis Mapper

```java
public interface DocumentExtractionMapper {
    void insertJob(DocumentExtractionJobRow job);
    void markStarted(String jobId);
    void saveResult(DocumentExtractionResultRow result);
    void saveEmbeddedResults(List<EmbeddedExtractionResultRow> embeddedResults);
    void markCompleted(String jobId, String status, long extractedTextLength, int embeddedCount);
    void markFailed(String jobId, String failureCode, String failureMessage);
}
```

```xml
<insert id="saveResult">
    INSERT INTO document_extraction_result (
        job_id,
        tenant_id,
        file_object_id,
        extracted_text,
        metadata_json,
        warnings_json,
        created_at
    )
    VALUES (
        #{jobId},
        #{tenantId},
        #{fileObjectId},
        #{extractedText},
        #{metadataJson}::jsonb,
        #{warningsJson}::jsonb,
        now()
    )
</insert>
```

## 23. Event Handoff

Setelah extraction selesai, publish event agar search indexing/classification berjalan decoupled.

```json
{
  "eventId": "evt_001",
  "eventType": "DocumentExtracted",
  "eventVersion": 1,
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-13T14:30:00Z",
  "tenantId": "tenant_001",
  "correlationId": "corr_001",
  "payload": {
    "fileId": "file_001",
    "extractionJobId": "ext_001",
    "detectedContentType": "application/pdf",
    "extractedTextLength": 124500,
    "embeddedCount": 0,
    "status": "EXTRACTED"
  }
}
```

Downstream consumers:

```text
DocumentExtracted
│
├─ Search Indexer
├─ Classification Worker
├─ PII Detection Worker
├─ OCR Router
├─ Retention Policy Engine
└─ Audit Evidence Builder
```

---

## 24. Search Indexing Pattern

```text
Tika extraction
   │
   ▼
Normalize text
   │
   ├─ remove control chars
   ├─ collapse whitespace
   ├─ truncate if needed
   ├─ language detection optional
   └─ redact sensitive fields optional
   │
   ▼
Search index document
   │
   ├─ tenantId
   ├─ fileId
   ├─ content type
   ├─ title
   ├─ extracted text
   ├─ metadata
   └─ access control fields
```

Important invariant:

```text
Search index must enforce tenant/domain authorization.
Do not index documents into a global searchable corpus without access control metadata.
```

---

## 25. Generic Extraction vs Business Extraction

| Generic extraction | Business extraction |
|---|---|
| “Ambil semua text dari dokumen” | “Ambil invoice number, due date, amount” |
| Output noisy tapi luas | Output structured dan validated |
| Cocok untuk search | Cocok untuk transaction processing |
| Tika cocok | Parser/domain extractor khusus lebih cocok |
| Tidak wajib memahami schema | Harus memahami schema/domain |
| Partial quality bisa diterima | Akurasi harus tinggi |

Jangan gunakan Tika text extraction sebagai satu-satunya sumber untuk automatic business mutation tanpa validation tambahan.

Contoh buruk:

```text
Extract text from PDF invoice
  -> regex amount
  -> auto-create payment obligation
```

Contoh lebih aman:

```text
Extract text
  -> propose candidate fields
  -> validate confidence
  -> human review or deterministic validation
  -> domain command
```

---

## 26. OCR Routing

Tika extraction bisa menghasilkan text kosong untuk scanned PDF atau image-only document.

```text
Tika result
   │
   ├─ extracted text length > threshold
   │      └─ index/store result
   │
   └─ extracted text empty/low
          ├─ if content type PDF/image
          │      └─ create OCR job
          └─ else
                 └─ mark NO_TEXT_EXTRACTED
```

### OCR decision code

```java
public class OcrRoutingPolicy {

    public boolean shouldRouteToOcr(
            String detectedContentType,
            String extractedText,
            DocumentExtractionPolicy policy
    ) {
        if (!policy.routeEmptyTextToOcr()) {
            return false;
        }

        boolean emptyText = extractedText == null || extractedText.trim().length() < 50;

        boolean ocrCandidate = detectedContentType.equals("application/pdf")
                || detectedContentType.startsWith("image/");

        return emptyText && ocrCandidate;
    }
}
```

OCR harus punya budget sendiri: CPU, memory, timeout, language, confidence threshold, dan manual review fallback.

---

## 27. Security Model

| Risk | Contoh | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| Parser exploit | Malicious PDF/DOCX | Worker isolation, patch dependencies |
| Zip bomb | DOCX/XLSX/PPTX/archive | Intake size limits, embedded limits, parser limits |
| Embedded file explosion | Office file berisi banyak embedded docs | Max embedded count/depth |
| Resource exhaustion | Huge text extraction | Max chars, timeout, memory limit |
| Sensitive text leak | Extracted legal/customer data | Access control, no raw logs |
| Search index leak | Index tidak tenant-aware | Index ACL metadata |
| External resource fetch | HTML/XML/document references | Disable external fetch where possible |
| Metadata spoofing | Fake author/title/date | Treat metadata as untrusted |
| AV bypass assumption | “Tika parsed it, so safe” | Tika parse ≠ malware clean |
| Dependency CVE | Parser libs outdated | Patch process, dependency scanning |

---

## 28. Tika Server vs Embedded Library

| Mode | Kelebihan | Risiko / catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Embedded library | Simple deployment, direct Java control | Parser crash dapat memengaruhi worker JVM |
| Tika Server | Extraction service terpisah via HTTP | Network/service management, still needs isolation |
| Tika Pipes/forked style | Stronger isolation model | Lebih kompleks, cocok high-volume extraction |
| Custom worker subprocess | Full control | Lebih banyak engineering |

Untuk microservice internal dengan volume sedang: embedded Tika di worker pod bisa cukup. Untuk volume tinggi atau dokumen tidak terpercaya dari publik: pertimbangkan dedicated extraction service/process isolation.

---

## 29. API Contract

### Upload document for extraction

```http
POST /v1/document-extractions
Content-Type: multipart/form-data
```

Parts:

```text
file: document.pdf
metadata:
{
  "purpose": "SEARCH_INDEXING",
  "parseEmbeddedDocuments": false,
  "routeToOcr": true
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "extractionJobId": "ext_001",
  "status": "PENDING",
  "links": {
    "status": "/v1/document-extractions/ext_001"
  }
}
```

### Status

```http
GET /v1/document-extractions/{jobId}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "extractionJobId": "ext_001",
  "status": "EXTRACTED",
  "detectedContentType": "application/pdf",
  "extractedTextLength": 124500,
  "embeddedCount": 0,
  "warnings": []
}
```

### Download extracted text

```http
GET /v1/document-extractions/{jobId}/text
```

Response headers:

```http
Content-Type: text/plain; charset=UTF-8
Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="ext_001.txt"
Cache-Control: no-store
```

---

## 30. Jersey Resource Skeleton

```java
@Path("/document-extractions")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class DocumentExtractionResource {

    private final FileIntakeService fileIntakeService;
    private final DocumentExtractionJobService jobService;

    @POST
    @Consumes(MediaType.MULTIPART_FORM_DATA)
    public Response submitExtraction(
            @FormDataParam("file") InputStream fileStream,
            @FormDataParam("file") FormDataContentDisposition fileMeta,
            @FormDataParam("metadata") String metadataJson
    ) {
        authorizeExtractionSubmit();

        StagedFile staged = fileIntakeService.stage(
                fileStream,
                fileMeta.getFileName(),
                null,
                FileIntakePolicies.genericDocument()
        );

        DocumentExtractionJob job = jobService.createJob(staged, metadataJson);

        return Response.accepted(new DocumentExtractionSubmittedResponse(
                job.jobId(),
                "PENDING",
                Map.of("status", "/v1/document-extractions/" + job.jobId())
        )).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{jobId}")
    public Response getStatus(@PathParam("jobId") String jobId) {
        authorizeJobAccess(jobId);

        DocumentExtractionStatusResponse response = jobService.getStatus(jobId);
        return Response.ok(response).build();
    }
}
```

Resource tetap tipis. Parsing Tika berjalan di worker.

### Detection test

```java
@Test
void shouldDetectPdfEvenWhenExtensionIsWrong() {
    String detected = detectionService.detect(Path.of("fixtures/mislabeled-pdf.bin"));

    assertEquals("application/pdf", detected);
}
```

### Extraction limit test

```java
@Test
void shouldLimitExtractedTextLength() {
    TikaTextResult result = extractionService.extract(
            inputStreamFor("fixtures/large-document.pdf"),
            10_000
    );

    assertTrue(result.text().length() <= 10_000);
}
```

## 33. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Memakai Tika langsung di API request | Timeout/latency spike |
| Tidak batasi extracted text | Memory/DB/index bloat |
| Parse embedded docs tanpa limit | Resource exhaustion |
| Menganggap Tika output selalu akurat | Text order/format bisa noisy |
| Menggunakan Tika untuk business import | Validation kurang presisi |
| Log extracted text | PII/legal leak |
| Tidak patch Tika/parser dependencies | Security risk |
| Tidak simpan original file/checksum | Audit/reprocess sulit |
| Treat metadata as trusted | Metadata bisa palsu |
| Search index tanpa ACL | Data leak lintas tenant |
| Tidak punya OCR route | Scanned document terlihat “kosong” |
| Tidak punya parser timeout | Worker bisa stuck |
| Tidak test corrupt/malicious files | Production surprise |

---

## 34. Production Checklist

```text
Intake
[ ] File size limit
[ ] Extension allowlist by purpose
[ ] Tika/server-side MIME detection
[ ] Checksum SHA-256
[ ] AV scan/quarantine policy
[ ] Original file retained if audit required

Tika config
[ ] tika-core and parser package managed by BOM
[ ] Tika config versioned
[ ] Parser dependencies scanned/patched
[ ] Max extracted chars
[ ] Embedded parsing policy
[ ] Embedded count/depth limits
[ ] Timeout strategy

Worker runtime
[ ] Extraction runs outside API pod
[ ] Worker memory/cpu limits
[ ] Optional process isolation for untrusted docs
[ ] Retry cap
[ ] Dead-letter/failure state
[ ] Temp cleanup

Persistence
[ ] Extraction job table
[ ] Metadata JSON stored
[ ] Extracted text stored/indexed safely
[ ] Embedded docs summary if enabled
[ ] Parser version/config recorded if regulatory

Security
[ ] No raw text logging
[ ] Search index ACL metadata
[ ] Download authorization
[ ] Cache-Control no-store for extracted text
[ ] Metadata treated as untrusted
[ ] OCR route controlled

Observability
[ ] Duration metrics
[ ] Extracted text size metrics
[ ] Embedded count metrics
[ ] Failure reason metrics
[ ] Timeout metrics
[ ] OCR routed metrics
[ ] Correlation id
```

---

## 35. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| Tika is a generic extraction façade | Bukan pengganti parser domain-specific |
| Extraction must be bounded | Max chars, time, embedded count, depth |
| Untrusted document parsing belongs in worker isolation | Lindungi API pod |
| Extracted text is sensitive data | Jangan log/ekspos tanpa authz |
| Metadata is untrusted | Simpan sebagai metadata, bukan domain truth |
| Embedded document parsing must be explicit | Jangan recursive parse tanpa policy |
| Search index must carry access-control fields | Hindari data leak lintas tenant |
| OCR is a separate pipeline | Tika text kosong bukan berarti dokumen kosong |
| Original file remains source of truth | Extraction result adalah derivative artifact |
| Tika adapter should be isolated in codebase | Memudahkan upgrade dan perubahan API |

---

## 36. Kesimpulan Seri 12

Apache Tika sangat berguna untuk **unified document detection and extraction**, terutama untuk search indexing dan content analysis lintas format.

Pola sehat:

```text
Upload document
  -> intake/security
  -> stage original
  -> create extraction job
  -> Tika worker
  -> detect content type
  -> parse with limits
  -> extract text/metadata
  -> handle embedded docs by policy
  -> route OCR if needed
  -> persist result
  -> publish event
  -> index/search/classify downstream
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text
Never treat Tika as a magic business parser.
Never parse untrusted documents without limits.
Never recursively parse embedded documents without explicit policy.
Never run heavy extraction in the API request path.
Never log extracted document text.
```
