## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:45:59 PM

Lanjut **Seri 13 dari 20**. Fokusnya Parquet sebagai format columnar untuk analytics/export data besar, bukan pengganti CSV/Excel untuk interaksi user bisnis biasa.

# Seri 13/20 - Parquet Processing: Schema, Compression, Columnar Model, Row Group, Predicate Pushdown, Object Storage & Analytics Export dari Java Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **Parquet processing** di Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice.

Parquet bukan format utama untuk user bisnis membuka file di Excel. Parquet adalah format **columnar** untuk analytics, data lake, batch export besar, dan integrasi dengan engine seperti Spark, Trino/Presto, Hive, Athena, DuckDB, BigQuery external table, atau data platform internal.

Apache Parquet adalah format file open-source berbasis kolom yang dirancang untuk storage dan retrieval data yang efisien, dengan compression dan encoding yang cocok untuk data bulk. Dokumentasi resmi Apache Parquet juga menekankan bahwa spesifikasi formatnya berada di repository `parquet-format`, sedangkan implementasi Java berada di project `parquet-java`. ([Parquet](https://parquet.apache.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text
Operational DB / Domain Data
        │
        ▼
Java Microservice Export
        │
        ├─ query data incrementally
        ├─ map domain/report rows
        ├─ write Parquet schema
        ├─ encode/compress columnar data
        ├─ partition output path
        ├─ store to object storage
        └─ publish dataset/export metadata
        │
        ▼
Analytics / Data Lake / BI / ML Consumers
```

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text
Parquet Processing
│
├─ 1. Use Case Boundary
│   ├─ analytics export
│   ├─ data lake handoff
│   ├─ regulatory bulk extract
│   ├─ ML feature dataset
│   ├─ archival dataset
│   └─ downstream batch integration
│
├─ 2. Data Model
│   ├─ logical schema
│   ├─ physical Parquet schema
│   ├─ required / optional fields
│   ├─ decimal/date/timestamp types
│   ├─ nested structures
│   └─ schema evolution
│
├─ 3. Write Pipeline
│   ├─ query source data
│   ├─ stream/page rows
│   ├─ map to Parquet record
│   ├─ write row groups
│   ├─ compress pages
│   ├─ write metadata/footer
│   └─ upload/store object
│
├─ 4. Read Pipeline
│   ├─ open Parquet file
│   ├─ read schema/footer
│   ├─ apply projection
│   ├─ apply predicate pushdown
│   ├─ read selected row groups/columns
│   └─ map to DTO/domain read model
│
├─ 5. Storage Layout
│   ├─ bucket/container/path
│   ├─ partitioning
│   ├─ file naming
│   ├─ manifest/catalog
│   ├─ retention
│   └─ checksum
│
├─ 6. Performance
│   ├─ row group size
│   ├─ page size
│   ├─ compression codec
│   ├─ dictionary encoding
│   ├─ column projection
│   ├─ predicate pushdown
│   └─ file sizing
│
└─ 7. Governance
    ├─ dataset version
    ├─ schema compatibility
    ├─ PII classification
    ├─ lineage
    ├─ audit
    └─ reproducibility
```

---

## 3. Kapan Pakai Parquet?

| Use case | Parquet cocok? | Alasan |
|---|---:|---|
| Export data besar untuk analytics | Ya | Columnar, compressed, bisa scan kolom tertentu |
| Data lake / object storage dataset | Ya | Format umum untuk engine analytics |
| ML feature dataset | Ya | Efficient untuk batch read kolom tertentu |
| Regulatory bulk archival extract | Ya, jika consumer mendukung | Compact dan schema-aware |
| Download user bisnis | Biasanya tidak | User lebih familiar dengan CSV/Excel/PDF |
| REST API payload | Tidak | JSON/XML lebih cocok |
| Row-by-row transaction update | Tidak | Parquet bukan OLTP database |
| Small ad-hoc report | Biasanya tidak | CSV/Excel lebih sederhana |
| Inter-service command/event | Tidak | JSON/Avro/Protobuf lebih cocok |
| Append frequent single row | Tidak ideal | Parquet lebih cocok batch file |

Rule praktis:

```text
Human-facing export     -> CSV / Excel / PDF
System analytics export -> Parquet
Transactional API       -> JSON / XML
Event streaming         -> JSON / Avro / Protobuf
Bulk data lake          -> Parquet
```

---

## 4. Parquet vs CSV vs JSON vs Excel

| Format | Strength | Weakness |
|---|---|---|
| CSV | Simple, portable, human-readable | No schema, weak types, large size, parsing ambiguity |
| JSON | Flexible/nested, API-friendly | Larger size, slower analytics scan |
| Excel | Business-friendly, styled, multi-sheet | Heavy, limited scale, not analytics-native |
| Parquet | Columnar, typed, compressed, analytics-friendly | Not human-readable, schema governance required |
| XML | Schema/namespace strong, legacy enterprise | Verbose, parsing/security complexity |

Parquet menyimpan data secara columnar, sehingga query analytics yang hanya butuh beberapa kolom bisa membaca lebih sedikit data dibanding row-oriented text formats. Dokumentasi Parquet menjelaskan konsep row group dan column chunk: satu row group adalah partisi horizontal data, dan setiap row group berisi column chunk untuk setiap kolom. ([Parquet](https://parquet.apache.org/docs/concepts/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5. Struktur File Parquet

```text
Parquet File
│
├─ Magic bytes: PAR1
│
├─ Row Group 1
│   ├─ Column Chunk: customer_id
│   │   ├─ Page
│   │   └─ Page
│   ├─ Column Chunk: name
│   │   ├─ Page
│   │   └─ Page
│   └─ Column Chunk: created_at
│       ├─ Page
│       └─ Page
│
├─ Row Group 2
│   ├─ Column Chunk: customer_id
│   ├─ Column Chunk: name
│   └─ Column Chunk: created_at
│
├─ File Metadata / Footer
│   ├─ schema
│   ├─ row group metadata
│   ├─ column statistics
│   ├─ compression metadata
│   └─ key-value metadata
│
└─ Magic bytes: PAR1
```

Dokumentasi format Parquet menjelaskan bahwa formatnya dirancang untuk memisahkan metadata dari data, sehingga metadata dapat digunakan untuk membaca file secara efisien. ([Parquet](https://parquet.apache.org/docs/file-format/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. Konsep Penting

| Konsep | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Columnar storage | Data disimpan per kolom, bukan per row |
| Row group | Blok horizontal berisi sejumlah row |
| Column chunk | Data satu kolom dalam satu row group |
| Page | Unit lebih kecil dalam column chunk |
| Footer | Metadata schema, row groups, statistics, offsets |
| Projection | Membaca hanya kolom tertentu |
| Predicate pushdown | Melewati row group/page berdasarkan statistics/filter |
| Encoding | Cara nilai dikodekan, misalnya dictionary/RLE/delta |
| Compression | Kompresi page data, misalnya Snappy/ZSTD/Gzip |
| Logical type | Makna tipe seperti decimal, date, timestamp, uuid |

Dokumentasi resmi Parquet menyatakan compression dapat diterapkan pada data block di dictionary pages dan data pages, dan Parquet mendukung beberapa codec dengan trade-off rasio kompresi dan biaya processing. ([Parquet](https://parquet.apache.org/docs/file-format/data-pages/compression/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Maven Dependencies

Untuk Java, biasanya memakai `parquet-avro`, `parquet-hadoop`, atau API Parquet Java lain sesuai pendekatan.

```xml
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.apache.parquet</groupId>
    <artifactId>parquet-avro</artifactId>
</dependency>

<dependency>
    <groupId>org.apache.parquet</groupId>
    <artifactId>parquet-hadoop</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Gunakan versi dari dependency management/BOM perusahaan. Per Juli 2026, blog Apache Parquet menampilkan rilis `parquet-java` terbaru seperti 1.16.0 dan 1.17.0 di halaman rilis/blog, jadi jangan mengandalkan versi lama dari contoh internet tanpa dicek ke BOM internal. ([Parquet](https://parquet.apache.org/blog/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. Pilihan API Java

| API | Cocok untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `parquet-avro` | Schema Avro + Parquet, paling umum untuk Java data pipeline | Cocok jika sudah nyaman dengan Avro schema |
| `parquet-hadoop` | Hadoop-style input/output format | Banyak contoh lama memakai Hadoop `Path`/`Configuration` |
| Low-level Parquet API | Kontrol detail | Lebih kompleks |
| Spark/Flink writer | Data platform job, bukan microservice biasa | Cocok kalau export dilakukan di batch engine |
| Arrow/Parquet integration | Advanced analytics | Cocok untuk Arrow-based pipeline |

Untuk microservice Java/Jersey yang hanya perlu export dataset periodik, pendekatan paling praktis biasanya:

```text
Domain/report DTO
  -> Avro GenericRecord
  -> Parquet Avro writer
  -> local temp file
  -> object storage upload
  -> metadata row in PostgreSQL
```

---

## 9. Schema Design

Parquet adalah schema-aware. Jangan menulis dataset tanpa governance schema.

Contoh logical schema untuk customer snapshot:

```text
customer_snapshot_v1
│
├─ tenant_id: string, required
├─ snapshot_date: date, required
├─ customer_id: string, required
├─ customer_name: string, optional
├─ email: string, optional
├─ status: string, required
├─ registered_at: timestamp, optional
├─ lifetime_value: decimal(18,2), optional
└─ updated_at: timestamp, required
```

### Rule schema

| Rule | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Gunakan ID sebagai string | Hindari leading zero hilang |
| Money pakai decimal | Jangan pakai double untuk uang |
| Date bisnis pakai date | Jangan timestamp kalau hanya tanggal |
| Timestamp pakai UTC / instant semantics | Hindari ambiguity timezone |
| Field required/optional jelas | Schema evolution lebih aman |
| Status/enum distabilkan sebagai code | Jangan rename sembarangan |
| PII ditandai | Governance dan masking |
| Version schema | Compatibility |

---

## 10. Avro Schema Example

```json
{
  "type": "record",
  "name": "CustomerSnapshot",
  "namespace": "com.company.analytics.customer",
  "fields": [
    {
      "name": "tenant_id",
      "type": "string"
    },
    {
      "name": "snapshot_date",
      "type": {
        "type": "int",
        "logicalType": "date"
      }
    },
    {
      "name": "customer_id",
      "type": "string"
    },
    {
      "name": "customer_name",
      "type": ["null", "string"],
      "default": null
    },
    {
      "name": "email",
      "type": ["null", "string"],
      "default": null
    },
    {
      "name": "status",
      "type": "string"
    },
    {
      "name": "registered_at",
      "type": ["null", {
        "type": "long",
        "logicalType": "timestamp-micros"
      }],
      "default": null
    },
    {
      "name": "lifetime_value",
      "type": ["null", {
        "type": "bytes",
        "logicalType": "decimal",
        "precision": 18,
        "scale": 2
      }],
      "default": null
    },
    {
      "name": "updated_at",
      "type": {
        "type": "long",
        "logicalType": "timestamp-micros"
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

Avro + Parquet umum digunakan karena Avro schema dapat menjadi model intermediate yang jelas sebelum data ditulis ke Parquet.

---

## 11. Java DTO untuk Export

```java
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;

public record CustomerSnapshotRow(
        String tenantId,
        LocalDate snapshotDate,
        String customerId,
        String customerName,
        String email,
        String status,
        Instant registeredAt,
        BigDecimal lifetimeValue,
        Instant updatedAt
) {}
```

DTO export harus berbeda dari entity database. Dataset analytics punya contract sendiri dan tidak selalu identik dengan table operational.

---

## 12. Parquet Write Pipeline

```text
Export request/job
   │
   ▼
Authorize dataset export
   │
   ▼
Create export job
   │
   ▼
Query PostgreSQL incrementally
   │
   ▼
Map row -> CustomerSnapshotRow
   │
   ▼
Map row -> Avro GenericRecord
   │
   ▼
Write Parquet file
   │
   ▼
Upload to object storage
   │
   ▼
Persist dataset metadata
   │
   ▼
Publish DatasetExported event
```

Untuk microservice, jangan menulis langsung ke object storage stream jika writer/library tidak robust untuk failure recovery. Lebih aman generate ke temp file dulu, hitung checksum/size, lalu upload.

---

## 13. Avro GenericRecord Mapper

```java
import org.apache.avro.Schema;
import org.apache.avro.generic.GenericData;
import org.apache.avro.generic.GenericRecord;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;

public class CustomerSnapshotAvroMapper {

    private final Schema schema;

    public CustomerSnapshotAvroMapper(Schema schema) {
        this.schema = schema;
    }

    public GenericRecord toRecord(CustomerSnapshotRow row) {
        GenericRecord record = new GenericData.Record(schema);

        record.put("tenant_id", row.tenantId());
        record.put("snapshot_date", toEpochDay(row.snapshotDate()));
        record.put("customer_id", row.customerId());
        record.put("customer_name", row.customerName());
        record.put("email", row.email());
        record.put("status", row.status());
        record.put("registered_at", toMicros(row.registeredAt()));
        record.put("lifetime_value", toDecimalBytes(row.lifetimeValue(), 2));
        record.put("updated_at", toMicros(row.updatedAt()));

        return record;
    }

    private int toEpochDay(LocalDate date) {
        return Math.toIntExact(date.toEpochDay());
    }

    private Long toMicros(Instant instant) {
        if (instant == null) {
            return null;
        }

        return Math.addExact(
                Math.multiplyExact(instant.getEpochSecond(), 1_000_000L),
                instant.getNano() / 1_000L
        );
    }

    private ByteBuffer toDecimalBytes(BigDecimal value, int scale) {
        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }

        BigDecimal scaled = value.setScale(scale);
        return ByteBuffer.wrap(scaled.unscaledValue().toByteArray());
    }
}
```

Catatan: decimal encoding harus konsisten dengan schema precision/scale. Untuk money, lakukan rounding di domain/report layer sebelum export jika business rule mengharuskan.

---

## 14. Parquet Writer Example

Contoh konseptual menggunakan AvroParquetWriter:

```java
import org.apache.avro.Schema;
import org.apache.avro.generic.GenericRecord;
import org.apache.hadoop.conf.Configuration;
import org.apache.hadoop.fs.Path;
import org.apache.parquet.avro.AvroParquetWriter;
import org.apache.parquet.hadoop.ParquetWriter;
import org.apache.parquet.hadoop.metadata.CompressionCodecName;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.util.Iterator;

public class CustomerSnapshotParquetWriter {

    private final Schema schema;
    private final CustomerSnapshotAvroMapper mapper;

    public CustomerSnapshotParquetWriter(Schema schema) {
        this.schema = schema;
        this.mapper = new CustomerSnapshotAvroMapper(schema);
    }

    public java.nio.file.Path writeToTempFile(
            Iterator<CustomerSnapshotRow> rows
    ) {
        try {
            java.nio.file.Path tempFile = Files.createTempFile(
                    "customer-snapshot-",
                    ".parquet"
            );

            Path hadoopPath = new Path(tempFile.toUri());

            try (ParquetWriter<GenericRecord> writer =
                         AvroParquetWriter.<GenericRecord>builder(hadoopPath)
                                 .withSchema(schema)
                                 .withConf(new Configuration())
                                 .withCompressionCodec(CompressionCodecName.SNAPPY)
                                 .withRowGroupSize(128L * 1024 * 1024)
                                 .withPageSize(1 * 1024 * 1024)
                                 .build()) {

                while (rows.hasNext()) {
                    writer.write(mapper.toRecord(rows.next()));
                }
            }

            return tempFile;
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new ParquetExportException("Failed to write Parquet file", e);
        }
    }
}
```

`parquet-java` menyediakan writer/reader Java untuk file Parquet; dokumentasi Parquet Java/Hadoop menunjukkan konfigurasi input/output format dapat dilakukan lewat Hadoop configuration atau setter programmatic. ([GitHub](https://github.com/apache/parquet-java/blob/master/parquet-hadoop/README.md?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 15. Query Data Incrementally

Jangan load semua data ke memory.

### MyBatis cursor idea

```java
import org.apache.ibatis.cursor.Cursor;

public interface CustomerSnapshotExportMapper {
    Cursor<CustomerSnapshotRow> streamCustomerSnapshot(CustomerSnapshotFilter filter);
}
```

```java
public class CustomerSnapshotExportService {

    private final CustomerSnapshotExportMapper mapper;
    private final CustomerSnapshotParquetWriter parquetWriter;
    private final ObjectStorage objectStorage;
    private final DatasetExportRepository repository;

    public void export(CustomerSnapshotExportJob job) {
        repository.markRunning(job.jobId());

        try (Cursor<CustomerSnapshotRow> cursor =
                     mapper.streamCustomerSnapshot(job.filter())) {

            java.nio.file.Path parquetFile =
                    parquetWriter.writeToTempFile(cursor.iterator());

            StoredObject stored = objectStorage.upload(
                    job.storageKey(),
                    parquetFile,
                    "application/octet-stream"
            );

            repository.markSuccess(
                    job.jobId(),
                    stored.storageKey(),
                    stored.sizeBytes(),
                    stored.sha256Hex()
            );

        } catch (Exception e) {
            repository.markFailed(job.jobId(), "PARQUET_EXPORT_FAILED", e.getClass().getSimpleName());
        }
    }
}
```

Kalau MyBatis cursor tidak tersedia/nyaman, gunakan keyset pagination, bukan offset pagination untuk dataset besar.

---

## 16. Row Group Size

Row group adalah salah satu tuning paling penting. Row group terlalu kecil membuat metadata overhead tinggi dan query kurang efisien; terlalu besar bisa membuat memory tinggi dan predicate skipping kurang granular.

| Row group size | Efek |
|---|---|
| Terlalu kecil | Banyak row group, metadata overhead, file fragmented |
| Terlalu besar | Memory writer/reader lebih tinggi, predicate skip kurang granular |
| Umum awal | 128 MB sebagai starting point |
| Data lake heavy scan | Bisa 128-512 MB tergantung engine |
| Low-memory microservice | Mungkin 64-128 MB |

Dokumentasi Parquet menjelaskan row group sebagai partition horizontal dan column chunk sebagai data kolom dalam row group. Ini alasan row group size memengaruhi paralelisme, memory, dan pruning. ([Parquet](https://parquet.apache.org/docs/concepts/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Rekomendasi awal untuk microservice export:

```text
row_group_size = 128 MB
page_size      = 1 MB
compression    = SNAPPY atau ZSTD, sesuai engine support
target_file    = 128 MB - 1 GB per file, tergantung platform
```

---

## 17. Compression Codec

| Codec | Karakteristik | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| SNAPPY | Cepat, kompresi sedang, sangat umum | Default aman untuk banyak analytics engine |
| GZIP | Kompresi lebih tinggi, CPU lebih mahal | Storage-sensitive, read tidak terlalu sering |
| ZSTD | Kompresi bagus dan relatif cepat | Modern data platform, pastikan compatibility |
| LZ4 | Cepat | Workload speed-sensitive |
| Uncompressed | Debug/special case | Jarang untuk production |

Parquet mendukung beberapa compression codec dengan trade-off compression ratio dan processing cost. Dokumentasi compression resmi Parquet menempatkan codec sebagai bagian dari spesifikasi data page/dictionary page compression. ([Parquet](https://parquet.apache.org/docs/file-format/data-pages/compression/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Rule praktis:

```text
Default enterprise analytics export:
- SNAPPY jika compatibility paling penting
- ZSTD jika platform sudah standard dan ingin kompresi lebih baik
```

---

## 18. Encoding & Dictionary

Parquet mendukung berbagai encoding. Dokumentasi encodings resmi menyebut definisi encoding Parquet dan bahwa encoding dapat digunakan pada page type kecuali jika dokumentasi page/encoding menyatakan lain. ([Parquet](https://parquet.apache.org/docs/file-format/data-pages/encodings/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Encoding concern | Makna |
|---|---|
| Dictionary encoding | Bagus untuk low-cardinality string/status/code |
| RLE/bit packing | Efisien untuk repeated/boolean/definition levels |
| Delta encoding | Berguna untuk beberapa numeric/ordered value |
| Plain encoding | Fallback sederhana |
| Page statistics | Mendukung skipping/pushdown |

Untuk export dari microservice, biasanya tidak perlu tuning encoding low-level di awal. Fokus dulu ke schema, compression, row group, file layout, dan compatibility engine.

---

## 19. Predicate Pushdown

Predicate pushdown berarti reader/engine dapat menggunakan metadata/statistics untuk melewati data yang tidak relevan.

Contoh query:

```sql
SELECT customer_id, status
FROM customer_snapshot
WHERE snapshot_date = DATE '2026-07-13'
  AND status = 'ACTIVE';
```

Jika dataset dipartition dan Parquet statistics baik, engine bisa:

```text
1. Skip partition folder yang snapshot_date != 2026-07-13
2. Skip row group yang min/max/status tidak cocok
3. Read hanya kolom customer_id dan status
```

Parquet footer/metadata dan column statistics membuat skipping seperti ini mungkin, tetapi hasilnya sangat bergantung pada layout data, partitioning, row group size, dan engine reader.

---

## 20. Column Projection

Column projection berarti reader hanya membaca kolom yang dibutuhkan.

```text
Parquet columns:
tenant_id, snapshot_date, customer_id, customer_name, email, status,
registered_at, lifetime_value, updated_at

Query needs:
customer_id, status
```

Engine cukup baca column chunks `customer_id` dan `status`, bukan seluruh row seperti CSV/JSON.

Inilah alasan Parquet unggul untuk analytics wide tables.

---

## 21. Partitioning Strategy

Object storage dataset biasanya dipartition dengan path.

```text
s3://data-lake/customer_snapshot/
  tenant_id=acme/
    snapshot_date=2026-07-13/
      part-00001.parquet
      part-00002.parquet
```

Atau untuk multi-tenant governance lebih ketat:

```text
s3://tenant-data/acme/customer_snapshot/
  snapshot_date=2026-07-13/
    part-00001.parquet
```

### Partition design

| Partition field | Cocok? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Date/snapshot date | Ya | Sangat umum |
| Tenant | Ya untuk multi-tenant isolation | Perhatikan small files |
| Region/business unit | Bisa | Jika sering difilter |
| Status | Hati-hati | Bisa skew dan banyak small files |
| Customer ID | Biasanya tidak | Cardinality terlalu tinggi |
| Timestamp full | Tidak | Terlalu granular |

Rule:

```text
Partition by columns that are:
- frequently filtered
- low/medium cardinality
- stable
- not too skewed
```

---

## 22. Small Files Problem

Parquet bekerja buruk jika dataset berisi terlalu banyak file kecil.

```text
Bad:
snapshot_date=2026-07-13/
  part-000001.parquet  50 KB
  part-000002.parquet  70 KB
  part-000003.parquet  40 KB
  ... thousands

Better:
snapshot_date=2026-07-13/
  part-000001.parquet  256 MB
  part-000002.parquet  240 MB
```

| Problem | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Banyak file kecil | Query planning overhead tinggi |
| Object storage request banyak | Cost/latency naik |
| Metadata/catalog berat | Engine lambat |
| Compaction dibutuhkan | Pipeline tambah kompleks |

Untuk microservice export kecil per tenant, small files sering terjadi. Solusinya bisa berupa compaction job di data platform, bukan memaksa microservice menulis file besar.

---

## 23. Object Storage Layout

```text
bucket/
  datasets/
    customer_snapshot/
      version=v1/
        tenant_id=acme/
          snapshot_date=2026-07-13/
            part-00000-<jobId>.parquet
            _SUCCESS
            manifest.json
```

Manifest example:

```json
{
  "dataset": "customer_snapshot",
  "datasetVersion": "v1",
  "tenantId": "acme",
  "snapshotDate": "2026-07-13",
  "exportJobId": "exp_001",
  "files": [
    {
      "path": "version=v1/tenant_id=acme/snapshot_date=2026-07-13/part-00000-exp_001.parquet",
      "sizeBytes": 268435456,
      "sha256": "abc123...",
      "rowCount": 1000000
    }
  ],
  "schemaId": "customer_snapshot_v1",
  "generatedAt": "2026-07-13T14:30:00Z"
}
```

Manifest berguna untuk reproducibility, audit, dan downstream ingestion.

---

## 24. Dataset Metadata Table

```sql
CREATE TABLE dataset_export_job (
    id                  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
    dataset_name         TEXT NOT NULL,
    dataset_version      TEXT NOT NULL,

    export_format        TEXT NOT NULL,
    partition_json       JSONB NOT NULL,
    filter_json          JSONB NOT NULL,

    status               TEXT NOT NULL,
    storage_provider     TEXT,
    storage_prefix       TEXT,
    manifest_storage_key TEXT,

    row_count            BIGINT,
    file_count           INTEGER,
    total_size_bytes     BIGINT,
    sha256_manifest_hex  TEXT,

    schema_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
    schema_fingerprint   TEXT,

    failure_code         TEXT,
    failure_message      TEXT,

    requested_by         TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    started_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at         TIMESTAMPTZ
);
```

Status:

```text
PENDING
RUNNING
SUCCESS
FAILED
CANCELLED
EXPIRED
```

---

## 25. Schema Registry / Schema Catalog

Untuk Parquet dataset, minimal punya table schema catalog internal.

```sql
CREATE TABLE dataset_schema (
    id                  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    dataset_name         TEXT NOT NULL,
    dataset_version      TEXT NOT NULL,
    schema_format        TEXT NOT NULL,
    schema_json          JSONB NOT NULL,
    schema_fingerprint   TEXT NOT NULL,
    compatibility_mode   TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_by           TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE(dataset_name, dataset_version)
);
```

Compatibility mode:

```text
BACKWARD
FORWARD
FULL
NONE
```

Untuk dataset analytics yang dipakai banyak consumer, schema change harus review seperti API/event contract.

---

## 26. Schema Evolution Rules

| Perubahan | Compatibility |
|---|---|
| Tambah optional column | Biasanya aman |
| Tambah required column | Breaking |
| Hapus column | Breaking |
| Rename column | Breaking |
| Ubah tipe column | Breaking |
| Ubah decimal precision/scale | Compatibility review |
| Ubah timestamp unit/semantics | Breaking |
| Ubah enum/status code | Compatibility review |
| Ubah partition layout | Breaking untuk downstream path readers |
| Ubah meaning field tanpa rename | Dangerous semantic breaking |

Prinsip:

```text
Parquet schema evolution harus diperlakukan seperti API evolution.
Column name, type, nullability, logical type, dan partition path adalah contract.
```

---

## 27. PII & Governance

Parquet sering masuk data lake. Kalau governance lemah, PII bisa tersebar lebih luas daripada operational DB.

| Concern | Control |
|---|---|
| Email/phone/address | PII classification |
| Export permission | Role/action-specific authorization |
| Tenant isolation | Partition/bucket/prefix/IAM |
| Masking | Mask field sebelum write |
| Encryption | Object storage encryption |
| Retention | TTL/lifecycle policy |
| Discovery | Catalog metadata |
| Access audit | Object access log + job audit |
| Downstream sharing | Data contract + ACL |
| Deletion request | Data lifecycle strategy |

Contoh field classification:

```json
{
  "dataset": "customer_snapshot",
  "fields": {
    "customer_id": { "classification": "INTERNAL_ID" },
    "customer_name": { "classification": "PII" },
    "email": { "classification": "PII" },
    "lifetime_value": { "classification": "CONFIDENTIAL" }
  }
}
```

---

## 28. Parquet Import / Read Use Cases

Walaupun sering untuk export, microservice juga bisa membaca Parquet:

| Use case | Catatan |
|---|---|
| Import analytics result | Read batch result dari data platform |
| Load ML scoring output | Map row ke domain candidate |
| Data reconciliation | Compare Parquet extract dengan DB |
| Offline migration | Batch import terkontrol |
| Regulatory dataset intake | Schema validation wajib |

Tetapi hati-hati: Parquet import ke OLTP system sebaiknya async, staged, validated, dan idempotent, sama seperti CSV/Excel import.

---

## 29. Parquet Read Pipeline

```text
Parquet file received
   │
   ▼
Intake/security
   │
   ▼
Schema validation
   │
   ▼
Read projected columns
   │
   ▼
Map record -> DTO
   │
   ▼
Validate row/domain
   │
   ▼
Stage rows
   │
   ▼
Apply domain mutation
```

### Read concern

| Concern | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| Schema mismatch | Validate schema before processing |
| Missing required column | Reject |
| Type mismatch | Reject |
| Huge file | Worker + page/row group streaming |
| Nested data unexpected | Strict schema |
| PII | Access control |
| Duplicate row | Business key validation |
| Partial failure | Staging table + error report |

---

## 30. Error Handling

Parquet export errors:

| Error | Example | Status |
|---|---|---|
| Schema invalid | Missing schema id | `FAILED_SCHEMA` |
| Query failed | DB error | `FAILED_SOURCE_QUERY` |
| Type mapping failed | Decimal overflow | `FAILED_MAPPING` |
| Writer failed | Parquet IOException | `FAILED_WRITE` |
| Upload failed | Object storage unavailable | `FAILED_STORAGE` |
| Manifest failed | Metadata write failed | `FAILED_MANIFEST` |
| Authorization failed | User cannot export PII | `REJECTED_UNAUTHORIZED` |

Error response untuk job status:

```json
{
  "exportJobId": "exp_001",
  "status": "FAILED",
  "failure": {
    "code": "FAILED_MAPPING",
    "message": "One or more rows could not be mapped to the dataset schema."
  }
}
```

Jangan expose raw SQL/object storage internals ke user.

---

### Create export job

```http
POST /v1/dataset-exports
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json
{
  "datasetName": "customer_snapshot",
  "datasetVersion": "v1",
  "format": "PARQUET",
  "filters": {
    "snapshotDate": "2026-07-13",
    "tenantId": "acme"
  }
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "exportJobId": "exp_001",
  "status": "PENDING",
  "links": {
    "status": "/v1/dataset-exports/exp_001",
    "manifest": "/v1/dataset-exports/exp_001/manifest"
  }
}
```

### Status

```http
GET /v1/dataset-exports/{exportJobId}
```

```json
{
  "exportJobId": "exp_001",
  "status": "SUCCESS",
  "datasetName": "customer_snapshot",
  "datasetVersion": "v1",
  "rowCount": 1000000,
  "fileCount": 4,
  "totalSizeBytes": 834123456,
  "schemaId": "customer_snapshot_v1",
  "storagePrefix": "datasets/customer_snapshot/version=v1/tenant_id=acme/snapshot_date=2026-07-13/"
}
```

Untuk Parquet, sering lebih baik expose manifest/presigned location ke authorized data platform, bukan file download biasa ke browser.

---

## 32. Async Worker Pattern

```text
API Pod
  ├─ authorize export
  ├─ create export job
  └─ return 202

Export Worker
  ├─ load schema
  ├─ query DB incrementally
  ├─ write temp parquet file(s)
  ├─ upload object storage
  ├─ write manifest
  ├─ update job status
  └─ publish event
```

Jangan generate Parquet besar di request path.

---

## 33. Performance Tuning Checklist

```text
Dataset shape
[ ] Columns selected intentionally
[ ] Types are compact and correct
[ ] No accidental huge string/blob columns

Writer config
[ ] Compression codec chosen
[ ] Row group size configured
[ ] Page size configured
[ ] Dictionary encoding considered
[ ] Target file size considered

Storage layout
[ ] Partition columns chosen
[ ] No high-cardinality partition
[ ] Small files monitored
[ ] Compaction strategy exists

Query/export source
[ ] MyBatis cursor/keyset pagination
[ ] No full List in memory
[ ] DB query indexed
[ ] Snapshot consistency considered

Consumer
[ ] Target engine compatibility tested
[ ] Schema readable by Spark/Trino/Athena/etc
[ ] Decimal/timestamp semantics verified
[ ] Partition discovery tested
```

---

## 34. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Pakai Parquet untuk user download biasa | User tidak bisa membuka/memahami |
| Tidak punya schema governance | Downstream break diam-diam |
| Money pakai double | Precision bug |
| Timestamp tanpa timezone semantics | Interpretasi beda antar engine |
| Partition by customer_id | Jutaan folder/small files |
| Banyak file kecil | Query lambat dan object storage overhead |
| Export semua row ke memory | OOM |
| Tidak simpan manifest | Downstream tidak tahu file mana valid |
| Tidak hitung checksum | Audit/integrity lemah |
| Tidak catat schema version | Reproducibility buruk |
| Parquet export berisi PII tanpa governance | Data leak |
| Tidak test di target analytics engine | File valid tapi tidak usable |
| Ubah column type diam-diam | Consumer gagal |
| Menganggap Parquet = database | Salah mental model |

---

## 35. Testing Strategy

```text
parquet-tests
│
├─ schema
│   ├─ schema-v1-valid.json
│   ├─ schema-v2-add-optional.json
│   ├─ schema-breaking-type-change.json
│   └─ schema-missing-required.json
│
├─ writer
│   ├─ write-small-dataset
│   ├─ decimal-field
│   ├─ timestamp-field
│   ├─ null-optional-fields
│   └─ compression-codec
│
├─ reader
│   ├─ read-back-generated-file
│   ├─ projection-read
│   ├─ schema-validation
│   └─ predicate-filter-smoke
│
├─ storage
│   ├─ partition-path
│   ├─ manifest-generation
│   ├─ checksum
│   └─ object-storage-upload
│
├─ compatibility
│   ├─ spark-read
│   ├─ trino-read
│   ├─ duckdb-read
│   └─ python-pyarrow-read
│
└─ security
    ├─ unauthorized-export
    ├─ pii-masking
    ├─ tenant-isolation
    └─ retention-policy
```

### Read-back test idea

```java
@Test
void generatedParquetShouldBeReadable() {
    Path file = writer.writeToTempFile(sampleRows.iterator());

    assertTrue(Files.size(file) > 0);

    // In real test: read back with parquet-avro and assert schema/values.
}
```

Compatibility testing dengan target engine sangat penting. File Parquet yang valid secara teknis masih bisa bermasalah jika decimal/timestamp/partition convention tidak sesuai platform data Anda.

Metrics:

| Metric | Type |
|---|---|
| `parquet_export_started_total{dataset}` | Counter |
| `parquet_export_completed_total{dataset,status}` | Counter |
| `parquet_export_duration_seconds{dataset}` | Histogram |
| `parquet_export_rows_total{dataset}` | Counter |
| `parquet_export_files_total{dataset}` | Histogram |
| `parquet_export_file_size_bytes{dataset}` | Histogram |
| `parquet_export_failed_total{reason}` | Counter |
| `parquet_export_small_file_total{dataset}` | Counter |
| `parquet_schema_compatibility_failed_total{dataset}` | Counter |

Structured log:

```json
{
  "event": "parquet_export_completed",
  "exportJobId": "exp_001",
  "tenantId": "acme",
  "datasetName": "customer_snapshot",
  "datasetVersion": "v1",
  "schemaId": "customer_snapshot_v1",
  "rowCount": 1000000,
  "fileCount": 4,
  "totalSizeBytes": 834123456,
  "storagePrefix": "datasets/customer_snapshot/version=v1/tenant_id=acme/snapshot_date=2026-07-13/",
  "durationMs": 182000,
  "status": "SUCCESS",
  "correlationId": "corr_001"
}
```

Jangan log row data atau PII. Log metadata dataset/export.

---

## 37. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| Parquet is analytics storage, not user-facing report | Jangan ganti Excel/CSV untuk user biasa |
| Schema is a contract | Column name/type/nullability adalah API |
| Object storage path is part of contract | Partition layout tidak boleh berubah sembarangan |
| Export must be async for large datasets | Hindari HTTP timeout/OOM |
| Query source must be incremental | Cursor/keyset, bukan full memory list |
| Manifest improves reproducibility | Downstream tahu file valid dan metadata |
| PII governance is mandatory | Data lake memperluas blast radius |
| Small files are operational debt | Perlu monitoring/compaction |
| Timestamp/decimal semantics must be explicit | Cross-engine compatibility |
| Test with target analytics engine | Valid Parquet belum tentu usable oleh consumer Anda |

---

## 38. Production Checklist

```text
Use case
[ ] Parquet memang dibutuhkan, bukan CSV/Excel
[ ] Consumer analytics jelas
[ ] Target engine compatibility diketahui
[ ] Dataset owner jelas

Schema
[ ] Schema id/version
[ ] Field type/nullability jelas
[ ] Decimal precision/scale jelas
[ ] Timestamp semantics jelas
[ ] PII classification
[ ] Compatibility rules

Export pipeline
[ ] Async job
[ ] Authorization
[ ] Incremental DB query
[ ] Mapping validation
[ ] Temp file strategy
[ ] Compression configured
[ ] Row group/page size configured
[ ] Object storage upload
[ ] Manifest generated
[ ] Job metadata persisted

Storage
[ ] Partition layout reviewed
[ ] No high-cardinality partitions
[ ] Small file monitoring
[ ] Retention/lifecycle policy
[ ] Encryption at rest
[ ] Access audit

Governance
[ ] Dataset catalog
[ ] Schema catalog
[ ] Lineage
[ ] Checksum
[ ] Export audit
[ ] Downstream contract

Testing
[ ] Read-back test
[ ] Decimal/timestamp test
[ ] Nullability test
[ ] Target engine test
[ ] Unauthorized export test
[ ] PII masking test
```

---

## 39. Kesimpulan Seri 13

Parquet processing production-ready adalah gabungan dari:

```text
schema contract
+ columnar mental model
+ incremental export
+ compression/row-group tuning
+ object storage layout
+ manifest/catalog
+ governance
+ analytics engine compatibility
```

Pola sehat:

```text
Export request
  -> authorize
  -> create async job
  -> load dataset schema
  -> query DB incrementally
  -> map rows to typed schema
  -> write Parquet temp file(s)
  -> upload to object storage
  -> write manifest
  -> persist metadata/checksum
  -> publish DatasetExported event
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text
Never treat Parquet as just “compressed CSV”.
Never write Parquet without schema/version governance.
Never export large datasets from memory.
Never partition by high-cardinality fields.
Never put PII into data lake exports without governance and audit.
```

---

# Seri 14/20 - Canonical Mapping Layer: Raw Record → DTO → Domain Command → Entity, Multi-format Mapping, Schema Compatibility & Anti-Corruption Layer

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **canonical mapping layer** untuk file processing di Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice.

Setelah kita punya CSV, Excel, JSON, XML, PDF, Word, Tika, dan Parquet, masalah berikutnya adalah:

```text id="8vajnh"
Bagaimana semua format itu masuk ke domain model
tanpa membuat domain tercemar oleh detail file format?
```

Pola sehatnya:

```text id="shcm6f"
External Format
  ├─ CSV row
  ├─ Excel row
  ├─ JSON object
  ├─ XML element
  ├─ Parquet row
  └─ Extracted document text/metadata
        │
        ▼
Raw Record
        │
        ▼
Format DTO
        │
        ▼
Canonical DTO
        │
        ▼
Domain Command
        │
        ▼
Application Service
        │
        ▼
Entity / Database / Event
```

Mapping layer adalah tempat kita mengendalikan **format compatibility, validation boundary, normalization, schema versioning, domain command construction, dan anti-corruption**.

MapStruct sering dipakai di Java untuk mapping bean karena ia adalah annotation processor yang menghasilkan mapper type-safe saat compile time; generated code memakai plain method invocation sehingga cepat, type-safe, dan mudah dibaca. ([mapstruct.org](https://mapstruct.org/documentation/stable/reference/html/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Diagram Besar

```text id="ps2e5s"
Canonical Mapping Layer
│
├─ 1. External Input Layer
│   ├─ CSVRecord
│   ├─ Excel Row/Cell
│   ├─ JSON DTO
│   ├─ XML DTO
│   ├─ Parquet Record
│   └─ Tika/PDF extracted text
│
├─ 2. Raw Record Layer
│   ├─ row number
│   ├─ sheet name
│   ├─ page number
│   ├─ source path
│   ├─ raw values
│   └─ source metadata
│
├─ 3. Format DTO Layer
│   ├─ CsvCustomerRow
│   ├─ ExcelCustomerRow
│   ├─ JsonCustomerPayload
│   ├─ XmlCustomerElement
│   └─ ParquetCustomerRecord
│
├─ 4. Canonical DTO Layer
│   ├─ CanonicalCustomerInput
│   ├─ normalized fields
│   ├─ typed values
│   ├─ source reference
│   └─ schema version
│
├─ 5. Validation Layer
│   ├─ required fields
│   ├─ type conversion
│   ├─ enum conversion
│   ├─ date/money conversion
│   ├─ cross-field validation
│   ├─ cross-record validation
│   └─ domain reference validation
│
├─ 6. Domain Command Layer
│   ├─ CreateCustomerCommand
│   ├─ UpsertCustomerCommand
│   ├─ AttachDocumentCommand
│   └─ GenerateReportCommand
│
├─ 7. Persistence/Event Layer
│   ├─ Entity
│   ├─ MyBatis mapper parameter
│   ├─ staging row
│   ├─ outbox event
│   └─ audit record
│
└─ 8. Anti-Corruption Layer
    ├─ external naming isolation
    ├─ legacy code conversion
    ├─ schema version adapter
    ├─ source-system mapping
    └─ domain invariant protection
```

Anti-Corruption Layer pada dasarnya menerjemahkan antara dua model domain agar model internal tidak tercemar oleh model sistem luar; Microservices.io mendeskripsikan solusinya sebagai layer yang menerjemahkan antara dua domain model, dan Martin Fowler menekankan isolating layer yang berbicara ke sistem lain melalui interface lama tetapi menyajikan model dalam istilah domain sendiri. ([microservices.io](https://microservices.io/patterns/refactoring/anti-corruption-layer.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3. Masalah yang Diselesaikan Mapping Layer

| Problem | Tanpa mapping layer | Dengan mapping layer |
|---|---|---|
| CSV header berubah | Domain service ikut berubah | Adapter CSV versioned |
| Excel cell type aneh | Entity menerima data salah | Conversion error terkontrol |
| JSON unknown field | Mass assignment risk | DTO strict |
| XML namespace legacy | Domain tercemar istilah legacy | XML adapter menerjemahkan |
| Parquet schema evolution | Consumer/producer kacau | Schema compatibility layer |
| Multiple source systems | Banyak `if sourceSystem` di service | Source-system adapter |
| Error reporting | Error tidak tahu row/field asal | Source reference disimpan |
| Audit | Nilai original hilang | Raw value + canonical value disimpan |
| Domain invariant | File format bisa bypass rule | Command divalidasi domain service |
| Testing | Sulit test format variants | Mapper bisa dites isolated |

---

## 4. Layering yang Direkomendasikan

```text id="q553ux"
External file/API structure
  -> Raw source record
  -> Format-specific DTO
  -> Canonical input DTO
  -> Domain command
  -> Domain service
  -> Persistence entity / event
```

### Jangan

```text id="4rv9px"
CSVRecord
  -> CustomerEntity
  -> MyBatis insert
```

### Jangan juga

```text id="pjdbmx"
Excel Row
  -> Domain Entity
  -> Direct mutation
```

### Lebih sehat

```text id="r374dv"
Excel Row
  -> RawCustomerExcelRow
  -> CustomerImportInput
  -> UpsertCustomerCommand
  -> CustomerApplicationService.upsert(command)
  -> CustomerEntity / MyBatis mapper
```

---

## 5. Istilah Penting

| Istilah | Makna |
|---|---|
| Raw Record | Representasi mentah dari sumber, plus source location |
| Format DTO | DTO yang masih mengikuti struktur format tertentu |
| Canonical DTO | DTO normal yang sama untuk banyak format |
| Domain Command | Perintah bisnis yang valid untuk application service |
| Entity | Model persistence internal |
| Staging Row | Representasi sementara untuk audit/batch |
| Source Reference | Informasi asal data: file, row, sheet, page, path |
| Normalization | Trim, case conversion, date format, enum code, null policy |
| Validation | Proses membuktikan data layak dipakai |
| Anti-Corruption Layer | Adapter yang melindungi domain dari model eksternal |

Jakarta Validation 3.1 mendefinisikan metadata model dan API untuk JavaBean/method validation, dan update 3.1 menargetkan Jakarta EE 11 dengan Java 17 sebagai minimum serta klarifikasi dukungan record validation; ini relevan jika canonical DTO/request DTO memakai Java records. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/bean-validation/3.1/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. Contoh Domain: Customer Import

Kita ingin mendukung banyak format:

```text id="wc601k"
Customer import sources:
│
├─ CSV
│   └─ customer_id,name,email,status,birth_date
│
├─ Excel
│   └─ Customer ID | Name | Email | Status | Birth Date
│
├─ JSON
│   └─ { "customerId": "...", "name": "...", ... }
│
├─ XML
│   └─ <customer><customerId>...</customerId></customer>
│
└─ Parquet
    └─ customer_id:string, name:string, status:string
```

Semua harus menjadi canonical input yang sama:

```java id="ffl9lx"
import java.time.LocalDate;

public record CanonicalCustomerInput(
        SourceReference source,
        String schemaVersion,
        String customerId,
        String name,
        String email,
        CustomerStatus status,
        String phone,
        LocalDate birthDate
) {}
```

---

## 7. Source Reference

Source reference penting untuk error report, audit, dan debugging.

```java id="rmvp02"
public record SourceReference(
        String fileId,
        String sourceFormat,
        String sourceSystem,
        String schemaVersion,
        Long rowNumber,
        String sheetName,
        Integer pageNumber,
        String jsonPointer,
        String xmlPath,
        String parquetColumnPath
) {
    public static SourceReference csv(String fileId, String sourceSystem, String schemaVersion, long rowNumber) {
        return new SourceReference(
                fileId,
                "CSV",
                sourceSystem,
                schemaVersion,
                rowNumber,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null
        );
    }

    public static SourceReference excel(
            String fileId,
            String sourceSystem,
            String schemaVersion,
            String sheetName,
            long rowNumber
    ) {
        return new SourceReference(
                fileId,
                "EXCEL",
                sourceSystem,
                schemaVersion,
                rowNumber,
                sheetName,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null
        );
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="kyx9j1"
Every mapped record must know where it came from.
Without source reference, error reporting is weak.
```

## 9. Canonical Mapper Manual

```java id="qz4xob"
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.format.DateTimeParseException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class CustomerCanonicalMapper {

    public MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> fromCsv(RawCustomerCsvRow raw) {
        return map(
                raw.source(),
                raw.customerId(),
                raw.name(),
                raw.email(),
                raw.status(),
                raw.phone(),
                raw.birthDate()
        );
    }

    public MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> fromExcel(RawCustomerExcelRow raw) {
        return map(
                raw.source(),
                raw.customerId(),
                raw.name(),
                raw.email(),
                raw.status(),
                raw.phone(),
                raw.birthDate()
        );
    }

    public MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> fromXml(RawCustomerXmlRecord raw) {
        return map(
                raw.source(),
                raw.customerId(),
                raw.name(),
                raw.email(),
                raw.status(),
                raw.phone(),
                raw.birthDate()
        );
    }

    private MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> map(
            SourceReference source,
            String customerIdRaw,
            String nameRaw,
            String emailRaw,
            String statusRaw,
            String phoneRaw,
            String birthDateRaw
    ) {
        List<MappingError> errors = new ArrayList<>();

        String customerId = normalizeId(customerIdRaw);
        String name = normalizeText(nameRaw);
        String email = normalizeEmail(emailRaw);
        CustomerStatus status = parseStatus(statusRaw, source, errors);
        LocalDate birthDate = parseDate(birthDateRaw, source, "birthDate", errors);

        require(customerId, source, "customerId", customerIdRaw, errors);
        require(name, source, "name", nameRaw, errors);
        require(email, source, "email", emailRaw, errors);

        if (email != null && !email.contains("@")) {
            errors.add(MappingError.of(source, "email", emailRaw, "INVALID_EMAIL", "Email is invalid"));
        }

        if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
            return MappingResult.invalid(errors);
        }

        return MappingResult.valid(new CanonicalCustomerInput(
                source,
                source.schemaVersion(),
                customerId,
                name,
                email,
                status,
                normalizeText(phoneRaw),
                birthDate
        ));
    }

    private String normalizeId(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : value.trim();
    }

    private String normalizeText(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : value.trim();
    }

    private String normalizeEmail(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : value.trim().toLowerCase();
    }

    private CustomerStatus parseStatus(
            String value,
            SourceReference source,
            List<MappingError> errors
    ) {
        String normalized = normalizeText(value);

        if (normalized == null) {
            errors.add(MappingError.of(source, "status", value, "REQUIRED_FIELD", "Status is required"));
            return null;
        }

        try {
            return CustomerStatus.valueOf(normalized.toUpperCase());
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            errors.add(MappingError.of(source, "status", value, "INVALID_STATUS", "Status must be ACTIVE or INACTIVE"));
            return null;
        }
    }

    private LocalDate parseDate(
            String value,
            SourceReference source,
            String field,
            List<MappingError> errors
    ) {
        String normalized = normalizeText(value);

        if (normalized == null) {
            return null;
        }

        try {
            return LocalDate.parse(normalized);
        } catch (DateTimeParseException e) {
            errors.add(MappingError.of(source, field, value, "INVALID_DATE", "Date must use yyyy-MM-dd"));
            return null;
        }
    }

    private void require(
            String normalized,
            SourceReference source,
            String field,
            String rawValue,
            List<MappingError> errors
    ) {
        if (normalized == null) {
            errors.add(MappingError.of(source, field, rawValue, "REQUIRED_FIELD", field + " is required"));
        }
    }
}
```

Manual mapper lebih verbose, tetapi jelas untuk logic conversion, error collection, dan source reference.

---

## 10. Mapping Result & Error Model

```java id="ocbvpc"
import java.util.List;

public record MappingResult<T>(
        T value,
        List<MappingError> errors
) {
    public static <T> MappingResult<T> valid(T value) {
        return new MappingResult<>(value, List.of());
    }

    public static <T> MappingResult<T> invalid(List<MappingError> errors) {
        return new MappingResult<>(null, List.copyOf(errors));
    }

    public boolean isValid() {
        return errors == null || errors.isEmpty();
    }
}
```

```java id="gxcj31"
public record MappingError(
        SourceReference source,
        String field,
        String rawValue,
        String errorCode,
        String message
) {
    public static MappingError of(
            SourceReference source,
            String field,
            String rawValue,
            String errorCode,
            String message
    ) {
        return new MappingError(source, field, rawValue, errorCode, message);
    }
}
```

Error model harus cukup kaya untuk dibuat menjadi CSV/Excel error report:

```text id="7mc80g"
fileId, sourceFormat, sheetName, rowNumber, field, rawValue, errorCode, message
```

---

```java id="6srlsp"
public record UpsertCustomerCommand(
        String tenantId,
        String customerId,
        String name,
        String email,
        CustomerStatus status,
        String phone,
        java.time.LocalDate birthDate,
        String sourceFileId,

```

Mapper:

```java id="glrrde"
public class CustomerCommandMapper {

    public UpsertCustomerCommand toCommand(
            CanonicalCustomerInput input,
            String tenantId,
            String requestedBy
    ) {
        return new UpsertCustomerCommand(
                tenantId,
                input.customerId(),
                input.name(),
                input.email(),
                input.status(),
                input.phone(),
                input.birthDate(),
                input.source().fileId(),
                input.source().sourceFormat(),
                input.source().rowNumber(),
                requestedBy
        );
    }
}
```

### Important rule

```text id="od3wkq"
Domain command tidak boleh membawa raw external field yang tidak dibutuhkan domain.
Raw value tetap disimpan untuk audit/error report di staging layer.
```

## 12. Domain Command → Entity / MyBatis Parameter

```java id="wuu6ya"
public record CustomerEntityRow(
        String tenantId,

        String name,

```

```java id="rgnzai"
public class CustomerEntityMapper {

    public CustomerEntityRow toEntityRow(UpsertCustomerCommand command) {
        return new CustomerEntityRow(
                command.tenantId(),
                command.customerId(),
                command.name(),
                command.email(),
                command.status().name(),
                command.phone(),
                command.birthDate(),

        );
    }
```

MyBatis XML:

```xml id="enp7x7"
<insert id="upsertCustomer">
    INSERT INTO customer (
        tenant_id,
        customer_id,
        name,
        email,
        status,
        phone,
        birth_date,
        updated_by,
        updated_at
    )
    VALUES (
        #{tenantId},
        #{customerId},
        #{name},
        #{email},
        #{status},
        #{phone},
        #{birthDate},
        #{updatedBy},
        now()
    )
    ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, customer_id)
    DO UPDATE SET
        name = EXCLUDED.name,
        email = EXCLUDED.email,
        status = EXCLUDED.status,
        phone = EXCLUDED.phone,
        birth_date = EXCLUDED.birth_date,
        updated_by = EXCLUDED.updated_by,
        updated_at = now()
</insert>
```

Entity mapper harus dekat persistence layer. Jangan campur logic parser CSV/Excel di mapper MyBatis.

## 13. Multi-Format Adapter Pattern

```java id="8marzl"
public interface CustomerImportAdapter<T> {
    MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> toCanonical(T raw);
    String supportedFormat();
    String supportedSchemaVersion();
}
```

CSV adapter:

```java id="ngs0oj"
public class CsvCustomerImportAdapter
        implements CustomerImportAdapter<RawCustomerCsvRow> {

    private final CustomerCanonicalMapper mapper;

    public CsvCustomerImportAdapter(CustomerCanonicalMapper mapper) {
        this.mapper = mapper;
    }

    @Override
    public MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> toCanonical(RawCustomerCsvRow raw) {
        return mapper.fromCsv(raw);
    }

    @Override
    public String supportedFormat() {
        return "CSV";
    }

    @Override
    public String supportedSchemaVersion() {
        return "customer-import-v1";
    }
}
```

Excel adapter:

```java id="zv7fr1"
public class ExcelCustomerImportAdapter
        implements CustomerImportAdapter<RawCustomerExcelRow> {

    private final CustomerCanonicalMapper mapper;

    public ExcelCustomerImportAdapter(CustomerCanonicalMapper mapper) {
        this.mapper = mapper;
    }

    @Override
    public MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> toCanonical(RawCustomerExcelRow raw) {
        return mapper.fromExcel(raw);
    }

    @Override
    public String supportedFormat() {
        return "EXCEL";
    }

    @Override
    public String supportedSchemaVersion() {
        return "customer-import-v1";
    }
}
```

---

## 14. Adapter Registry

```java id="ng0zn8"
import java.util.Map;

public class CustomerImportAdapterRegistry {

    private final Map<String, CustomerImportAdapter<?>> adapters;

    public CustomerImportAdapterRegistry(Map<String, CustomerImportAdapter<?>> adapters) {
        this.adapters = adapters;
    }

    public CustomerImportAdapter<?> find(String format, String schemaVersion) {
        String key = key(format, schemaVersion);

        CustomerImportAdapter<?> adapter = adapters.get(key);

        if (adapter == null) {
            throw new UnsupportedImportSchemaException(
                    "Unsupported import format/schema: " + key
            );
        }

        return adapter;
    }

    public static String key(String format, String schemaVersion) {
        return format + ":" + schemaVersion;
    }
}
```

Registry membuat dukungan schema version eksplisit:

```text id="ap51e8"
CSV:customer-import-v1
CSV:customer-import-v2
EXCEL:customer-import-v1
JSON:customer-import-v2
XML:customer-import-v1
```

---

## 15. Schema Version Adapter

Ketika schema berubah, jangan tambahkan `if v1/v2` bertebaran.

```text id="vwpuk8"
CSV v1:
customer_id,name,email,status

CSV v2:
customer_id,full_name,email,status,phone,birth_date
```

Buat adapter terpisah:

```java id="59nuzw"
public class CsvCustomerImportV1Adapter
        implements CustomerImportAdapter<RawCustomerCsvV1Row> {
    // v1 mapping rules
}
```

```java id="wp9iwm"
public class CsvCustomerImportV2Adapter
        implements CustomerImportAdapter<RawCustomerCsvV2Row> {
    // v2 mapping rules
}
```

### Compatibility table

| Change | Adapter strategy |
|---|---|
| Add optional field | Same adapter bisa handle |
| Rename field | New adapter/version |
| Change date format | New adapter/version |
| Add required field | New adapter/version |
| Change enum semantics | New adapter/version |
| Remove field | New adapter/version |
| Change business meaning | New adapter/version, even if type same |

---

## 16. Normalization Rules

Normalization harus eksplisit dan testable.

| Field type | Normalization |
|---|---|
| ID | Trim, preserve leading zero, uppercase only if business code says so |
| Name | Trim, collapse excessive whitespace optionally |
| Email | Trim + lowercase domain/full email depending policy |
| Phone | Normalize to E.164 only if rules clear |
| Date | ISO `yyyy-MM-dd` |
| Timestamp | UTC `Instant` |
| Money | `BigDecimal` scale/rounding |
| Enum | Map external code → internal enum |
| Boolean | Define allowed values |
| Text | Remove invalid control chars, preserve meaningful unicode |
| Empty string | Convert to null or reject based on field policy |

### Example enum code mapper

```java id="u8v3i5"
import java.util.Map;

public class CustomerStatusCodeMapper {

    private static final Map<String, CustomerStatus> EXTERNAL_CODES = Map.of(
            "A", CustomerStatus.ACTIVE,
            "ACTIVE", CustomerStatus.ACTIVE,
            "I", CustomerStatus.INACTIVE,
            "INACTIVE", CustomerStatus.INACTIVE
    );

    public CustomerStatus map(String raw) {
        if (raw == null || raw.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }

        return EXTERNAL_CODES.get(raw.trim().toUpperCase());
    }
}
```

---

## 17. Validation Stages

```text id="04oq2l"
Validation Stages
│
├─ 1. Syntax / Format Validation
│   ├─ malformed CSV
│   ├─ invalid Excel workbook
│   ├─ malformed JSON
│   ├─ invalid XML
│   └─ unreadable Parquet
│
├─ 2. Schema Validation
│   ├─ required headers/elements/fields
│   ├─ data type expected
│   ├─ unknown fields
│   └─ version compatibility
│
├─ 3. Mapping Validation
│   ├─ parse date
│   ├─ parse enum
│   ├─ parse money
│   ├─ normalize ID
│   └─ field length
│
├─ 4. Cross-Field Validation
│   ├─ start <= end
│   ├─ status requires reason
│   └─ currency + amount pair
│
├─ 5. Cross-Record Validation
│   ├─ duplicate key in file
│   ├─ inconsistent duplicate values
│   └─ parent-child record relation
│
└─ 6. Domain Validation
    ├─ tenant ownership
    ├─ reference exists
    ├─ state transition allowed
    ├─ permission allowed
    └─ uniqueness in DB
```

Jangan meletakkan semua validasi di mapper. Mapper fokus pada conversion. Domain validation tetap di application/domain service.

---

## 18. Jakarta Validation di Canonical DTO

```java id="r8mhl6"
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;

public record CanonicalCustomerInput(
        @NotNull SourceReference source,

        @NotBlank String schemaVersion,

        @NotBlank
        @Size(max = 64)
        String customerId,

        @NotBlank
        @Size(max = 200)
        String name,

        @NotBlank
        @Email
        @Size(max = 320)
        String email,

        @NotNull
        CustomerStatus status,

        @Size(max = 32)
        String phone,

        java.time.LocalDate birthDate
) {}
```

Bean validation cocok untuk required, length, dan format dasar. Untuk error report yang butuh raw value dan source reference, Anda tetap perlu custom error mapping agar violation bisa dikaitkan ke row/sheet/path.

---

## 19. MapStruct untuk Mapping Sederhana

MapStruct cocok untuk mapping yang **deterministic dan structurally similar**.

```java id="ca3a07"
import org.mapstruct.Mapper;
import org.mapstruct.Mapping;

@Mapper(componentModel = "jakarta")
public interface CustomerResponseMapper {

    @Mapping(target = "status", expression = "java(entity.status().name())")
    CustomerResponse toResponse(CustomerEntityRow entity);
}
```

MapStruct adalah compile-time mapper; dokumentasi stable MapStruct menjelaskan Anda cukup membuat mapper interface, lalu annotation processor menghasilkan implementasi type-safe. ([mapstruct.org](https://mapstruct.org/documentation/stable/reference/html/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Cocok untuk MapStruct

| Mapping | Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Entity → Response DTO | Ya |
| Domain result → API response | Ya |
| Command → MyBatis parameter | Ya |
| Canonical DTO → command sederhana | Ya |
| CSV raw string → typed canonical dengan banyak error collection | Kurang ideal |
| Multi-error mapping dengan raw value/source reference | Manual lebih jelas |
| Complex version adapter | Manual/explicit adapter lebih baik |

Rule:

```text id="yb8619"
Use MapStruct for straightforward bean mapping.
Use manual mapper for parsing, normalization, error accumulation, and domain-specific conversion.
```

---

## 20. Jackson `convertValue` - Kapan Boleh?

Jackson `ObjectMapper` menyediakan fungsi read/write JSON dan conversion POJO/tree; `convertValue` dapat melakukan conversion dua tahap dari object ke target type. ([fasterxml.github.io](https://fasterxml.github.io/jackson-databind/javadoc/2.8/com/fasterxml/jackson/databind/ObjectMapper.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Namun, jangan jadikan `convertValue` sebagai mapping domain utama jika butuh auditability.

### Boleh

```java id="lrvvqm"
CustomerResponse response = objectMapper.convertValue(result, CustomerResponse.class);
```

Untuk mapping sederhana dan internal.

### Hati-hati

```java id="q3l9nm"
CustomerEntity entity = objectMapper.convertValue(requestBody, CustomerEntity.class);
```

Risiko:

| Risiko | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Mass assignment | Field internal ikut termap |
| Unknown conversion | Conversion error kurang domain-specific |
| Raw value hilang | Error report buruk |
| Mapping implicit | Sulit review |
| Version handling kabur | Schema compatibility sulit |

---

## 21. Anti-Corruption Layer untuk Legacy Source

Contoh legacy source mengirim:

```csv id="1667fw"
CUST_NO,FULL_NM,EMAIL_ADDR,STAT_CD,DOB
000123,Alice,alice@example.com,A,1990-01-01
```

Internal canonical:

```java id="wyudj7"
CanonicalCustomerInput(
    customerId = "000123",
    name = "Alice",
    email = "alice@example.com",
    status = ACTIVE,
    birthDate = 1990-01-01
)
```

Adapter:

```java id="fiv3sj"
public class LegacyCustomerCsvAdapter {

    private final CustomerStatusCodeMapper statusMapper = new CustomerStatusCodeMapper();

    public MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> toCanonical(RawLegacyCustomerCsvRow raw) {
        List<MappingError> errors = new ArrayList<>();

        CustomerStatus status = statusMapper.map(raw.statCd());

        if (status == null) {
            errors.add(MappingError.of(
                    raw.source(),
                    "STAT_CD",
                    raw.statCd(),
                    "INVALID_STATUS_CODE",
                    "Legacy status code is not supported"
            ));
        }

        if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
            return MappingResult.invalid(errors);
        }

        return MappingResult.valid(new CanonicalCustomerInput(
                raw.source(),
                raw.source().schemaVersion(),
                normalizeId(raw.custNo()),
                normalizeText(raw.fullNm()),
                normalizeEmail(raw.emailAddr()),
                status,
                null,
                parseDate(raw.dob())
        ));
    }

    private String normalizeId(String value) {
        return value == null ? null : value.trim();
    }

    private String normalizeText(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : value.trim();
    }

    private String normalizeEmail(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : value.trim().toLowerCase();
    }

    private LocalDate parseDate(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : LocalDate.parse(value.trim());
    }
}
```

Internal domain tidak pernah tahu istilah `CUST_NO`, `FULL_NM`, atau `STAT_CD`.

---

## 22. Staging Row: Simpan Raw + Canonical + Error

Untuk batch import enterprise, staging row sangat berguna.

```sql id="wqtqx2"
CREATE TABLE customer_import_staging (
    job_id              TEXT NOT NULL,
    record_number       BIGINT NOT NULL,

    source_format       TEXT NOT NULL,
    source_system       TEXT,
    schema_version      TEXT NOT NULL,
    sheet_name          TEXT,
    source_path         TEXT,

    raw_json            JSONB NOT NULL,
    canonical_json      JSONB,
    row_status          TEXT NOT NULL,
    error_json          JSONB,

    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (job_id, record_number)
);
```

### Kenapa simpan raw JSON?

| Alasan | Manfaat |
|---|---|
| Audit | Bisa lihat nilai original |
| Debugging | Bisa re-check mapping |
| Reprocessing | Bisa map ulang dengan adapter baru |
| Error report | Raw value tersedia |
| Dispute resolution | Bisa membuktikan input user/source |
| Data quality analysis | Bisa hitung error by field/source |

Raw JSON di sini bukan domain entity. Ini snapshot teknis import.

---

## 23. Staging Row Model

```java id="i35z9n"
public record CustomerImportStagingRow(
        String jobId,
        long recordNumber,
        String sourceFormat,
        String sourceSystem,
        String schemaVersion,
        String sheetName,
        String sourcePath,
        String rawJson,
        String canonicalJson,
        String rowStatus,
        String errorJson
) {}
```

Mapping to staging:

```java id="2905f8"
public class CustomerStagingRowMapper {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    public CustomerStagingRowMapper(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }

    public CustomerImportStagingRow toStagingRow(
            String jobId,
            long recordNumber,
            Object rawRecord,
            MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> result
    ) {
        SourceReference source = extractSource(rawRecord);

        return new CustomerImportStagingRow(
                jobId,
                recordNumber,
                source.sourceFormat(),
                source.sourceSystem(),
                source.schemaVersion(),
                source.sheetName(),
                sourcePath(source),
                toJson(rawRecord),
                result.isValid() ? toJson(result.value()) : null,
                result.isValid() ? "VALID" : "INVALID",
                result.isValid() ? null : toJson(result.errors())
        );
    }

    private SourceReference extractSource(Object rawRecord) {
        if (rawRecord instanceof RawCustomerCsvRow csv) {
            return csv.source();
        }
        if (rawRecord instanceof RawCustomerExcelRow excel) {
            return excel.source();
        }
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported raw record type: " + rawRecord.getClass());
    }

    private String sourcePath(SourceReference source) {
        if (source.sheetName() != null && source.rowNumber() != null) {
            return source.sheetName() + "!" + source.rowNumber();
        }
        if (source.rowNumber() != null) {
            return "row " + source.rowNumber();
        }
        return null;
    }

    private String toJson(Object value) {
        try {
            return objectMapper.writeValueAsString(value);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new MappingPersistenceException("Failed to serialize mapping snapshot", e);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 24. Error Report dari Mapping Errors

```csv id="b7rcwi"
source_format,sheet_name,row_number,field,raw_value,error_code,message
CSV,,15,email,not-an-email,INVALID_EMAIL,Email is invalid
EXCEL,Customers,20,status,UNKNOWN,INVALID_STATUS,Status must be ACTIVE or INACTIVE
XML,,31,birthDate,31-02-2026,INVALID_DATE,Date must use yyyy-MM-dd
```

Excel error report bisa berisi:

| Source Format | Sheet | Row | Field | Raw Value | Error Code | Message |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|---|
| EXCEL | Customers | 20 | Status | UNKNOWN | INVALID_STATUS | Status must be ACTIVE or INACTIVE |

Mapping error harus user-actionable.

---

## 25. Cross-Record Validation

Setelah canonical DTO terbentuk, lakukan cross-record validation.

```java id="t38byx"
import java.util.*;

public class CustomerImportCrossRecordValidator {

    public List<MappingError> validateDuplicateCustomerId(
            List<CanonicalCustomerInput> inputs
    ) {
        Map<String, CanonicalCustomerInput> seen = new HashMap<>();
        List<MappingError> errors = new ArrayList<>();

        for (CanonicalCustomerInput input : inputs) {
            CanonicalCustomerInput previous = seen.putIfAbsent(input.customerId(), input);

            if (previous != null) {
                errors.add(MappingError.of(
                        input.source(),
                        "customerId",
                        input.customerId(),
                        "DUPLICATE_CUSTOMER_ID_IN_FILE",
                        "Customer ID already appears earlier in this import"
                ));
            }
        }

        return errors;
    }
}
```

Cross-record validation butuh canonical shape. Jangan lakukan duplicate detection di parser format jika semua format harus konsisten.

---

## 26. Domain Validation

Domain validation bukan tugas mapper.

```java id="z0hutt"
public class CustomerApplicationService {

    private final CustomerRepository customerRepository;

    public CustomerResult upsert(UpsertCustomerCommand command) {
        assertTenantPermission(command.tenantId(), command.requestedBy());

        if (!isAllowedStatus(command.status())) {
            throw new DomainValidationException("STATUS_NOT_ALLOWED");
        }

        // Reference ownership, state transition, uniqueness, etc.
        return customerRepository.upsert(command);
    }
}
```

Mapping layer memastikan data menjadi command yang well-formed. Domain service memastikan command boleh dijalankan menurut aturan bisnis.

---

## 27. Idempotency Mapping

File import harus bisa retry tanpa double mutation.

```text id="w03avg"
Idempotency key:
tenantId + importType + sourceFileChecksum + sourceRecordKey + schemaVersion
```

Contoh:

```java id="585kqk"
public record ImportRecordIdempotencyKey(
        String tenantId,
        String importType,
        String fileSha256,
        String sourceRecordBusinessKey,
        String schemaVersion
) {}
```

Untuk customer:

```text id="jnwqz6"
tenant_id + "CUSTOMER_IMPORT" + file_sha256 + customer_id + schema_version
```

Gunakan idempotency di staging/apply step agar retry job tidak membuat duplicate side effect.

---

## 28. Outbox Event Mapping

Jika import berhasil, mungkin publish event.

```java id="f1fbrm"
public record CustomerUpsertedEventPayload(
        String tenantId,
        String customerId,
        String status,
        String sourceFileId,
        Long sourceRowNumber
) {}
```

Mapper:

```java id="yxx8ll"
public class CustomerEventMapper {

    public CustomerUpsertedEventPayload toEvent(
            UpsertCustomerCommand command
    ) {
        return new CustomerUpsertedEventPayload(
                command.tenantId(),
                command.customerId(),
                command.status().name(),
                command.sourceFileId(),
                command.sourceRowNumber()
        );
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="qwr0dw"
Event payload should use event contract DTO,
not database entity and not raw file row.
```

## 30. End-to-End Import Flow

```text id="ka0kq7"
1. Upload file
2. Intake validates and stages file
3. Worker reads file by format
4. Parser emits RawCustomer* rows
5. Adapter maps raw rows to CanonicalCustomerInput
6. Mapping errors go to staging/error report
7. Valid canonical inputs go to cross-record validation
8. Valid inputs map to UpsertCustomerCommand
9. Application service performs domain validation and upsert
10. Outbox event emitted
11. Job status and report updated
```

Pseudo-code:

```java id="a4pfl9"
public class CustomerImportWorker {

    public void process(CustomerImportJob job) {
        List<CanonicalCustomerInput> validInputs = new ArrayList<>();
        List<MappingError> allErrors = new ArrayList<>();

        for (Object rawRecord : parser.parse(job)) {
            MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> result = adapter.toCanonical(rawRecord);

            stagingRepository.save(rawRecord, result);

            if (result.isValid()) {
                validInputs.add(result.value());
            } else {
                allErrors.addAll(result.errors());
            }
        }

        List<MappingError> crossRecordErrors =
                crossRecordValidator.validateDuplicateCustomerId(validInputs);

        allErrors.addAll(crossRecordErrors);

        if (!allErrors.isEmpty() && job.policy().failOnAnyError()) {
            jobRepository.markFailed(job.id(), "VALIDATION_FAILED");
            errorReportService.generate(job.id(), allErrors);
            return;
        }

        for (CanonicalCustomerInput input : validInputs) {
            UpsertCustomerCommand command =
                    commandMapper.toCommand(input, job.tenantId(), job.requestedBy());

            customerApplicationService.upsert(command);
        }

        jobRepository.markCompleted(job.id());
    }
}
```

---

## 31. Mapping Testing Strategy

```text id="y0gm7d"
mapping-tests
│
├─ raw-to-canonical
│   ├─ csv-v1-valid
│   ├─ csv-v2-valid
│   ├─ excel-v1-valid
│   ├─ xml-v1-valid
│   └─ legacy-csv-valid
│
├─ normalization
│   ├─ trim-whitespace
│   ├─ email-lowercase
│   ├─ id-leading-zero-preserved
│   ├─ date-iso-parse
│   └─ money-scale
│
├─ validation-errors
│   ├─ missing-required
│   ├─ invalid-enum
│   ├─ invalid-date
│   ├─ invalid-email
│   └─ over-length-field
│
├─ source-reference
│   ├─ csv-row-number
│   ├─ excel-sheet-row
│   ├─ xml-path
│   └─ json-pointer
│
├─ schema-version
│   ├─ v1-supported
│   ├─ v2-supported
│   └─ unsupported-version
│
└─ domain-command
    ├─ canonical-to-command
    ├─ command-no-raw-fields
    └─ event-payload-mapping
```

### Unit test example

```java id="6wio9r"
@Test
void shouldPreserveLeadingZeroCustomerId() {
    RawCustomerCsvRow raw = new RawCustomerCsvRow(
            SourceReference.csv("file_001", "CRM", "customer-import-v1", 2),
            "000123",
            " Alice ",
            "ALICE@EXAMPLE.COM ",
            "ACTIVE",
            null,
            "1990-01-01"
    );

    MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> result = mapper.fromCsv(raw);

    assertTrue(result.isValid());
    assertEquals("000123", result.value().customerId());
    assertEquals("alice@example.com", result.value().email());
}
```

### Error test

```java id="97xias"
@Test
void shouldReturnSourceAwareErrorForInvalidStatus() {
    RawCustomerCsvRow raw = new RawCustomerCsvRow(
            SourceReference.csv("file_001", "CRM", "customer-import-v1", 5),
            "C001",
            "Alice",
            "alice@example.com",
            "UNKNOWN",
            null,
            null
    );

    MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> result = mapper.fromCsv(raw);

    assertFalse(result.isValid());
    assertEquals(5L, result.errors().get(0).source().rowNumber());
    assertEquals("status", result.errors().get(0).field());
    assertEquals("INVALID_STATUS", result.errors().get(0).errorCode());
}
```
