## 33. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| CSV/Excel langsung ke entity | Domain tercemar format eksternal |
| Satu DTO untuk semua layer | Boundary kabur |
| Hilangkan raw value terlalu cepat | Error report dan audit buruk |
| Tidak simpan source reference | User tidak tahu row mana yang salah |
| Normalization tersebar di banyak tempat | Inconsistent behavior |
| Mapping implicit/reflection-heavy | Sulit review dan debug |
| Unknown schema diproses best-effort | Data corruption |
| Version handling pakai if bertebaran | Sulit maintain |
| Domain validation dimasukkan semua ke mapper | Mapper terlalu gemuk |
| MapStruct dipakai untuk parsing kompleks | Error collection sulit |
| Event payload dari entity langsung | Contract event rapuh |
| Tidak test leading zero ID | Data ID rusak |
| Money pakai double | Precision bug |
| Date parsing ikut locale default | Environment-dependent bug |

---

## 34. Production Checklist

```text id="c8fdcy"
Layering
[ ] Raw record model ada
[ ] Format DTO dipisah dari canonical DTO
[ ] Canonical DTO dipisah dari domain command
[ ] Domain command dipisah dari entity
[ ] Event DTO dipisah dari entity

Source reference
[ ] fileId
[ ] source format
[ ] source system
[ ] schema version
[ ] row number / sheet / path
[ ] raw value available for errors

Mapping
[ ] Normalization rules documented
[ ] Enum/code mapping explicit
[ ] Date/time mapping explicit
[ ] Money/decimal mapping explicit
[ ] ID leading zero preserved
[ ] Null/blank policy explicit

Schema/versioning
[ ] Adapter per format/version
[ ] Unsupported schema rejected
[ ] Compatibility rules
[ ] Template/header/schema tests

Validation
[ ] Mapping validation
[ ] Bean validation where useful
[ ] Cross-field validation
[ ] Cross-record validation
[ ] Domain validation in service

Persistence/audit
[ ] Staging row stores raw snapshot
[ ] Canonical snapshot optional/available
[ ] Error JSON stored
[ ] Job status updated
[ ] Idempotency key

Testing
[ ] Raw-to-canonical tests
[ ] Canonical-to-command tests
[ ] Error source reference tests
[ ] Schema version tests
[ ] Golden fixture tests per format
```

---

## 35. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| External format must not leak into domain | Domain tidak tahu CSV header/XML namespace |
| Raw value must survive until error reporting | User perlu tahu nilai yang salah |
| Every record must carry source reference | Audit/debug/error report |
| Canonical DTO is not domain command | Canonical masih input model |
| Domain command is not entity | Command menyatakan intent bisnis |
| Mapper should not bypass domain service | Domain invariant tetap dijaga |
| Version adapter must be explicit | Jangan best-effort schema unknown |
| Normalization must be deterministic | Hasil sama di semua environment |
| Mapping errors must be collectable | Bulk import butuh partial error report |
| Anti-corruption layer protects long-term maintainability | Legacy/source model tidak mencemari core |

---

## 36. Kesimpulan Seri 14

Canonical mapping layer adalah pusat kendali agar banyak format file bisa masuk ke sistem tanpa merusak domain model.

Pola sehat:

```text id="3qcc57"
CSV / Excel / JSON / XML / Parquet
  -> raw record with source reference
  -> format adapter
  -> canonical DTO
  -> mapping validation
  -> domain command
  -> domain/application service
  -> entity/event/audit
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="0ebhs5"
Never map external file rows directly to database entities.
Never lose source reference before validation completes.
Never let legacy/source-system names leak into domain commands.
Never process unsupported schema versions best-effort.
Never confuse mapping validation with domain validation.
```

---

## Seri 14 selesai

Rekomendasi lanjut: **Seri 15 - Validation & Error Model: Row-Level Error, Partial Success, Reject Report, Severity, Error Taxonomy, dan User-Fixable Feedback untuk File Import Pipeline**.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:51:10 PM

# Seri 15/20 - Validation & Error Model: Row-Level Error, Partial Success, Reject Report, Severity, Error Taxonomy & User-Fixable Feedback

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **validation dan error model** untuk file import pipeline.

Setelah file berhasil dibaca dan dimapping ke canonical DTO, sistem harus bisa menjawab:

```text id="ulg3no"
Apakah file ini valid?
Kalau tidak valid, bagian mana yang salah?
Apakah seluruh file ditolak?
Apakah sebagian row boleh diproses?
Bagaimana user memperbaiki file?
Bagaimana developer/operator menginvestigasi?
Bagaimana status job direpresentasikan secara konsisten?
```

Untuk HTTP error response, standar modern yang relevan adalah **RFC 9457 Problem Details for HTTP APIs**, yang mendefinisikan format machine-readable untuk error HTTP dan secara resmi menggantikan RFC 7807. ([rfc-editor.org](https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc9457/)) Untuk validasi object/DTO di Java/Jakarta, **Jakarta Validation 3.1** mendefinisikan API validasi JavaBean/method dan membutuhkan Java SE 17 atau lebih tinggi, sesuai stack modern Java 17+. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/bean-validation/3.1/))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="f82t4e"
File Validation & Error Model
│
├─ 1. Boundary Error
│   ├─ missing file
│   ├─ unsupported content type
│   ├─ too large
│   ├─ unsafe filename
│   └─ authz failure
│
├─ 2. File-Level Error
│   ├─ malformed CSV/Excel/XML/JSON
│   ├─ missing required sheet
│   ├─ missing required header
│   ├─ wrong schema version
│   └─ encrypted/unsupported document
│
├─ 3. Record-Level Error
│   ├─ required field missing
│   ├─ invalid enum
│   ├─ invalid date
│   ├─ invalid money
│   ├─ invalid email
│   └─ field too long
│
├─ 4. Cross-Record Error
│   ├─ duplicate key in file
│   ├─ parent row missing
│   ├─ inconsistent duplicate
│   └─ row ordering issue
│
├─ 5. Domain Error
│   ├─ reference not found
│   ├─ tenant mismatch
│   ├─ state transition invalid
│   ├─ duplicate existing object
│   └─ permission denied
│
├─ 6. System Error
│   ├─ DB unavailable
│   ├─ object storage unavailable
│   ├─ parser timeout
│   ├─ worker killed
│   └─ downstream failure
│
└─ 7. Feedback Output
    ├─ API error response
    ├─ job status
    ├─ CSV/Excel reject report
    ├─ metrics/logs
    └─ audit trail
```

---

## 3. Error Boundary: HTTP Error vs Import Job Error

Penting membedakan dua jenis error:

| Jenis error | Terjadi kapan | Response |
|---|---|---|
| **Synchronous HTTP error** | Request tidak bisa diterima sama sekali | `400`, `401`, `403`, `413`, `415`, `422` |
| **Asynchronous job error** | File sudah diterima, tapi processing gagal/partial | `202 Accepted` awal, lalu status job `FAILED` / `PARTIAL_SUCCESS` |

Contoh:

```text id="kgq7f3"
POST /customer-imports
  ├─ no file part              -> HTTP 400
  ├─ file too large            -> HTTP 413
  ├─ unsupported file type     -> HTTP 415
  ├─ unauthorized              -> HTTP 403
  └─ accepted for processing   -> HTTP 202 + jobId
```

Setelah `202 Accepted`, error parsing/row/domain masuk ke **job status + error report**, bukan lagi HTTP error upload.

---

## 4. Status Code Mapping

| Scenario | HTTP status | Kenapa |
|---|---:|---|
| File part missing | `400 Bad Request` | Request invalid |
| Invalid metadata JSON | `400 Bad Request` | Request malformed |
| Unauthorized upload | `401 Unauthorized` | Belum authenticated |
| Forbidden import type | `403 Forbidden` | Authenticated tapi tidak berhak |
| File too large | `413 Content Too Large` | Payload melebihi limit |
| Unsupported media type | `415 Unsupported Media Type` | Format/MIME tidak didukung |
| Boundary valid, async job created | `202 Accepted` | Processing lanjut async |
| Synchronous validation failed | `422 Unprocessable Content` | Payload syntax OK, semantic invalid |
| Job not found | `404 Not Found` | Job tidak ada/tidak accessible |
| Duplicate idempotent request | `200` atau `202` | Tergantung return existing job/result |
| System temporarily unavailable | `503 Service Unavailable` | Storage/worker/downstream unavailable |

Catatan: gunakan `422` hanya kalau organisasi/API guideline Anda memang memakainya. Sebagian API memilih `400` untuk semua validation error. Yang penting konsisten.

---

## 5. Problem Details untuk HTTP Error

RFC 9457 menyediakan format problem detail agar error HTTP machine-readable tanpa membuat format baru untuk setiap API. ([rfc-editor.org](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9457.html))

### 5.1 Base response

```json id="0n3pnl"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.example/problems/unsupported-file-type",
  "title": "Unsupported file type",
  "status": 415,
  "detail": "Only CSV and XLSX files are supported for customer import.",
  "instance": "/v1/customer-imports/requests/req_001",
  "correlationId": "corr_001",
  "errorCode": "UNSUPPORTED_FILE_TYPE"
}
```

### 5.2 Validation problem with details

```json id="xrgkto"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.example/problems/request-validation-failed",
  "title": "Request validation failed",
  "status": 400,
  "detail": "One or more request fields are invalid.",
  "instance": "/v1/customer-imports",
  "correlationId": "corr_001",
  "errorCode": "REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED",
  "violations": [
    {
      "field": "metadata.schemaVersion",
      "code": "REQUIRED_FIELD",
      "message": "schemaVersion is required"
    }
  ]
}
```

Gunakan problem details untuk **HTTP boundary errors**. Untuk row-level import errors, gunakan report terpisah karena bisa ribuan/jutaan row.

## 6. Import Job Status Model

```text id="se0aer"
PENDING
VALIDATING_FILE
PARSING
VALIDATING_RECORDS
STAGING
APPLYING
GENERATING_ERROR_REPORT
SUCCESS
PARTIAL_SUCCESS
FAILED
REJECTED
CANCELLED
```

### Status semantics

| Status | Makna |
|---|---|
| `PENDING` | Job dibuat, belum diproses |
| `VALIDATING_FILE` | Validasi file-level/schema/header/sheet |
| `PARSING` | Parser membaca records |
| `VALIDATING_RECORDS` | Row/cross-row/domain validation |
| `STAGING` | Data valid/error disimpan di staging |
| `APPLYING` | Data valid diterapkan ke domain tables |
| `GENERATING_ERROR_REPORT` | Membuat CSV/XLSX error report |
| `SUCCESS` | Semua valid dan diterapkan |
| `PARTIAL_SUCCESS` | Sebagian berhasil, sebagian error |
| `FAILED` | System/fatal error; tidak selesai normal |
| `REJECTED` | Ditolak karena file/schema/security/validation policy |
| `CANCELLED` | Dibatalkan user/system |

## 9. Error Taxonomy

Error code harus stabil dan bisa dipakai:

```text id="p36i7u"
- oleh user interface
- oleh support team
- oleh metrics/alerting
- oleh automated retry/reconciliation
- oleh error report
- oleh audit
```

### Error code categories

```text id="wfffnx"
BOUNDARY.*
FILE.*
SCHEMA.*
PARSER.*
MAPPING.*
VALIDATION.*
DOMAIN.*
SECURITY.*
SYSTEM.*
```

Contoh:

| Category | Code | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `BOUNDARY` | `MISSING_FILE_PART` | Multipart tidak berisi file |
| `FILE` | `FILE_TOO_LARGE` | Melebihi limit |
| `FILE` | `UNSUPPORTED_FILE_TYPE` | Extension/MIME tidak didukung |
| `SCHEMA` | `MISSING_REQUIRED_HEADER` | Header wajib tidak ada |
| `SCHEMA` | `UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION` | Versi tidak didukung |
| `PARSER` | `MALFORMED_CSV` | CSV tidak bisa diparse |
| `PARSER` | `CORRUPT_EXCEL_WORKBOOK` | Workbook rusak |
| `MAPPING` | `INVALID_DATE` | Date tidak bisa diparse |
| `MAPPING` | `INVALID_ENUM` | Enum/code tidak dikenal |
| `VALIDATION` | `REQUIRED_FIELD` | Field wajib kosong |
| `VALIDATION` | `DUPLICATE_KEY_IN_FILE` | Duplicate dalam file |
| `DOMAIN` | `REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND` | Referensi domain tidak ada |
| `DOMAIN` | `INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION` | State transition tidak valid |
| `SECURITY` | `UNSAFE_FILE_REJECTED` | File gagal security validation |
| `SYSTEM` | `DATABASE_UNAVAILABLE` | DB/downstream bermasalah |
| `SYSTEM` | `PARSER_TIMEOUT` | Parser melewati waktu budget |

---

## 10. Severity Model

Tidak semua error sama.

```java id="1oqqh5"
public enum ErrorSeverity {
    INFO,
    WARNING,
    ERROR,
    FATAL
}
```

| Severity | Makna | Contoh | Effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| `INFO` | Informasi non-problem | Duplicate request returned existing job | Tidak gagal |
| `WARNING` | Data diterima tapi ada caveat | Unknown optional column ignored | Bisa lanjut |
| `ERROR` | Record/file invalid tapi job masih bisa partial | Row email invalid | Row invalid |
| `FATAL` | Processing tidak bisa lanjut | Corrupt workbook, parser timeout | Job failed/rejected |

Rule:

```text id="k7lo7t"
WARNING boleh muncul di successful job.
ERROR biasanya invalid row/file.
FATAL menghentikan pipeline.
```

---

## 11. User-Fixable vs System-Fixable

Error harus dibedakan agar support/action jelas.

```java id="610ou5"
public enum ErrorOwner {
    USER,
    SOURCE_SYSTEM,
    SUPPORT,
    PLATFORM
}
```

| Error | Owner | User action |
|---|---|---|
| Missing required header | USER / SOURCE_SYSTEM | Perbaiki template/header |
| Invalid email | USER | Perbaiki row |
| Invalid enum | USER / SOURCE_SYSTEM | Gunakan value yang didukung |
| Duplicate in file | USER | Hapus/gabung duplicate |
| Reference not found | USER / SUPPORT | Buat referensi atau perbaiki ID |
| Permission denied | USER / SUPPORT | Minta akses |
| Parser timeout | PLATFORM | Cek file size/worker budget |
| DB unavailable | PLATFORM | Retry setelah system pulih |
| AV unavailable | PLATFORM | Retry/quarantine |

Error report sebaiknya menyertakan `fixHint`.

## 14. Error Location Model

Setiap format punya lokasi berbeda.

| Format | Location |
|---|---|
| CSV | row number, column/header |
| Excel | sheet name, row number, column name, cell address |
| JSON | JSON Pointer, array index |
| XML | XPath-like path, line/column, record number |
| Parquet | row number if available, column path, row group optional |
| PDF | page number, field name, extraction section |
| Word | paragraph/table/placeholder name |
| Tika generic | embedded path, metadata key |

Source reference harus cukup fleksibel.

```java id="4uxahc"
        String sourceFormat,
        String sheetName,
        Long rowNumber,
        String columnName,
        String cellAddress,
        String jsonPointer,

        Integer pageNumber,
        String embeddedPath

```

## 15. Reject vs Partial Success Policy

```java id="u5s8oo"
public enum ImportFailurePolicy {
    FAIL_FAST,
    FAIL_FILE_ON_ANY_ERROR,
    PARTIAL_SUCCESS,
    DRY_RUN_ONLY
}
```

| Policy | Behavior | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| `FAIL_FAST` | Stop pada error pertama | System-to-system strict payload |
| `FAIL_FILE_ON_ANY_ERROR` | Kumpulkan error, tapi tidak apply satupun | Regulatory/all-or-nothing import |
| `PARTIAL_SUCCESS` | Apply valid rows, reject invalid rows | User-facing bulk import |
| `DRY_RUN_ONLY` | Validasi saja, tidak mutate | Pre-check/template validation |

### Decision matrix

| Condition | Fail-fast | Fail-file | Partial |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Missing required header | Reject | Reject | Reject |
| Malformed file | Reject | Reject | Reject |
| Row-level invalid email | Stop | Reject file | Reject row |
| Duplicate within file | Stop/reject | Reject file | Usually reject duplicate rows |
| Domain reference missing | Stop/reject | Reject file | Reject affected rows |
| DB unavailable | Failed | Failed | Failed/retry |
| Security issue | Reject/quarantine | Reject/quarantine | Reject/quarantine |

File-level/schema/security errors biasanya tidak boleh partial. Row-level/domain errors bisa partial jika policy mengizinkan.

---

## 16. Validation Pipeline

```text id="ory3sg"
File accepted
   │
   ▼
File-level validation
   ├─ fail -> REJECTED
   ▼
Parse records
   ├─ parser fatal -> FAILED/REJECTED
   ▼
Record mapping validation
   ├─ invalid record -> record error
   ▼
Cross-record validation
   ├─ duplicate/conflict -> record/file error
   ▼
Domain validation
   ├─ invalid command -> record error
   ▼
Apply policy
   ├─ fail-file-on-any-error -> REJECTED
   ├─ partial-success -> apply valid
   └─ dry-run -> no apply
   ▼
Generate report
   ▼
SUCCESS / PARTIAL_SUCCESS / REJECTED / FAILED
```

---

## 18. Jakarta Validation Integration

DTO validation example:

```java id="id6ybs"
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;

public record CanonicalCustomerInput(
        @NotNull SourceReference source,

        @NotBlank
        @Size(max = 64)
        String customerId,

        @NotBlank
        @Size(max = 200)
        String name,

        @NotBlank
        @Email
        @Size(max = 320)
        String email,

        @NotNull
        CustomerStatus status
) {}
```

Programmatic validator:

```java id="hkiq6i"
import jakarta.validation.ConstraintViolation;
import jakarta.validation.Validator;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;

public class JakartaRecordValidator<T> implements RecordValidator<T> {

    private final Validator validator;
    private final ImportErrorFactory errorFactory;

    public JakartaRecordValidator(Validator validator, ImportErrorFactory errorFactory) {
        this.validator = validator;
        this.errorFactory = errorFactory;
    }

    @Override
    public List<ImportRecordError> validate(T record) {
        Set<ConstraintViolation<T>> violations = validator.validate(record);

        return violations.stream()
                .map(v -> errorFactory.fromViolation(record, v))
                .toList();
    }
}
```

Jakarta Validation cocok untuk field-level constraints; cross-row/domain checks tetap butuh validator khusus.

---

## 19. Import Error Factory

```java id="c14wal"
import jakarta.validation.ConstraintViolation;

public class ImportErrorFactory {

    public <T> ImportRecordError fromViolation(T record, ConstraintViolation<T> violation) {
        SourceReference source = extractSource(record);

        String field = violation.getPropertyPath().toString();

        return new ImportRecordError(
                null,
                source,
                ErrorSeverity.ERROR,
                "VALIDATION",
                toErrorCode(violation),
                field,
                null,
                violation.getMessage(),
                "Check the value for field: " + field,
                ErrorOwner.USER
        );
    }

    private String toErrorCode(ConstraintViolation<?> violation) {
        String annotationName = violation.getConstraintDescriptor()
                .getAnnotation()
                .annotationType()
                .getSimpleName();

        return switch (annotationName) {
            case "NotBlank", "NotNull" -> "REQUIRED_FIELD";
            case "Email" -> "INVALID_EMAIL";
            case "Size" -> "FIELD_LENGTH_INVALID";
            default -> "VALIDATION_FAILED";
        };
    }

    private SourceReference extractSource(Object record) {
        if (record instanceof CanonicalCustomerInput input) {
            return input.source();
        }

        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported validated record: " + record.getClass());
    }
}
```

---

## 20. Error Aggregation

Untuk import besar, jangan simpan semua error dalam memory kalau bisa ratusan ribu.

```text id="65813n"
Record processing
   │
   ├─ validate record
   ├─ if error -> write to staging/error table
   ├─ if valid -> write to valid staging batch
   └─ continue
```

### Error collector with flush

```java id="843x0w"
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

public class ImportErrorBuffer {

    private final int batchSize;
    private final Consumer<List<ImportRecordError>> flusher;
    private final List<ImportRecordError> buffer = new ArrayList<>();

    public ImportErrorBuffer(int batchSize, Consumer<List<ImportRecordError>> flusher) {
        this.batchSize = batchSize;
        this.flusher = flusher;
    }

    public void add(ImportRecordError error) {
        buffer.add(error);

        if (buffer.size() >= batchSize) {
            flush();
        }
    }

    public void addAll(List<ImportRecordError> errors) {
        for (ImportRecordError error : errors) {
            add(error);
        }
    }

    public void flush() {
        if (!buffer.isEmpty()) {
            flusher.accept(List.copyOf(buffer));
            buffer.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 23. Raw Value Preview & PII

Jangan asal menyimpan full raw value di error table/report.

```java id="cxotzm"
public final class RawValuePreview {

    private RawValuePreview() {}

    public static String preview(String rawValue, boolean sensitive) {
        if (rawValue == null) {
            return null;
        }

        if (sensitive) {
            return "***REDACTED***";
        }

        String normalized = rawValue
                .replace('\r', ' ')
                .replace('\n', ' ')
                .replace('\t', ' ')
                .trim();

        if (normalized.length() > 200) {
            return normalized.substring(0, 200) + "...";
        }

        return normalized;
    }
}
```

| Field | Raw value di report? |
|---|---|
| Status code | Ya |
| Date | Ya |
| Email | Mask/partial sesuai policy |
| Phone | Mask/partial |
| Address | Biasanya mask atau tidak ditampilkan |
| Password/token/secret | Tidak pernah |
| Free text note | Preview/truncate/mask jika PII |
| Document extracted text | Jangan tampilkan di error report umum |

## 24. Error Report Format

### 24.1 CSV error report

```csv id="m205w2"
severity,source_format,sheet_name,row_number,column_name,field_name,raw_value,error_code,message,fix_hint
ERROR,EXCEL,Customers,20,D,status,UNKNOWN,INVALID_STATUS,Status is not supported,Use ACTIVE or INACTIVE
ERROR,CSV,,15,email,email,not-an-email,INVALID_EMAIL,Email is invalid,Use a valid email address
```

### 24.2 Excel error report

| Severity | Source Format | Sheet | Row | Column | Field | Raw Value | Error Code | Message | Fix Hint |
|---|---|---|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ERROR | EXCEL | Customers | 20 | D | status | UNKNOWN | INVALID_STATUS | Status is not supported | Use ACTIVE or INACTIVE |

Excel report lebih user-friendly untuk user bisnis. CSV report lebih cocok untuk automation.

## 25. Error Report Writer

```java id="1hsqbc"
import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVFormat;
import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVPrinter;

import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Iterator;

public class ImportCsvErrorReportWriter {

    public void write(Path output, Iterator<ImportRecordError> errors) {
        CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.builder()
                .setHeader(
                        "severity",
                        "source_format",
                        "sheet_name",
                        "row_number",
                        "column_name",
                        "field_name",
                        "raw_value",
                        "error_code",
                        "message",
                        "fix_hint"
                )
                .build();

        try (BufferedWriter writer = Files.newBufferedWriter(output, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
             CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format)) {

            while (errors.hasNext()) {
                ImportRecordError error = errors.next();

                printer.printRecord(
                        error.severity(),
                        error.source().sourceFormat(),
                        error.source().sheetName(),
                        error.source().rowNumber(),
                        null,
                        error.field(),
                        error.rawValue(),
                        error.errorCode(),
                        error.message(),
                        error.fixHint()
                );
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new ImportReportException("Failed to write import error report", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Untuk error report besar, stream dari DB cursor ke file; jangan load semua error ke memory.

---

## 26. Partial Success Semantics

Partial success harus jelas secara bisnis.

```text id="d8gq81"
PARTIAL_SUCCESS means:
- file accepted
- some records valid
- valid records were applied
- invalid records were not applied
- error report is available
```

Jangan pakai `SUCCESS` jika ada invalid rows. Jangan pakai `FAILED` jika sebagian valid sudah diterapkan normal sesuai policy.

### Partial success response

```json id="x7cs1r"
{
  "jobId": "imp_001",
  "status": "PARTIAL_SUCCESS",
  "totalRecords": 1000,
  "appliedRecords": 970,
  "invalidRecords": 30,
  "message": "970 records were imported. 30 records require correction.",
  "links": {
    "errorReport": "/v1/import-jobs/imp_001/errors.xlsx"
  }
}
```

---

## 27. All-or-Nothing Semantics

Untuk regulatory/system import:

```text id="ij684o"
REJECTED means:
- file was parsed/validated
- one or more validation errors found
- no domain records were applied
- error report is available
```

Response:

```json id="gf0e4z"
{
  "jobId": "imp_002",
  "status": "REJECTED",
  "totalRecords": 1000,
  "appliedRecords": 0,
  "invalidRecords": 30,
  "message": "No records were imported because the file contains validation errors.",
  "links": {
    "errorReport": "/v1/import-jobs/imp_002/errors.xlsx"
  }
}
```

---

## 28. Dry Run

Dry run penting untuk template validation sebelum user benar-benar import.

```http id="nj3air"
POST /v1/customer-imports?dryRun=true
```

Dry run semantics:

```text id="34axkz"
- parse file
- validate file/rows/domain references
- do not mutate domain tables
- generate validation report
- status DRY_RUN_SUCCESS / DRY_RUN_FAILED
```

Response:

```json id="stn8i2"
{
  "jobId": "imp_dry_001",
  "status": "DRY_RUN_FAILED",
  "totalRecords": 1000,
  "validRecords": 970,
  "invalidRecords": 30,
  "appliedRecords": 0,
  "links": {
    "validationReport": "/v1/import-jobs/imp_dry_001/errors.xlsx"
  }
}
```

---

## 29. Retry Semantics

Tidak semua error boleh retry otomatis.

| Error | Retry? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| DB unavailable | Ya | Retry with backoff |
| Object storage timeout | Ya | Retry |
| Parser timeout | Tidak otomatis berulang tanpa limit | Bisa stuck lagi |
| Invalid email | Tidak | User fix |
| Missing header | Tidak | User fix |
| Unsupported file type | Tidak | User fix |
| AV scanner unavailable | Ya/quarantine | Tergantung policy |
| Worker killed | Ya, dengan attempt limit | Idempotency wajib |
| Duplicate import | Return existing result | Idempotent behavior |

```java id="luxcz6"
public enum Retryability {
    RETRYABLE,
    NON_RETRYABLE,
    RETRYABLE_AFTER_USER_ACTION
}
```

---

## 30. Idempotency & Error Model

Jika job retry, error record tidak boleh duplicate tanpa kontrol.

```text id="1qyb00"
Option A:
- delete previous transient errors for job attempt
- insert fresh errors

Option B:
- store attempt_no
- keep history

Option C:
- deterministic error key
- upsert error
```

Untuk audit-heavy system, simpan `attempt_no`.

```sql id="j71tut"
ALTER TABLE import_record_error
ADD COLUMN attempt_no INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1;
```

---

## 31. Validation Summary

Buat summary agar UI/support tidak harus scan ribuan error.

```sql id="e61qpn"
CREATE TABLE import_error_summary (
    job_id          TEXT NOT NULL,
    error_code      TEXT NOT NULL,
    severity        TEXT NOT NULL,
    field_name      TEXT,
    error_count     BIGINT NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (job_id, error_code, severity, field_name)
);
```

Example:

```json id="4qa8t2"
{
  "jobId": "imp_001",
  "summary": [
    {
      "errorCode": "INVALID_EMAIL",
      "field": "email",
      "count": 12
    },
    {
      "errorCode": "INVALID_STATUS",
      "field": "status",
      "count": 18
    }
  ]
}
```

---

## 32. UI / UX Feedback Principles

Error feedback harus **actionable**.

| Bad message | Better message |
|---|---|
| `Validation failed` | `Row 20: Status "UNKNOWN" is not supported. Use ACTIVE or INACTIVE.` |
| `Parse error` | `The file could not be read as XLSX. Please use the customer import template.` |
| `Invalid date` | `Birth Date must use yyyy-MM-dd, for example 1990-01-31.` |
| `Reference missing` | `Product ID P123 does not exist for this tenant.` |
| `Duplicate` | `Customer ID C001 appears more than once in the file.` |

Rule:

```text id="fsrljv"
A good import error tells:
where, what value, what rule, how to fix.
```

---

## 33. Security Considerations

OWASP File Upload Cheat Sheet menekankan kontrol seperti extension allowlist, validasi file type, tidak percaya `Content-Type`, filename generated by application, size limit, dan hanya user authorized yang boleh upload. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html)) Error model jangan melemahkan kontrol ini dengan memberi detail yang membantu attacker.

| Error area | Jangan expose | Aman expose |
|---|---|---|
| Security rejection | Exact AV signature/internal scanner config | “File failed security validation” |
| Parser exception | Stack trace/classpath | “File could not be parsed” |
| Storage error | Bucket/path/credential | “Storage temporarily unavailable” |
| SQL error | Constraint/table/query | “A database error occurred” |
| Path traversal | Normalized internal path | “Filename is not allowed” |

---

Metrics:

| `import_validation_duration_seconds{import_type}` | Histogram |
| `import_partial_success_total{import_type}` | Counter |
| `import_rejected_total{import_type,reason}` | Counter |

Structured log:

```json id="6g74y7"
{

  "topErrorCodes": ["INVALID_STATUS", "INVALID_EMAIL"],
  "durationMs": 8420,
  "correlationId": "corr_001"
}
```

Jangan log full raw rows.

## 35. Testing Strategy

```text id="1o32s0"
validation-error-tests
│
├─ boundary
│   ├─ missing-file
│   ├─ unsupported-content-type
│   ├─ too-large
│   └─ unauthorized
│
├─ file-level
│   ├─ missing-header
│   ├─ wrong-sheet
│   ├─ unsupported-schema-version
│   ├─ malformed-json
│   └─ corrupt-excel
│
├─ record-level
│   ├─ required-field
│   ├─ invalid-email
│   ├─ invalid-date
│   ├─ invalid-enum
│   └─ field-too-long
│
├─ cross-record
│   ├─ duplicate-key
│   ├─ parent-missing
│   └─ inconsistent-duplicate
│
├─ domain
│   ├─ reference-not-found
│   ├─ tenant-mismatch

│
├─ policy
│   ├─ fail-fast
│   ├─ fail-file-on-any-error
│   ├─ partial-success
│   └─ dry-run
│
└─ reports
    ├─ csv-error-report
    ├─ excel-error-report
    ├─ pii-redaction
    └─ large-error-report-streaming
```

### Unit test example

```java id="slf7kw"
@Test
void shouldCreateActionableInvalidStatusError() {
    SourceReference source = SourceReference.excel(
            "file_001",
            "CRM",
            "customer-import-v1",
            "Customers",
            20
    );

    ImportRecordError error = new ImportRecordError(
            "imp_001",
            source,
            ErrorSeverity.ERROR,
            "VALIDATION",
            "INVALID_STATUS",
            "status",
            "UNKNOWN",
            "Status is not supported.",
            "Use ACTIVE or INACTIVE.",
            ErrorOwner.USER
    );

    assertEquals("Customers", error.source().sheetName());
    assertEquals(20L, error.source().rowNumber());
    assertEquals("UNKNOWN", error.rawValue());
    assertEquals("Use ACTIVE or INACTIVE.", error.fixHint());
}
```

## 36. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Satu error string bebas | Tidak bisa aggregate/translate/metric |
| Tidak punya stable error code | UI/support/alerting sulit |
| Tidak punya row/sheet/cell info | User tidak bisa fix |
| Full fail tanpa report | User frustrasi |
| Partial success tanpa semantics jelas | Data reconciliation sulit |
| Error report load semua ke memory | OOM untuk file besar |
| Raw PII masuk error report | Data leak |
| Stack trace dikirim ke client | Security leak |
| Parser error dianggap row error | Status job salah |
| Domain error tidak dikaitkan ke row | User tidak tahu record mana |
| Retry duplicate errors/mutations | Idempotency buruk |
| Warning dianggap success penuh tanpa ditampilkan | Caveat hilang |
| Unsupported schema diproses best-effort | Data corruption |

---

## 37. Production Checklist

```text id="cqlkey"
Error taxonomy
[ ] Category jelas
[ ] Stable error codes
[ ] Severity model
[ ] Owner model
[ ] Retryability model
[ ] User-fixable message
[ ] Fix hint

HTTP errors
[ ] Problem Details style response
[ ] Correlation id
[ ] No stack trace
[ ] Correct status codes
[ ] Validation details bounded

Job status
[ ] Status state machine jelas
[ ] Counts: total/valid/invalid/applied
[ ] Failure code/message
[ ] Error report links
[ ] Original file link authorized
[ ] Dry-run status

Record errors
[ ] Source reference
[ ] Row/sheet/cell/path
[ ] Field name
[ ] Raw value preview
[ ] PII redaction
[ ] Error persistence
[ ] Error aggregation

Policies
[ ] Fail-fast
[ ] All-or-nothing
[ ] Partial success
[ ] Dry run
[ ] Retry semantics
[ ] Idempotency

Reports
[ ] CSV report
[ ] Excel report
[ ] Streaming large report
[ ] Summary by error code
[ ] User-friendly fix hint
[ ] Download authorization

Observability
[ ] Metrics by error code
[ ] Structured logs
[ ] No raw row logs
[ ] Correlation id
[ ] Audit trail
```

---

## 38. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| HTTP boundary errors and async job errors are different | Jangan campur response model |
| Stable error codes are product/API contract | Jangan ubah sembarangan |
| Error must include source location | Row/sheet/path wajib untuk import |
| Error must be user-actionable | Message + fix hint |
| File-level errors usually reject file | Missing header/schema/security bukan partial |
| Row-level errors may allow partial success | Jika policy mengizinkan |
| Domain validation still maps back to source row | User perlu tahu record mana |
| Raw value must be bounded/redacted | Hindari PII leak |
| Error report generation must stream | Jangan load semua error |
| Partial success must be semantically clear | Applied vs invalid counts wajib |

---

## 39. Kesimpulan Seri 15

Validation dan error model adalah bagian inti dari file import pipeline. Tanpa model error yang baik, sistem mungkin technically working, tapi tidak usable untuk user bisnis maupun support team.

Pola sehat:

```text id="kyd8zg"
Upload
  -> boundary validation
  -> async job
  -> file-level validation
  -> record mapping validation
  -> cross-record validation
  -> domain validation
  -> apply policy
  -> persist errors
  -> generate report
  -> expose status + report
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="7iph4z"
Never return only “validation failed”.
Never lose row/sheet/path location.
Never expose stack traces or raw sensitive values.
Never call partial import “success” without counts and report.
Never design error codes as ad-hoc strings without taxonomy.
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:53:35 PM

# Seri 16/20 - Async File Processing: Job Model, Queue, Retry, Checkpoint, Cancellation, Idempotency & Worker Runtime

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas bagaimana membuat file import/export pipeline yang **asynchronous, retry-safe, observable, cancellable, dan idempotent**.

Pola besarnya:

```text id="taz0m0"
Jersey API
  -> receive upload / export request
  -> stage file / create job
  -> return 202 Accepted
  -> enqueue work
  -> worker processes job
  -> persist progress/checkpoint
  -> update status
  -> generate result/error report
  -> user polls status/downloads result
```

Untuk file besar, parsing CSV/Excel/PDF/Tika/Parquet tidak boleh berjalan lama di request HTTP. Worker harus bisa retry, resume, checkpoint, dan tidak membuat side effect ganda.

RabbitMQ consumer acknowledgement dan publisher confirm adalah fitur untuk data safety: consumer ack menandai message sudah diproses, sedangkan publisher confirm memberi tahu publisher bahwa broker sudah menerima message. Dead Letter Exchange dipakai ketika message perlu dialihkan karena rejected, expired, melebihi queue length, atau delivery limit. ([RabbitMQ](https://www.rabbitmq.com/docs/confirms?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="2e661z"
Async File Processing
│
├─ 1. API Boundary
│   ├─ upload file
│   ├─ create import/export job
│   ├─ return 202
│   ├─ expose job status
│   └─ expose result/error report
│
├─ 2. Job Model
│   ├─ job id
│   ├─ job type
│   ├─ file id
│   ├─ status
│   ├─ progress
│   ├─ attempt count
│   ├─ checkpoint
│   └─ failure reason
│
├─ 3. Dispatch Mechanism
│   ├─ RabbitMQ/Kafka message
│   ├─ PostgreSQL queue table
│   ├─ Kubernetes Job
│   ├─ Camunda worker task
│   └─ scheduled poller
│
├─ 4. Worker Runtime
│   ├─ claim job
│   ├─ lock job
│   ├─ process chunk
│   ├─ update checkpoint
│   ├─ ack/commit
│   ├─ retry if transient
│   └─ mark final status
│
├─ 5. Reliability
│   ├─ idempotency
│   ├─ retry policy
│   ├─ dead-letter
│   ├─ timeout
│   ├─ cancellation
│   ├─ resume
│   └─ reconciliation
│
└─ 6. Observability
    ├─ progress
    ├─ metrics
    ├─ structured logs
    ├─ audit events
    ├─ worker heartbeat
    └─ stuck-job detection
```

---

## 3. Job State Machine

```text id="gjsms7"
CREATED
  │
  ▼
QUEUED
  │
  ▼
RUNNING
  │
  ├─ user cancel ───────────────► CANCELLING ─► CANCELLED
  ├─ transient failure ─────────► RETRY_WAIT ─► QUEUED
  ├─ permanent validation fail ─► REJECTED
  ├─ partial row errors ────────► PARTIAL_SUCCESS
  ├─ fatal system fail ─────────► FAILED
  └─ all done ─────────────────► SUCCESS
```

### Status semantics

| Status | Makna |
|---|---|
| `CREATED` | Job row dibuat, belum dikirim ke queue |
| `QUEUED` | Siap diproses worker |
| `RUNNING` | Sedang diproses |
| `RETRY_WAIT` | Menunggu retry karena transient failure |
| `CANCELLING` | User/system minta cancel, worker harus stop aman |
| `CANCELLED` | Job berhenti sebelum selesai |
| `REJECTED` | Ditolak karena file/schema/security/validation policy |
| `PARTIAL_SUCCESS` | Sebagian records berhasil, sebagian error |
| `SUCCESS` | Selesai penuh |
| `FAILED` | Fatal/system failure |
| `EXPIRED` | Job/result melewati retention |

---

## 4. Job Table

```sql id="eqrou8"
CREATE TABLE file_processing_job (
    id                    TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id              TEXT NOT NULL,
    job_type               TEXT NOT NULL,
    requested_by           TEXT NOT NULL,

    file_object_id         UUID,
    source_format          TEXT,
    schema_version         TEXT,

    status                 TEXT NOT NULL,
    priority               INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,

    total_units            BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    processed_units        BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    successful_units       BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    failed_units           BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    checkpoint_json        JSONB,
    input_json             JSONB NOT NULL,
    result_json            JSONB,

    attempt_count          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    max_attempts           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 3,
    next_attempt_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    locked_by              TEXT,
    locked_until           TIMESTAMPTZ,
    heartbeat_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,

    cancellation_requested BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    cancellation_reason    TEXT,

    failure_code           TEXT,
    failure_message        TEXT,

    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    queued_at              TIMESTAMPTZ,
    started_at             TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,
    expires_at             TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE INDEX idx_file_processing_job_poll
ON file_processing_job (status, next_attempt_at, priority, created_at);

CREATE INDEX idx_file_processing_job_tenant_created
ON file_processing_job (tenant_id, created_at DESC);
```

### Field penting

| Field | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `status` | State machine utama |
| `checkpoint_json` | Resume point |
| `attempt_count` | Retry tracking |
| `locked_by` | Worker identity |
| `locked_until` | Lease timeout |
| `heartbeat_at` | Deteksi worker mati/stuck |
| `cancellation_requested` | Cooperative cancellation |
| `input_json` | Parameter job |
| `result_json` | Summary hasil |
| `expires_at` | Retention result file/report |

---

## 5. Dispatch Mechanism Options

| Mechanism | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| RabbitMQ | Work queue, file processing jobs, retry/DLX | Natural queue, ack, DLX | Butuh idempotency, message/job consistency |
| Kafka | Event stream, high-throughput, audit log | Durable log, replay | Job queue semantics lebih kompleks |
| PostgreSQL queue table | Simpler stack, low/medium throughput | Tidak perlu broker tambahan | Harus hati-hati locking/polling |
| Kubernetes Job | Heavy isolated batch job | Resource isolation bagus | Startup overhead, orchestration complexity |
| Camunda job worker | Business workflow orchestration | Cocok jika file processing bagian BPMN | Worker tetap harus idempotent |
| Cron/poller | Simple scheduled processing | Mudah | Latency dan scaling terbatas |

PostgreSQL `SKIP LOCKED` dapat dipakai untuk queue-like table karena row yang terkunci dilewati, sehingga beberapa consumer tidak saling menunggu; dokumentasi PostgreSQL juga memperingatkan bahwa ini memberi view yang tidak konsisten dan tidak cocok untuk general-purpose query, tetapi cocok untuk menghindari lock contention pada queue-like table. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. Recommended Architecture untuk File Import

```text id="3m5v8t"
POST /v1/customer-imports
  │
  ├─ validate boundary
  ├─ stage original file
  ├─ insert file_processing_job
  ├─ enqueue job message
  └─ return 202 jobId

Worker
  │
  ├─ receive message / claim DB job
  ├─ mark RUNNING
  ├─ parse chunks
  ├─ persist staging rows/errors
  ├─ update checkpoint
  ├─ apply valid rows
  ├─ generate error report
  └─ mark SUCCESS/PARTIAL/REJECTED/FAILED
```

Rule:

```text id="s65la7"
Queue message should contain jobId, not the whole file or huge payload.
Database job row is the source of truth.
Worker must be idempotent.
```

---

## 8. Message Payload

```json id="9v9hbd"
{
  "messageId": "msg_001",
  "jobId": "imp_001",
  "jobType": "CUSTOMER_IMPORT",
  "tenantId": "tenant_001",
  "attempt": 1,
  "correlationId": "corr_001",
  "createdAt": "2026-07-13T14:30:00Z"
}
```

Message jangan berisi:

```text id="6de52s"
- file binary
- semua rows
- raw extracted text
- secrets
- huge metadata
```

Message cukup membawa pointer ke job/file.

---

## 9. Transactional Job Creation

Masalah klasik:

```text id="66dsdz"
DB insert job sukses
RabbitMQ publish gagal
  -> job tidak pernah diproses

RabbitMQ publish sukses
DB commit gagal
  -> worker dapat job yang tidak ada
```

### Solusi lebih aman: transactional outbox

```sql id="h7jf8k"
CREATE TABLE outbox_message (
    id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    aggregate_type  TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id    TEXT NOT NULL,
    event_type      TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload_json    JSONB NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    attempt_count   INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    next_attempt_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at    TIMESTAMPTZ
);
```

Flow:

```text id="rbkfem"
Transaction:
  insert file_processing_job
  insert outbox_message(jobId)

Outbox publisher:
  read pending outbox
  publish to RabbitMQ
  wait for publisher confirm
  mark outbox PUBLISHED
```

RabbitMQ publisher confirms membantu memastikan broker sudah menerima publish, tetapi tetap perlu outbox agar perubahan DB dan publish message tidak kehilangan consistency. ([RabbitMQ](https://www.rabbitmq.com/docs/confirms?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. PostgreSQL Queue dengan SKIP LOCKED

Kalau belum memakai RabbitMQ/Kafka, PostgreSQL table bisa menjadi queue sederhana.

### Claim job

```sql id="h4dskf"
WITH candidate AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM file_processing_job
    WHERE status IN ('QUEUED', 'RETRY_WAIT')
      AND (next_attempt_at IS NULL OR next_attempt_at <= now())
    ORDER BY priority ASC, created_at ASC
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    LIMIT 1
)
UPDATE file_processing_job job
SET status = 'RUNNING',
    locked_by = #{workerId},
    locked_until = now() + interval '10 minutes',
    heartbeat_at = now(),
    started_at = COALESCE(started_at, now()),
    attempt_count = attempt_count + 1
FROM candidate
WHERE job.id = candidate.id
RETURNING job.*;
```

PostgreSQL documentation explicitly notes `SKIP LOCKED` can be used to avoid lock contention with multiple consumers accessing a queue-like table. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Heartbeat

```sql id="twguqq"
UPDATE file_processing_job
SET heartbeat_at = now(),
    locked_until = now() + interval '10 minutes'
WHERE id = #{jobId}
  AND locked_by = #{workerId}
  AND status = 'RUNNING';
```

### Requeue stale job

```sql id="p8yedo"
UPDATE file_processing_job
SET status = 'RETRY_WAIT',
    locked_by = NULL,
    locked_until = NULL,
    next_attempt_at = now() + interval '5 minutes',
    failure_code = 'WORKER_HEARTBEAT_TIMEOUT',
    failure_message = 'Worker heartbeat expired'
WHERE status = 'RUNNING'
  AND locked_until < now();
```

---

## 11. RabbitMQ Worker Pattern

```text id="fo6gy3"
RabbitMQ queue
  -> worker receives jobId
  -> worker loads job from DB
  -> worker marks RUNNING
  -> worker processes idempotently
  -> worker marks final status
  -> worker ACKs message
```

### Ack decision

| Situation | Worker action |
|---|---|
| Job processed successfully | ACK |
| Job rejected permanently | ACK, job marked REJECTED |
| Transient failure, want broker retry | NACK/requeue carefully or publish delayed retry |
| Poison message | ACK + mark FAILED, or dead-letter |
| Job not found | ACK + log warning |
| Worker crash before ACK | Broker redelivers message |

Consumer acknowledgement prevents message loss when a consumer fails before finishing work; RabbitMQ can redeliver unacknowledged messages. Dead-lettering lets broker republish messages to a configured exchange when messages are rejected/nacked without requeue, expire, exceed length, or hit delivery limits. ([RabbitMQ](https://www.rabbitmq.com/docs/confirms?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Retry Policy

```java id="8yb9q4"
public record RetryPolicy(
        int maxAttempts,
        java.time.Duration initialDelay,
        java.time.Duration maxDelay,
        double multiplier
) {
    public java.time.Duration delayForAttempt(int attempt) {
        double factor = Math.pow(multiplier, Math.max(0, attempt - 1));
        long millis = Math.min(
                maxDelay.toMillis(),
                (long) (initialDelay.toMillis() * factor)
        );
        return java.time.Duration.ofMillis(millis);
    }
}
```

Example:

```text id="u5g11c"
attempt 1 -> 1 minute
attempt 2 -> 5 minutes
attempt 3 -> 15 minutes
attempt 4 -> 1 hour
```

### Retry classification

| Error | Retry? |
|---|---:|
| DB connection timeout | Ya |
| Object storage timeout | Ya |
| RabbitMQ publish timeout | Ya |
| File missing from storage | Tidak, kecuali eventual consistency case |
| Invalid CSV header | Tidak |
| Invalid row data | Tidak |
| Unsupported file type | Tidak |
| Parser timeout | Biasanya tidak otomatis terus-menerus |
| Worker pod killed | Ya dengan attempt limit |
| Cancellation requested | Tidak |

---

## 13. Retry Handler

```java id="1gcme3"
public class JobFailureHandler {

    private final RetryPolicy retryPolicy;
    private final FileProcessingJobRepository repository;

    public void handleFailure(
            FileProcessingJob job,
            Throwable error
    ) {
        FailureClassification classification = classify(error);

        if (!classification.retryable()) {
            repository.markFailed(
                    job.id(),
                    classification.code(),
                    classification.safeMessage()
            );
            return;
        }

        if (job.attemptCount() >= job.maxAttempts()) {
            repository.markFailed(
                    job.id(),
                    "MAX_ATTEMPTS_EXCEEDED",
                    "Job failed after maximum retry attempts"
            );
            return;
        }

        java.time.Instant nextAttemptAt = java.time.Instant.now()
                .plus(retryPolicy.delayForAttempt(job.attemptCount()));

        repository.scheduleRetry(
                job.id(),
                classification.code(),
                classification.safeMessage(),
                nextAttemptAt
        );
    }

    private FailureClassification classify(Throwable error) {
        if (error instanceof TransientStorageException) {
            return new FailureClassification(true, "STORAGE_TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE", "Storage temporarily unavailable");
        }

        if (error instanceof ValidationRejectedException e) {
            return new FailureClassification(false, e.code(), e.getMessage());
        }

        return new FailureClassification(false, "JOB_FAILED", "Job failed");
    }
}
```

```java id="le2fvg"
public record FailureClassification(
        boolean retryable,
        String code,
        String safeMessage
) {}
```

## 14. Checkpoint Model

Checkpoint menyimpan progress supaya worker bisa resume.

```json id="ksrjgx"
{
  "format": "CSV",
  "lastProcessedRow": 45000,
  "lastCommittedBatch": 45,
  "validRowsWritten": 44000,
  "errorRowsWritten": 1000,
  "phase": "PARSING"
}
```

Untuk Excel:

```json id="yh6ylg"
{
  "format": "EXCEL",
  "sheetName": "Customers",
  "lastProcessedRow": 12000,
  "phase": "VALIDATING_RECORDS"
}
```

Untuk PDF:

```json id="usvvp5"
{
  "format": "PDF",
  "lastProcessedPage": 14,
  "phase": "TEXT_EXTRACTION"
}
```

Untuk Parquet export:

```json id="4fchij"
{
  "phase": "WRITING_PARQUET",
  "lastSourceId": "CUST-900000",
  "filePart": 3,
  "rowsWritten": 900000
}
```

## 15. Checkpoint Strategy

| Strategy | Cocok untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Row number checkpoint | CSV/Excel sequential | Resume mudah jika parser bisa skip |
| Business key checkpoint | DB export/keyset pagination | Lebih stabil daripada offset |
| Page checkpoint | PDF/OCR | Resume per page |
| Batch number checkpoint | Staging/apply batch | Bagus untuk chunk commit |
| Phase checkpoint | Multi-step pipeline | Hindari mengulang step mahal |
| No checkpoint | File kecil | Simpler |

Rule:

```text id="e5bkln"
Checkpoint only after durable side effects are committed.
```

Jangan update checkpoint sebelum staging batch benar-benar committed.

---

## 16. Chunk Processing Pattern

```java id="qb0sav"
public class CustomerImportChunkWorker {

    private final int batchSize = 1000;

    public void process(FileProcessingJob job) {
        ImportCheckpoint checkpoint = checkpointRepository.load(job.id());

        CsvRecordIterator iterator = csvParser.openFromCheckpoint(
                job.fileObjectId(),
                checkpoint.lastProcessedRow()
        );

        List<CustomerImportStagingRow> batch = new ArrayList<>();

        while (iterator.hasNext()) {
            checkCancellation(job.id());

            RawCustomerCsvRow raw = iterator.next();
            MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> result = mapper.fromCsv(raw);

            batch.add(toStagingRow(job, raw, result));

            if (batch.size() >= batchSize) {
                stagingRepository.insertBatch(batch);

                checkpointRepository.save(job.id(), new ImportCheckpoint(
                        raw.source().rowNumber(),
                        "PARSING"
                ));

                batch.clear();
            }
        }

        if (!batch.isEmpty()) {
            stagingRepository.insertBatch(batch);
            checkpointRepository.save(job.id(), iterator.currentRowNumber(), "PARSING");
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 17. Idempotency

Async worker hampir pasti akan mengalami at-least-once execution. Camunda 8 docs juga menyatakan job worker timeout dapat membuat job dikerjakan lebih dari sekali, sehingga worker code harus idempotent. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Idempotency levels

| Level | Key |
|---|---|
| Job creation | `tenantId + fileSha256 + importType + schemaVersion` |
| Record staging | `jobId + recordNumber` |
| Domain mutation | `tenantId + importType + fileSha256 + businessKey` |
| Event outbox | `aggregateId + eventType + idempotencyKey` |
| Generated file | `jobId + artifactType + contentHash` |

### Staging primary key

```sql id="0r4i8d"
ALTER TABLE customer_import_staging
ADD CONSTRAINT pk_customer_import_staging
PRIMARY KEY (job_id, row_number);
```

### Applied record idempotency

```sql id="dv7lnf"
CREATE TABLE import_applied_record (
    tenant_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    import_type     TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_sha256     TEXT NOT NULL,
    business_key    TEXT NOT NULL,
    job_id          TEXT NOT NULL,
    applied_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, import_type, file_sha256, business_key)
);
```

---

## 18. Idempotent Apply Pattern

```java id="xl5i3x"
public class CustomerImportApplier {

    public void apply(UpsertCustomerCommand command, ImportContext context) {
        ImportAppliedRecordKey key = new ImportAppliedRecordKey(
                command.tenantId(),
                "CUSTOMER_IMPORT",
                context.fileSha256(),
                command.customerId()
        );

        boolean firstTime = appliedRecordRepository.tryInsert(key, context.jobId());

        if (!firstTime) {
            return; // already applied in previous attempt
        }

        customerRepository.upsert(command);
        outboxRepository.insert(customerEventMapper.toEvent(command));
    }
}
```

Jika `tryInsert` dan `upsert` perlu atomic, bungkus dalam satu DB transaction.

---

## 19. Cancellation

Cancellation harus cooperative.

```text id="4myx8u"
User requests cancel
  -> API sets cancellation_requested=true
  -> worker checks flag between chunks
  -> worker stops after current safe checkpoint
  -> job marked CANCELLED
```

### Cancel request

```sql id="blvpje"
UPDATE file_processing_job
SET cancellation_requested = true,
    cancellation_reason = #{reason}
WHERE id = #{jobId}
  AND tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND status IN ('QUEUED', 'RUNNING', 'RETRY_WAIT');
```

### Worker check

```java id="3h0srh"
public void checkCancellation(String jobId) {
    if (jobRepository.isCancellationRequested(jobId)) {
        throw new JobCancelledException("Job cancellation requested");
    }
}
```

### Handling

```java id="xjsxzc"
catch (JobCancelledException e) {
    jobRepository.markCancelled(job.id(), "Cancelled by user");
    return;
}
```

Rule:

```text id="ei2w1a"
Do not kill worker abruptly if it can leave partial side effects inconsistent.
Cancel between durable checkpoints.
```

---

## 20. Worker Heartbeat & Lease

Lease mencegah dua worker memproses job sama terlalu lama.

```text id="mvpvqr"
locked_by = worker-abc
locked_until = now + 10 minutes
heartbeat_at = now
```

Worker memperpanjang lease secara periodik. Jika worker mati, `locked_until` expired dan reaper bisa requeue.

Camunda 8 job workers juga memakai konsep job activation timeout; dokumentasi Camunda menjelaskan jika job tidak complete/fail dalam timeout, job dapat ditugaskan ulang ke worker lain, sehingga idempotency wajib. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 21. Worker Runtime Configuration

```java id="94nq4j"
public record WorkerRuntimeConfig(
        String workerId,
        int maxConcurrentJobs,
        int batchSize,
        java.time.Duration leaseDuration,
        java.time.Duration heartbeatInterval,
        java.time.Duration pollInterval,
        java.time.Duration gracefulShutdownTimeout
) {}
```

Initial default:

```text id="8mr5no"
maxConcurrentJobs        = 2-4 per pod, depending CPU/memory
batchSize                = 500-2000 records
leaseDuration            = 10 minutes
heartbeatInterval        = 1 minute
pollInterval             = 1-5 seconds
gracefulShutdownTimeout  = 30-120 seconds
```

Tune berdasarkan format. PDF/OCR dan Excel besar lebih berat daripada CSV row mapping.

---

## 22. Graceful Shutdown

Kubernetes bisa terminate pod kapan saja saat rollout/scale down. Worker harus menangani SIGTERM.

```text id="4v0zn9"
SIGTERM received
  -> stop polling new jobs
  -> finish current chunk
  -> persist checkpoint
  -> release/extend lock decision
  -> exit before terminationGracePeriodSeconds
```

### Worker lifecycle

```java id="vxzio2"
public class WorkerLifecycle {

    private volatile boolean shuttingDown = false;

    public void start() {
        Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(new Thread(() -> {
            shuttingDown = true;
        }));

        while (!shuttingDown) {
            worker.pollAndProcessOne();
        }
    }

    public boolean isShuttingDown() {
        return shuttingDown;
    }
}
```

Between chunks:

```java id="jw922n"
if (lifecycle.isShuttingDown()) {
    checkpointRepository.save(job.id(), checkpoint);
    jobRepository.releaseLock(job.id(), workerId);
    return;
}
```

---

## 23. Kubernetes Job vs Long-Running Worker

Kubernetes Job runs pods until a task completes. Kubernetes docs note `.spec.backoffLimit` and `.spec.activeDeadlineSeconds` can cause permanent Job failure requiring intervention. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/controllers/job/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Mode | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|
| Long-running worker Deployment | Continuous imports/exports, queue consumers |
| Kubernetes Job per file | Heavy isolated processing, OCR, one-off conversion |
| CronJob | Scheduled batch exports/reconciliation |
| Hybrid | API creates DB job; controller creates K8s Job for heavy tasks |

### K8s Job example

```yaml id="w8kfwt"
apiVersion: batch/v1
kind: Job
metadata:
  name: file-import-imp-001
spec:
  backoffLimit: 2
  activeDeadlineSeconds: 3600
  template:
    spec:
      restartPolicy: Never
      containers:
        - name: worker
          image: registry.example.com/file-worker:1.0.0
          env:
            - name: JOB_ID
              value: "imp_001"
          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "1Gi"
              ephemeral-storage: "1Gi"
            limits:
              cpu: "2"
              memory: "2Gi"
              ephemeral-storage: "5Gi"
```

---

## 24. Queue Backpressure

Backpressure mencegah worker/storage/DB overload.

| Control | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Max concurrent jobs per worker | Batasi CPU/memory |
| Queue prefetch | Jangan ambil terlalu banyak message |
| DB connection pool limit | Lindungi DB |
| Batch size | Kontrol transaction size |
| Rate limit per tenant | Hindari noisy tenant |
| Priority queue | Critical job dulu |
| Worker autoscaling | Scale sesuai queue depth |
| Circuit breaker | Stop processing saat dependency down |

RabbitMQ consumer prefetch umum dipakai agar broker tidak mengirim terlalu banyak unacknowledged messages ke satu consumer. Combine dengan worker thread pool.

---

## 25. Priority & Fairness

Multi-tenant system perlu fairness.

```text id="j51usm"
Problem:
Tenant A upload 100 huge files
Tenant B upload 1 small urgent file
```

Solusi:

| Strategy | Cara |
|---|---|
| Priority | `priority` di job |
| Per-tenant concurrency limit | Max N running jobs per tenant |
| Separate queue | Queue per job type/priority |
| Weighted scheduling | Tenant/business priority |
| Small/large job split | Worker pool berbeda |
| Quota | Daily/hourly import limit |

SQL idea:

```sql id="yv1bia"
SELECT tenant_id, count(*)
FROM file_processing_job
WHERE status = 'RUNNING'
GROUP BY tenant_id;
```

Worker claim bisa menolak tenant yang sudah melewati concurrency cap.

```text id="xn9z3x"
IMPORT phases:
1. FILE_VALIDATION
2. PARSING
3. MAPPING_VALIDATION
4. CROSS_RECORD_VALIDATION
5. DOMAIN_VALIDATION
6. STAGING
7. APPLYING
8. ERROR_REPORT_GENERATION
9. FINALIZATION

EXPORT phases:
1. REQUEST_VALIDATION
2. SOURCE_QUERY
3. FILE_WRITING
4. STORAGE_UPLOAD
5. MANIFEST_GENERATION
6. FINALIZATION
```

Simpan phase di `checkpoint_json` atau dedicated column.

```sql id="m66sg5"
ALTER TABLE file_processing_job
ADD COLUMN current_phase TEXT;
```

## 27. Worker Error Classification

```java id="n0k1yd"
public enum JobErrorType {
    USER_FIXABLE,
    SOURCE_FIXABLE,
    TRANSIENT_SYSTEM,
    PERMANENT_SYSTEM,
    SECURITY_REJECTED,
    CANCELLED
}
```

| Error type | Final handling |
|---|---|
| `USER_FIXABLE` | `REJECTED` + report |
| `SOURCE_FIXABLE` | `REJECTED` + report |
| `TRANSIENT_SYSTEM` | retry |
| `PERMANENT_SYSTEM` | `FAILED` |
| `SECURITY_REJECTED` | `REJECTED` or `QUARANTINED` |
| `CANCELLED` | `CANCELLED` |

---

## 28. Dead Letter / Failed Job Handling

Dead-letter queue bukan tempat “lupakan problem”. Harus ada remediation.

```text id="8rgtsh"
DLQ message
  -> inspect
  -> link to jobId
  -> view failure code
  -> requeue if safe
  -> mark failed if poison
  -> alert if systemic
```

### DLQ record

```sql id="nmw9rr"
CREATE TABLE job_dead_letter (
    id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    job_id          TEXT,
    message_id      TEXT,
    queue_name      TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload_json    JSONB NOT NULL,
    reason          TEXT,
    received_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    resolved_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    resolution      TEXT
);
```

RabbitMQ Dead Letter Exchange republishes messages to another exchange when configured dead-lettering events occur, so pair DLX with operational dashboard and re-drive tooling. ([RabbitMQ](https://www.rabbitmq.com/docs/dlx?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 29. Reconciliation Job

Async systems butuh reconciliation.

```text id="ekrfv7"
Periodic reconciliation:
- queued job but no queue message
- running job with expired lock
- outbox pending too long
- job success but no result file
- result file exists but job not finalized
- error report missing for rejected job
- file expired but job still downloadable
```

### Query examples

```sql id="i4b8yy"
-- stuck running jobs
SELECT *
FROM file_processing_job
WHERE status = 'RUNNING'
  AND locked_until < now();
```

```sql id="i3txe1"
-- outbox stuck
SELECT *
FROM outbox_message
WHERE status = 'PENDING'
  AND next_attempt_at < now()
  AND attempt_count > 5;
```

---

Progress unit berbeda per job.

| Job type | Unit |
|---|---|
| CSV import | rows |

| XML import | records |
| PDF extraction | pages |
| OCR | pages |
| Tika extraction | files/embedded docs |
| Parquet export | rows/files |
| DOCX/PDF generation | phases/pages |

```json id="96gkdz"
  "totalUnits": null,
  "percentage": null,
  "message": "Parsed 45,000 rows"
}
```

```java id="zlrndp"
}
```

## 33. Finalization Must Be Atomic

Finalization should commit:

```text id="mq4nkv"
- final job status
- counts
- result file id
- error report file id
- result JSON
- completed_at
```

```sql id="4pqtb5"
UPDATE file_processing_job
SET status = #{status},
    total_units = #{totalUnits},
    processed_units = #{processedUnits},
    successful_units = #{successfulUnits},
    failed_units = #{failedUnits},
    result_json = #{resultJson}::jsonb,
    completed_at = now(),
    locked_by = NULL,
    locked_until = NULL
WHERE id = #{jobId}
  AND locked_by = #{workerId};
```

## 34. Outbox Events

Publish events after job completion.

```json id="j7s438"
{
  "eventType": "FileProcessingJobCompleted",
  "eventVersion": 1,
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-13T14:30:00Z",
  "tenantId": "tenant_001",
  "payload": {
    "jobId": "imp_001",
    "jobType": "CUSTOMER_IMPORT",
    "status": "PARTIAL_SUCCESS",
    "totalUnits": 1000,
    "successfulUnits": 970,
    "failedUnits": 30
  }
}
```

Use outbox, not direct event publish inside transaction without recovery plan.

---

## 35. Camunda Integration Positioning

Jika file processing adalah bagian dari BPMN workflow, Camunda bisa mengorkestrasi high-level process:

```text id="0jfdhv"
BPMN:
Receive file
  -> Validate file task
  -> Import records task
  -> Generate report task
  -> Human review if partial
  -> Notify requester
```

Tetapi worker task tetap harus idempotent. Camunda 8 docs menyebut timeout job dapat membuat job diambil worker lain; hanya satu completion yang berhasil, sehingga worker code harus tahan dieksekusi lebih dari sekali. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Rule:

```text id="ayjr26"
Camunda orchestrates process state.
File worker owns technical checkpoint/idempotency.
Database job table remains useful for detailed progress.
```

---

## 36. Security & Authorization

| Action | Authorization |
|---|---|
| Submit import | User can import this domain type |
| View job status | User has tenant/job access |
| Cancel job | User owns job or has admin permission |
| Download original | Stronger permission, maybe restricted |
| Download error report | Same import access; check PII |
| Retry job | Admin/support or owner depending policy |
| View internal failure | Support/admin only |
| Re-drive DLQ | Operator only |

Never let user access job by guessing `jobId`. Always check tenant/domain ownership.

```text id="rueovz"
│   ├─ excel-resume-from-row
│   ├─ pdf-resume-from-page
│   └─ parquet-export-resume-from-key
│
├─ idempotency
│   ├─ duplicate-message-delivery
│   ├─ worker-crash-after-db-commit-before-ack
│   ├─ retry-does-not-duplicate-domain-record
│   └─ outbox-does-not-duplicate-event
│
├─ cancellation
│   ├─ cancel-queued-job
│   ├─ cancel-running-job-between-chunks
│   └─ cannot-cancel-final-job
│
├─ concurrency
│   ├─ two-workers-claim
│   ├─ skip-locked-claim
│   ├─ lease-expiry-reclaim
│   └─ per-tenant-concurrency-limit
│
└─ operations
    ├─ stuck-job-reaper
    ├─ dlq-redrive
    ├─ graceful-shutdown
    └─ metrics-emitted
```

---

## 39. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Proses file besar di HTTP request | Timeout, bad UX |
| Queue message membawa file besar | Broker overload |
| Tidak ada DB job source of truth | Status sulit dipercaya |
| ACK message sebelum durable commit | Job hilang saat crash |
| Tidak idempotent | Retry membuat duplicate data |
| Tidak ada checkpoint | Retry ulang dari nol |
| Checkpoint sebelum commit | Data hilang/skipped |
| Tidak ada cancellation | User tidak bisa stop job salah |
| Tidak ada heartbeat/lease | Stuck job tidak terdeteksi |
| Retry semua error | Invalid file diproses berulang |
| Tidak ada DLQ | Poison message loop |
| Tidak ada reconciliation | Job hilang/stuck diam-diam |
| Progress hanya “running” | User/support buta |
| Worker ambil terlalu banyak job | Memory/CPU spike |
| Tidak graceful shutdown | Rollout merusak job |

---

## 40. Production Checklist

```text id="5do8ul"
API
[ ] Upload returns 202 + jobId
[ ] Status endpoint
[ ] Cancel endpoint
[ ] Result/error report endpoint
[ ] Authorization per action
[ ] Correlation id

Job model
[ ] State machine jelas
[ ] Progress counters
[ ] Checkpoint JSON
[ ] Attempt count
[ ] Locked_by/locked_until
[ ] Heartbeat
[ ] Failure code
[ ] Result metadata
[ ] Retention/expiry

Dispatch
[ ] Queue/outbox or DB queue
[ ] Message contains jobId only
[ ] Publisher confirm if RabbitMQ
[ ] DLQ configured
[ ] Re-drive procedure
[ ] Reconciliation job

Worker
[ ] Idempotent
[ ] Chunk-based processing
[ ] Checkpoint after commit
[ ] Retry classification
[ ] Cancellation checks
[ ] Graceful shutdown
[ ] Resource limits
[ ] Per-tenant fairness

Persistence
[ ] Staging table
[ ] Applied-record idempotency key
[ ] Outbox events
[ ] Error report persisted
[ ] Original file retained
[ ] Result file checksum

Operations
[ ] Metrics
[ ] Structured logs
[ ] Stuck job detection
[ ] DLQ dashboard
[ ] Alerting
[ ] Runbook
```

---

## 41. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| Job table is source of truth | Queue hanya trigger |
| Worker execution is at-least-once | Idempotency wajib |
| ACK after durable state transition | Jangan hilangkan job |
| Checkpoint after commit | Hindari skip data |
| Retry only retryable failures | Invalid file tidak perlu diulang |
| Cancellation is cooperative | Stop di safe checkpoint |
| Heartbeat/lease required | Worker crash harus terdeteksi |
| Progress must be observable | Support/user butuh visibility |
| DLQ needs remediation | Bukan tempat sampah permanen |
| Async pipeline needs reconciliation | Distributed systems can lose signals |

---

## 42. Kesimpulan Seri 16

Async file processing yang production-ready adalah gabungan dari:

```text id="fs05yk"
job state machine
+ durable job table
+ queue/outbox
+ worker lease
+ retry policy
+ checkpoint
+ idempotency
+ cancellation
+ observability
+ reconciliation
```

Pola sehat:

```text id="e8pu9z"
API submit
  -> stage file
  -> create job
  -> enqueue via outbox
  -> worker claims job
  -> process chunks
  -> commit staging/progress/checkpoint
  -> handle retry/cancel
  -> finalize job
  -> publish completion event
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="8ivg64"
Never process large files synchronously in HTTP request.
Never rely on queue message as the only job state.
Never write non-idempotent workers.
Never checkpoint before durable commit.
Never retry validation errors like system failures.
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:56:17 PM

Lanjut **Seri 17 dari 20**. Fokusnya persistence: bagaimana file import yang sudah valid masuk ke PostgreSQL/MyBatis dengan staging, batch insert, upsert, transaksi, dan reconciliation tanpa bikin DB overload atau data ganda.

# Seri 17/20 - Persistence Integration with PostgreSQL/MyBatis: Batch Insert, Staging Table, Upsert, COPY Strategy, Transaction Boundary & Data Reconciliation

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas bagaimana hasil file processing masuk ke PostgreSQL dengan aman dan efisien memakai MyBatis.

Fokusnya:

```text id="yk1p8d"
Validated File Records
   │
   ▼
Staging Table
   │
   ├─ raw snapshot
   ├─ canonical snapshot
   ├─ row status
   ├─ error JSON
   └─ source reference
   │
   ▼
Validation / Reconciliation SQL
   │
   ▼
Apply to Domain Tables
   │
   ├─ INSERT
   ├─ UPDATE
   ├─ UPSERT
   ├─ soft delete / deactivate
   └─ outbox event
```

PostgreSQL mendukung `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT` untuk menangani konflik unique/exclusion constraint; `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` menjamin outcome atomic insert-or-update untuk setiap row selama tidak ada error independen. Ini menjadikannya dasar yang kuat untuk idempotent upsert pada import. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-insert.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="0xsk13"
Persistence Integration
│
├─ 1. Import Job Metadata
│   ├─ job id
│   ├─ file id
│   ├─ checksum
│   ├─ status
│   ├─ counters
│   └─ failure reason
│
├─ 2. Staging Layer
│   ├─ raw row
│   ├─ canonical row
│   ├─ row status
│   ├─ error json
│   ├─ source location
│   └─ batch id
│
├─ 3. Batch Loading
│   ├─ MyBatis foreach insert
│   ├─ JDBC batch
│   ├─ PostgreSQL COPY
│   └─ temp/unlogged staging
│
├─ 4. Validation in DB
│   ├─ duplicates
│   ├─ references
│   ├─ tenant ownership
│   ├─ cross-record checks
│   └─ constraint checks
│
├─ 5. Apply Strategy
│   ├─ insert only
│   ├─ update only
│   ├─ upsert
│   ├─ merge/apply procedure
│   └─ domain event outbox
│
├─ 6. Transaction Boundary
│   ├─ per batch
│   ├─ per phase
│   ├─ all-or-nothing
│   ├─ partial success
│   └─ retry-safe commit
│
└─ 7. Reconciliation
    ├─ expected vs actual counts
    ├─ staging vs target
    ├─ applied record ledger
    ├─ duplicate detection
    └─ audit trail
```

---

## 3. Kenapa Perlu Staging Table?

Jangan langsung insert hasil parsing ke domain table.

```text id="hf2bcw"
Bad:
CSV/Excel row
  -> mapper
  -> customer table

Better:
CSV/Excel row
  -> staging table
  -> validate/reconcile
  -> apply to customer table
```

| Alasan | Manfaat staging |
|---|---|
| Audit | Bisa lihat input original dan canonical |
| Error report | Row invalid tetap tersimpan |
| Retry | Job bisa resume tanpa parse ulang semua |
| Reconciliation | Bisa bandingkan total/valid/applied |
| Partial success | Valid dan invalid row bisa dipisah |
| Domain safety | Apply ke target dilakukan setelah validasi |
| Debugging | Support bisa trace row mana bermasalah |
| Idempotency | `job_id + row_number` jadi natural key |

---

## 4. Core Table: Import Job

```sql id="vxyspo"
CREATE TABLE import_job (
    id                    TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id              TEXT NOT NULL,
    import_type            TEXT NOT NULL,
    requested_by           TEXT NOT NULL,

    file_object_id         UUID NOT NULL,
    file_sha256_hex        TEXT NOT NULL,
    source_format          TEXT NOT NULL,
    schema_version         TEXT NOT NULL,

    status                 TEXT NOT NULL,
    failure_code           TEXT,
    failure_message        TEXT,

    total_records          BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    staged_records         BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    valid_records          BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    invalid_records        BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    applied_records        BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    skipped_records        BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    checkpoint_json        JSONB,
    result_json            JSONB,

    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    started_at             TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at           TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE INDEX idx_import_job_tenant_created
ON import_job (tenant_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_import_job_idempotency
ON import_job (tenant_id, import_type, file_sha256_hex, schema_version);
```

`uq_import_job_idempotency` membantu mencegah upload file yang sama diproses berkali-kali untuk import type/schema yang sama.

## 5. Core Table: Staging Row

```sql id="mios4d"
CREATE TABLE customer_import_staging (
    job_id              TEXT NOT NULL,
    row_number          BIGINT NOT NULL,

    tenant_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
    source_format        TEXT NOT NULL,
    source_system        TEXT,
    schema_version       TEXT NOT NULL,
    sheet_name           TEXT,
    source_path          TEXT,

    customer_id          TEXT,
    name                 TEXT,
    email                TEXT,
    status               TEXT,
    phone                TEXT,
    birth_date           DATE,

    raw_json             JSONB NOT NULL,
    canonical_json       JSONB,
    row_status           TEXT NOT NULL,
    error_json           JSONB,

    applied              BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    applied_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,

    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (job_id, row_number)
);

CREATE INDEX idx_customer_import_staging_job_status
ON customer_import_staging (job_id, row_status);

CREATE INDEX idx_customer_import_staging_customer_id
ON customer_import_staging (tenant_id, customer_id)
WHERE row_status = 'VALID';
```

### Row status

```text id="nvlp50"
RAW
VALID
INVALID
DUPLICATE_IN_FILE
DOMAIN_INVALID
APPLIED
SKIPPED
```
