## 6. Target Table Example

```sql id="anjcq0"
CREATE TABLE customer (
    tenant_id      TEXT NOT NULL,
    customer_id    TEXT NOT NULL,

    name           TEXT NOT NULL,
    email          TEXT NOT NULL,
    status         TEXT NOT NULL,
    phone          TEXT,
    birth_date     DATE,

    created_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by     TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_by     TEXT NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, customer_id)
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_customer_tenant_email
ON customer (tenant_id, lower(email));
```

Target table harus punya constraint yang merepresentasikan invariant penting. Jangan bergantung hanya pada validasi aplikasi.

---

## 7. Batch Insert Options

| Strategy | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| MyBatis `<foreach>` multi-row insert | Ratusan-ribuan row per batch | Simple dan familiar | Parameter count bisa besar |
| JDBC batch via MyBatis executor | Banyak row dengan statement sama | Lebih scalable daripada huge SQL | Perlu session/executor config |
| PostgreSQL `COPY FROM STDIN` | Load data sangat besar ke staging | Sangat cepat untuk bulk load | Perlu format strict dan error handling khusus |
| Temp table + `INSERT SELECT` | Transform/validate di DB | Bagus untuk apply phase | Perlu cleanup |
| Stored procedure/function apply | Logic dekat DB, atomic | Cocok untuk complex reconciliation | Harus governance PL/pgSQL |

MyBatis Dynamic SQL docs memperingatkan bahwa multi-row insert dapat menghasilkan parameter sangat banyak; contoh 1.000 records × 5 fields menghasilkan 5.000 parameters, dan untuk banyak records dokumentasi menyarankan JDBC batch insert. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/insert.html))

---

## 8. MyBatis Foreach Batch Insert

### Mapper interface

```java id="d658qg"
public interface CustomerImportStagingMapper {
    int insertBatch(List<CustomerImportStagingRow> rows);
}
```

### Row DTO

```java id="c7kq8w"
import java.time.LocalDate;

public record CustomerImportStagingRow(
        String jobId,
        long rowNumber,
        String tenantId,
        String sourceFormat,
        String sourceSystem,
        String schemaVersion,
        String sheetName,
        String sourcePath,

        String customerId,
        String name,
        String email,
        String status,
        String phone,
        LocalDate birthDate,

        String rawJson,
        String canonicalJson,
        String rowStatus,
        String errorJson
) {}
```

### XML mapper

```xml id="z6i7c7"
<insert id="insertBatch">
    INSERT INTO customer_import_staging (
        job_id,
        row_number,
        tenant_id,
        source_format,
        source_system,
        schema_version,
        sheet_name,
        source_path,
        customer_id,
        name,
        email,
        status,
        phone,
        birth_date,
        raw_json,
        canonical_json,
        row_status,
        error_json,
        created_at
    )
    VALUES
    <foreach collection="list" item="row" separator=",">
        (
            #{row.jobId},
            #{row.rowNumber},
            #{row.tenantId},
            #{row.sourceFormat},
            #{row.sourceSystem},
            #{row.schemaVersion},
            #{row.sheetName},
            #{row.sourcePath},
            #{row.customerId},
            #{row.name},
            #{row.email},
            #{row.status},
            #{row.phone},
            #{row.birthDate},
            #{row.rawJson}::jsonb,
            #{row.canonicalJson}::jsonb,
            #{row.rowStatus},
            #{row.errorJson}::jsonb,
            now()
        )
    </foreach>
</insert>
```

### Practical batch size

```text id="3kvz8f"
Small row width     -> 500 - 2.000 rows per batch
Wide row / JSONB    -> 100 - 500 rows per batch
Large error JSON    -> 50 - 200 rows per batch
COPY strategy       -> much larger stream
```

---

## 9. JDBC Batch dengan MyBatis

Untuk volume besar, pertimbangkan MyBatis `ExecutorType.BATCH`.

```java id="7sm72x"
import org.apache.ibatis.session.ExecutorType;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSession;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactory;

public class CustomerStagingBatchInserter {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    public CustomerStagingBatchInserter(SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = sqlSessionFactory;
    }

    public void insertInJdbcBatch(List<CustomerImportStagingRow> rows, int flushSize) {
        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(ExecutorType.BATCH, false)) {
            CustomerImportStagingMapper mapper =
                    session.getMapper(CustomerImportStagingMapper.class);

            int count = 0;

            for (CustomerImportStagingRow row : rows) {
                mapper.insertOne(row);
                count++;

                if (count % flushSize == 0) {
                    session.flushStatements();
                    session.commit();
                }
            }

            session.flushStatements();
            session.commit();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new PersistenceException("Failed to batch insert staging rows", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Mapper `insertOne`:

```xml id="jr7fai"
<insert id="insertOne">
    INSERT INTO customer_import_staging (
        job_id,
        row_number,
        tenant_id,
        source_format,
        schema_version,
        customer_id,
        name,
        email,
        status,
        raw_json,
        canonical_json,
        row_status,
        error_json,
        created_at
    )
    VALUES (
        #{jobId},
        #{rowNumber},
        #{tenantId},
        #{sourceFormat},
        #{schemaVersion},
        #{customerId},
        #{name},
        #{email},
        #{status},
        #{rawJson}::jsonb,
        #{canonicalJson}::jsonb,
        #{rowStatus},
        #{errorJson}::jsonb,
        now()
    )
</insert>
```

---

## 10. PostgreSQL COPY Strategy

`COPY FROM` sangat berguna untuk load bulk data ke staging. PostgreSQL docs menjelaskan `COPY` dengan `STDIN`/`STDOUT` mengirim data melalui connection client-server, sedangkan `COPY` dengan nama file membaca/menulis file dari perspektif server database. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

PostgreSQL `COPY` mendukung format `text`, `csv`, dan `binary`; opsi CSV mencakup `HEADER`, delimiter, quote, null handling, encoding, dan `ON_ERROR`/`REJECT_LIMIT` untuk skenario tertentu. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

### Kapan pakai COPY

| Cocok | Tidak cocok |
|---|---|
| Load staging besar | Row-level domain mapping kompleks langsung ke target |
| Data sudah canonical/flat | Butuh generated keys per row |
| Millions rows | Error report business-level belum dibuat |
| ETL internal | File user raw yang belum divalidasi |
| Temp/staging table | Direct target mutation tanpa audit |

### Java PostgreSQL CopyManager concept

```java id="s7lmu0"
import org.postgresql.PGConnection;
import org.postgresql.copy.CopyManager;

import javax.sql.DataSource;
import java.io.Reader;
import java.sql.Connection;

public class PostgresCopyService {

    private final DataSource dataSource;

    public PostgresCopyService(DataSource dataSource) {
        this.dataSource = dataSource;
    }

    public long copyCustomerStaging(Reader csvReader) {
        String sql = """
                COPY customer_import_staging (
                    job_id,
                    row_number,
                    tenant_id,
                    source_format,
                    schema_version,
                    customer_id,
                    name,
                    email,
                    status,
                    row_status,
                    raw_json,
                    canonical_json,
                    error_json
                )
                FROM STDIN WITH (
                    FORMAT csv,
                    HEADER true,
                    ENCODING 'UTF8'
                )
                """;

        try (Connection connection = dataSource.getConnection()) {
            PGConnection pgConnection = connection.unwrap(PGConnection.class);
            CopyManager copyManager = pgConnection.getCopyAPI();

            return copyManager.copyIn(sql, csvReader);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new PersistenceException("Failed to COPY staging rows", e);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 11. COPY Safety Notes

`COPY FROM` akan menjalankan trigger dan check constraints pada destination table, tetapi jika gagal jauh di tengah, PostgreSQL docs mencatat row yang sudah dimasukkan tidak terlihat tetapi tetap menempati disk sampai `VACUUM` membersihkan space. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

Practical rules:

```text id="2nm5re"
COPY into staging, not directly into final domain table.
Use constraints sparingly on raw staging if you want to collect bad rows yourself.
Use canonical staging for COPY after app-level validation.
Keep COPY transaction bounded.
Monitor disk bloat if large COPY fails.
```

---

## 12. Staging Validation dengan SQL

Setelah staging, validasi bisa dilakukan dengan SQL.

### Duplicate customer_id dalam file

```sql id="l1dbur"
WITH duplicates AS (
    SELECT customer_id
    FROM customer_import_staging
    WHERE job_id = #{jobId}
      AND row_status = 'VALID'
    GROUP BY customer_id
    HAVING count(*) > 1
)
UPDATE customer_import_staging s
SET row_status = 'DUPLICATE_IN_FILE',
    error_json = jsonb_build_array(
        jsonb_build_object(
            'errorCode', 'DUPLICATE_CUSTOMER_ID_IN_FILE',
            'field', 'customerId',
            'message', 'Customer ID appears more than once in this import.'
        )
    )
FROM duplicates d
WHERE s.job_id = #{jobId}
  AND s.customer_id = d.customer_id;
```

### Reference validation

```sql id="fzv3g9"
UPDATE customer_import_staging s
SET row_status = 'DOMAIN_INVALID',
    error_json = coalesce(s.error_json, '[]'::jsonb) ||
        jsonb_build_array(
            jsonb_build_object(
                'errorCode', 'INVALID_STATUS',
                'field', 'status',
                'message', 'Status is not allowed.'
            )
        )
WHERE s.job_id = #{jobId}
  AND s.row_status = 'VALID'
  AND s.status NOT IN ('ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE');
```

---

## 13. Apply Strategy: Insert Only

```sql id="3eaqor"
INSERT INTO customer (
    tenant_id,
    customer_id,
    name,
    email,
    status,
    phone,
    birth_date,
    created_by,
    updated_by
)
SELECT
    tenant_id,
    customer_id,
    name,
    email,
    status,
    phone,
    birth_date,
    #{requestedBy},
    #{requestedBy}
FROM customer_import_staging
WHERE job_id = #{jobId}
  AND row_status = 'VALID';
```

Insert-only cocok jika import type memang hanya untuk data baru. Kalau ada duplicate target, biarkan constraint fail atau pre-validate dulu.

---

## 14. Apply Strategy: Upsert

```sql id="rx78pj"
INSERT INTO customer (
    tenant_id,
    customer_id,
    name,
    email,
    status,
    phone,
    birth_date,
    created_by,
    updated_by
)
SELECT
    tenant_id,
    customer_id,
    name,
    email,
    status,
    phone,
    birth_date,
    #{requestedBy},
    #{requestedBy}
FROM customer_import_staging
WHERE job_id = #{jobId}
  AND row_status = 'VALID'
ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, customer_id)
DO UPDATE SET
    name = EXCLUDED.name,
    email = EXCLUDED.email,
    status = EXCLUDED.status,
    phone = EXCLUDED.phone,
    birth_date = EXCLUDED.birth_date,
    updated_by = EXCLUDED.updated_by,
    updated_at = now();
```

PostgreSQL `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` memakai row proposed untuk insertion melalui special `excluded` table, dan untuk `DO UPDATE` conflict target harus ditentukan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-insert.html))

---

## 15. Upsert dengan WHERE untuk Menghindari Update Tidak Perlu

```sql id="s47qg6"
INSERT INTO customer (
    tenant_id,
    customer_id,
    name,
    email,
    status,
    phone,
    birth_date,
    created_by,
    updated_by
)
SELECT
    tenant_id,
    customer_id,
    name,
    email,
    status,
    phone,
    birth_date,
    #{requestedBy},
    #{requestedBy}
FROM customer_import_staging
WHERE job_id = #{jobId}
  AND row_status = 'VALID'
ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, customer_id)
DO UPDATE SET
    name = EXCLUDED.name,
    email = EXCLUDED.email,
    status = EXCLUDED.status,
    phone = EXCLUDED.phone,
    birth_date = EXCLUDED.birth_date,
    updated_by = EXCLUDED.updated_by,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE customer.name IS DISTINCT FROM EXCLUDED.name
   OR customer.email IS DISTINCT FROM EXCLUDED.email
   OR customer.status IS DISTINCT FROM EXCLUDED.status
   OR customer.phone IS DISTINCT FROM EXCLUDED.phone
   OR customer.birth_date IS DISTINCT FROM EXCLUDED.birth_date;
```

Ini mengurangi unnecessary writes, trigger execution, WAL, dan replication traffic.

---

## 16. Apply Ledger untuk Idempotency

```sql id="3hmxgz"
CREATE TABLE import_applied_record (
    tenant_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    import_type     TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_sha256_hex TEXT NOT NULL,
    business_key    TEXT NOT NULL,
    job_id          TEXT NOT NULL,
    row_number      BIGINT NOT NULL,
    applied_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, import_type, file_sha256_hex, business_key)
);
```

### Insert ledger dulu

```sql id="f7etdw"
INSERT INTO import_applied_record (
    tenant_id,
    import_type,
    file_sha256_hex,
    business_key,
    job_id,
    row_number
)
SELECT
    s.tenant_id,
    #{importType},
    #{fileSha256Hex},
    s.customer_id,
    s.job_id,
    s.row_number
FROM customer_import_staging s
WHERE s.job_id = #{jobId}
  AND s.row_status = 'VALID'
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
```

### Apply hanya row yang ledger-nya milik job ini

```sql id="rprfk9"
INSERT INTO customer (
    tenant_id,
    customer_id,
    name,
    email,
    status,
    phone,
    birth_date,
    created_by,
    updated_by
)
SELECT
    s.tenant_id,
    s.customer_id,
    s.name,
    s.email,
    s.status,
    s.phone,
    s.birth_date,
    #{requestedBy},
    #{requestedBy}
FROM customer_import_staging s
JOIN import_applied_record a
  ON a.tenant_id = s.tenant_id
 AND a.business_key = s.customer_id
 AND a.job_id = s.job_id
 AND a.row_number = s.row_number
WHERE s.job_id = #{jobId}
  AND s.row_status = 'VALID'
ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, customer_id)
DO UPDATE SET
    name = EXCLUDED.name,
    email = EXCLUDED.email,
    status = EXCLUDED.status,
    phone = EXCLUDED.phone,
    birth_date = EXCLUDED.birth_date,
    updated_by = EXCLUDED.updated_by,
    updated_at = now();
```

---

## 17. Mark Applied Rows

```sql id="kj2p2y"
UPDATE customer_import_staging s
SET applied = true,
    applied_at = now(),
    row_status = 'APPLIED'
FROM import_applied_record a
WHERE s.job_id = #{jobId}
  AND a.job_id = s.job_id
  AND a.row_number = s.row_number
  AND a.business_key = s.customer_id;
```

---

## 18. Transaction Boundary

### Option A - Satu transaksi besar

```text id="pmb0vp"
parse all
stage all
validate all
apply all
commit
```

| Pros | Cons |
|---|---|
| Atomic total | Lock lama |
| Simple rollback | Memory/temp/WAL besar |
| Cocok file kecil/all-or-nothing | Buruk untuk file besar |

### Option B - Per phase transaction

```text id="rpfdub"
transaction 1: create job
transaction 2..N: stage batches
transaction 3: validate staging
transaction 4: apply valid rows
transaction 5: finalize job
```

| Pros | Cons |
|---|---|
| Cocok file besar | Perlu reconciliation |
| Resume/retry lebih mudah | Perlu idempotency |
| Lock lebih pendek | Lebih banyak state |

### Option C - Per batch apply

```text id="8v1lwr"
apply 1.000 rows
commit
checkpoint
apply next 1.000 rows
commit
```

| Pros | Cons |
|---|---|
| Scalable | Partial side effect harus jelas |
| Retry chunk | Reconciliation wajib |
| Cocok partial success | Tidak cocok all-or-nothing strict |

---

## 19. Isolation Level

PostgreSQL default isolation adalah `Read Committed`; pada level ini, setiap `SELECT` melihat data yang committed sebelum query dimulai, dan dua `SELECT` berturut-turut dalam transaksi yang sama bisa melihat data berbeda jika transaksi lain commit di antara keduanya. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html))

Untuk import pipeline:

| Use case | Isolation suggestion |
|---|---|
| Stage rows | `READ COMMITTED` cukup |
| Apply idempotent upsert | `READ COMMITTED` + constraints biasanya cukup |
| Complex all-or-nothing business rule | Consider explicit locks atau `SERIALIZABLE` |
| Snapshot reconciliation | `REPEATABLE READ` jika perlu stable view |
| Concurrent import same business key | Unique constraints + `ON CONFLICT` |

PostgreSQL `Repeatable Read` melihat data committed sebelum transaksi dimulai dan aplikasi harus siap retry jika serialization failure terjadi. `Serializable` memberi isolation paling ketat tetapi juga perlu retry saat serialization failure. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html))

---

## 20. Savepoint untuk Row-Level Apply?

Secara teori, savepoint bisa membuat row-level rollback dalam satu transaksi. Namun untuk import besar, ini sering mahal dan kompleks.

```sql id="m5c1x8"
SAVEPOINT row_100;
-- apply row
-- if fail:
ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT row_100;
```

Rekomendasi:

```text id="w8xwk4"
Prefer validate-before-apply + staging errors.
Avoid row-by-row savepoints for large imports.
Use savepoints only for small, complex, exceptional workflows.
```

---

## 21. Apply via Stored Procedure / PL/pgSQL

Untuk apply logic yang banyak SQL-nya, procedure/function bisa berguna.

```sql id="st1i2s"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION apply_customer_import(
    p_job_id TEXT,
    p_requested_by TEXT
)
RETURNS TABLE(applied_count BIGINT, skipped_count BIGINT)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO customer (
        tenant_id,
        customer_id,
        name,
        email,
        status,
        phone,
        birth_date,
        created_by,
        updated_by
    )
    SELECT
        tenant_id,
        customer_id,
        name,
        email,
        status,
        phone,
        birth_date,
        p_requested_by,
        p_requested_by
    FROM customer_import_staging
    WHERE job_id = p_job_id
      AND row_status = 'VALID'
    ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, customer_id)
    DO UPDATE SET
        name = EXCLUDED.name,
        email = EXCLUDED.email,
        status = EXCLUDED.status,
        phone = EXCLUDED.phone,
        birth_date = EXCLUDED.birth_date,
        updated_by = EXCLUDED.updated_by,
        updated_at = now();

    GET DIAGNOSTICS applied_count = ROW_COUNT;
    skipped_count := 0;
    RETURN NEXT;
END;
$$;
```

MyBatis call:

```xml id="zrucqb"
<select id="applyCustomerImport" resultType="ApplyImportResult">
    SELECT *
    FROM apply_customer_import(
        #{jobId},
        #{requestedBy}
    )
</select>
```

Use PL/pgSQL untuk set-based apply yang jelas, bukan untuk menyembunyikan business logic yang seharusnya di domain service.

---

## 22. Reconciliation Queries

### Count staging by status

```sql id="f1x7ci"
SELECT row_status, count(*) AS count
FROM customer_import_staging
WHERE job_id = #{jobId}
GROUP BY row_status;
```

### Expected vs actual applied

```sql id="b177uj"
SELECT
    (SELECT count(*)
     FROM customer_import_staging
     WHERE job_id = #{jobId}
       AND row_status IN ('VALID', 'APPLIED')) AS expected_valid,

    (SELECT count(*)
     FROM customer_import_staging
     WHERE job_id = #{jobId}
       AND applied = true) AS marked_applied,

    (SELECT count(*)
     FROM import_applied_record
     WHERE job_id = #{jobId}) AS ledger_applied;
```

### Missing target rows

```sql id="vv0dvo"
SELECT s.*
FROM customer_import_staging s
LEFT JOIN customer c
  ON c.tenant_id = s.tenant_id
 AND c.customer_id = s.customer_id
WHERE s.job_id = #{jobId}
  AND s.applied = true
  AND c.customer_id IS NULL;
```

Reconciliation harus menjadi bagian finalization, bukan hanya manual support query.

---

## 23. Job Finalization

```sql id="23s2v5"
WITH counts AS (
    SELECT
        count(*) AS total_records,
        count(*) FILTER (WHERE row_status IN ('VALID', 'APPLIED')) AS valid_records,
        count(*) FILTER (WHERE row_status IN ('INVALID', 'DUPLICATE_IN_FILE', 'DOMAIN_INVALID')) AS invalid_records,
        count(*) FILTER (WHERE applied = true) AS applied_records
    FROM customer_import_staging
    WHERE job_id = #{jobId}
)
UPDATE import_job j
SET total_records = c.total_records,
    valid_records = c.valid_records,
    invalid_records = c.invalid_records,
    applied_records = c.applied_records,
    status = CASE
        WHEN c.invalid_records = 0 AND c.applied_records = c.valid_records THEN 'SUCCESS'
        WHEN c.applied_records > 0 AND c.invalid_records > 0 THEN 'PARTIAL_SUCCESS'
        WHEN c.invalid_records > 0 AND c.applied_records = 0 THEN 'REJECTED'
        ELSE 'FAILED'
    END,
    completed_at = now()
FROM counts c
WHERE j.id = #{jobId};
```

---

## 24. Outbox Integration

Jika apply import menghasilkan event, gunakan outbox.

```sql id="69woio"
CREATE TABLE domain_outbox (
    id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_type  TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id    TEXT NOT NULL,
    event_type      TEXT NOT NULL,
    event_version   INTEGER NOT NULL,
    payload_json    JSONB NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at    TIMESTAMPTZ
);
```

Insert event saat apply:

```sql id="xoj4r7"
INSERT INTO domain_outbox (
    id,
    tenant_id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    event_type,
    event_version,
    payload_json
)
SELECT
    gen_random_uuid()::text,
    s.tenant_id,
    'Customer',
    s.customer_id,
    'CustomerUpserted',
    1,
    jsonb_build_object(
        'customerId', s.customer_id,
        'sourceFileId', j.file_object_id,
        'sourceRowNumber', s.row_number
    )
FROM customer_import_staging s
JOIN import_job j ON j.id = s.job_id
WHERE s.job_id = #{jobId}
  AND s.applied = true;
```

---

## 25. Performance Concerns di PostgreSQL

| Concern | Dampak | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| Huge transaction | WAL besar, lock lama | Batch/phase commit |
| Too many indexes on staging | Insert lambat | Minimal index saat load |
| JSONB besar | Storage/WAL besar | Store preview/canonical only as needed |
| Constraint terlalu banyak di staging | Bad row gagal insert | Validate in app/staging status |
| Upsert massive update | WAL/replication bengkak | `WHERE ... IS DISTINCT FROM` |
| Autovacuum lag | Bloat | Monitor, vacuum strategy |
| Deadlocks | Concurrent imports same keys | Consistent order, constraints, retry |
| Connection pool exhaustion | Worker terlalu banyak | Limit concurrency |
| Long-running queries | DB load | Index/filter, chunking |
| Small commit too frequent | overhead | Reasonable batch size |

---

## 26. Index Strategy

### Staging

```sql id="w6alm7"
CREATE INDEX idx_customer_import_staging_valid
ON customer_import_staging (job_id, customer_id)
WHERE row_status = 'VALID';

CREATE INDEX idx_customer_import_staging_errors
ON customer_import_staging (job_id, row_status)
WHERE row_status <> 'VALID';
```

### Target

```sql id="mu1xe7"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_customer_pk
ON customer (tenant_id, customer_id);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_customer_email
ON customer (tenant_id, lower(email));
```

Rule:

```text id="y5fss2"
Index target table for business constraints.
Index staging table only for job-scoped validation/apply queries.
Drop or avoid unnecessary indexes on huge transient staging.
```

---

## 27. Partitioning / Retention Staging

Jika import volume besar, staging table bisa cepat membengkak.

Options:

| Strategy | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|
| TTL cleanup by `created_at` | Simple |
| Partition by month | High volume import |
| Partition by tenant | Strong tenant isolation, but many partitions risk |
| Separate staging schema | Operational clarity |
| Archive to object storage | Audit-heavy |
| Drop job-specific temp table | Isolated big imports |

### TTL cleanup

```sql id="aeexxp"
DELETE FROM customer_import_staging
WHERE created_at < now() - interval '90 days';
```

Untuk volume besar, delete masif bisa berat. Pertimbangkan partition drop per periode.

---

## 28. Temporary / Unlogged Tables

| Table type | Pros | Cons |
|---|---|---|
| Regular staging | Durable, auditable | WAL/storage lebih besar |
| Temporary table | Cepat dan session-local | Hilang setelah session, kurang cocok async multi-step |
| Unlogged table | Lebih cepat, less WAL | Tidak crash-safe, tidak replicated |
| Partitioned regular | Manage retention | More DDL complexity |

Untuk import yang perlu audit/retry, regular staging lebih aman. Untuk internal ETL transient yang bisa diulang dari source, unlogged/temp bisa dipertimbangkan.

---

## 29. Handling Partial Success

### Partial success apply

```text id="rojsh4"
VALID rows     -> apply to target
INVALID rows   -> stay in staging with error_json
Job status     -> PARTIAL_SUCCESS
Error report   -> generated from staging invalid rows
```

### All-or-nothing

```text id="7j4xwh"
If any invalid row:
  no target apply
  job status REJECTED
  error report generated
```

SQL check:

```sql id="wstigi"
SELECT count(*)
FROM customer_import_staging
WHERE job_id = #{jobId}
  AND row_status <> 'VALID';
```

If count > 0 and policy is all-or-nothing, do not apply.

---

## 30. Concurrency Scenarios

| Scenario | Risk | Control |
|---|---|---|
| Same file uploaded twice | Duplicate job/apply | Idempotency unique index |
| Two jobs update same customer | Last write wins unexpectedly | Job ordering, locks, version check |
| Upsert conflict on email | Unique violation | Pre-validation or handle domain error |
| Worker retry after partial apply | Duplicate side effect | Applied ledger |
| Concurrent import + manual edit | Lost update | `updated_at`/version checks |
| Parallel apply chunks | Deadlocks | Consistent key ordering |

### Apply ordering

```sql id="tbr7g4"
SELECT *
FROM customer_import_staging
WHERE job_id = #{jobId}
  AND row_status = 'VALID'
ORDER BY customer_id;
```

Consistent ordering reduces deadlock risk when concurrent transactions touch overlapping keys.

---

## 31. Optimistic Versioning for Target Updates

```sql id="ub84kk"
ALTER TABLE customer
ADD COLUMN version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
```

Upsert update increments version:

```sql id="e52vjf"
ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, customer_id)
DO UPDATE SET
    name = EXCLUDED.name,
    email = EXCLUDED.email,
    status = EXCLUDED.status,
    version = customer.version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
```

For workflows where manual edit should not be overwritten by stale import, add business rule:

```sql id="ctgff7"
WHERE customer.updated_at <= #{importSnapshotAt}
```

Rows not updated due to condition should be recorded as skipped/domain conflict.

---

## 32. Error Handling in Persistence

| DB error | Meaning | Handling |
|---|---|---|
| unique violation | Duplicate target/key | Convert to domain error if expected |
| foreign key violation | Reference missing | Pre-validate or domain error |
| check violation | Invalid target data | Mapper/validation bug |
| serialization failure | Concurrency conflict | Retry transaction |
| deadlock detected | Lock ordering issue | Retry with backoff |
| connection timeout | Transient system | Retry job |
| disk full | Platform failure | Alert, stop jobs |
| statement timeout | Query too heavy | Optimize/chunk |

Do not show raw SQL exceptions to user. Map to stable error code.

---

## 33. Statement Timeout

Set timeout for heavy apply operations.

```sql id="cjxzoc"
SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '5min';
```

In MyBatis transaction, call before heavy SQL:

```xml id="vtmnnu"
<update id="setStatementTimeout">
    SET LOCAL statement_timeout = #{timeout}
</update>
```

Use with caution: timeout aborts the statement and transaction may need rollback.

---

## 34. Advisory Locks

Untuk mencegah dua import type yang sama berjalan bersamaan per tenant:

```sql id="0i4ld4"
SELECT pg_try_advisory_xact_lock(
    hashtext(#{tenantId} || ':' || #{importType})
);
```

If false, reject or queue.

Use case:

```text id="b1mh4z"
Only one CUSTOMER_IMPORT per tenant at a time.
```

Advisory locks harus dipakai disiplin dan biasanya dalam transaction.

---

## 35. MyBatis Mapper Structure

```text id="6gv4qa"
persistence
│
├─ ImportJobMapper.java
├─ ImportJobMapper.xml
├─ CustomerImportStagingMapper.java
├─ CustomerImportStagingMapper.xml
├─ CustomerImportApplyMapper.java
├─ CustomerImportApplyMapper.xml
├─ ImportReconciliationMapper.java
└─ ImportReconciliationMapper.xml
```

Pisahkan mapper berdasarkan phase:

| Mapper | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `ImportJobMapper` | job lifecycle |
| `StagingMapper` | staging insert/update |
| `ApplyMapper` | apply to target |
| `ReconciliationMapper` | counts/checks |
| `ErrorReportMapper` | stream errors |

---

## 36. End-to-End Worker Flow

```java id="8ultve"
public class CustomerImportPersistenceWorker {

    public void process(CustomerImportJob job) {
        jobRepository.markRunning(job.id());

        try {
            parseAndStage(job);
            validateStaging(job);

            ImportCounts counts = reconciliationRepository.count(job.id());

            if (job.policy().failOnAnyError() && counts.invalidRecords() > 0) {
                errorReportService.generate(job.id());
                jobRepository.markRejected(job.id(), counts);
                return;
            }

            applyRepository.insertAppliedLedger(job.id(), job.importType(), job.fileSha256Hex());
            applyRepository.upsertCustomers(job.id(), job.requestedBy());
            applyRepository.markAppliedRows(job.id());

            ImportCounts finalCounts = reconciliationRepository.count(job.id());

            if (finalCounts.invalidRecords() > 0) {
                jobRepository.markPartialSuccess(job.id(), finalCounts);
            } else {
                jobRepository.markSuccess(job.id(), finalCounts);
            }

            outboxRepository.insertImportCompletedEvent(job.id());

        } catch (TransientDatabaseException e) {
            jobRepository.scheduleRetry(job.id(), "DATABASE_TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            jobRepository.markFailed(job.id(), "IMPORT_PERSISTENCE_FAILED", "Import failed");
        }
    }
}
```

```text id="2f1q5v"
    ├─ foreach-batch-1000
```

## 39. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Direct insert file rows to target table | Audit/retry/error handling buruk |
| Tidak punya staging primary key | Retry duplicate rows |
| Batch terlalu besar | Parameter limit/timeout/memory |
| Batch terlalu kecil | Overhead tinggi |
| Tidak punya idempotency ledger | Retry membuat double side effect |
| Upsert tanpa `WHERE IS DISTINCT FROM` | WAL/trigger/replication bengkak |
| All-or-nothing pakai transaksi besar untuk file huge | Lock/WAL/timeouts |
| Tidak reconcile counts | Job status tidak trustworthy |
| Raw PII full di staging tanpa retention | Compliance risk |
| Terlalu banyak index di staging | Load lambat |
| COPY langsung ke target | Bad data sulit dilacak |
| Tidak handle concurrency | Deadlock/lost update |
| SQL error dikirim ke user | Security leak |

---

## 40. Production Checklist

```text id="y6434f"
Schema
[ ] import_job table
[ ] staging table
[ ] target constraints
[ ] applied ledger
[ ] outbox table
[ ] indexes for job-scoped queries

Batch load
[ ] MyBatis foreach batch size defined
[ ] JDBC batch considered
[ ] COPY strategy considered for huge load
[ ] Batch transaction boundary clear
[ ] Parameter count monitored

Validation
[ ] Duplicate-in-file SQL
[ ] Reference validation SQL
[ ] Tenant ownership validation
[ ] Domain conflict validation
[ ] Error JSON persisted

Apply
[ ] Insert/upsert/update strategy explicit
[ ] ON CONFLICT target matches unique constraint
[ ] Update only changed rows if needed
[ ] Applied rows marked
[ ] Outbox events inserted

Reliability
[ ] Idempotency unique index
[ ] Applied ledger
[ ] Retryable DB errors classified
[ ] Deadlock/serialization retry
[ ] Reconciliation finalization
[ ] Statement timeout

Operations
[ ] Staging retention
[ ] Vacuum/bloat monitoring
[ ] Metrics
[ ] Structured logs
[ ] No raw SQL errors to user
[ ] Runbook for failed imports
```

---

## 41. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| Staging is the persistence boundary for imports | Jangan langsung ke target |
| Target constraints are domain safety net | App validation saja tidak cukup |
| Apply must be idempotent | Worker retry pasti terjadi |
| Job status must be derived from reconciled counts | Jangan asal set success |
| Checkpoint must follow durable commit | Hindari skipped rows |
| COPY is for trusted/canonical bulk load | Bukan magic parser user file |
| Upsert needs a precise conflict target | Unique index adalah arbiter |
| Transaction boundary must match business policy | Partial vs all-or-nothing |
| Error details must map back to source row | Support/user needs traceability |
| Retention matters | Staging/audit data bisa besar dan sensitif |

---

## 42. Kesimpulan Seri 17

Persistence integration yang production-ready adalah gabungan dari:

```text id="ep45ro"
staging table
+ batch loading
+ validation SQL
+ idempotent apply
+ upsert strategy
+ transaction boundary
+ reconciliation
+ outbox
+ retention
+ observability
```

Pola sehat:

```text id="4flkzy"
Parsed/validated records
  -> batch insert/COPY to staging
  -> SQL validation/reconciliation
  -> apply valid rows via insert/upsert
  -> mark applied
  -> generate error report
  -> finalize job from counts
  -> publish outbox event
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="pi1vx0"
Never import directly into domain tables without staging.
Never rely on application validation without database constraints.
Never make import apply non-idempotent.
Never finalize job status without reconciliation counts.
Never use COPY into target tables for untrusted user files.
```

---

## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:58:49 PM

Lanjut **Seri 18 dari 20**. Fokusnya export/download lintas format: kapan streaming langsung, kapan async ke object storage, bagaimana header HTTP aman, dan bagaimana access control/audit dipasang di setiap download.

# Seri 18/20 - File Generation & Export Patterns: CSV/Excel/PDF/JSON/XML/Parquet Download, Content-Disposition, Streaming, Async Export, Object Storage & Access Control

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas pola **file generation dan export/download** dalam Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice.

Setelah kita membahas CSV, Excel, PDF, Word, JSON, XML, Parquet, mapping, validation, async worker, dan persistence, sekarang fokusnya adalah:

```text id="7qxn2h"
Business Data / Report Query / Import Result / Audit Result
        │
        ▼
Export Request
        │
        ├─ authorize
        ├─ decide export mode
        ├─ query data incrementally
        ├─ generate file
        ├─ stream or store
        ├─ expose download
        ├─ audit access
        └─ cleanup by retention policy
```

Di JAX-RS/Jakarta REST, `StreamingOutput` memang disediakan sebagai tipe entity/return value ketika aplikasi ingin melakukan streaming output; ini adalah alternatif ringan dari `MessageBodyWriter`. Cocok untuk file download yang tidak ingin dibangun seluruhnya di memory. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/platform/9/apidocs/?jakarta%2Fws%2Frs%2Fcore%2FStreamingOutput.html=&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="6sq5ag"
File Export / Download
│
├─ 1. Export Use Case
│   ├─ user report
│   ├─ import error report
│   ├─ audit evidence
│   ├─ document download
│   ├─ data lake export
│   └─ system-to-system bulk extract
│
├─ 2. Format Choice
│   ├─ CSV
│   ├─ Excel XLSX
│   ├─ PDF
│   ├─ DOCX
│   ├─ JSON / NDJSON
│   ├─ XML
│   └─ Parquet
│
├─ 3. Generation Mode
│   ├─ direct HTTP streaming
│   ├─ generated temp file
│   ├─ async export job
│   ├─ object storage file
│   ├─ precomputed scheduled export
│   └─ signed/presigned download
│
├─ 4. HTTP Download Contract
│   ├─ Content-Type
│   ├─ Content-Disposition
│   ├─ filename / filename*
│   ├─ Content-Length
│   ├─ Cache-Control
│   ├─ ETag
│   └─ Range support if needed
│
├─ 5. Security
│   ├─ domain authorization
│   ├─ tenant isolation
│   ├─ PII masking
│   ├─ no-store cache
│   ├─ filename sanitization
│   ├─ audit download
│   └─ retention/expiry
│
├─ 6. Runtime
│   ├─ DB cursor / pagination
│   ├─ StreamingOutput
│   ├─ backpressure
│   ├─ timeout
│   ├─ object storage upload
│   ├─ retry
│   └─ cleanup
│
└─ 7. Operations
    ├─ metrics
    ├─ structured logs
    ├─ export job status
    ├─ failed export report
    ├─ storage lifecycle
    └─ reconciliation
```

---

## 3. Export Mode Decision Matrix

| Mode | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Direct streaming** | File kecil-menengah, cepat, tidak perlu disimpan | Simple, user langsung download | Kalau gagal di tengah response, error handling terbatas |
| **Generate temp file lalu stream** | Perlu checksum/size sebelum response | Bisa hitung `Content-Length` dan verify file | Butuh disk/temp cleanup |
| **Async export job** | File besar, query lama, PDF berat, Parquet, Excel besar | Tidak timeout HTTP, bisa retry, bisa progress | Perlu job table, worker, storage |
| **Object storage download via API proxy** | File resmi/sensitif, butuh audit kuat | Access control central di service | Service menanggung bandwidth |
| **Presigned/SAS URL** | File besar, controlled expiry, offload bandwidth | Efisien, client download langsung dari storage | Harus hati-hati expiry, permission, audit |
| **Scheduled/precomputed export** | Report periodik | Cepat diunduh, stabil | Data freshness dan storage lifecycle perlu jelas |

Rule praktis:

```text id="y7izfd"
Small and quick       -> direct StreamingOutput
Large or slow         -> async export job
Official/audit file   -> generate + store + checksum + authorized download
Data lake export      -> async + object storage + manifest
Sensitive download    -> proxy or tightly scoped short-lived URL
```

---

## 4. Format Choice Matrix

| Format | Cocok untuk | Jangan untuk |
|---|---|---|
| CSV | Simple tabular export, automation, error report ringan | Rich formatting, multi-sheet, complex types |
| XLSX | Business-friendly report, multi-sheet, styled error report | Millions of rows, data lake export |
| PDF | Final-form document, invoice, letter, regulatory evidence | Editable data exchange |
| DOCX | Editable draft/template document | Final immutable evidence tanpa PDF final |
| JSON | API/export machine-readable object | Human spreadsheet workflow |
| NDJSON | Large event/record stream | Rich nested document with single root |
| XML | Legacy/enterprise schema exchange | Simple analytics export |
| Parquet | Analytics/data lake/bulk dataset | Browser/user manual download |
| ZIP bundle | Multi-file export package | Unbounded nested files without security controls |

---

## 5. Direct Streaming vs Async Export

### Direct streaming

```text id="e443nc"
GET /v1/reports/customers.csv
  -> authorize
  -> query DB cursor
  -> write CSV rows to response stream
```

Cocok jika:

```text id="mvlrjg"
- query cepat
- file tidak terlalu besar
- tidak perlu result retention
- tidak perlu retry
- user menunggu download langsung
```

### Async export

```text id="dqggae"
POST /v1/exports
  -> create export job
  -> return 202

Worker
  -> generate file
  -> upload to object storage
  -> mark READY

GET /v1/exports/{id}/content
  -> authorize
  -> stream/download stored file
```

Cocok jika:

```text id="23m5qz"
- query lama
- file besar
- PDF/Excel generation berat
- perlu audit/checksum
- perlu retry
- perlu approval/signature
- perlu share link dengan expiry
```

---

## 6. HTTP Headers untuk Download

RFC 6266 mendefinisikan penggunaan `Content-Disposition` di HTTP dan membahas aspek interoperabilitas serta internationalization, termasuk parameter filename. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6266?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Header | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `Content-Type` | Tipe file |
| `Content-Disposition` | Inline atau attachment + filename |
| `Content-Length` | Ukuran file jika diketahui |
| `Cache-Control` | Caching policy |
| `ETag` | Entity tag untuk cache/revalidation |
| `Last-Modified` | Timestamp file |
| `Accept-Ranges` | Dukungan range download |
| `Content-Range` | Response partial content |
| `X-Correlation-Id` | Traceability |
| `X-Content-Type-Options` | Biasanya `nosniff` untuk browser |

### Recommended sensitive download headers

```http id="xggpwr"
Content-Type: application/pdf
Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="invoice-INV-001.pdf"
Cache-Control: no-store
Pragma: no-cache
X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff
X-Correlation-Id: corr_001
```

---

## 7. Content-Type Cheat Sheet

| Format | Content-Type |
|---|---|
| CSV | `text/csv; charset=UTF-8` |
| TSV | `text/tab-separated-values; charset=UTF-8` |
| Excel XLSX | `application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet` |
| Word DOCX | `application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document` |
| PDF | `application/pdf` |
| JSON | `application/json` |
| NDJSON | `application/x-ndjson` |
| XML | `application/xml` |
| ZIP | `application/zip` |
| Parquet | `application/octet-stream` atau internal registered type jika platform Anda punya convention |

Untuk Parquet, banyak platform tetap memakai `application/octet-stream` karena MIME support Parquet tidak selalu konsisten di semua client/tool. Yang lebih penting adalah metadata object storage/catalog menyimpan `format=PARQUET`.

---

## 8. Content-Disposition dan Filename

### Basic

```http id="jvpipx"
Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="customers.csv"
```

### Filename dengan karakter non-ASCII

RFC 6266 membahas internationalization; secara praktis, gunakan `filename` fallback ASCII dan `filename*` RFC 5987 style untuk UTF-8 jika client mendukung. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6266?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```http id="65vy3e"
Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="report.csv"; filename*=UTF-8''laporan-pelanggan-2026.csv
```

### Filename rule

```text id="6npja4"
- jangan pakai filename mentah dari user
- hapus path separator
- batasi panjang
- gunakan extension sesuai content type
- hindari control characters
- hindari newline untuk mencegah header injection
- gunakan server-generated filename untuk official files
```

---

## 9. Filename Sanitizer

```java id="hfdke6"
import java.text.Normalizer;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.Locale;

public final class DownloadFilename {

    private DownloadFilename() {}

    public static String csv(String prefix, String businessId) {
        return safe(prefix) + "-" + safe(businessId) + "-" + LocalDate.now() + ".csv";
    }

    public static String safe(String input) {
        if (input == null || input.isBlank()) {
            return "download";
        }

        String normalized = Normalizer.normalize(input, Normalizer.Form.NFKC);

        String sanitized = normalized
                .replaceAll("[\\\\/\\r\\n\\t\\x00]", "_")
                .replaceAll("[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]", "_")
                .replaceAll("_+", "_");

        if (sanitized.length() > 80) {
            sanitized = sanitized.substring(0, 80);
        }

        if (sanitized.isBlank() || sanitized.equals(".") || sanitized.equals("..")) {
            return "download";
        }

        return sanitized.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }
}
```

## 10. Direct CSV Streaming dengan Jersey

```java id="0ukv9s"
import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Produces;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.StreamingOutput;

import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

@Path("/customer-exports")
public class CustomerCsvExportResource {

    private final CustomerExportService exportService;

    public CustomerCsvExportResource(CustomerExportService exportService) {
        this.exportService = exportService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/customers.csv")
    @Produces("text/csv")
    public Response exportCustomersCsv() {
        authorizeExport();

        StreamingOutput stream = output -> {
            try (BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(
                    new OutputStreamWriter(output, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
            )) {
                exportService.writeCustomersCsv(writer);
            }
        };

        String filename = "customers.csv";

        return Response.ok(stream)
                .type("text/csv; charset=UTF-8")
                .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"" + filename + "\"")
                .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
                .header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
                .build();
    }
}
```

Catatan: untuk stream dari container/JAX-RS, biasanya aplikasi menulis ke `OutputStream`; lifecycle final stream dikelola runtime. Jangan membangun seluruh CSV menjadi `String` raksasa di memory.

---

## 11. CSV Writer dengan Query Incremental

```java id="fg3cia"
import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVFormat;
import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVPrinter;

import java.io.Writer;

public class CustomerExportService {

    private final CustomerQueryService queryService;

    public void writeCustomersCsv(Writer writer) {
        CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.builder()
                .setHeader("customer_id", "name", "email", "status")
                .build();

        try (CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, format)) {
            queryService.streamCustomers(customer -> {
                try {
                    printer.printRecord(
                            escapeForSpreadsheet(customer.customerId()),
                            escapeForSpreadsheet(customer.name()),
                            escapeForSpreadsheet(customer.email()),
                            escapeForSpreadsheet(customer.status())
                    );
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    throw new ExportWriteException("Failed to write CSV row", e);
                }
            });
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new ExportException("Failed to export customers as CSV", e);
        }
    }

    private String escapeForSpreadsheet(String value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return "";
        }

        if (value.startsWith("=")
                || value.startsWith("+")
                || value.startsWith("-")
                || value.startsWith("@")
                || value.startsWith("\t")
                || value.startsWith("\r")
                || value.startsWith("\n")) {
            return "'" + value;
        }

        return value;
    }
}
```

Untuk CSV/Excel-compatible exports, formula injection tetap perlu dicegah ketika nilai berasal dari user/source external.

## 12. Direct JSON Streaming

Untuk JSON besar, hindari `List` besar.

```java id="sn1kuw"
import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonGenerator;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import java.io.OutputStream;

public class CustomerJsonExportService {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final CustomerQueryService queryService;

    public void writeCustomersJson(OutputStream output) {
        try (JsonGenerator generator = objectMapper.getFactory().createGenerator(output)) {
            generator.writeStartArray();

            queryService.streamCustomers(customer -> {
                try {
                    objectMapper.writeValue(generator, new CustomerResponse(
                            customer.customerId(),
                            customer.name(),
                            customer.email(),
                            customer.status()
                    ));
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    throw new ExportWriteException("Failed to write JSON record", e);
                }
            });

            generator.writeEndArray();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new ExportException("Failed to export JSON", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Endpoint:

```java id="bks80m"
@GET
@Path("/customers.json")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public Response exportCustomersJson() {
    authorizeExport();

    StreamingOutput stream = output -> jsonExportService.writeCustomersJson(output);

    return Response.ok(stream)
            .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
            .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"customers.json\"")
            .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            .build();
}
```

## 13. NDJSON Export

NDJSON lebih cocok untuk export record besar karena setiap line adalah JSON object.

```text id="x4a5gx"
{"customerId":"C001","name":"Alice"}
{"customerId":"C002","name":"Bob"}
{"customerId":"C003","name":"Charlie"}
```

Endpoint:

```java id="lmlre8"
@GET
@Path("/customers.ndjson")
@Produces("application/x-ndjson")
public Response exportCustomersNdjson() {
    authorizeExport();

    StreamingOutput stream = output -> ndjsonExportService.writeCustomers(output);

    return Response.ok(stream)
            .type("application/x-ndjson; charset=UTF-8")
            .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"customers.ndjson\"")
            .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            .build();
}
```

NDJSON bagus untuk system-to-system streaming karena parser consumer bisa membaca line-by-line tanpa menunggu JSON array selesai.

## 14. XML Streaming Export

```java id="ik5rkk"
import javax.xml.stream.XMLOutputFactory;
import javax.xml.stream.XMLStreamWriter;

public class CustomerXmlExportService {

    private final CustomerQueryService queryService;

    public void writeCustomersXml(OutputStream output) {
        try {
            XMLStreamWriter writer = XMLOutputFactory.newFactory()
                    .createXMLStreamWriter(output, "UTF-8");

            writer.writeStartDocument("UTF-8", "1.0");
            writer.writeStartElement("customers");

            queryService.streamCustomers(customer -> {
                try {
                    writer.writeStartElement("customer");

                    writer.writeStartElement("customerId");
                    writer.writeCharacters(customer.customerId());
                    writer.writeEndElement();

                    writer.writeStartElement("name");
                    writer.writeCharacters(customer.name());
                    writer.writeEndElement();

                    writer.writeStartElement("status");
                    writer.writeCharacters(customer.status());
                    writer.writeEndElement();

                    writer.writeEndElement();
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    throw new ExportWriteException("Failed to write XML customer", e);
                }
            });

            writer.writeEndElement();
            writer.writeEndDocument();
            writer.flush();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new ExportException("Failed to export XML", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Gunakan XML writer, bukan string concatenation, agar escaping XML benar.

## 15. Excel Export Pattern

Excel export sebaiknya:

```text id="r5cfnj"
- small/medium direct stream
- large async export
- SXSSFWorkbook untuk row besar
- style reusable
- no formula dari user input
- Content-Type XLSX benar
```

Endpoint direct:

```java id="m99d7w"
@GET
@Path("/customers.xlsx")
@Produces("application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet")
public Response exportCustomersXlsx() {
    authorizeExport();

    StreamingOutput stream = output -> excelExportService.writeCustomersWorkbook(output);

    return Response.ok(stream)
            .type("application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet")
            .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"customers.xlsx\"")
            .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            .build();
}
```

Untuk Excel besar, lebih baik async karena workbook generation bisa memakai temp file dan CPU/memory cukup tinggi.

---

## 16. PDF/DOCX Download Pattern

Untuk dokumen resmi seperti PDF invoice atau DOCX letter, rekomendasi:

```text id="6a457v"
generate document
  -> store file
  -> calculate checksum
  -> persist metadata
  -> expose authorized download
  -> audit download
```

Direct PDF generation boleh untuk dokumen kecil, tetapi dokumen legal/audit sebaiknya disimpan dulu.

```java id="0qcw12"
@GET
@Path("/documents/{documentId}/content")
public Response downloadDocument(@PathParam("documentId") String documentId) {
    DocumentFile file = documentService.getAuthorizedDocument(
            CurrentTenant.required(),
            CurrentUser.required(),
            documentId
    );

    StreamingOutput stream = output -> fileStorage.stream(file.storageKey(), output);

    return Response.ok(stream)
            .type(file.contentType())
            .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"" + file.safeFilename() + "\"")
            .header("Content-Length", file.sizeBytes())
            .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            .header("ETag", "\"" + file.sha256Hex() + "\"")
            .build();
}
```

---

## 17. Parquet Export Pattern

Parquet biasanya bukan direct browser download. Pattern yang lebih baik:

```text id="4s8vyk"
POST /v1/dataset-exports
  -> async job
  -> write Parquet file(s)
  -> upload object storage
  -> write manifest
  -> expose manifest/download location
```

Response status:

```json id="6y0m9w"
{
  "exportJobId": "exp_001",
  "status": "SUCCESS",
  "datasetName": "customer_snapshot",
  "format": "PARQUET",
  "fileCount": 4,
  "totalSizeBytes": 834123456,
  "links": {
    "manifest": "/v1/dataset-exports/exp_001/manifest"
  }
}
```

Parquet download biasanya untuk data platform, bukan UI business user.

## 20. Export Worker Flow

```text id="5z3o7d"
Worker receives exportJobId
  -> load job
  -> authorize system execution context
  -> validate filters/options
  -> query source incrementally
  -> generate file to temp path
  -> compute checksum + size
  -> upload to storage
  -> persist file_object_id
  -> mark READY
  -> publish ExportReady event
```

Pseudo-code:

```java id="dp5c0e"
public class ExportWorker {

    private final ExportJobRepository jobRepository;
    private final ExportGeneratorRegistry generatorRegistry;
    private final FileStorage fileStorage;

    public void process(String exportJobId) {
        ExportJob job = jobRepository.markRunning(exportJobId);

        try {
            ExportGenerator generator = generatorRegistry.find(
                    job.exportType(),
                    job.exportFormat()
            );

            Path tempFile = generator.generateToTempFile(job);

            StoredFile stored = fileStorage.store(
                    job.tenantId(),
                    tempFile,
                    generator.contentType(),
                    generator.filename(job)
            );

            jobRepository.markReady(
                    job.id(),
                    stored.fileObjectId(),
                    stored.sizeBytes(),
                    stored.sha256Hex(),
                    stored.expiresAt()
            );

        } catch (Exception e) {
            jobRepository.markFailed(job.id(), "EXPORT_FAILED", "Export generation failed");
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 21. Export Generator Interface

```java id="7oaq7o"
import java.nio.file.Path;

public interface ExportGenerator {

    String exportType();

    String format();

    String contentType();

    String filename(ExportJob job);

    Path generateToTempFile(ExportJob job);
}
```

Implementations:

```text id="42tq00"
CustomerCsvExportGenerator
CustomerExcelExportGenerator
CustomerPdfExportGenerator
CustomerJsonExportGenerator
CustomerParquetExportGenerator
ImportErrorExcelReportGenerator
```

Registry:

```java id="xcjta4"
public class ExportGeneratorRegistry {

    private final Map<String, ExportGenerator> generators;

    public ExportGenerator find(String exportType, String format) {
        ExportGenerator generator = generators.get(exportType + ":" + format);

        if (generator == null) {
            throw new UnsupportedExportFormatException(exportType, format);
        }

        return generator;
    }
}
```

```sql id="79u7xy"
CREATE TABLE file_object (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
    owner_user_id        TEXT,

    file_purpose         TEXT NOT NULL,
    original_filename    TEXT,
    safe_filename        TEXT NOT NULL,
    content_type         TEXT NOT NULL,

    sha256_hex           TEXT NOT NULL,

);

CREATE INDEX idx_file_object_tenant_created
ON file_object (tenant_id, created_at DESC);
```

`file_object` menjadi central metadata untuk upload, generated export, error report, PDF, DOCX, Parquet, dan lainnya.

## 23. Object Storage Path Design

```text id="0zreje"
tenant/{tenantId}/exports/{exportType}/{yyyy}/{mm}/{exportJobId}/{filename}
tenant/{tenantId}/imports/{importJobId}/errors/errors.xlsx
tenant/{tenantId}/documents/{documentJobId}/document.pdf
tenant/{tenantId}/datasets/{datasetName}/version={version}/date={date}/part-00000.parquet
```

Rule:

```text id="p9kzzc"
- storage key server-generated
- jangan pakai original filename sebagai path utama
- path mengandung tenant/job untuk isolation
- file metadata menyimpan display filename
- sensitive object private by default
```

---

## 24. Proxy Download vs Presigned URL

### Proxy download

```text id="w7ct85"
Client -> API -> Object Storage -> API -> Client
```

Pros:

```text id="234k61"
- authorization full di API
- audit download akurat
- bisa redaction/transform on the fly
- storage key tidak bocor
```

Cons:

```text id="lzqssk"
- API menanggung bandwidth
- cocok untuk file kecil/menengah/sensitif
```

### Presigned/SAS download

```text id="hkcetn"
Client -> API authorize -> API returns short-lived URL -> Client downloads from storage
```

AWS S3 presigned URL valid untuk periode yang ditentukan saat dibuat; dokumentasi AWS menyebut console dapat mengatur 1 menit sampai 12 jam, sedangkan CLI/SDK dapat sampai 7 hari. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/using-presigned-url.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Azure Storage SAS memberikan delegated access terbatas ke resource storage, termasuk kontrol resource mana yang bisa diakses, permission apa yang diberikan, dan durasi berlakunya SAS. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/storage/common/storage-sas-overview?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Pros:

```text id="52uyrv"
- offload bandwidth ke storage
- cocok untuk file besar
- expiry bisa dibatasi
```

Cons:

```text id="0ilnpz"
- audit actual download lebih sulit
- URL dapat dibagikan selama masih valid
- permission harus sangat sempit
```

---

## 25. Recommended Download Policy

| File type | Recommended mode |
|---|---|
| Small CSV error report | API proxy streaming |
| Sensitive PDF invoice | API proxy atau very short-lived URL |
| Large Parquet data lake export | Presigned/SAS to data platform |
| Excel report medium | API proxy or stored file |
| Regulatory evidence | API proxy + audit, or controlled signed URL |
| Public/non-sensitive static file | CDN/object storage possible |
| User-specific PII export | API proxy or very short-lived URL + audit |

Rule:

```text id="e4iax5"
If download is sensitive and audit-heavy, prefer API proxy.
If file is huge and consumer is trusted system, use tightly scoped short-lived URL.
```

---

## 26. Access Control Model

Download authorization harus berbasis domain, bukan hanya file id.

```text id="z2hqzg"
User requests fileId
  -> load file_object
  -> verify tenant
  -> verify file purpose
  -> verify domain resource permission
  -> verify expiry
  -> audit
  -> stream or issue URL
```

### Authorization examples

| File purpose | Authorization rule |
|---|---|
| Import original file | Import owner/support/admin |
| Import error report | Import owner/support/admin |
| Customer report | User can export customer data |
| Invoice PDF | User can view invoice |
| Legal document | User has case/document access |
| Parquet dataset | Data platform role + tenant/dataset permission |
| Admin audit export | Admin/support role |

Never:

```text id="q218aq"
GET /files/{fileId}/content
  -> stream if file exists
```

Always check domain access.

---

## 27. Download Audit Event

```sql id="i8msbu"
CREATE TABLE file_download_audit (
    id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_object_id  UUID NOT NULL,
    actor_user_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    action          TEXT NOT NULL,
    decision        TEXT NOT NULL,
    reason          TEXT,
    ip_address      TEXT,
    user_agent      TEXT,
    correlation_id  TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Audit event:

```json id="etn6do"
{
  "event": "file_download_authorized",
  "tenantId": "tenant_001",
  "fileObjectId": "file_001",
  "actorUserId": "user_001",
  "filePurpose": "CUSTOMER_REPORT",
  "contentType": "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
  "sizeBytes": 18432192,
  "decision": "ALLOW",
  "correlationId": "corr_001"
}
```

Untuk presigned/SAS URL, audit minimal harus mencatat URL issuance. Jika storage access logs tersedia, korelasikan dengan file id/request id.

---

## 28. Cache-Control Policy

| File | Cache-Control |
|---|---|
| Sensitive user export | `no-store` |
| Import original/error report | `no-store` |
| Invoice/legal PDF | `no-store` |
| Public static template | `public, max-age=...` |
| Internal non-sensitive report | `private, max-age=...` jika policy mengizinkan |
| Presigned object | Expiry controlled by URL + storage policy |

Default enterprise-safe:

```http id="x2s6a4"
Cache-Control: no-store
Pragma: no-cache
```

---

## 29. ETag and Checksum

Untuk stored generated files, gunakan checksum sebagai metadata.

```http id="44kjij"
ETag: "sha256-abc123..."
```

Atau simpan SHA-256 di response body/status:

```json id="dujhf4"
{
  "fileId": "file_001",
  "sha256": "abc123...",
  "sizeBytes": 18432192
}
```

Caution: object storage ETag tidak selalu sama dengan MD5/checksum, terutama multipart upload. Lebih baik simpan SHA-256 sendiri di `file_object`.

---

## 30. Range Download

Range support berguna untuk file besar/resume.

| Situation | Need Range? |
|---|---:|
| Small CSV | Tidak |
| Large PDF/video-like preview | Bisa |
| Large Parquet/ZIP | Bisa |
| API proxy from object storage | Bisa diteruskan |
| Direct generated streaming | Sulit jika tidak ada file |

Range support membutuhkan handling:

```text id="ur9hx1"
Range request
  -> validate authorization
  -> parse Range header
  -> stream byte range from storage
  -> return 206 Partial Content
  -> Content-Range
```

Jika belum perlu, jangan implement setengah matang. Untuk large object, presigned URL ke storage biasanya lebih praktis.

---

## 31. Temporary File Strategy

Untuk async export, generate ke temp file lalu upload.

```java id="0izc5t"
public class TempFileExportTarget implements AutoCloseable {

    private final Path path;

    public TempFileExportTarget(String prefix, String suffix) {
        try {
            this.path = Files.createTempFile(prefix, suffix);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new ExportException("Failed to create temp export file", e);
        }
    }

    public Path path() {
        return path;
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        try {
            Files.deleteIfExists(path);
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
            // log warning in real implementation
        }
    }
}
```

Rules:

```text id="45u68s"
- use explicit temp directory if possible
- enforce ephemeral storage limit
- cleanup in finally
- do not put secrets in temp filename
- calculate checksum before upload
```

---

## 32. Storage Upload Result

```java id="ncs4gk"
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

public record StoredFile(
        UUID fileObjectId,
        String storageProvider,
        String storageKey,
        String contentType,
        String safeFilename,
        long sizeBytes,
        String sha256Hex,
        Instant expiresAt
) {}
```

File storage interface:

```java id="w2qssu"
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.nio.file.Path;

public interface FileStorage {

    StoredFile storeGeneratedFile(
            String tenantId,
            String filePurpose,
            Path file,
            String contentType,
            String safeFilename,
            RetentionPolicy retentionPolicy
    );

    void stream(String storageKey, OutputStream output);

    DownloadUrl createTemporaryDownloadUrl(
            String storageKey,
            java.time.Duration ttl
    );
}
```

---

## 33. Streaming Stored File via API Proxy

```java id="3npq6s"
@GET
@Path("/files/{fileId}/content")
public Response downloadFile(@PathParam("fileId") UUID fileId) {
    FileObject file = fileService.getAuthorizedFile(
            CurrentTenant.required(),
            CurrentUser.required(),
            fileId
    );

    fileAuditService.recordDownloadAllowed(file, CurrentUser.required());

    StreamingOutput stream = output -> fileStorage.stream(file.storageKey(), output);

    return Response.ok(stream)
            .type(file.contentType())
            .header("Content-Disposition", contentDisposition(file.safeFilename()))
            .header("Content-Length", file.sizeBytes())
            .header("Cache-Control", cacheControl(file))
            .header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
            .header("ETag", "\"" + file.sha256Hex() + "\"")
            .build();
}

private String contentDisposition(String filename) {
    return "attachment; filename=\"" + filename + "\"";
}

private String cacheControl(FileObject file) {
    return file.sensitive() ? "no-store" : "private, max-age=300";
}
```

---

## 34. Presigned/SAS URL Issuance API

```http id="tybmo5"
POST /v1/files/{fileId}/temporary-download-url
```

Response:

```json id="jvdzxc"
{
  "fileId": "file_001",
  "url": "https://storage.example/...",
  "expiresAt": "2026-07-13T15:00:00Z"
}
```

Policy:

```text id="3k4r0z"
- authorize before issuing URL
- TTL short
- permission read-only
- single object only
- no list permission
- record audit event
- do not expose for highly sensitive docs unless approved
```

---

## 35. Retention Policy

```java id="ie2z8m"
public enum RetentionPolicy {
    SHORT_LIVED_EXPORT,
    IMPORT_ERROR_REPORT,
    OFFICIAL_DOCUMENT,
    DATASET_EXPORT,
    AUDIT_EVIDENCE
}
```

| Policy | Example TTL |
|---|---|
| Short-lived export | 7 days |
| Import error report | 30-90 days |
| Official document | Business/legal retention |
| Dataset export | Data platform lifecycle |
| Audit evidence | Regulatory retention |
| Temp file | Delete immediately after upload |

Retention must match legal/compliance/company policy.

---

## 36. Export Result Manifest

Untuk multi-file export atau Parquet, manifest penting.

```json id="86298s"
{
  "exportJobId": "exp_001",
  "exportType": "CUSTOMER_SNAPSHOT",
  "format": "PARQUET",
  "tenantId": "tenant_001",
  "schemaVersion": "customer_snapshot_v1",
  "createdAt": "2026-07-13T14:30:00Z",
  "files": [
    {
      "fileId": "file_001",
      "path": "tenant/tenant_001/exports/exp_001/part-00000.parquet",
      "sizeBytes": 268435456,
      "sha256": "abc123",
      "rowCount": 1000000
    }
  ],
  "totalRows": 1000000,
  "totalSizeBytes": 268435456
}
```

Manifest membantu downstream consumer, audit, dan retry/reconciliation.

---

## 37. Export Reconciliation

Async export harus dicek:

```text id="w973i7"
- job READY tapi file_object_id null
- file_object exists tapi storage object missing
- row_count mismatch with manifest
- temp file leftover
- expired export still downloadable
- object storage upload succeeded but DB update failed
```

Example query:

```sql id="o8mp3k"
SELECT *
FROM export_job
WHERE status = 'READY'
  AND file_object_id IS NULL;
```

```sql id="0rbm6q"
SELECT *
FROM export_job
WHERE expires_at < now()
  AND status = 'READY';
```

---

## 38. Security: Data Disclosure Controls

| Risk | Control |
|---|---|
| User exports data they cannot view | Domain authorization |
| Export includes hidden PII | Field-level permission/masking |
| URL shared externally | Short TTL, proxy for sensitive |
| Browser caches sensitive file | `Cache-Control: no-store` |
| Filename injection | Sanitized filename |
| Object storage public access | Private bucket/container |
| Tenant data leak | Tenant-scoped storage path + authz |
| Error report contains PII | Redact raw value preview |
| Download not audited | Audit table/event |
| Long-lived export | Expiry + lifecycle cleanup |

---

## 39. Field-Level Export Permission

Example:

```text id="ol9vtx"
User role: CUSTOMER_SUPPORT
  allowed fields:
    customer_id
    name
    status
  denied fields:
    email
    phone
    address
    lifetime_value
```

Export query/model should be built from allowed fields.

```java id="i44kar"
public record ExportFieldPolicy(
        boolean includeEmail,
        boolean includePhone,
        boolean includeAddress,
        boolean includeFinancialFields
) {}
```

Masking:

```java id="5slbp4"
public class CustomerExportMasker {

    public CustomerExportRow apply(Customer customer, ExportFieldPolicy policy) {
        return new CustomerExportRow(
                customer.customerId(),
                customer.name(),
                policy.includeEmail() ? customer.email() : maskEmail(customer.email()),
                policy.includePhone() ? customer.phone() : "***",
                customer.status()
        );
    }

    private String maskEmail(String email) {
        if (email == null || !email.contains("@")) {
            return "***";
        }

        String[] parts = email.split("@", 2);
        return parts[0].substring(0, 1) + "***@" + parts[1];
    }
}
```

---

## 40. Backpressure and Limits

```text id="c958ir"
Export limits:
- max rows
- max date range
- max concurrent exports per user
- max concurrent exports per tenant
- max file size
- max runtime
- max storage retained
```

API response if too large:

```json id="tip5yw"
{
  "error": {
    "code": "EXPORT_TOO_LARGE",
    "message": "The requested export is too large. Narrow the date range or use async dataset export.",
    "correlationId": "corr_001"
  }
}
```

For direct streaming, validate estimated size before starting response. Once bytes are sent, you cannot return a clean JSON error.

---

## 41. Failure Handling During Streaming

Direct streaming limitation:

```text id="6jz151"
If error occurs before response body starts:
  -> return normal error response

If error occurs after bytes are written:
  -> client receives broken/partial file
  -> server logs error
  -> cannot reliably send JSON error
```

Therefore:

```text id="lf49gh"
Use direct streaming only for predictable/low-risk exports.
Use async stored export for expensive/high-value files.
```

---

## 44. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Build seluruh file di memory | OOM |
| Direct streaming untuk report sangat besar | Timeout/broken download |
| Tidak set `Content-Disposition` | Browser behavior tidak konsisten |
| Filename dari user tanpa sanitization | Header injection/path confusion |
| Salah `Content-Type` | Client gagal buka file |
| Tidak pakai `no-store` untuk sensitive file | Data bocor via cache |
| Download by fileId tanpa domain authz | Broken access control |
| Tidak audit download | Data leak sulit diinvestigasi |
| Presigned URL terlalu lama | Link sharing risk |
| Object storage public by accident | Massive data exposure |
| Parquet sebagai user report | User tidak bisa pakai |
| Tidak simpan checksum | Integrity/reconciliation lemah |
| Temp file tidak dibersihkan | Disk penuh |
| Export query load full list | Memory spike |
| Error setelah streaming dimulai | Client dapat partial file tanpa clear error |

---

## 45. Production Checklist

```text id="jn0f89"
Format
[ ] Format sesuai use case
[ ] Content-Type benar
[ ] Extension sesuai format
[ ] File open/read test

Download headers
[ ] Content-Disposition
[ ] Filename sanitized
[ ] Cache-Control policy
[ ] X-Content-Type-Options nosniff
[ ] Content-Length if known
[ ] ETag/checksum if stored

Streaming
[ ] Uses StreamingOutput
[ ] Query incremental/cursor
[ ] No full List in memory
[ ] Handles client disconnect
[ ] Direct streaming only for safe size/time

Async export
[ ] export_job table
[ ] Worker generation
[ ] Temp file cleanup
[ ] Object storage upload
[ ] File metadata persisted
[ ] Checksum computed
[ ] Status endpoint
[ ] Expiry/retention

Security
[ ] Domain authorization
[ ] Tenant isolation
[ ] Field-level export permission
[ ] PII masking
[ ] Download audit
[ ] Private object storage
[ ] Short-lived URL if used
[ ] No raw file logs

Operations
[ ] Metrics
[ ] Structured logs
[ ] Storage lifecycle
[ ] Reconciliation
[ ] Alerts for failed exports
[ ] Cleanup expired files
```

---

## 46. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| Export is a data disclosure event | Authorization dan audit wajib |
| Format must match consumer | Jangan pilih format hanya karena mudah dibuat |
| Direct streaming must be bounded | Untuk file besar gunakan async |
| Download filename is untrusted surface | Sanitization wajib |
| Sensitive downloads should not be cached | `Cache-Control: no-store` |
| Stored generated files need checksum | Integrity dan reconciliation |
| Object storage key must be server-generated | Jangan pakai filename user sebagai path |
| Presigned/SAS URL must be short-lived and scoped | Link sharing risk |
| Field-level permission applies to export | User view permission belum tentu export-all permission |
| Export job status must be reconciled with stored file | READY harus berarti file benar-benar ada |

---

## 47. Kesimpulan Seri 18

File export/download production-ready adalah gabungan dari:

```text id="ll8sm8"
format choice
+ generation mode
+ StreamingOutput
+ async export job
+ object storage
+ HTTP headers
+ access control
+ checksum
+ audit
+ retention
```

Pola sehat:

```text id="3lv8th"
Export request
  -> authorize
  -> choose direct or async
  -> generate with incremental query
  -> stream or store
  -> set correct headers
  -> audit download
  -> expire/cleanup result
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="di6eyi"
Never build large exports entirely in memory.
Never expose file download by fileId without domain authorization.
Never use raw user filename in headers or storage path.
Never cache sensitive exports.
Never issue long-lived broad presigned/SAS URLs for sensitive files.
```
