## Response:
7/13/2026, 10:01:39 PM

Lanjut **Seri 19 dari 20**. Fokusnya observability, audit, dan lineage: bagaimana men-trace file dari upload sampai output, melihat kualitas data, dan melakukan debugging incident tanpa membuka raw PII.

# Seri 19/20 - Observability, Audit & Lineage: Metrics, Structured Logs, Correlation ID, File Lineage, Audit Trail, Data Quality Dashboard & Incident Debugging

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas bagaimana membuat file pipeline **mudah di-debug, mudah diaudit, dan bisa dipercaya**.

Pipeline file yang sudah kita bahas cukup panjang:

```text id="bu0j9n"
Upload / Export Request
   │
   ▼
File Intake
   │
   ▼
Staging Storage
   │
   ▼
Async Job
   │
   ▼
Parser / Mapper / Validator
   │
   ▼
Staging Table
   │
   ▼
Apply / Generate Output
   │
   ▼
Download / Publish Event
```

Tanpa observability dan lineage, saat ada incident kita hanya tahu:

```text id="3nky5i"
"Import gagal."
"Report salah."
"File tidak bisa di-download."
"Data customer berubah."
```

Dengan observability yang benar, kita bisa menjawab:

```text id="x5r5d7"
File apa yang diproses?
Siapa yang upload?
Checksum-nya apa?
Job mana yang memproses?
Parser versi berapa?
Row mana yang invalid?
Berapa row valid/applied?
Output file mana yang dihasilkan?
Siapa yang download?
Event apa yang dipublish?
Kapan dan di worker mana terjadi error?
```

OpenTelemetry adalah framework observability open-source untuk cloud-native software yang menyediakan API, SDK, dan collector untuk telemetry seperti traces, metrics, dan logs. Semantic Conventions OpenTelemetry memberi nama/atribut umum untuk operasi dan data agar telemetry konsisten lintas codebase/platform. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="jaqosq"
Observability, Audit & Lineage
│
├─ 1. Observability
│   ├─ metrics
│   ├─ structured logs
│   ├─ traces
│   ├─ dashboards
│   ├─ alerts
│   └─ SLOs
│
├─ 2. Audit
│   ├─ who did what
│   ├─ when
│   ├─ from where
│   ├─ decision allow/deny
│   ├─ before/after summary
│   ├─ immutable-ish audit log
│   └─ retention policy
│
├─ 3. Lineage
│   ├─ input file
│   ├─ checksum
│   ├─ job
│   ├─ parser/mapper/schema version
│   ├─ staging rows
│   ├─ domain mutations
│   ├─ generated files
│   └─ emitted events
│
├─ 4. Data Quality
│   ├─ total records
│   ├─ valid records
│   ├─ invalid records
│   ├─ top error codes
│   ├─ duplicate rate
│   ├─ null rate
│   ├─ rejected file rate
│   └─ trend by source system
│
└─ 5. Incident Debugging
    ├─ correlation id
    ├─ trace id
    ├─ job id
    ├─ file id
    ├─ tenant id
    ├─ error code
    ├─ worker id
    └─ replay/reprocess evidence
```

---

## 3. Observability vs Audit vs Lineage

| Area | Pertanyaan utama | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| **Observability** | Sistem sehat atau bermasalah? | Latency import naik, worker error rate tinggi |
| **Audit** | Siapa melakukan apa dan apakah diizinkan? | User A download invoice PDF jam 10:01 |
| **Lineage** | Data/output berasal dari mana? | customer row berasal dari file X row 20 |
| **Data Quality** | Apakah input/output valid dan trustworthy? | 12% row invalid karena `INVALID_STATUS` |
| **Incident Debugging** | Bagaimana root cause ditemukan cepat? | Trace `corr_123` menunjukkan parser timeout di worker-2 |

Rule praktis:

```text id="f8nlby"
Observability tells system behavior.
Audit tells accountable actions.
Lineage tells data origin and transformation.
Data quality tells whether the data is usable.
```

---

## 4. Correlation ID dan Trace Context

Setiap request/job/message/file harus punya correlation.

```text id="e29ky2"
HTTP Request
  correlationId = corr_001
  traceId       = trace_abc

Upload creates:
  fileId = file_001
  jobId  = imp_001

Worker logs:
  correlationId = corr_001
  traceId       = trace_abc
  fileId        = file_001
  jobId         = imp_001
```

W3C Trace Context mendefinisikan header seperti `traceparent` dan `tracestate` agar trace dapat dipropagasikan lintas sistem dan vendor; `traceparent` merepresentasikan incoming request dalam format yang bisa dipahami tracing system. ([W3C](https://www.w3.org/TR/trace-context/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Header yang direkomendasikan

```http id="e2u6aa"
traceparent: 00-0af7651916cd43dd8448eb211c80319c-b7ad6b7169203331-01
X-Correlation-Id: corr_001
```

`traceparent` untuk distributed tracing standard. `X-Correlation-Id` tetap berguna sebagai business/support-friendly id yang mudah dicari di log.

---

## 5. Correlation ID Filter di Jersey

```java id="oq8mra"
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.UUID;

@Provider
public class CorrelationIdFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    public static final String HEADER = "X-Correlation-Id";
    private static final String PROPERTY = "correlationId";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) throws IOException {
        String incoming = requestContext.getHeaderString(HEADER);

        String correlationId = isSafeCorrelationId(incoming)
                ? incoming
                : "corr-" + UUID.randomUUID();

        requestContext.setProperty(PROPERTY, correlationId);
        CorrelationContext.set(correlationId);
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
            ContainerResponseContext responseContext
    ) throws IOException {
        Object correlationId = requestContext.getProperty(PROPERTY);

        if (correlationId != null) {
            responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle(HEADER, correlationId.toString());
        }

        CorrelationContext.clear();
    }

    private boolean isSafeCorrelationId(String value) {
        return value != null
                && value.length() <= 128
                && value.matches("[a-zA-Z0-9._:-]+");
    }
}
```

```java id="5gcruj"
public final class CorrelationContext {

    private static final ThreadLocal<String> CURRENT = new ThreadLocal<>();

    private CorrelationContext() {}

    public static void set(String correlationId) {
        CURRENT.set(correlationId);
    }

    public static String get() {
        return CURRENT.get();
    }

    public static void clear() {
        CURRENT.remove();
    }
}
```

Untuk async worker, jangan bergantung pada `ThreadLocal` dari HTTP request. Simpan `correlationId` di job/message payload.

## 7. Structured Logging

Jangan log string bebas yang sulit dicari.

### Jangan

```text id="d4ynz2"
Import failed for file
```

### Lebih baik

```json id="sry8ln"
{
  "event": "file_import_failed",
  "tenantId": "tenant_001",
  "jobId": "imp_001",
  "fileId": "file_001",
  "sourceFormat": "EXCEL",
  "schemaVersion": "customer-import-v1",
  "failureCode": "CORRUPT_EXCEL_WORKBOOK",
  "correlationId": "corr_001"
}
```

OWASP Logging Cheat Sheet memberikan guidance untuk membangun logging aplikasi, khususnya security logging. OWASP juga menekankan bahwa logging tidak boleh membocorkan informasi sensitif dan perlu dirancang untuk monitoring/alerting yang aman. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Logging_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. Field Wajib di Log File Pipeline

| Field | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| `event` | Nama event log stabil |
| `timestamp` | Waktu kejadian |
| `level` | INFO/WARN/ERROR |
| `tenantId` | Multi-tenant debugging |
| `actorUserId` | Audit/user action |
| `jobId` | Trace async job |
| `fileId` | Trace file object |
| `correlationId` | Trace request/job |
| `traceId` | Distributed tracing |
| `workerId` | Debug worker |
| `sourceFormat` | CSV/Excel/PDF/etc |
| `schemaVersion` | Compatibility debugging |
| `status` | State |
| `errorCode` | Aggregation |
| `durationMs` | Performance |
| `recordCounts` | Data quality |

---

## 9. Jangan Log Ini

| Data | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Raw uploaded file content | PII/secrets/legal data |
| Full extracted PDF/Tika text | PII/legal leakage |
| Full CSV/Excel row | Bisa mengandung PII |
| Password/token/API key | Secret leak |
| Raw Authorization header | Credential leak |
| Full presigned/SAS URL | Tokenized access leak |
| Internal storage bucket/path jika sensitif | Recon/security |
| Stack trace ke client | Info leak |
| SQL query dengan raw parameter PII | Data leak |
| Large JSON payload | Cost dan PII risk |

OWASP Top 10 2025 draft area untuk logging/alerting failures menyoroti risiko sensitive information leakage ketika logging/alerting event terekspos ke user atau attacker; prinsipnya relevan untuk pipeline file karena raw file dan extracted text sering berisi data sensitif. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/Top10/2025/A09_2025-Security_Logging_and_Alerting_Failures/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. Log Redaction Helper

```java id="qk9zsh"
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

public final class LogRedactor {

    private static final Set<String> SENSITIVE_KEYS = Set.of(
            "password",
            "token",
            "accessToken",
            "refreshToken",
            "authorization",
            "apiKey",
            "secret",
            "presignedUrl",
            "sasUrl",
            "rawValue",
            "documentText"
    );

    private LogRedactor() {}

    public static Map<String, Object> redact(Map<String, Object> input) {
        return input.entrySet()
                .stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(
                        Map.Entry::getKey,
                        entry -> redactValue(entry.getKey(), entry.getValue())
                ));
    }

    private static Object redactValue(String key, Object value) {
        if (key == null) {
            return value;
        }

        if (SENSITIVE_KEYS.contains(key)) {
            return "***REDACTED***";
        }

        if (value instanceof String text && text.length() > 500) {
            return text.substring(0, 500) + "...";
        }

        return value;
    }
}
```

## 11. Metrics Model

Metrics harus menjawab pertanyaan operasional.

```text id="ps9m0o"
Are imports failing?
Are workers slow?
Which source system sends bad data?
Which format causes parser errors?
Are exports too large?
Are users downloading sensitive files?
Are jobs stuck?
```

### Core metrics

| Metric | Type | Label |
|---|---|---|
| `file_upload_total` | Counter | `tenant`, `purpose`, `format`, `result` |
| `file_intake_rejected_total` | Counter | `reason`, `format` |
| `file_processing_job_total` | Counter | `job_type`, `status` |
| `file_processing_duration_seconds` | Histogram | `job_type`, `format`, `status` |
| `file_processing_records_total` | Counter | `job_type`, `result` |
| `file_validation_errors_total` | Counter | `job_type`, `error_code`, `field` |
| `file_parser_errors_total` | Counter | `format`, `parser`, `error_code` |
| `file_worker_active_jobs` | Gauge | `worker_id`, `job_type` |
| `file_worker_heartbeat_lag_seconds` | Gauge | `worker_id` |
| `file_download_total` | Counter | `purpose`, `content_type`, `decision` |
| `file_export_duration_seconds` | Histogram | `export_type`, `format` |
| `file_storage_bytes_total` | Gauge | `purpose`, `tenant` |

OpenTelemetry Semantic Conventions mendefinisikan common names/attributes untuk telemetry seperti traces, metrics, logs, dan resources agar data observability lebih konsisten dan portable. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/specs/semconv/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Label Cardinality Warning

Jangan sembarang menjadikan ID sebagai metric label.

### Jangan

```text id="q69ctx"
file_processing_duration_seconds{jobId="imp_001"}
file_upload_total{fileName="customer-data-final-v2-real.xlsx"}
```

Ini membuat cardinality meledak.

### Aman

```text id="etn4ga"
file_processing_duration_seconds{job_type="CUSTOMER_IMPORT", format="EXCEL", status="SUCCESS"}
```

| Jangan label | Boleh di log |
|---|---|
| `jobId` | Ya |
| `fileId` | Ya |
| `filename` | Ya, sanitized/truncated |
| `userId` | Ya, jika policy mengizinkan |
| `correlationId` | Ya |
| `rawErrorMessage` | Hati-hati |

Rule:

```text id="7g3zsz"
Metrics labels must be low-cardinality.
Logs can carry high-cardinality debug identifiers.
```

---

## 13. Tracing Spans untuk File Pipeline

Trace membantu melihat waktu habis di mana.

```text id="pk6irq"
HTTP POST /customer-imports
  ├─ file.intake.stage
  ├─ db.import_job.insert
  ├─ outbox.message.insert
  └─ response 202

Worker process job
  ├─ storage.file.open
  ├─ parser.excel.open_workbook
  ├─ parser.excel.read_rows
  ├─ mapping.customer.to_canonical
  ├─ db.staging.batch_insert
  ├─ db.validation.duplicate_check
  ├─ db.apply.upsert_customer
  ├─ report.error.generate
  └─ db.job.finalize
```

### Span attributes

| Attribute | Example |
|---|---|
| `file.id` | `file_001` |
| `file.size_bytes` | `10485760` |
| `file.sha256` | avoid full hash in metrics, OK in trace/log if policy |
| `file.format` | `XLSX` |
| `job.id` | `imp_001` |
| `job.type` | `CUSTOMER_IMPORT` |
| `tenant.id` | depends on privacy policy |
| `schema.version` | `customer-import-v1` |
| `records.total` | `1000` |
| `records.invalid` | `30` |
| `error.code` | `INVALID_STATUS` |

## 14. OpenTelemetry Instrumentation Concept

```java id="7wis41"
import io.opentelemetry.api.GlobalOpenTelemetry;
import io.opentelemetry.api.trace.Span;
import io.opentelemetry.api.trace.Tracer;

public class CustomerImportWorker {

    private final Tracer tracer = GlobalOpenTelemetry
            .getTracer("com.company.file-pipeline");

    public void process(FileProcessingJob job) {
        Span span = tracer.spanBuilder("file.import.process")
                .setAttribute("job.id", job.id())
                .setAttribute("job.type", job.jobType())
                .setAttribute("file.id", job.fileObjectId())
                .setAttribute("file.format", job.sourceFormat())
                .setAttribute("schema.version", job.schemaVersion())
                .startSpan();

        try (var scope = span.makeCurrent()) {
            processInternal(job);
            span.setAttribute("job.status", "SUCCESS");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            span.recordException(e);
            span.setAttribute("job.status", "FAILED");
            span.setAttribute("error.code", classifyError(e));
            throw e;
        } finally {
            span.end();
        }
    }
}
```

Gunakan instrumentation otomatis untuk HTTP/JDBC jika tersedia, lalu tambahkan custom spans untuk domain/file-specific phases.

## 15. Audit Trail Model

Audit menjawab:

```text id="2u77gb"
Who did what, when, from where, against which resource, and what was the decision?
```

### Audit event categories

| Category | Event |
|---|---|
| Upload | `FILE_UPLOAD_SUBMITTED` |
| Intake | `FILE_ACCEPTED`, `FILE_REJECTED`, `FILE_QUARANTINED` |
| Import | `IMPORT_JOB_CREATED`, `IMPORT_JOB_COMPLETED` |
| Export | `EXPORT_JOB_CREATED`, `EXPORT_READY` |
| Download | `FILE_DOWNLOAD_ALLOWED`, `FILE_DOWNLOAD_DENIED` |
| Admin | `JOB_RETRIED`, `JOB_CANCELLED`, `DLQ_REDRIVEN` |
| Security | `UNSAFE_FILE_REJECTED`, `AV_SCAN_FAILED` |
| Data mutation | `CUSTOMER_IMPORT_APPLIED` |
| Retention | `FILE_EXPIRED`, `FILE_DELETED` |

## 16. Audit Table

```sql id="zljbk8"
CREATE TABLE audit_event (
    id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id       TEXT NOT NULL,

    actor_user_id   TEXT,
    actor_type      TEXT NOT NULL, -- USER, SYSTEM, WORKER, ADMIN
    action          TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_type   TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_id     TEXT NOT NULL,

    decision        TEXT NOT NULL, -- ALLOW, DENY, SUCCESS, FAILURE
    reason_code     TEXT,

    job_id          TEXT,
    file_object_id  UUID,
    correlation_id  TEXT,
    trace_id        TEXT,

    ip_address      TEXT,
    user_agent      TEXT,

    metadata_json   JSONB,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_audit_event_tenant_time
ON audit_event (tenant_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_audit_event_resource
ON audit_event (resource_type, resource_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_audit_event_job
ON audit_event (job_id, created_at DESC);
```

### Audit metadata guideline

```json id="e2jyka"
{
  "sourceFormat": "EXCEL",
  "schemaVersion": "customer-import-v1",
  "fileSizeBytes": 10485760,
  "sha256": "abc123...",
  "status": "PARTIAL_SUCCESS",
  "totalRecords": 1000,
  "invalidRecords": 30
}
```

Metadata boleh kaya, tetapi jangan simpan raw PII atau full row payload.

## 17. Audit Event Writer

```java id="yzjzpe"
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public class AuditService {

    private final AuditEventMapper mapper;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    public void record(AuditEventCommand command) {
        AuditEventRow row = new AuditEventRow(
                "audit-" + UUID.randomUUID(),
                command.tenantId(),
                command.actorUserId(),
                command.actorType(),
                command.action(),
                command.resourceType(),
                command.resourceId(),
                command.decision(),
                command.reasonCode(),
                command.jobId(),
                command.fileObjectId(),
                command.correlationId(),
                command.traceId(),
                command.ipAddress(),
                command.userAgent(),
                toJson(LogRedactor.redact(command.metadata())),
                Instant.now()
        );

        mapper.insert(row);
    }

    private String toJson(Map<String, Object> metadata) {
        try {
            return objectMapper.writeValueAsString(metadata);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new AuditWriteException("Failed to serialize audit metadata", e);
        }
    }
}
```

## 19. File Lineage Model

Lineage menjawab asal-usul data/output.

```text id="dn9n2j"
Input File
  fileId=file_001
  sha256=abc123
  sourceFormat=EXCEL
        │
        ▼
Import Job
  jobId=imp_001
  parserVersion=poi-5.x
  mapperVersion=customer-import-v1
        │
        ▼
Staging Rows
  jobId + rowNumber
        │
        ▼
Domain Records
  customer tenant_001/C001
        │
        ▼
Outbox Events
  CustomerUpserted evt_001
        │
        ▼
Generated Reports
  errorReport file_002
```

OpenLineage object model mendefinisikan konsep run/job/dataset metadata; `RunEvent` mendeskripsikan update state dari eksekusi job yang diemit saat runtime, sehingga bisa menjadi referensi desain lineage untuk pipeline data. ([OpenLineage](https://openlineage.io/docs/spec/object-model/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 20. Lineage Tables

### File lineage edge

```sql id="w24l75"
CREATE TABLE file_lineage_edge (
    id                  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id            TEXT NOT NULL,

    source_type          TEXT NOT NULL,
    source_id            TEXT NOT NULL,

    target_type          TEXT NOT NULL,
    target_id            TEXT NOT NULL,

    relation_type        TEXT NOT NULL,
    job_id               TEXT,
    metadata_json        JSONB,

    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_file_lineage_source
ON file_lineage_edge (tenant_id, source_type, source_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_file_lineage_target
ON file_lineage_edge (tenant_id, target_type, target_id);
```

### Relation examples

| Relation | Source → Target |
|---|---|
| `UPLOADED_AS` | HTTP request → file_object |
| `PROCESSED_BY` | file_object → import_job |
| `STAGED_AS` | import_job → staging rows/table |
| `APPLIED_TO` | staging row → domain record |
| `GENERATED` | import_job → error report file |
| `EXPORTED_AS` | export_job → file_object |
| `PUBLISHED_EVENT` | job/domain record → outbox event |

## 22. Record-Level Lineage

Untuk import critical, simpan mapping row ke domain record.

```sql id="w87goa"
CREATE TABLE import_record_lineage (
    tenant_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    job_id          TEXT NOT NULL,
    row_number      BIGINT NOT NULL,

    source_format   TEXT NOT NULL,
    source_location TEXT,

    target_type     TEXT NOT NULL,
    target_id       TEXT NOT NULL,

    action          TEXT NOT NULL, -- INSERTED, UPDATED, SKIPPED, REJECTED
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, job_id, row_number, target_type, target_id)
);
```

Example:

```text id="p7kjii"
job imp_001 row 20
  -> customer tenant_001/C001
  -> UPDATED
```

Ini sangat berguna untuk dispute:

```text id="rq69xi"
"Kenapa customer C001 berubah?"
"Karena import imp_001, file file_001, row 20, uploaded by user_123."
```

## 23. Data Quality Metrics

Data quality harus dihitung per source system, format, schema, dan periode.

| Metric | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `total_records` | Jumlah row/record diproses |
| `valid_records` | Record valid |
| `invalid_records` | Record invalid |
| `invalid_rate` | `invalid / total` |
| `duplicate_rate` | Duplicate dalam file |
| `missing_required_rate` | Missing mandatory fields |
| `unknown_enum_rate` | Code/status tidak dikenal |
| `schema_rejection_rate` | File salah schema |
| `parser_failure_rate` | File gagal parse |
| `partial_success_rate` | Banyak partial |
| `avg_processing_time` | Runtime |
| `p95_processing_time` | Tail latency |
| `records_per_second` | Throughput |

### Data quality summary table

```sql id="y8epl5"
CREATE TABLE import_data_quality_summary (
    id                  TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
    import_type          TEXT NOT NULL,
    source_system        TEXT,
    source_format        TEXT NOT NULL,
    schema_version       TEXT NOT NULL,
    summary_date         DATE NOT NULL,

    file_count           BIGINT NOT NULL,
    total_records        BIGINT NOT NULL,
    valid_records        BIGINT NOT NULL,
    invalid_records      BIGINT NOT NULL,
    duplicate_records    BIGINT NOT NULL,
    rejected_files       BIGINT NOT NULL,
    partial_success_jobs BIGINT NOT NULL,

    top_errors_json      JSONB,
    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (
        tenant_id,
        import_type,
        source_system,
        source_format,
        schema_version,
        summary_date
    )
);
```

## 25. Example Dashboard Queries

### Daily invalid rate

```sql id="b9y8lh"
SELECT
    date_trunc('day', completed_at)::date AS day,
    import_type,
    source_format,
    sum(total_records) AS total_records,
    sum(invalid_records) AS invalid_records,
    round(
        100.0 * sum(invalid_records) / nullif(sum(total_records), 0),
        2
    ) AS invalid_rate_percent
FROM import_job
WHERE completed_at >= now() - interval '30 days'
  AND status IN ('SUCCESS', 'PARTIAL_SUCCESS', 'REJECTED')
GROUP BY 1, 2, 3
ORDER BY day DESC;
```

### Top error codes

```sql id="bklamd"
SELECT
    error_code,
    field_name,
    count(*) AS error_count
FROM import_record_error
WHERE created_at >= now() - interval '7 days'
GROUP BY error_code, field_name
ORDER BY error_count DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Processing p95

```sql id="flee84"
SELECT
    import_type,
    source_format,
    percentile_cont(0.95)
        WITHIN GROUP (
            ORDER BY extract(epoch FROM (completed_at - started_at))
        ) AS p95_seconds
FROM import_job
WHERE completed_at >= now() - interval '7 days'
  AND started_at IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY import_type, source_format;
```

---

## 26. Alerting Rules

| Alert | Condition | Severity |
|---|---|---|
| Import failure spike | Failed jobs > threshold in 15 min | High |
| Parser timeout spike | `PARSER_TIMEOUT` > threshold | High |
| Invalid rate spike | Invalid rate > baseline + threshold | Medium/High |
| Queue backlog | Queued jobs > N or oldest queued age > X | High |
| Worker heartbeat stale | Worker no heartbeat > X | High |
| DLQ messages | DLQ count > 0 | High |
| Storage upload failures | Upload failure rate > threshold | High |
| Download denied spike | Denied download spike | Medium/Security |
| Quarantined file spike | Unsafe files > baseline | Security |
| Temp disk high | Ephemeral storage > 80% | High |
| Export file size spike | p95 size > threshold | Medium |
| Audit write failure | audit insert failed | Critical |

Rule:

```text id="xf0u87"
Alert on symptoms that require action.
Dashboard everything else.
```

---

## 27. SLO Ideas

| SLO | Example |
|---|---|
| Import job availability | 99.9% accepted jobs eventually reach terminal state |
| Import processing latency | 95% CSV imports under 5 minutes for <=100k rows |
| Export readiness latency | 95% standard exports ready under 10 minutes |
| Error report availability | 99% rejected/partial jobs have report within 2 minutes |
| Download availability | 99.9% authorized file downloads succeed |
| Stuck job rate | <0.1% jobs stuck beyond lease |
| Audit completeness | 100% download decisions produce audit event |

SLO harus dipisah per file type/size/format. PDF OCR dan CSV kecil tidak punya latency expectation yang sama.

---

## 28. Incident Debugging Playbook

### Scenario: “Import customer gagal”

```text id="z57xwt"
1. Minta jobId / correlationId / fileId.
2. Cek import_job status + failure_code.
3. Cek structured logs by jobId/correlationId.
4. Cek metrics around failure time.
5. Cek import_record_error summary.
6. Cek file_object metadata: size, checksum, content type.
7. Cek parser/worker logs.
8. Cek lineage: file -> job -> staging -> report.
9. Cek apakah error user-fixable atau system.
10. Jika system, cek retry/DLQ/stuck worker.
```

### SQL quick check

```sql id="z3i0lh"
SELECT *
FROM import_job
WHERE id = #{jobId};
```

```sql id="fj9fr3"
SELECT error_code, field_name, count(*)
FROM import_record_error
WHERE job_id = #{jobId}
GROUP BY error_code, field_name
ORDER BY count(*) DESC;
```

```sql id="j43uqx"
SELECT *
FROM file_lineage_edge
WHERE job_id = #{jobId}
ORDER BY created_at;
```

---

## 29. Incident Debugging: “Report salah”

```text id="fronch"
1. Identify exportJobId/fileObjectId.
2. Verify export filters/options.
3. Verify actor permission and field-level policy.
4. Check export generator version/template version.
5. Check source query snapshot time.
6. Compare row_count in job vs file/manifest.
7. Verify checksum and generated file metadata.
8. Check lineage from export job to source dataset/domain query.
9. Reproduce in lower env with same input snapshot if available.
```

Export metadata should store:

```json id="cw8ndf"
{
  "exportType": "CUSTOMER_REPORT",
  "format": "XLSX",
  "filters": {
    "status": "ACTIVE",
    "createdFrom": "2026-01-01",
    "createdTo": "2026-07-13"
  },
  "generatorVersion": "customer-report-xlsx-v3",
  "templateVersion": "customer-report-template-v2",
  "rowCount": 100000
}
```

---

## 30. Incident Debugging: “Data customer berubah”

```text id="e51jf2"
1. Identify customerId/tenantId.
2. Query domain audit/outbox event.
3. Query import_record_lineage by target_id.
4. Find jobId + rowNumber.
5. Load staging row canonical snapshot.
6. Load original file metadata/checksum.
7. Check actor who uploaded/imported.
8. Check whether update was insert/upsert/manual edit.
9. Check applied ledger for idempotency.
10. Report source evidence without exposing unrelated rows.
```

SQL:

```sql id="mylygi"
SELECT *
FROM import_record_lineage
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND target_type = 'Customer'
  AND target_id = #{customerId}
ORDER BY created_at DESC;
```

---

## 31. Reprocess and Replay Metadata

Jika perlu reprocess, simpan informasi cukup.

```text id="5fb62p"
Reprocess requires:
- original file still available
- file checksum
- parser version or current parser policy
- schema version
- mapping adapter version
- import policy
- source system
- requested options
```

Job metadata:

```json id="djoos7"
{
  "parser": {
    "name": "apache-poi",
    "version": "5.x",
    "policyVersion": "excel-import-policy-v2"
  },
  "mapper": {
    "adapter": "customer-import-excel-v1",
    "version": "2026-07-01"
  },
  "validation": {
    "policy": "PARTIAL_SUCCESS",
    "rulesVersion": "customer-import-validation-v3"
  }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="ymjerc"
If you cannot explain or reproduce a generated result, it is weak audit evidence.
```

Internal/support endpoint:

```http id="2pv3xt"
GET /v1/file-lineage/jobs/{jobId}
```

Response:

```json id="rz4fe3"
{
  "jobId": "imp_001",
  "inputFiles": [
    {
      "fileId": "file_001",

      "format": "XLSX"
    }
  ],

    "inserted": 500,
    "updated": 470,
    "rejected": 30
  },

    {
      "eventType": "CustomerUpserted",
      "count": 970
    }
  ]
}
```

Protect this endpoint. It may reveal operational and business-sensitive information.

---

## 33. Audit vs Lineage Retention

| Data | Suggested retention logic |
|---|---|
| Raw original file | Depends on legal/business retention |
| Staging raw JSON | Shorter if PII-heavy, unless audit required |
| Error report | 30-90 days typical, policy-dependent |
| Audit events | Longer, compliance-dependent |
| Lineage edges | Longer than raw file often useful |
| Metrics | Aggregated long-term |
| Logs | Shorter, cost/privacy dependent |
| Traces | Shorter, high volume |
| Generated official document | Legal retention |
| Presigned URL audit | At least security audit retention |

Prinsip:

```text id="mxvuul"
Keep enough metadata to prove lineage.
Do not keep raw sensitive content longer than necessary.
```

---

## 34. Security Logging Controls

OWASP logging guidance includes protecting logs from injection and ensuring logged data is appropriate for monitoring/security needs. For file pipeline, this means sanitize untrusted filename/error messages, avoid raw payloads, and encode values so log consumers/dashboards are not abused. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Logging_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Log injection example

User filename:

```text id="716fln"
customer.xlsx
ERROR admin logged in
```

Bad log:

```text id="jhghph"
uploaded filename=customer.xlsx
ERROR admin logged in
```

Better:

```json id="jrklrx"
{
  "event": "file_uploaded",
  "safeFilename": "customer.xlsx_ERROR_admin_logged_in"
}
```

---

## 35. Tamper Resistance

Audit log di DB biasa bisa diubah oleh admin DB, jadi jangan mengklaim immutable kecuali benar-benar didesain demikian.

Options:

| Level | Control |
|---|---|
| Basic | Append-only table convention |
| Stronger | Restrict update/delete permissions |
| Stronger | Trigger prevent update/delete |
| Stronger | Hash chain audit rows |
| Stronger | Stream audit events to external SIEM/WORM storage |
| Strongest | Dedicated immutable audit store/legal archive |

### Hash chain concept

```sql id="5p8rx6"
ALTER TABLE audit_event
ADD COLUMN previous_hash TEXT;

ALTER TABLE audit_event
ADD COLUMN event_hash TEXT;
```

Hash input:

```text id="1eosd2"
previous_hash + canonical_json(audit_event)
```

This helps detect tampering, but operational key management and verification process must exist.

---

## 36. Example Audit Trigger: Prevent Update/Delete

```sql id="m0qe7w"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION prevent_audit_mutation()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'audit_event table is append-only';
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_prevent_audit_update
BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON audit_event
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION prevent_audit_mutation();
```

This is not absolute immutability, but it prevents accidental mutation by normal application roles.

---

## 37. Operational Runbooks

### Runbook: stuck jobs

```text id="53p19r"
1. Query jobs RUNNING with locked_until < now().
2. Check worker heartbeat and pod status.
3. Check logs by jobId and workerId.
4. Determine if side effects committed.
5. Requeue if idempotency is guaranteed.
6. Mark FAILED if permanent.
7. Create incident if repeated.
```

SQL:

```sql id="k1fuer"
SELECT id, job_type, tenant_id, locked_by, locked_until, heartbeat_at
FROM file_processing_job
WHERE status = 'RUNNING'
  AND locked_until < now();
```

### Runbook: DLQ

```text id="5qv962"
1. Identify message and jobId.
2. Load job status.
3. Check failure code and attempt count.
4. Confirm whether retry is safe.
5. Re-drive only if idempotency holds.
6. Record audit event for operator action.
```

## 40. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Tidak ada correlationId di async worker | Log HTTP dan worker tidak tersambung |
| Metrics pakai `jobId` sebagai label | Cardinality explosion |
| Log raw file row | PII leak |
| Log full presigned URL | Unauthorized access risk |
| Audit download tidak dicatat | Data disclosure tidak terlacak |
| Audit event bisa diupdate/delete bebas | Bukti audit lemah |
| Lineage hanya file-level, tidak record-level | Sulit investigasi data berubah |
| Tidak simpan parser/mapper/schema version | Reprocess tidak reproducible |
| Semua error hanya `FAILED` | Dashboard tidak actionable |
| Tidak ada top error dashboard | Source system quality buruk tidak terlihat |
| Alert terlalu banyak/noisy | Alert fatigue |
| Tidak ada runbook | Incident lambat |
| Trace tidak melewati queue/message | Distributed trace putus |
| Staging retention tanpa governance | Storage/PII risk |

---

## 41. Production Checklist

```text id="mbuhvl"
Correlation & tracing
[ ] X-Correlation-Id filter
[ ] W3C traceparent propagation
[ ] Job/message carries correlationId
[ ] Worker logs include jobId/fileId/correlationId
[ ] Custom spans for major phases

Structured logs
[ ] Stable event names
[ ] Stable error codes
[ ] No raw PII/file content
[ ] Filename sanitized
[ ] Redaction helper
[ ] Logs searchable by jobId/fileId/correlationId

Metrics
[ ] Job counters by type/status
[ ] Duration histograms
[ ] Error counters by code
[ ] Worker gauges
[ ] Queue depth/stuck jobs
[ ] Download counters
[ ] No high-cardinality labels

Audit
[ ] Upload audit
[ ] Intake accept/reject audit
[ ] Import/export job audit
[ ] Download allow/deny audit
[ ] Temporary URL issuance audit
[ ] Admin action audit
[ ] Append-only control
[ ] Retention policy

Lineage
[ ] File -> job edge
[ ] Job -> generated file edge
[ ] Row -> domain record lineage if needed
[ ] Parser/mapper/schema version recorded
[ ] Outbox event lineage
[ ] Lineage query endpoint/tool

Data quality
[ ] Daily summary
[ ] Invalid rate
[ ] Top error codes
[ ] Source system quality
[ ] Partial success trend
[ ] Rejected file trend

Incident readiness
[ ] Stuck job query
[ ] DLQ runbook
[ ] Failed import debug query
[ ] Report lineage query
[ ] Reprocess metadata
[ ] Dashboard and alerts
```

---

## 42. Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| Every file/job must be traceable by correlationId | Async boundary tidak boleh memutus trace |
| Job table is not enough; logs/metrics/audit/lineage each answer different questions | Jangan campur semua menjadi satu |
| Metrics labels must be low-cardinality | Hindari `jobId`/filename sebagai label |
| Raw file content must not be logged | PII/legal/security risk |
| Download is an audit event | Data disclosure harus tercatat |
| Lineage must connect input → processing → output | Tanpa lineage, output sulit dipercaya |
| Parser/mapper/schema versions are evidence | Reproducibility |
| Error codes must be stable | Dashboard/alert/support bergantung padanya |
| Audit logs should be append-only in practice | Minimal cegah accidental mutation |
| Data quality is product feedback | Invalid rate tinggi berarti source/template/process bermasalah |

---

## 43. Kesimpulan Seri 19

Observability, audit, dan lineage membuat file pipeline bisa **dioperasikan**, bukan hanya bisa berjalan.

Pola sehat:

```text id="8badzg"
Request
  -> correlation id / trace context
  -> structured logs
  -> metrics
  -> audit event
  -> lineage edge
  -> data quality summary
  -> dashboard / alert / runbook
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="4wv69f"
Never build a file pipeline that cannot explain what happened.
Never log raw sensitive file content.
Never use high-cardinality IDs as metric labels.
Never allow downloads without audit.
Never generate output files without lineage back to input/job/schema.
```

7/13/2026, 10:04:40 PM

Lanjut **Seri 20 dari 20**. Ini seri penutup utama: testing, deployment, dan runtime Kubernetes supaya seluruh file pipeline siap production, bukan hanya benar di local/dev.

# Seri 20/20 - Testing, Deployment & Kubernetes Runtime: Test Fixtures, Contract Tests, Performance Tests, Resource Limits, Temp Storage, Security Scans & Production Readiness

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini adalah penutup utama untuk topik:

> **Files Serialization/Deserialization/Mapping, Extraction, Processing & Generation in Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice Application**

Fokusnya bukan lagi format spesifik, tetapi bagaimana memastikan seluruh file pipeline:

```text id="3yoe5p"
- benar secara fungsional
- aman terhadap file berbahaya
- tahan file besar
- tidak membuat API pod crash
- bisa dipantau
- bisa di-deploy di Kubernetes
- bisa di-debug saat incident
- siap production
```

Kubernetes menyediakan probes untuk memonitor health container; kubelet menjalankan probe secara periodik dan dapat me-restart container yang tidak sehat atau berhenti mengirim traffic ke container yang belum siap. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/probes/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk file pipeline, ini penting karena API pod dan worker pod punya karakteristik berbeda: API harus cepat dan responsive, sementara worker boleh CPU/memory-heavy tetapi harus dikontrol.

## 3. Testing Pyramid untuk File Pipeline

```text id="hml11i"
File Pipeline Testing Pyramid

        ┌────────────────────────┐
        │ Manual exploratory      │
        │ UAT with real templates │
        └───────────▲────────────┘
                    │
        ┌───────────┴────────────┐
        │ End-to-end tests        │
        │ API + worker + DB       │
        └───────────▲────────────┘
                    │
        ┌───────────┴────────────┐
        │ Integration tests       │

        └───────────▲────────────┘
                    │

        └───────────▲────────────┘
                    │

        └────────────────────────┘
```

### Fokus tiap level

| Level | Fokus |
|---|---|
| Unit | Mapping, normalization, validator, filename sanitizer, policy |
| Parser fixture | CSV/Excel/PDF/XML/JSON edge cases |
| Contract | Header/schema/template/API compatibility |
| Integration | PostgreSQL/MyBatis, storage, queue, worker lifecycle |
| E2E | Upload → job → process → report/download |
| Performance | Large file, memory, throughput, DB load |
| Security | Malicious file, XXE, zip bomb, path traversal |
| Regression | File/template lama tetap diproses |

## 5. Fixture Metadata

Untuk file testing yang banyak, simpan manifest.

```json id="3w8aui"
{
  "fixture": "customers-v1-valid.csv",
  "format": "CSV",
  "schemaVersion": "customer-import-v1",
  "expected": {
    "totalRecords": 3,
    "validRecords": 3,
    "invalidRecords": 0,
    "status": "SUCCESS"
  },
  "notes": "Basic valid customer import"
}
```

Manfaat:

```text id="0lu5cn"
- test case lebih eksplisit
- fixture bisa direview
- regression lebih mudah
- expected behavior terdokumentasi
```

## 6. Unit Tests yang Wajib

| Component | Test |
|---|---|
| Filename sanitizer | path traversal, unicode, newline, length |
| MIME policy | extension/MIME mismatch |
| Checksum | same file same SHA-256 |
| CSV mapper | trim, required, enum, date |
| Excel cell reader | string/date/formula/blank/error |
| XML parser config | XXE blocked |
| JSON ObjectMapper | unknown field strict/lenient |
| Error code mapper | stable taxonomy |
| Source reference | row/sheet/path preserved |
| Idempotency key | deterministic |
| Retry policy | retryable vs non-retryable |
| Export filename | safe and correct extension |
| CSV export escape | spreadsheet formula injection |

---

## 7. Mapper Unit Test Example

```java id="ml075i"
@Test
void shouldMapCsvRowToCanonicalInput() {
    RawCustomerCsvRow raw = new RawCustomerCsvRow(
            SourceReference.csv("file_001", "CRM", "customer-import-v1", 2),
            " 000123 ",
            " Alice ",
            "ALICE@EXAMPLE.COM ",
            "ACTIVE",
            "08123456789",
            "1990-01-01"
    );

    MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> result = mapper.fromCsv(raw);

    assertTrue(result.isValid());
    assertEquals("000123", result.value().customerId());
    assertEquals("Alice", result.value().name());
    assertEquals("alice@example.com", result.value().email());
    assertEquals(CustomerStatus.ACTIVE, result.value().status());
    assertEquals(2L, result.value().source().rowNumber());
}
```

---

## 8. Validation Error Test Example

```java id="v9sogk"
@Test
void shouldReturnActionableErrorForInvalidEmail() {
    RawCustomerCsvRow raw = new RawCustomerCsvRow(
            SourceReference.csv("file_001", "CRM", "customer-import-v1", 10),
            "C001",
            "Alice",
            "not-an-email",
            "ACTIVE",
            null,
            null
    );

    MappingResult<CanonicalCustomerInput> result = mapper.fromCsv(raw);

    assertFalse(result.isValid());

    MappingError error = result.errors().get(0);
    assertEquals("email", error.field());
    assertEquals("INVALID_EMAIL", error.errorCode());
    assertEquals("not-an-email", error.rawValue());
    assertEquals(10L, error.source().rowNumber());
}
```

---

## 9. Parser Fixture Tests

### CSV

```java id="o449eq"
@Test
void shouldRejectCsvWithMissingRequiredHeader() {
    Path fixture = fixture("csv/invalid/missing-header.csv");

    CsvImportResult result = csvParser.parse(fixture, CustomerCsvSchema.v1());

    assertEquals("REJECTED", result.status());
    assertTrue(result.fileErrors().stream()
            .anyMatch(error -> error.errorCode().equals("MISSING_REQUIRED_HEADER")));
}
```

### Excel

```java id="rmu54b"
@Test
void shouldRejectFormulaCellWhenPolicyDisallowsFormula() {
    Path fixture = fixture("excel/invalid/formula-cell.xlsx");

    ExcelImportResult result = excelParser.parse(
            fixture,
            ExcelImportPolicy.rejectFormulaCells()
    );

    assertTrue(result.recordErrors().stream()
            .anyMatch(error -> error.errorCode().equals("FORMULA_CELL_NOT_ALLOWED")));
}
```

### XML XXE

```java id="ei8jwq"
@Test
void shouldRejectXmlExternalEntity() {
    Path fixture = fixture("xml/security/xxe.xml");

    assertThrows(XmlSecurityException.class, () ->
            secureXmlReader.read(fixture, CustomerImportXml.class)
    );
}
```

OWASP File Upload Cheat Sheet merekomendasikan validasi extension allowlist, validasi file type, tidak percaya `Content-Type`, mengganti filename dengan nama generated application, membatasi panjang/nama file, membatasi ukuran file, dan memastikan hanya user authorized yang dapat upload. Semua ini harus punya automated tests, bukan hanya checklist manual. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. Golden File Tests

Golden file test memastikan output tidak berubah tanpa sadar.

Contoh:

```text id="y5zy8y"
input:
  customers-invalid.csv

expected output:
  expected-error-report.csv
```

Test:

```java id="psssoe"
@Test
void shouldGenerateStableCsvErrorReport() {
    ImportJobResult result = importService.processFixture(
            fixture("csv/invalid/customers-invalid.csv")
    );

    String actual = Files.readString(result.errorReportPath());
    String expected = Files.readString(fixture("golden/expected-error-report.csv"));

    assertEquals(normalizeLineEndings(expected), normalizeLineEndings(actual));
}
```

Untuk Excel/PDF, golden binary comparison sering rapuh karena timestamp/metadata. Lebih baik buka file dan assert isi penting:

```text id="6pczjk"
Excel:
- sheet exists
- header exists
- row count
- specific cell values
- no unresolved placeholders

PDF:
- page count
- text contains invoice number
- metadata expected
- optional render smoke test
```

---

## 11. Integration Tests dengan PostgreSQL

Untuk stack PostgreSQL/MyBatis, test integration wajib. Testcontainers Postgres module menyediakan PostgreSQL container yang bisa dijalankan dari Java tests; dokumentasi Testcontainers Java menunjukkan usage dengan `PostgreSQLContainer`. ([java.testcontainers.org](https://java.testcontainers.org/modules/databases/postgres/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```java id="y15wv4"
@Testcontainers
class CustomerImportPersistenceIT {

    @Container
    static PostgreSQLContainer<?> postgres =
            new PostgreSQLContainer<>("postgres:17")
                    .withDatabaseName("testdb")
                    .withUsername("test")
                    .withPassword("test");

    @Test
    void shouldStageAndApplyCustomerImport() {
        DataSource dataSource = createDataSource(
                postgres.getJdbcUrl(),
                postgres.getUsername(),
                postgres.getPassword()
        );

        runMigrations(dataSource);

        CustomerImportRepository repository =
                createRepository(dataSource);

        repository.insertStagingRows(sampleRows());
        repository.applyImport("imp_001", "user_001");

        CustomerRow customer = repository.findCustomer("tenant_001", "C001");

        assertNotNull(customer);
        assertEquals("Alice", customer.name());
    }
}
```

Testcontainers JUnit 5 integration mendukung container per test method atau container shared per test class melalui extension Jupiter. ([java.testcontainers.org](https://java.testcontainers.org/test_framework_integration/junit_5/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Integration Tests: Storage & Queue

### Object storage fake/local

Pilihan:

```text id="xbj6s2"
- filesystem fake storage for unit/integration
- MinIO container for S3-compatible integration
- cloud storage emulator if available
- real dev bucket only for limited smoke tests
```

### Queue

Pilihan:

```text id="n26fi7"
- in-memory fake queue for unit tests
- RabbitMQ Testcontainer for integration
- PostgreSQL queue table integration
- contract test for message payload
```

Test scenarios:

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| DB job created but queue publish fails | Outbox retries |
| Worker crashes before ack | Message redelivered / job retry |
| Duplicate message | Idempotent, no duplicate apply |
| DLQ message | Job linked to dead-letter record |
| Cancel request | Worker stops at checkpoint |

---

## 13. API Contract Tests

API contract perlu menjamin:

```text id="rs8dio"
- endpoint path stable
- status code stable
- request/response JSON shape stable
- error code stable
- links stable
- Content-Type/Content-Disposition correct
```

Example test:

```java id="tq71ot"
@Test
void uploadShouldReturnAcceptedJobResponse() {
    Response response = client.target(baseUrl)
            .path("/v1/customer-imports")
            .request()
            .post(multipartWithFixture("csv/valid/customers-v1-valid.csv"));

    assertEquals(202, response.getStatus());

    ImportSubmittedResponse body = response.readEntity(ImportSubmittedResponse.class);

    assertNotNull(body.jobId());
    assertEquals("QUEUED", body.status());
    assertTrue(body.links().containsKey("status"));
}
```

### Error contract test

```java id="9vjq09"
@Test
void missingFileShouldReturnProblemDetails() {
    Response response = client.target(baseUrl)
            .path("/v1/customer-imports")
            .request()
            .post(emptyMultipart());

    assertEquals(400, response.getStatus());
    assertEquals("application/problem+json", response.getHeaderString("Content-Type"));

    ProblemDetails problem = response.readEntity(ProblemDetails.class);

    assertEquals("MISSING_FILE_PART", problem.errorCode());
    assertNotNull(problem.correlationId());
}
```

## 15. Performance Tests

File pipeline perlu diuji dengan volume realistis.

```text id="3m8dbz"
Performance scenarios:
- CSV 10k / 100k / 1M rows
- Excel 10k / 100k rows
- JSON array 100k records
- XML 100k elements
- PDF 10 / 100 / 500 pages
- Tika extraction for mixed docs
- Parquet export 1M / 10M rows
- Error report with 100k errors
```

Metrics yang diukur:

| Metric | Target |
|---|---|
| Processing duration | p50/p95/p99 |
| Records/second | Throughput |
| Memory usage | Peak heap |
| CPU usage | Parser/generator load |
| Temp disk usage | `/tmp`, `emptyDir` |
| DB write rate | staging/apply |
| DB transaction time | batch commit |
| Queue lag | time queued |
| Worker restart rate | stability |
| Error report generation time | user feedback latency |

---

## 16. Performance Test Harness

```java id="6zk5z2"
@Test
void shouldImportLargeCsvWithinBudget() {
    Path csv = largeFixtureGenerator.generateCsv(100_000);

    Instant start = Instant.now();

    ImportJobResult result = importService.process(csv);

    Duration duration = Duration.between(start, Instant.now());

    assertEquals("SUCCESS", result.status());
    assertEquals(100_000, result.totalRecords());
    assertTrue(duration.toSeconds() < 120);
    assertTrue(memoryProbe.peakHeapMb() < 1024);
}
```

Jangan masukkan semua performance test berat ke unit test pipeline default. Pisahkan:

```text id="tgetyl"
- fast tests: setiap PR
- medium integration tests: PR/main branch
- heavy performance tests: nightly/release candidate
```

---

## 17. Security Tests

Security test cases:

```text id="9r8gq1"
Upload/input:
[ ] extension spoofing
[ ] fake Content-Type
[ ] path traversal filename
[ ] huge file
[ ] empty file
[ ] double extension
[ ] macro-enabled Office file
[ ] encrypted PDF
[ ] corrupt file
[ ] zip bomb-like OOXML
[ ] XML XXE
[ ] XML entity expansion
[ ] CSV formula injection
[ ] JSON deep nesting
[ ] JSON huge string
[ ] unauthorized upload
[ ] unauthorized download
```

OWASP menjelaskan unrestricted file upload sebagai risiko besar karena upload file bisa menjadi langkah awal attacker untuk memasukkan payload ke sistem; kontrol upload harus dilakukan berlapis. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/www-community/vulnerabilities/Unrestricted_File_Upload?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 18. Dependency & Container Security

Checklist:

```text id="h0qcep"
[ ] Maven dependency scan
[ ] transitive dependency review for parsers
[ ] CVE scan for PDFBox/POI/Tika/Jackson
[ ] container image scan
[ ] base image pinned
[ ] no unnecessary OS packages
[ ] non-root user
[ ] read-only root filesystem where possible
[ ] secret not baked into image
[ ] SBOM generated if company requires
```

Parser libraries seperti PDFBox, POI, Tika, Jackson, XML parsers, dan image libraries adalah attack surface. Patch cadence harus jelas.

---

## 19. Kubernetes Runtime Architecture

Rekomendasi pemisahan deployment:

```text id="cj9fp6"
file-api Deployment
  ├─ Jersey endpoints
  ├─ upload/download boundary
  ├─ creates jobs
  └─ no heavy parsing

file-worker Deployment
  ├─ CSV/Excel/JSON/XML import
  ├─ validation
  ├─ staging/apply
  └─ moderate CPU/memory

document-worker Deployment
  ├─ PDF/Tika/DOCX extraction
  ├─ PDF/DOCX generation
  └─ higher memory/temp disk

ocr-worker Deployment
  ├─ OCR pages/images
  ├─ CPU-heavy
  └─ separate scaling/profile

export-worker Deployment
  ├─ Excel/PDF/Parquet generation
  ├─ object storage upload
  └─ async export jobs
```

Kenapa dipisah?

| Reason | Benefit |
|---|---|
| Different resource profile | CPU/memory tuning tepat |
| Different scaling trigger | API traffic vs queue depth |
| Different failure blast radius | PDF parser crash tidak kill API |
| Different security context | Worker can have storage access |
| Different temp storage | OCR/export butuh disk lebih besar |

---

## 20. Kubernetes Resource Requests/Limits

Kubernetes resource management memakai requests untuk scheduling dan limits untuk enforcement; CPU/memory/ephemeral-storage dapat dideklarasikan pada container. Untuk file pipeline, ephemeral storage penting karena parser/generator sering memakai temp file. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/storage/volumes/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### API pod

```yaml id="upp18x"
resources:
  requests:
    cpu: "250m"
    memory: "512Mi"
    ephemeral-storage: "512Mi"
  limits:
    cpu: "1"
    memory: "1Gi"
    ephemeral-storage: "1Gi"
```

### Worker pod

```yaml id="eh7asi"
resources:
  requests:
    cpu: "500m"
    memory: "1Gi"
    ephemeral-storage: "2Gi"
  limits:
    cpu: "2"
    memory: "2Gi"
    ephemeral-storage: "8Gi"
```

### OCR/export heavy worker

```yaml id="hn521x"
resources:
  requests:
    cpu: "1"
    memory: "2Gi"
    ephemeral-storage: "5Gi"
  limits:
    cpu: "4"
    memory: "8Gi"
    ephemeral-storage: "20Gi"
```

Initial values harus divalidasi lewat performance tests.

---

## 21. emptyDir untuk Temp Storage

Kubernetes `emptyDir` dibuat saat Pod ditempatkan di node dan akan dihapus permanen saat Pod dihapus dari node. `emptyDir` bisa diberi `sizeLimit`; jika `medium: "Memory"` digunakan, volume menjadi tmpfs dan file yang ditulis akan menghitung terhadap memory limit container. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/storage/volumes/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Disk-backed emptyDir

```yaml id="3rbctn"
volumes:
  - name: file-temp
    emptyDir:
      sizeLimit: 8Gi

containers:
  - name: file-worker
    volumeMounts:
      - name: file-temp
        mountPath: /var/app/tmp
```

### Jangan sembarang memory-backed emptyDir

```yaml id="04vnf7"
emptyDir:
  medium: Memory
```

Ini cepat, tetapi file temp besar akan makan memory container dan bisa menyebabkan OOM.

Rule:

```text id="lgmeha"
Use disk-backed emptyDir for large file temp.
Use memory-backed emptyDir only for small, bounded, performance-critical temp.
```

---

## 22. JVM Container Settings

Untuk Java 17+ di container, tetap explicit dengan memory behavior.

```text id="lqkozp"
JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS:
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=70
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=30
  -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError
```

Contoh:

```yaml id="q2951u"
env:
  - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
    value: >-
      -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=70
      -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=30
      -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError
      -Djava.io.tmpdir=/var/app/tmp
```

`-Djava.io.tmpdir` penting agar library yang memakai temp file tidak menulis ke lokasi tidak terkontrol.

---

## 23. Health Probes

Kubernetes probe bisa berupa HTTP, TCP, gRPC, atau exec. Liveness digunakan untuk restart container yang tidak sehat; readiness menentukan apakah Pod siap menerima traffic; startup probe dapat memberi waktu startup lebih panjang sebelum liveness aktif. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/probes/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### API pod probes

```yaml id="yiyh4e"
livenessProbe:
  httpGet:
    path: /health/live
    port: 8080
  initialDelaySeconds: 30
  periodSeconds: 10
  timeoutSeconds: 2
  failureThreshold: 3

readinessProbe:
  httpGet:
    path: /health/ready
    port: 8080
  initialDelaySeconds: 10
  periodSeconds: 5
  timeoutSeconds: 2
  failureThreshold: 3

startupProbe:
  httpGet:
    path: /health/startup
    port: 8080
  periodSeconds: 5
  failureThreshold: 30
```

### Probe semantics

| Probe | Should check |
|---|---|
| Liveness | JVM/server event loop alive, not deep dependencies |
| Readiness | App can handle request, DB/queue if required |
| Startup | App finished boot/migrations/config load |

Jangan buat liveness bergantung pada DB. Kalau DB down, semua pods restart loop tanpa memperbaiki DB.

---

## 24. Worker Readiness

Worker readiness berbeda dari API.

Worker ready jika:

```text id="jt9chv"
- config loaded
- DB reachable
- queue reachable
- storage reachable if required
- not in graceful shutdown
```

Worker liveness:

```text id="wmughr"
- process alive
- event loop/poller alive
- not deadlocked
```

Worker heartbeat ke DB/job table tetap dibutuhkan karena Kubernetes liveness hanya tahu container, bukan status job individual.

---

## 25. Graceful Shutdown

Deployment rollout akan mengirim SIGTERM ke pod. Worker harus:

```text id="dv5ypc"
1. stop claiming new jobs
2. finish current chunk
3. persist checkpoint
4. release or extend lock
5. ack/nack message correctly
6. exit before grace period
```

Kubernetes YAML:

```yaml id="t9w80s"
terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 120
```

Spring/MicroProfile/Jersey runtime detail berbeda, tetapi prinsipnya sama: jangan kill job di tengah batch commit.

---

## 26. Security Context

```yaml id="z9puaw"
securityContext:
  runAsNonRoot: true
  runAsUser: 10001
  runAsGroup: 10001
  allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
  readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
  capabilities:
    drop:
      - ALL
```

Jika `readOnlyRootFilesystem: true`, pastikan temp dir dan log dir yang perlu ditulis memakai mounted volume.

```yaml id="ql09zr"
volumeMounts:
  - name: file-temp
    mountPath: /var/app/tmp
  - name: app-cache
    mountPath: /var/app/cache
```

---

## 27. ConfigMaps, Secrets, and Runtime Config

Config:

```text id="mx3nm8"
- max upload size
- allowed file types
- parser limits
- worker concurrency
- batch size
- retry policy
- temp directory
- storage bucket/container name
- feature flags
```

Secrets:

```text id="grlptd"
- DB password
- storage credentials if not workload identity
- queue credentials
- encryption keys
- signing keys
```

Rules:

```text id="5e516c"
- do not bake secrets into image
- prefer workload identity/IAM role where possible
- rotate secrets
- do not log secrets
- separate config per environment
```

---

## 28. Network & RBAC

Minimum access:

| Component | Needs |
|---|---|
| API pod | DB job tables, storage upload/download, queue/outbox |
| Import worker | DB staging/target, storage read original, queue |
| Export worker | DB read, storage write, queue |
| Document worker | storage read/write, DB job/result |
| OCR worker | storage read/write, DB result |
| Cleanup job | storage delete, DB update |
| Reconciliation job | DB read/write, maybe queue |

Do not give all workers broad cloud admin access.

---

## 29. Deployment YAML Skeleton

```yaml id="jcvmlg"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: file-worker
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: file-worker
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: file-worker
    spec:
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 120
      securityContext:
        runAsNonRoot: true
        runAsUser: 10001
        runAsGroup: 10001
      containers:
        - name: file-worker
          image: registry.example.com/file-worker:1.0.0
          env:
            - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
              value: >-
                -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=70
                -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError
                -Djava.io.tmpdir=/var/app/tmp
            - name: WORKER_CONCURRENCY
              value: "2"
          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "1Gi"
              ephemeral-storage: "2Gi"
            limits:
              cpu: "2"
              memory: "2Gi"
              ephemeral-storage: "8Gi"
          volumeMounts:
            - name: file-temp
              mountPath: /var/app/tmp
          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: 8081
            periodSeconds: 10
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/ready
              port: 8081
            periodSeconds: 10
      volumes:
        - name: file-temp
          emptyDir:
            sizeLimit: 8Gi
```

---

## 30. Horizontal Scaling

Scale by:

```text id="v1lcdc"
API pod:
- HTTP request rate
- CPU
- latency

Worker pod:
- queue depth
- oldest queued job age
- CPU/memory
- active jobs per worker

OCR worker:
- OCR queue depth
- CPU
- job age

Export worker:
- export queue depth
- file generation duration
```

HPA CPU-only sering kurang untuk queue worker. Pertimbangkan custom metrics seperti queue depth / oldest message age.

---

## 31. Worker Concurrency

Concurrency harus dikontrol di dua level:

```text id="90vyj8"
Kubernetes replicas × jobs per pod = total concurrent jobs
```

Contoh:

```text id="idpk4m"
replicas = 3
WORKER_CONCURRENCY = 2
total concurrent jobs = 6
```

Jika tiap job bisa memakai 1-2GB memory, jangan set concurrency tinggi.

Rule:

```text id="ulg4e2"
Tune concurrency from performance tests, not guesswork.
```

---

## 32. Database Migration Readiness

Karena pipeline file banyak memakai staging/job/error/audit tables, migrasi DB harus aman.

Checklist:

```text id="ou2jyn"
[ ] migration backward-compatible
[ ] add nullable columns first
[ ] backfill separately
[ ] add constraints after data valid
[ ] large index created concurrently where needed
[ ] rollback plan
[ ] migration tested on production-like data volume
[ ] worker rollout coordinated with schema version
```

PostgreSQL large table index:

```sql id="y5fcbl"
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_import_job_status
ON import_job (status, created_at);
```

Jangan jalankan migrasi blocking besar saat traffic/import aktif tanpa plan.

---

## 33. Feature Flags

Gunakan feature flag untuk:

```text id="1kd58j"
- enable new file format
- enable new schema version
- switch parser implementation
- enable async export
- enable OCR route
- enable partial success
- enable Parquet export
- enable new validation rule
```

Flag harus bisa scoped:

```text id="hxrf83"
- by environment
- by tenant
- by import type
- by source system
- by user role
```

---

## 34. Release Strategy

```text id="vfbveb"
1. Deploy DB migration backward-compatible.
2. Deploy code with new feature disabled.
3. Run smoke tests.
4. Enable feature for internal tenant.
5. Process test fixtures in staging/prod-like env.
6. Enable for pilot tenant/source system.
7. Monitor dashboard and alerts.
8. Roll out gradually.
9. Keep rollback path.
```

For file schema changes:

```text id="5be6xm"
- support old and new schema in parallel
- publish template version
- reject unknown schema explicitly
- monitor usage by schema version
- deprecate old schema with notice
```

---

## 35. Smoke Tests After Deploy

Smoke tests should verify:

```text id="afy9dg"
API:
[ ] health ready
[ ] upload small valid CSV
[ ] job created
[ ] worker processes job
[ ] status becomes SUCCESS
[ ] result counts correct
[ ] error report generation for invalid fixture
[ ] download works
[ ] unauthorized download denied
[ ] audit event written
```

Optional:

```text id="afcf8m"
[ ] Excel fixture
[ ] PDF extraction fixture
[ ] async export fixture
[ ] object storage upload/download
[ ] queue/outbox publish
```

---

## 36. Production Runbook

### Import stuck

```text id="lqso80"
1. Query job by jobId.
2. Check status, locked_by, locked_until, heartbeat_at.
3. Check worker pod logs by workerId/jobId.
4. Check DB locks and queue depth.
5. Check if checkpoint exists.
6. If lock expired and idempotent, requeue.
7. If repeated failure, mark failed and attach incident.
```

### Temp storage full

```text id="4f925j"
1. Check pod ephemeral storage usage.
2. Identify job/file consuming temp.
3. Check cleanup failures.
4. Scale down/offending worker if needed.
5. Increase emptyDir/ephemeral limits only after root cause.
6. Verify temp cleanup in finally blocks.
```

### Parser CVE

```text id="446smo"
1. Identify affected library and formats.
2. Disable affected format if needed via feature flag.
3. Patch dependency.
4. Rebuild image.
5. Run security fixtures.
6. Deploy gradually.
7. Re-enable feature.
```

---

## 37. Production Readiness Checklist

```text id="il44ik"
Testing
[ ] Unit tests for mapper/validator/policy
[ ] Parser fixture tests
[ ] Security fixture tests
[ ] Golden output tests
[ ] PostgreSQL/MyBatis integration tests
[ ] Storage integration tests
[ ] Queue/outbox integration tests
[ ] API contract tests
[ ] Performance tests
[ ] Regression suite

Security
[ ] OWASP upload controls
[ ] XXE tests
[ ] Zip bomb/OOXML size guard
[ ] Formula injection escape
[ ] Dependency scan
[ ] Container image scan
[ ] Non-root container
[ ] Secret handling
[ ] Authorization tests
[ ] Audit tests

Kubernetes
[ ] API and worker separated
[ ] Resource requests/limits
[ ] Ephemeral storage limits
[ ] emptyDir mounted for temp
[ ] java.io.tmpdir set
[ ] Probes configured
[ ] Graceful shutdown
[ ] SecurityContext
[ ] HPA/scaling plan
[ ] Network/RBAC minimal access

Database
[ ] Staging tables
[ ] Job tables
[ ] Error tables
[ ] Audit/lineage tables
[ ] Idempotency constraints
[ ] Migrations tested
[ ] Index strategy
[ ] Retention cleanup
[ ] Reconciliation jobs

Operations
[ ] Dashboards
[ ] Alerts
[ ] Runbooks
[ ] DLQ process
[ ] Stuck job reaper
[ ] Cleanup job
[ ] Backup/restore consideration
[ ] Incident debug queries
[ ] Feature flags
[ ] Rollback plan
```

## 38. Common Production Failures

| Failure | Root cause | Prevention |
|---|---|---|
| API pod OOM | File parsed in request path | Async worker |
| Worker OOM | Batch too large / Excel/PDF heavy | Batch tuning, memory limits |
| Pod evicted | Temp disk full | ephemeral-storage + cleanup |
| Import duplicated | Retry not idempotent | ledger/unique keys |
| Job stuck RUNNING | Worker died no reaper | heartbeat/lease/reconciliation |
| Download unauthorized | fileId-only access | domain authorization |
| Error report missing | failure before generation | finalization/reconciliation |
| Parser CVE exposed | dependencies outdated | scanning/patching |
| Production template breaks | no fixture/golden test | template validation |
| DB slow | too many indexes/large transaction | batch/phase commit |
| Queue backlog | insufficient workers/backpressure | scaling/custom metrics |
| Invalid source spike unnoticed | no data quality dashboard | quality metrics/alerts |

---

## 39. Final Senior-Level Invariants

| Invariant | Makna |
|---|---|
| File handling is a platform capability, not just utility code | Butuh architecture, security, ops |
| API pod must stay thin | Heavy processing belongs to workers |
| Every parser/generator needs limits | Size, rows, pages, chars, time, temp |
| Every file job must be idempotent | Retry/crash is normal |
| Every output/download is data disclosure | Authz + audit |
| Every import needs source reference | Debug/error/audit |
| Every official artifact needs checksum/lineage | Trust/reproducibility |
| Kubernetes resources must include ephemeral storage | File pipeline uses disk |
| Tests must include malicious and edge-case files | Happy path tidak cukup |
| Production readiness includes runbooks | System without runbook is not ready |

---

## 40. Kesimpulan Seri 20

Testing, deployment, dan Kubernetes runtime adalah lapisan yang memastikan file pipeline benar-benar bisa hidup di production.

Pola sehat:

```text id="lzyan8"
Design file pipeline
  -> build format-specific tests
  -> add security fixtures
  -> add DB/storage/queue integration tests
  -> deploy API and workers separately
  -> set resource + ephemeral storage limits
  -> configure probes and graceful shutdown
  -> monitor dashboards and alerts
  -> maintain runbooks and rollback plan
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="gpixbl"
Never ship file processing with only happy-path tests.
Never parse large/untrusted files inside API pods.
Never deploy workers without resource and temp storage limits.
Never skip security fixtures for upload/parsing.
Never call a file pipeline production-ready without observability, audit, and runbooks.
```

Ada **4 optional deep dive** yang paling relevan:

2. **Optional B - Regulatory/Audit-Grade Document Processing**

4. **Optional D - File Processing Integration with Camunda Workflow**

Rekomendasi saya: lanjut ke **Optional A - High-Volume Import Architecture for Millions of Rows**, karena ini paling langsung memperdalam batch size, streaming, staging, PostgreSQL performance, worker scaling, checkpointing, dan reconciliation.
